# Foreldrabæklingur — flokkar og fyrirtæki (rannsókn)

*17. nóvember 2026. 228 flokkar í 21 geirum · 1826 færslur · 925 einstök fyrirtæki.*

**What this is.** A catalogue of narrow categories — each one a concrete problem a new parent faces (pregnancy → toddler, ~0–3 years) — and the Icelandic companies that could fill each slot in a SVIF-style book (one company per category, with parent discounts). Structure mirrors the existing SVIF category cards: geiri (sector) → flokkur (category/slot).

**How to read it.**
- `Mögulegir auglýsendur` = number of candidate companies found for that slot (more = more competition = higher slot price in your model). These are *candidates*, not customers.
- ✓ = the researcher saw the company's own page confirming the offering. ○ = seen only in a third-party mention (lead — check before calling).
- ⚠ = automatic check found a problem with the website (no response, HTTP error, or the company name was not found on the page).
- *Tenging* = how tied the category is to having a baby (Sterk = exists because of babies; Miðlungs = everyone uses it but parents need more; Veik = generic household). *Eftirspurn* = share of parents who need it (Allir / Margir / Sumir). *Stig* = when it matters.
- Contact details are intentionally not included: this is a company/category list, not a contact list.

## How reliable is this?

- **Found on live pages, not from memory.** Research agents read company sites, partner/discount lists and directories. ✓ = confirmed on the company's own page (1669 entries); ○ = a lead seen elsewhere (157 entries; check before calling).
- **Automatic website check of every link** (HTTP status, plus whether the company name appears on the page): 1755 of 1826 entries (96%) loaded; 43 sites exist but refuse automated access (403/429); 28 did not respond from the checking server (flagged ⚠ and downgraded to ○; some may only block non-Icelandic visitors).
- **Hand spot-check:** 27 randomly chosen entries re-read against their live pages: 24 fully confirmed, 3 partly (one detail not visible on the page), 0 wrong or non-existent.
- **Known gaps.** The list is a strong candidate pool, not a complete register. (1) Thin categories and smaller towns (including Akureyri) are probably under-covered; the research tool's search allowance ran out during the first pass, so a second pass re-searched about 60 of the thinnest high-value categories. (2) Some sites block automated access, so those entries rest on search-result evidence (noted in the comments). (3) Markets made of individuals (dagforeldrar, doulas, babysitters, many photographers) appear as platforms, associations or studio brands. (4) Businesses open and close: re-check a company's site before a sales call.

## Categories with few or no companies found

- **G1-03 Meðgöngunudd og slökun** (Prenatal massage & relaxation) — 2 found. Only one verified provider (Fæðingarheimilið) plus one unconfirmed Noona-booked massage studio. Prenatal massage is mostly sold by individual therapists, and the spa sites checked (Laugar Spa, Hreyfing) did not mention it.
- **G1-06 Einkareknar ljósmæður og heimafæðingar** (Private midwives & home birth) — 2 found. Two private practices found (Björkin, Fæðingarheimilið). Home-birth midwives otherwise work as individuals listed by region on the midwives' association site (about 20 across capital area, Akureyri, Suðurnes, Suðurland, Vesturland), so they are not listed here.
- **G1-07 Doula og fæðingarstuðningur** (Doulas & birth support) — 1 found. Only one doula brand/agency could be verified; most doulas work as individuals and are not listed. No doula association website was reachable.
- **G1-08 Sónar og fósturgreining á einkastofum** (Private ultrasound, 3D/4D scans & prenatal screening) — 3 found. Three private baby-scan providers verified, all non-diagnostic: 9 mánuðir (Kópavogur: early scan, 2D, gender, 3D/4D/5D), midwife-run Ljósa (Hafnarfjörður) and Lífsins Tré (Keflavík). Diagnostic fetal screening is done only by Landspítali's fetal medicine unit; Læknastöðin, Lækning, Klíníkin, Björkin and Fæðingarheimilið list no pregnancy scans, Domus Medica's site stays down (503), and nothing was found for Akureyri or the rest of the country.
- **G1-09 Heimaþjónusta eftir fæðingu** (Private postnatal home care (midwife/nurse visits, night support, postnatal doulas)) — 3 found. Three private providers found: a midwife practice with daily home visits, a doula agency with postnatal care, and a birth home with breastfeeding home visits. No night-nurse style service found.
- **G2-03 Leiga á brjóstapumpum** (Breast-pump rental (often called 'mjaltavélar')) — 3 found. Móðurást on Laugavegur is the only business seen renting hospital-grade pumps (Medela Symphony and Ardo, 350 kr per 24 h, own pump set required). Lyfja, Apótekarinn and Lyfjaval pages show pump sales only and no rental, baby shops such as Fífa, Nine Kids and Undrabörn appear only as pump sellers, and the Keflavík and Akureyri pharmacy leads stay unverified.
- **G2-05 Mjólkuraukandi te og bætiefni** (Lactation teas & supplements) — 2 found. Few clear sellers: Móðurást has a dedicated milk-boosting tea and tablet category; Lyfja has fenugreek, tea-blend and nursing-vitamin categories but no lactation tea was itemised.
- **G2-10 Lífrænn og ferskur barnamatur** (Organic, fresh & home-style baby food (incl. Icelandic producers)) — 5 found. No Icelandic fresh or home-style baby-food maker could be verified (barnamatur.is turns out to be Nestlé's brand site, and a 2011 Matís report says all ready-made baby food is imported). Organic imported baby food (Ella's Kitchen, Holle, HiPP, Organix) is sold by Krónan and aha.is and distributed by Icepharma and Innnes; Heilsuhúsið is being folded into Lyfja, and Maður lifandi and Systrasamlagið could not be read or show no baby food.
- **G2-11 Matvinnsluvélar og áhöld fyrir barnamat** (Baby-food makers, blenders & freezer trays) — 3 found. Three sellers verified; general electronics chains (ELKO, Heimilistæki) showed no baby food-maker category on their front pages.
- **G2-14 Snarl og millimál fyrir smábörn** (Toddler snacks & finger foods) — 5 found. Toddler snacks are sold mainly through grocers and importers: Krónan (Millimál) and Innnes (Organix) plus Hagkaup's Barnamatur category, the aha.is delivery platform and HiPP's distributor OJK-ÍSAM (Mini Stars/Mini Dinos snacks, fruit pouches); Bónus, Nettó, Heimkaup, Fjarðarkaup and Costco showed no verifiable toddler-snack category, and no Icelandic toddler-snack producer was found.
- **G2-15 Ofnæmisvæn og sérhæfð næring** (Allergy-friendly & specialised infant nutrition) — 1 found. Only Lyfja could be verified (Nutricia Pepticate for cow's milk allergy). The island.is page on approved purchase authorisations for special nutrition returned 403, and wholesalers Icepharma and Vistor list no formula brands.
- **G3-03 Tvíburavagnar og systkinakerrur** (Twin & sibling strollers) — 6 found. Twin and sibling strollers are a small niche: Húsgagnaheimilið has by far the widest range (ten sibling models), Petit sells Bugaboo Donkey in mono/duo/twin, Nine Kids has the Cybex Gazelle S, and Örninn has Thule Chariot double trailers.
- **G3-04 Hlaupakerrur og útivistarkerrur** (Jogging & all-terrain strollers) — 3 found. Jogging and all-terrain strollers are a thin market: only Húsgagnaheimilið has a dedicated jogging category (Baby Jogger, BOB Gear, Fillikid); Örninn covers cycling trailers and Nine Kids has the Cybex Avi Spin outdoor stroller.
- **G3-08 Uppsetning, eftirlit og leiga á bílstólum** (Car-seat fitting, safety checks & rental) — 6 found. Hands-on help is scarce: Barnabílstólar (BeSafe rental, fitting on request), Bílanaust (Axkid rental), Nine Kids and Fífa are the only fitting/rental players, plus MiniRent for short-term travel rentals and Kara Kids as the one Akureyri seat seller; insurers (Sjóvá, VÍS) publish guidance only, VÍS stopped renting seats in 2019, and Miðstöð slysavarna barna is a public information source.
- **G3-10 Göngubakpokar og hjólakerrur fyrir börn** (Hiking baby backpacks & bike trailers) — 4 found. Outdoor-with-baby gear has two clear sellers: Örninn (Thule Chariot trailers, Burley, Yepp child bike seats) and Everest (Little Life child-carrier backpacks); Markið and Hjólasprettur list child bike seats and Hjólasprettur a trailer category. No Akureyri seller was confirmed.
- **G3-13 Leiga á barnavörum** (Baby-gear rental (cots, strollers, seats)) — 3 found. Real baby-gear rental is a tiny market: Barnabílstólar (BeSafe car seats), Bílanaust (Axkid seats) and Móðurást (Medela breast pumps). No cot, stroller or travel-gear rental firm for visitors was found.
- **G4-01 Svefnráðgjöf fyrir ungbörn og smábörn** (Infant & toddler sleep consultants) — 3 found. Three private sleep-consulting services exist: Svefnráðgjöf.is (Hafnarfjörður or phone), the online-only Mín líðan (ages 0-7) and the Sofa Borða Elska course brand (4 months-2 years); all work remotely, none is based in Akureyri, and public clinics (Landspítali, health-centre nurses in G21) are the alternative.
- **G4-09 Barnvæn málning og efni án eiturefna** (Child-safe paint & low-emission materials) — 3 found. Three paint suppliers clearly offer Nordic Swan (Svansmerkt) interior paint: Slippfélagið (says it poses no risk to infants or allergy sufferers), Málning (Kópal, Svanurinn category, solvent-free Glitra 10) and Flügger (Dekso AÏR allergy-friendly); none markets specifically to nurseries.
- **G5-10 Fatnaður fyrir mömmur eftir fæðingu** (Postpartum clothing & recovery wear) — 9 found. Still a niche: maternity ranges at Lindex and KappAhl, STOÐ and Móðurást were the first finds; added Tvö Líf (post-birth belly binders, support wear), Undrabörn (postpartum underwear), plus-size shop Curvy, Mía verslun and Lyfja (recovery products). No shop is dedicated purely to postpartum clothing.
- **G5-12 Fyrirburaföt og búnaður** (Preemie clothing & gear) — 3 found. Three sellers with premature sizes: Petit (fyrirbura-föt collection), KappAhl (Fyrirburar category) and Móðurást (38-44 cm size category); most other shops start at 50-56.
- **G6-02 Taubleyjur og umhverfisvænar bleyjur** (Cloth & eco diapers) — 5 found. Two dedicated cloth-nappy sellers (Taubleyjur.is and the Icelandic brand Noah Nappies) plus Krónan's eco-labelled disposables; the market is small.
- **G6-03 Bleyjuáskrift og heimsending** (Diaper subscription & delivery) — 9 found. No true diaper-subscription service found. Delivery options exist via Krónan, Lyfja, Lyfjaval, Heimkaup, the cloth-nappy shops and delivery platforms Wolt and aha.is.
- **G7-01 Barnalæknar á einkastofum** (Private paediatricians) — 3 found. Essentially one private paediatric practice (Domus barnalæknar, Kópavogur, open every day of the year, already listed); second pass adds Læknastöðin (Glæsibær: 1 paediatrician + 1 paediatric surgeon + ENT) and Heilsuklasinn (paediatric cardiologist); the paediatric society's member list shows most members at Landspítali, SAk or other public units (18 at Domus barnalæknar); no private paediatric practice confirmed in Akureyri (SAk children's ward is public, Læknastofur Akureyrar at Glerártorg has no paediatrics; one society member lists a private Akureyri address, unverified).
- **G7-02 Læknavakt og kvöld- og helgarþjónusta** (After-hours & weekend doctors) — 3 found. Capital area: Læknavaktin (evenings, weekends, home visits) and Domus paediatric on-call (both already listed), plus Tannlæknavaktin for dental emergencies; the private health centres only run a one-hour afternoon duty (Höfða, Urðarhvarfi, Kirkjusandi). Outside the capital (incl. Akureyri/HSN, SAk, HSS) after-hours care is the public 1700 line and on-call clinics, so no private buyer; no private English-language home-visit doctor found.
- **G7-03 Fjarlækningar og netlæknir** (Online doctor / telemedicine) — 2 found. Private telemedicine still nascent: Fjarlækningar (questionnaire-based consults for common problems, HSU pilot patients 18+) and Kara Connect are the only private players found; no Icelandic private online paediatrician; public chat/phone advice via Heilsuvera and 1700; Lyfja offers digital psychology (in its app) and nursing services, not doctors and Doktor.is is only a health-information portal with written Q&A.
- **G7-07 Ofnæmispróf og ofnæmislæknar barna** (Allergy testing & allergists) — 2 found. Paediatric allergy is concentrated at Domus barnalæknar (paediatric allergy and immunology specialists, ages 0-17). Heilsuklasinn lists general allergy/immunology specialists but no child-specific allergy service. Other private allergy options (Landspítali's allergy clinic is public) were not found; no private allergy-testing lab found.
- **G7-08 Faðernispróf og erfðapróf** (Paternity & DNA tests) — 2 found. No private Icelandic paternity or consumer DNA-test seller found (Sameind, deCODE and Matís list no such service); Landspítali's pathology department does both court-ordered and private paternity tests (payment up front) and its prenatal diagnosis unit offers NIPT, so this is effectively a public monopoly; paternity declarations themselves go through sýslumaður and Þjóðskrá.
- **G7-12 Talmeinafræðingar og málþroski** (Speech therapists) — 1 found. Only one business-level provider verified (Trappa, a Reykjavík company placing speech therapists, OT and psychologists with families and schools). Demand far exceeds supply: official reports cite waits of up to four years and an average 17 months at private practices, and the state is piloting speech therapy in schools. The professional association (talmein.is) publishes no clinic directory, so many individual practices are not captured.
- **G7-13 Iðjuþjálfun og sjúkraþjálfun barna** (Paediatric occupational & physical therapy) — 4 found. Child therapy is dominated by the non-profit Gló Æfingastöð (physio and OT for infants upwards) plus a few private multi-therapist businesses (Trappa, Lífsbrunnur, Heilsuklasinn). The physiotherapists' association directory loads dynamically, so many small physio clinics are not captured.
- **G7-14 Barnasálfræðingar og atferlisráðgjöf** (Child psychologists & behaviour advice) — 4 found. A few business-level providers verified: a child-and-teen psychology practice (Sálstofan), a baby/child sleep consultancy (Svefnráðgjöf.is), Trappa, and a general psychology practice with child expertise. Many individual psychologists practise under their own names and were not listed; the large anxiety clinic (Kvíðameðferðarstöðin) is adult-focused.
- **G7-16 Næringarfræðingar og næringarráðgjöf barna** (Paediatric dietitians & nutrition advice) — 2 found. No dedicated paediatric dietitian business could be verified. Options found are medical (Domus barnalæknar paediatric gastroenterology and nutrition, allergy, growth/endocrine) and general dietitian services inside Heilsuklasinn. Most dietitians in Iceland work as individuals or in health centres; the dietitians' association directory was not reachable.
- **G7-17 Skyndihjálp og slysavarnir barna** (Infant first-aid, CPR & child-safety courses) — 6 found. Tiny market: Sjóvá (free course with Miðstöð slysavarna barna), the Red Cross and Skyndihjálparskólinn (mostly groups and workplaces), plus the newly found private Bráðaskólinn (4-hour infant course, also in English, held at Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur) and one unverified Reykjavík provider; libraries and health centres run only occasional free talks, and no Akureyri provider was found.
- **G8-02 Kvensjúkdómalæknar og kvenheilsa** (Gynaecologists & women's-health clinics (postnatal checks, contraception)) — 6 found. Private gynaecology sits in a few Reykjavik/Kópavogur practices: Kvensjúkdómalæknir.is and Domus Læknar (both Hlíðasmári, Kópavogur), Lækning Lágmúla and Kvenheilsuklíníkin, plus public Heilsubrú; Læknastöðin (Álfheimar) lists no gynaecologists. No private gynaecologist confirmed in Akureyri: Sjúkrahúsið á Akureyri's maternity ward is the main women's-health provider in North Iceland and visiting gynaecologists serve HSN units in Húsavík and Sauðárkrókur.
- **G8-04 Líkamsræktarstöðvar með barnagæslu** (Gyms with childcare) — 4 found. Few gyms advertise staffed childcare: World Class (4 stations), CrossFit Reykjavík and Sporthúsið's Reykjanesbær branch (Krílabær). CrossFit Katla's ForeldraFit lets parents bring babies into class instead of childcare. Hreyfing, Hress, Katla Fitness stations, GYM heilsa and Bjarg (Akureyri) show none on their sites; smaller regional gyms still unchecked.
- **G8-07 Parameðferð og samskiptaráðgjöf** (Couples therapy & relationship counselling) — 4 found. Couples and family therapy is offered by a few Reykjavik-area practices plus the church's low-cost family service; no couples-specialist brand or regional (Akureyri) provider confirmed.
- **G8-08 Uppeldisráðgjöf og foreldranámskeið** (Parenting coaching & courses) — 10 found. Few dedicated 0-3 parenting-course businesses: Samskiptastöðin (Gottman course), Fæðingarheimilið, Sálfræðistofan and Bráðaskólinn, plus birth/infant course providers Ljósa, Móðurafl and 9 mánuðir in Hafnarfjörður/Kópavogur, and Hlöðuloftið (parent training) and Tengslamat (attachment work). The free parenting courses (Heilsugæslan, Reykjavík city, Þroska- og hegðunarstöð) are public; unions reimburse fees (e.g. Ljósa). Akureyri: only METIS and the public HSN; no private parenting course found.
- **G8-14 Næringarráðgjöf og mataræði eftir fæðingu** (Postnatal nutrition coaching) — 1 found. No clinic or brand specialising in postnatal or breastfeeding nutrition could be confirmed; dietitians in Iceland mostly trade under personal names (excluded by the brief) and the web search budget ran out before brand-name searches. Only a general nutrition-education site is listed; check the Heilsuklasinn building and pharmacy chains' mother-and-child ranges (G8-13) as adjacent options.
- **G8-15 Foreldrahópar og mömmumorgnar** (Parent groups & meet-ups) — 7 found. Parent meet-ups are mostly free parish foreldramorgnar, library and community-house mornings, and the non-profit Opni leikskóli Memmm (Reykjavík and Hafnarfjörður); private operators are Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur (free Tuesday lunch meet-up), Ljósa in Hafnarfjörður (bump meet-ups, parent mornings) and mum-and-baby yoga studios. Akureyri: parish foreldramorgnar at Glerárkirkja and a Memmm trial at Amtsbókasafnið in winter 2026. Facebook-based mömmuhópar cannot be listed.
- **G9-06 Hitastýrð blöndunartæki og brunavarnir í vatnslögnum** (Thermostatic mixer taps & scald protection (plumbers)) — 4 found. No company markets scald protection to parents. Bathroom-fittings retailers (Tengi with Kópavogur, Akureyri and Selfoss shops; Ísleifur webshop) and plumbing contractors (AH Pípulagnir, Lagnir.is) could install or sell thermostatic mixers, but no page confirmed a thermostatic range. DIY chains (BYKO, Húsasmiðjan, BAUHAUS) exist but their product pages could not be read.
- **G10-07 Heimilishjálp fyrir foreldra** (Household help / 'mother's helper') — 2 found. No dedicated mother's-helper or au pair agency found; the closest are a doula brand offering postpartum home support with light household tasks and a home-cleaning firm with gift vouchers. Search budget ran out before further checks.
- **G11-N1 Meðgöngutrygging** (Pregnancy & birth insurance) — 1 found. Only Sjóvá sells a standalone pregnancy insurance (one-off 30,000 ISK, buy until week 32); no other insurer's equivalent was found. Its partner perks (Móðurást, Lyfja, 9 mánuðir, Kírópraktor Lindir) show who else courts expecting parents.
- **G12-01 Fjölskyldulögfræði: forsjá, umgengni og meðlag** (Family law (custody, access, child support)) — 4 found. Four firms confirmed on their own pages: OPUS (child law with custody, contact and child support), Pacta (custody and divorce advice; Reykjavík, Akureyri, Blönduós), Sókn (Egilsstaðir) and Lögfræðistofa Reykjavíkur (family matters, no custody wording). Found by direct site checks only (web search budget ran out); many more Icelandic law firms exist, most big ones focus on business clients. Public sýslumenn handle statutory mediation and custody decisions (G21).
- **G12-04 Sáttamiðlun** (Family mediation) — 1 found. Only Sáttamiðlun ehf. in Reykjavík was confirmed as a private mediation provider with a couple, divorce and custody mediation service. Sýslumenn (district commissioners) run the statutory mediation in custody disputes (public, G21); some individual mediators exist but are not listed here. Web search budget ran out, so smaller providers may be missing.
- **G12-05 Fæðingarorlof og réttindi á vinnumarkaði** (Parental-leave & employment-rights law) — 4 found. Few firms advertise employment law for employees. Sókn (Egilsstaðir), LEX and Lagastoð list employment law (LEX and Lagastoð mainly for business clients) and VR (union) publishes members' parental-leave rights incl. return to work and dismissal protection. Fæðingarorlofssjóður is public (G21). Web search budget ran out.
- **G13-05 Geymslur og geymsluhúsnæði** (Storage units) — 2 found. Only two self-storage operators confirmed (both capital area/Suðurnes); a third site (Geymslusvæðið in Hafnarfjörður) says it has stopped renting out space. No operator found in Akureyri; more small ones may exist but WebSearch was unavailable.
- **G13-07 Sérsmíði og innréttingar** (Custom joinery & fitted furniture (kids' rooms, storage)) — 4 found. Only four relevant sellers verified: two kitchen/wardrobe cabinet makers, IKEA with kids' storage and a design service, and a small construction joinery. Independent custom-joinery workshops (sérsmíði) are many but could not be discovered without WebSearch; Kvik's old domain now belongs to an unrelated union.
- **G14-01 Dagforeldrar** (Registered childminders (dagforeldrar)) — 7 found. Dagforeldrar are self-employed individuals, so only organisers exist: municipal registers (Reykjavík and Akureyri listed; Kópavogur, Hafnarfjörður, Garðabær, Mosfellsbær, Vogar, Múlaþing and others publish their own), three private directories/platforms (Dagmamma.is, Dagforeldri.is, Nanna) and two Reykjavík associations (Barnið, Barnavistun) that have no websites.
- **G14-04 Barnapíur og kvöldpössun** (Babysitters & evening sitters) — 3 found. Babysitters are individuals, so only intermediaries exist: babysitter.is, the Babysitting.is agency (Allt Mitt Barnapössun) and the international Babysits platform; sitters otherwise advertise in Facebook groups and on Bland.is, and tourist-focused services (e.g. Iceland Babysitter) are not parent-resident options.
- **G14-05 Au pair og barnfóstrur** (Au pair & nanny agencies) — 1 found. Au pair help comes mostly through international platforms (AuPair.com, AuPairWorld, not listed); one Icelandic agency, Nínukot, places au pairs aged 18-25 with host families; no Icelandic nanny (barnfóstra) agency found, nannies being individuals.
- **G14-06 Barnagæsla á staðnum** (Drop-in childcare (stores, gyms, events, play rooms)) — 7 found. Drop-in minding is rare: gyms (World Class, Hress, CrossFitReykjavik, Norður in Akureyri, Stöðin in Ísafjörður) and two play areas (Kringlan's Ævintýraland, IKEA's Småland for ages 3-7); gyms such as Hreyfing, Sporthúsið, Katla Fitness and GYM heilsa do not advertise it; childcare hours, ages and prices were unreadable on the two regional gym sites.
- **G15-02 Ungbarnanudd og ungbarnajóga** (Baby massage & baby yoga) — 5 found. Baby massage and baby yoga are taught by a few small studios and independent instructors: ungbarnanudd at Nálar & nudd and Dharma Heilsa, mömmujóga at Jógasetrið, Yogaljós and Faðmur; no pure baby-yoga studio and no Akureyri provider found, and library one-off talks and Facebook-only instructors are not listed.
- **G15-03 Tónlistarnámskeið fyrir ungbörn og foreldra** (Baby & toddler music classes) — 1 found. Tónagull (running since 2004) is the specialist for baby and toddler music classes; no competing commercial provider was found. Music schools checked (Allegro Suzuki, Tónmenntaskóli, Tónlistarskólinn á Akureyri) show no baby or toddler classes on their start pages, and Kramhúsið's start page lists none either.
- **G15-05 Listsmiðjur og skynjunarleikur fyrir smábörn** (Art & sensory-play classes) — 1 found. No commercial art or sensory-play class for under-3s was found. The only verified lead is the Akureyri Art Museum's family tours with art workshop (public, not toddler-specific). Sensory 'baby sensory'-type franchises and toddler art studios may exist but did not appear in direct domain checks.
- **G15-06 Táknmál og snemmbær tjáning** (Baby sign language & early communication) — 2 found. No dedicated baby-sign business exists; early signing in Iceland runs on Tákn með tali (TMT). Mimi Saga sells a self-paced online family course, the state diagnostic centre RGR runs one-day TMT courses with half-price family places, municipalities publish TMT dictionaries and SHH (public) teaches Icelandic Sign Language mainly to deaf families. No Akureyri or North Iceland provider found; speech-therapy clinics were not found as parent-course sellers.
- **G15-12 Innileikjagarðar og leiksvæði innanhúss** (Indoor playgrounds) — 8 found. Few true indoor playgrounds: Kringlan's Ævintýraland, Skopp (the Rush Iceland domain now redirects to it; it also runs 0-5 sessions) and Barnaland at Smárabíó; Fjölskylduland (Dugguvogur) has closed permanently. Free options fill the gap: Harpa's Hljóðhimnar sound-play space, the Memmm open preschool (a non-profit, free, ages 0-5) and library children's departments; parent guides also mention play areas in Smáralind and a VáVá Barnaklúbbur in Reykjavík that could not be verified. Akureyri has no indoor play centre (only the library's weekly Foreldramorgnar, an indoor skatepark and a climbing gym) and the cafés with play areas (Laundromat Café) are in G15-13.
- **G16-05 Bílaþrif og djúphreinsun** (Car valeting & interior cleaning) — 5 found. Only one verified interior-cleaning specialist (Bílaspa, seat deep cleaning with pick-up and return); the others are wash stations or exterior detailing. Many small valeting businesses exist but could not be discovered without WebSearch.
- **G16-07 Flug með ungbarn** (Airlines & baby flight policies) — 4 found. Tiny market: Icelandair (international and domestic, own site blocks automated reading) and the regional Norlandair are the only scheduled Icelandic carriers found; PLAY has collapsed, Ernir's old domain now redirects to a charter broker (Air Broker Iceland) and Mýflug's site was unreachable. Adjacent players are the airport operators (Keflavík and Isavia's domestic airports incl. Akureyri), which are the only other plausible advertisers; Fríhöfnin and Flybus pages showed no baby-specific offer.
- **G17-05 Veggmyndir og strigaprentun** (Canvas & wall art) — 4 found. Weak parent link and few specialists: a photo print shop with canvas prints, a Reykjavík custom framer and retailers selling ready-made frames and posters. No dedicated Icelandic canvas-print webshop could be found.
- **G17-08 Persónulegar gjafir og nafnaskilti** (Personalised gifts & name signs (engraving, laser)) — 4 found. No dedicated name-sign or laser-engraving shop could be verified (several guessed domains did not resolve; a sign maker's site showed a 2017 copyright). Credible buyers are jewellers with engraving/letter pieces and a print portal for custom photo products.
- **G17-10 Fæðingarkort og tilkynningar** (Birth announcement & thank-you cards) — 8 found. No Icelandic printer markets birth announcements by name; the options are general printers and design shops with invitation, christening and thank-you cards (Pixel, PRENT, Garðbúinn, Pedromyndir's online card portal) alongside the four already listed. Prentmet Oddi's Egilsstaðir branch (Héraðsprent) sells framed birth-detail prints and newborn text cards; a forum thread suggests announcements are largely made on Facebook.
- **G18-03 Nafngjöf og skírnarathafnir** (Naming & baptism ceremonies (churches, Siðmennt, other communities)) — 3 found. Only non-profits and sole practitioners provide this: under the two-NGO cap just the Þjóðkirkjan and Siðmennt stay listed (others offering naming or baptism are Fríkirkjan in Reykjavík and Hafnarfjörður, Ásatrúarfélagið, the Catholic Church and Óháði söfnuðurinn); the only private brand found is Úní, offering a pagan-style naming ceremony.
- **G18-07 Leiga á veisluvörum og borðbúnaði** (Party-ware & tableware rental) — 7 found. Small market: a few caterers and rental firms rent tableware (Matur og mörk in Akureyri, Nomy, Leir og Postulín), Efnalaugin Björg and Áskaffi (Skagafjörður) rent tablecloths and Exton rents tables and chairs; Rentaparty could not be reached and the old Salir.is Áhaldaleiga listing looks stale. Venues and caterers often include tableware, so the slot may attract a caterer.
- **G19-01 Matarkassar og uppskriftakassar** (Meal kits) — 11 found. Very concentrated: Eldum rétt (Hagar-owned, about 85-90% of kit sales per the 2022 Competition Authority decision) and Einn tveir are the only true recipe-kit brands; Móðir Jörð and vegetable-box subscriptions (Matland, FoodCoop, Reykjalundur), ready-to-cook fish and meat boxes (Fiskbúðin Sundlaugavegi, Kjötkompaní, Sælkerabúðin) and ready meals (Matseðill) are the adjacent alternatives; Eldabuskan is closed for now, the Fiskur dagsins fish-kit site looks stale and Borðum rétt could not be verified.
- **G21-03 Fæðingarorlof, barnabætur og réttindi** (Parental leave, benefits & rights (agencies)) — 5 found. Few agencies carry this topic: Fæðingarorlofssjóður (run by Vinnumálastofnun) pays parental leave, Skatturinn pays child benefits, and the union federations explain rights. Tryggingastofnun and Sjúkratryggingar are relevant but their sites redirect to island.is, which blocks fetching, so they are not listed. Applications for leave payments must be in at least six weeks before the due date.
- **G21-N2 Aðstoð við foreldra í fjárhagsvanda (hjálparsamtök)** (Charities helping families in financial hardship) — 3 found. Three charities seen: Mæðrastyrksnefnd Hafnarfjarðar (mainly Christmas aid), Hjálparstarf kirkjunnar (emergency and Christmas aid) and Fjölskylduhjálp Íslands (food aid in Reykjavik and Reykjanesbær). Mæðrastyrksnefnd Reykjavíkur and other local committees (e.g. Akureyri) exist but their sites could not be read. None of the pages mention baby items specifically.

## G1 · Meðganga og fæðing — Pregnancy & birth

### G1-01 · Meðgöngufatnaður — Maternity clothing

**Problem:** Nothing fits any more  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6

*Market:* One true specialist (Tvö Líf) plus baby/children's shops with maternity sections (Bíum Bíum, Mía), a plus-size shop, a dress-rental shop and a charity second-hand shop; fashion chains such as H&M and Lindex likely sell maternity lines but their sites blocked checking.

- ✓ **Tvö Líf** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Specialist shop for maternity and nursing clothing, nursing bras, support belts, compression wear and post-birth products; Glæsibær in Reykjavík plus webshop. · [tvolif.is](https://tvolif.is/) · *Brands listed include Bravado, Cache Coeur, Carriwell, Mamalicious, Noppies, Love2Wait, Proud Mama. Álfheimar 74 (Glæsibær). Also sells breast pumps and feeding items.*
- ✓ **Bíum Bíum** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Children's and baby store with a maternity-wear section (maternity leggings and trousers seen) plus breast pumps and baby gear; shop at Ármúli 38 and webshop. · [biumbium.is/collections/medgongufot](https://biumbium.is/collections/medgongufot) · *Operated as Bíum Bíum ehf. and Von verslun ehf. Maternity page was almost empty on the day checked, so stock varies.*
- ✓ **Mía** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Online baby and children's shop with a pregnancy section covering maternity clothing (e.g. maternity dresses), pregnancy pillows, breastfeeding items and skincare. · [miaverslun.is](https://miaverslun.is/) · *Search listing also showed maternity dresses and a store address at Ármúli 40, not confirmed on the site itself.*
- ✓ **Curvy & Stout** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Plus-size fashion shop with a maternity range: tops, trousers, dresses, underwear and maternity leggings under its own labels. · [curvy.is/collections/medgongu-buxur/medgongufatnadur](https://www.curvy.is/collections/medgongu-buxur/medgongufatnadur) · *Shop at Mörkin 1, Reykjavík. Mailing list for special offers; some maternity trousers were on sale. No loyalty programme seen.*
- ✓ **Kjólaleiga** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Party and evening dress rental with a maternity-dress (meðgöngukjólar) collection, for special occasions during pregnancy. · [kjolaleiga.is](https://kjolaleiga.is/) · *Grensásvegur 1A, Reykjavík. Rental, not purchase; the maternity collection showed no items on the day checked.*
- ✓ **Kubuneh** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Online shop with a maternity category of 26 unused or pre-owned items: maternity trousers and dresses, nursing tops; prices about 500-3,500 kr. · [kubuneh.is/product-category/domu/medgongufot-o-fl](https://kubuneh.is/product-category/domu/medgongufot-o-fl/) · *Linked to charity work in Gambia (clinic, scholarships). Brands seen include H&M Mama and Pieces Maternity. Second-hand character inferred from listings.*

### G1-02 · Meðgöngupúðar og stuðningsvörur — Pregnancy pillows, support belts & compression wear

**Problem:** Back pain, poor sleep, swollen legs  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6

*Market:* Pharmacies and baby/mother shops cover belts and pillows; Lyfja has the clearest range. Orthopaedic supplier Stoð is a possible compression-wear slot. Eirberg could not be checked (403).

- ✓ **Lyfja** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Pharmacy chain selling Protek pregnancy support belts, a Chicco Boppy pregnancy pillow, stretch-mark oils and pregnancy vitamins, in branches and webshop. · [lyfja.is/verslun/modir-og-barn/medganga-og-faeding](https://www.lyfja.is/verslun/modir-og-barn/medganga-og-faeding/) · **Discount:** Vöggugjöf Lyfju: free gift pack for expectant parents incl. a 4,000 kr 66°Norður voucher (sold out when checked) · *Branches include Akureyri, Selfoss, Reykjanesbær, Egilsstaðir, Ísafjörður. Free delivery over 9.900 kr. Heilsuhúsið products now sold through Lyfja.*
- ✓ **Tvö Líf** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Maternity specialist with support belts, support garments and compression socks for pregnancy; Glæsibær in Reykjavík plus webshop. · [tvolif.is](https://tvolif.is/) · *Brand list includes Theraline and Snoozle (pillow brands) but pillows are not named on the page checked.*
- ✓ **Mía** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Online baby shop whose pregnancy section lists pregnancy pillows, maternity clothing, breastfeeding items and skincare. · [miaverslun.is](https://miaverslun.is/) · *Exact pillow brands not seen.*
- ○ **Móðurást** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Baby shop with a mother-and-pregnancy section (pregnancy and birth products, nursing pillows); a Snoozzz pregnancy and breastfeeding pillow is listed under its name. · [modurast.is](https://modurast.is/) · *Laugavegur 178. The pillow listing was seen on the aha.is marketplace, not on its own site.*
- ✓ **aha.is** (Vettvangur/app · Netverslun) — Icelandic online marketplace with delivery; lists shops' products such as a Snoozzz pregnancy and breastfeeding pillow (seller Móðurást). · [cdn.aha.is/medgongu-og-brjostagjafapudi](https://cdn.aha.is/medgongu-og-brjostagjafapudi) · *Also lists Medela, Lansinoh and Haakaa nursing products. Home page returned 403 when fetched; delivery area not checked.*
- ✓ **Stoð** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Orthopaedic and mobility supplier whose range includes compression socks and braces, relevant for swollen legs and back strain. · [stod.is](https://stod.is) · *Draghálsi 14-16, Reykjavík. Pregnancy belts not named on the page checked; webshop access unclear.*

### G1-03 · Meðgöngunudd og slökun — Prenatal massage & relaxation

**Problem:** Aches, stress, needing to switch off  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 2

*Market:* Only one verified provider (Fæðingarheimilið) plus one unconfirmed Noona-booked massage studio. Prenatal massage is mostly sold by individual therapists, and the spa sites checked (Laugar Spa, Hreyfing) did not mention it.

- ✓ **Fæðingarheimilið (Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private birth centre at Hlíðarfótur 17 whose service list includes massage with pregnancy massage, plus prenatal yoga, acupuncture, physiotherapy and antenatal care. · [faedingarheimilid.is](https://faedingarheimilid.is) · *Massage is provided by a partner therapist; also offers free weekly meetup for families with young children.*
- ○ **Sense (nuddstofa, Noona-bókunarsíða)** (Þjónusta · Óstaðfest (Noona-bókun)) — Massage studio booking page listing Pregnancy (Prenatal) Massage alongside Thai, deep-tissue, lymphatic and relaxation massages, gift boxes and vouchers. · [noona.app/sense/book](https://noona.app/sense/book) · *Business name and location are not stated on the page (booking slug 'sense'); verify before approaching.*

### G1-04 · Meðgöngujóga og meðgönguleikfimi — Prenatal yoga & fitness classes

**Problem:** Staying fit and preparing the body for birth  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

*Market:* Seven providers now: two birth-centre/yoga studios (Jógasetrið, Yogaljós at Fæðingarheimilið), Faðmur in Hafnarfjörður, the pool class Meðgöngusund in Reykjavík, World Class's pregnancy/new-mum classes (also in Akureyri) and an Akureyri yoga studio with a pregnancy course (unconfirmed). Gyms such as Hreyfing, Hress, Sporthúsið, YogaLíf and Yoga Shala list no current pregnancy classes.

- ✓ **Jógasetrið** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netkennsla) — Yoga studio at Skipholt 50c with in-person prenatal yoga, a couples course, online prenatal yoga packages and mom-and-baby yoga from 6-8 weeks. · [jogasetrid.is](https://jogasetrid.is) · *New 8-week mömmujóga course starts 11 January 2027. Studio name taken from the domain.*
- ✓ **Yogaljós** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Prenatal yoga in a hall at Reykjavik Birth Center, plus mom yoga and a 'Birth Without Fear' course; a 4-week course starts 4 January 2027. · [yogaljos.is](https://yogaljos.is) · *Classes at Hlíðarfótur 17; no need to use other birth-centre services.*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimilið (Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Birth centre offering pregnancy and mother yoga (with Yogaljós) and physiotherapy, plus courses for parents-to-be. · [faedingarheimilid.is](https://faedingarheimilid.is) · *Same venue as Yogaljós.*
- ✓ **World Class (Pilates Mömmur og WorldFit Mömmur)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Vesturland (Akranes)) — Pilates and strength classes made for expecting and new mothers; babies are welcome in the room. · [worldclass.is/klubbar/worldfit/mommur](https://www.worldclass.is/klubbar/worldfit/mommur) · *Add-on to a World Class card, 9,800 kr a month, minimum 3 months. Pilates Mömmur is at Laugar; WorldFit Mömmur also at Tjarnarvellir, Akranes and Skólastígur in Akureyri. No parent discount seen.*
- ✓ **Meðgöngusund** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík, Grensáslaug)) — Pool exercise classes for pregnant women led by physiotherapists, focused on pelvic and lower-back stability and stretching; running since 2001. · [medgongusund.is](https://medgongusund.is/) · *Class cards 22,000 kr for 4 weeks and 58,000 kr for 12 weeks; lunchtime and evening classes on weekdays; places and waiting list by email. No union subsidy mentioned.*
- ✓ **Faðmur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Yoga and hypnotherapy studio offering private pregnancy yoga, pregnancy-swimming sessions, mum-and-baby yoga and an online birth-preparation course. · [fadmur.is/medgongujoga](https://fadmur.is/medgongujoga/) · *Pregnancy yoga is currently private sessions only (others may sign up together). Friðsæl Fæðing is an online course. Prices are on its own schedule page; no union subsidy or discount mentioned.*
- ○ ⚠ **Akureyri Yoga** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri yoga studio with a six-week pregnancy yoga course, one class a week, including online pregnancy-yoga videos for home practice. · [akureyriyoga.is/medgongujoga](https://www.akureyriyoga.is/medgongujoga) · *Seen only in search results: the site did not load from here and its course page shows December 2024 and February 2025 start dates, so check it still runs pregnancy courses. Also runs soft yoga and relaxation courses.*

### G1-05 · Fæðingarfræðsla og undirbúningsnámskeið — Private childbirth & parenting-preparation classes

**Problem:** Fear of the unknown: what happens at the birth and in the first weeks  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Several private midwife and doula businesses sell birth-preparation courses; public classes via health centres are excluded. Infant first-aid course (Bráðaskólinn) fits the parenting-preparation angle.

- ✓ **Björkin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private midwife practice at Síðumúli 10 running birth-preparation courses, an evening session for weeks 37-42 and a breastfeeding course. · [bjorkin.is](https://bjorkin.is) · *Care practice was fully booked with a waiting list until August 2026; check course availability separately.*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimilið (Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Courses preparing for birth, breastfeeding and the time after birth, a 'Faðir verður til' course for fathers, and infant first-aid courses. · [faedingarheimilid.is](https://faedingarheimilid.is) · *Hlíðarfótur 17. Partners include Yogaljós and Bráðaskólinn.*
- ✓ **Hönd í hönd doula (Hönd í hönd ráðgjöf ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Doula agency offering birth education, hypnobirthing education and an online birth-preparation course; also doula training courses. · [doula.is](https://doula.is) · *Based in Hafnarfjörður; also birth doula and postnatal visits.*
- ✓ **Yogaljós** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Prenatal yoga studio at the Reykjavik Birth Center that also runs a 'Birth Without Fear' course. · [yogaljos.is](https://yogaljos.is) · *4-week yoga and education course starts 4 January 2027.*
- ✓ **Bráðaskólinn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (óstaðfest)) — First-aid and acute-illness course for parents of infants (13,600 kr), also taught in English; gift vouchers available. · [bradaskolinn.is](https://www.bradaskolinn.is) · **Discount:** Mailing list gets discounts and offers first · *Course venue not stated on its site; Fæðingarheimilið lists it as a partner.*

### G1-06 · Einkareknar ljósmæður og heimafæðingar — Private midwives & home birth

**Problem:** Wanting a more personal birth or a home birth  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 2

*Market:* Two private practices found (Björkin, Fæðingarheimilið). Home-birth midwives otherwise work as individuals listed by region on the midwives' association site (about 20 across capital area, Akureyri, Suðurnes, Suðurland, Vesturland), so they are not listed here.

- ✓ **Björkin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private midwife practice offering home births, births at its own birth centre (Síðumúli 10), antenatal care from week 34 and postnatal home visits. · [bjorkin.is](https://bjorkin.is) · *Fully booked with a waiting list until August 2026 per its site; parents join the list online.*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimilið (Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Birth home at Hlíðarfótur 17 for healthy women with normal pregnancies: antenatal care, birth, birth debriefing, breastfeeding counselling and courses. · [faedingarheimilid.is](https://faedingarheimilid.is) · *Home birth is not mentioned on its site.*

### G1-07 · Doula og fæðingarstuðningur — Doulas & birth support

**Problem:** Wanting continuous support during labour  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1

*Market:* Only one doula brand/agency could be verified; most doulas work as individuals and are not listed. No doula association website was reachable.

- ✓ **Hönd í hönd doula (Hönd í hönd ráðgjöf ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Doula service offering continuous labour support, late-pregnancy visits, rebozo ceremony, birth and hypnobirthing education, and postnatal home care. · [doula.is](https://doula.is) · *Based in Hafnarfjörður; also runs doula training courses (2027 programme planned).*

### G1-08 · Sónar og fósturgreining á einkastofum — Private ultrasound, 3D/4D scans & prenatal screening

**Problem:** Wanting extra scans or to see the baby  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3

*Market:* Three private baby-scan providers verified, all non-diagnostic: 9 mánuðir (Kópavogur: early scan, 2D, gender, 3D/4D/5D), midwife-run Ljósa (Hafnarfjörður) and Lífsins Tré (Keflavík). Diagnostic fetal screening is done only by Landspítali's fetal medicine unit; Læknastöðin, Lækning, Klíníkin, Björkin and Fæðingarheimilið list no pregnancy scans, Domus Medica's site stays down (503), and nothing was found for Akureyri or the rest of the country.

- ✓ **9 mánuðir** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Independent family health centre offering early pregnancy scans, 2D, gender and 3D/4D/5D baby scans, plus birth and breastfeeding courses and pregnancy massage. · [9manudir.is/sonar](https://9manudir.is/sonar/) · *Scans are not diagnostic. Also birth-education, breastfeeding, twin and infant-massage courses, pregnancy massage and fertility treatment; gift vouchers valid 4 years. No union subsidy or discount mentioned.*
- ✓ **Ljósa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Midwife-run business offering micro, 2D, gender and 3D/4D/5D baby scans, plus birth education, birth debriefs and bump meetups. · [ljosa.is/s%C3%B3nar](https://www.ljosa.is/s%C3%B3nar) · **Discount:** Its site says unions often reimburse the birth-education course fee and on-site course attendees may get gifts and discounts. · *Scans are not diagnostic. Price list: 3D 22,990 kr, 2D 17,490 kr, micro scan 8,990 kr. An IBCLC consultant is announced to start on-site breastfeeding consultations. Gift cards available.*
- ✓ **Lífsins Tré** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes (Keflavík)) — Keflavík ultrasound clinic offering gender/2D scans from week 16 and 3D/4D/5D scans from weeks 26 to 32, with photos and videos sent digitally. · [lifsinstre.is](https://www.lifsinstre.is/) · *Price list: gender/2D 9,900 kr (30-45 min), 3D/4D/5D 15,900 kr (45-60 min). Scans are not diagnostic screening (done at Landspítali). Online booking.*

### G1-09 · Heimaþjónusta eftir fæðingu — Private postnatal home care (midwife/nurse visits, night support, postnatal doulas)

**Problem:** Exhaustion and uncertainty in the first weeks at home  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3

*Market:* Three private providers found: a midwife practice with daily home visits, a doula agency with postnatal care, and a birth home with breastfeeding home visits. No night-nurse style service found.

- ✓ **Björkin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private midwife practice with daily midwife home visits in the first days (up to 10 days after birth), including breastfeeding support. · [bjorkin.is](https://bjorkin.is) · *Fully booked with a waiting list until August 2026; also antenatal care and birth services.*
- ✓ **Hönd í hönd doula (Hönd í hönd ráðgjöf ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Postnatal care (sængurleguþjónusta): home visits to help with the baby, light household tasks and adjusting siblings to the new baby. · [doula.is](https://doula.is) · *Hafnarfjörður-based; birth doula and education services too.*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimilið (Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Postpartum follow-up with home visits for breastfeeding counselling as needed, birth debriefing and a free weekly meetup for families with young children. · [faedingarheimilid.is](https://faedingarheimilid.is) · **Discount:** Free weekly 'Hittumst í hádeginu' family meetup (Tuesdays) · *Hlíðarfótur 17, Reykjavík.*

### G1-N1 · Meðgönguvítamín og bætiefni — Prenatal & breastfeeding vitamins and supplements (folic acid, iodine, iron) *(new — added during research)*

**Problem:** Making sure mother and baby get enough folic acid, iodine and iron  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* Pharmacies and natural-health shops all stock prenatal vitamins; this is a high-volume, near-universal need and Lyfja has the widest visible range.

- ✓ **Lyfja** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Pharmacy chain with prenatal vitamins such as Pregnacare Original, Terranova Prenatal, an iodine supplement and a probiotic for pregnant women. · [lyfja.is/verslun/modir-og-barn/medganga-og-faeding](https://www.lyfja.is/verslun/modir-og-barn/medganga-og-faeding/) · **Discount:** Vöggugjöf Lyfju: free gift pack for expectant parents incl. 4,000 kr 66°Norður voucher (sold out when checked) · *Branches incl. Akureyri; free delivery over 9.900 kr; also vitamin category 'Meðganga & brjóstagjöf'.*
- ✓ **Apótekarinn** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Pharmacy chain whose mother-and-child page includes a breastfeeding-and-pregnancy vitamin category, plus breastfeeding products. · [apotekarinn.is/modir-og-barn](https://www.apotekarinn.is/modir-og-barn) · **Discount:** Apótekarinn+ loyalty programme (benefits not shown on page) · *About 28 stores incl. Akureyri (Glerártorg, Hrísalundur); Lyf og heilsa now redirects here.*
- ✓ **Mamma veit best** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Online health shop (Kópavogur) with a pregnancy category: organic pregnancy multivitamin and prenatal probiotic products. · [mammaveitbest.is/voruflokkur/medganga](https://mammaveitbest.is/voruflokkur/medganga/) · *Mamma Veit Best ehf.; category had only four products on the day checked.*
- ✓ **Systrasamlagið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Natural-health shop with a vitamins and supplements range including a pregnancy category; ships across Iceland. · [systrasamlagid.is](https://www.systrasamlagid.is) · **Discount:** Free shipping over 15,000 kr · *Óðinsgata 1, Reykjavík.*

## G2 · Brjóstagjöf, pelagjöf og matur — Breast & bottle feeding, baby food

### G2-01 · Brjóstagjafaráðgjöf — Lactation consultants (IBCLC)

**Problem:** Latching trouble, pain, worry about milk supply  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Five private businesses now: Fæðingarheimilið, Björkin and Von brjósti (the three the midwives' association lists), Lucina in Hafnarfjörður (15,000 kr an hour, video visits) and 9 mánuðir in Kópavogur (course plus home counselling by a midwife-IBCLC); the other consultants listed are individuals, one in Akureyri and one around Selfoss. Three home visits within six months are covered by Sjúkratryggingar through midwives, unions subsidise breastfeeding courses (a discount angle), and Ljósa has announced an on-site IBCLC consultant.

- ✓ **Von brjósti** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland + fjarþjónusta) — Breastfeeding counselling with clinics in Reykjavík and Hveragerði, remote video consultations nationwide, online courses, Zoom group meetings and free discussion meetings. · [vonibrjosti.is](https://vonibrjosti.is) · **Discount:** Most unions subsidise its breastfeeding courses (per its site); gift vouchers available · *Clinic locations taken from the midwives' association page; its own site does not use the IBCLC term.*
- ✓ **Björkin (brjóstagjafaráðgjöf)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + fjarfundur utan höfuðborgarsvæðis) — Breastfeeding counselling at Síðumúli 10 (18,000 kr per session without referral) and an online course 'Upphafið' on the first days of breastfeeding. · [brjostagjof.bjorkin.is](https://brjostagjof.bjorkin.is) · *Video visits only for mothers living outside the capital area; the midwife practice itself has a waiting list.*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimilið (Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Breastfeeding counselling for the first six months after birth, at the centre or through home visits when needed. · [faedingarheimilid.is](https://faedingarheimilid.is) · *Hlíðarfótur 17, Reykjavík.*
- ✓ **Lucina** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður) + fjarfundir um allt land) — Private breastfeeding counselling by appointment, one hour including a baby check-up and written plan, with video visits; also an online shop with baby carriers. · [lucina.is/timapontun-brjostagjafaradgjof](https://lucina.is/timapontun-brjostagjafaradgjof/) · *15,000 kr an hour, outside the Sjúkratryggingar scheme; based in Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó. Booking page showed bookings closed when checked. Also advises on bottle-feeding and starting solids; shop sells Baby K'tan carriers.*
- ✓ **9 mánuðir** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Family health centre whose 3-hour breastfeeding course, from week 28, is taught by a midwife who is an IBCLC and who also gives home breastfeeding counselling in the first days. · [9manudir.is/thjonusta/namskeid/brjostagjof](https://9manudir.is/thjonusta/namskeid/brjostagjof/) · *Course 18,990 kr, partner welcome; dates in December and January. Also birth-education, twin and infant-massage courses and baby scans. Union refunds not mentioned.*

### G2-02 · Brjóstapumpur til sölu — Breast pumps for sale

**Problem:** Needing to express milk (work, sharing feeds)  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Well covered: pharmacies (Lyfja, Apótekarinn), baby shops and the Chicco Ísland storefront all sell pumps; Móðurást is the long-standing Medela/Ardo specialist.

- ✓ **Móðurást** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Baby shop selling Medela Symphony and Ardo double pump sets (about 8,000-8,500 kr), breast shields in 17-36 mm and sterilising bags, plus pump rental. · [modurast.is/brjostapumpuleiga](https://modurast.is/brjostapumpuleiga) · *Laugavegur 178. Has supplied Medela pumps for decades; hours Mon-Fri 11-17, Sat 12-15.*
- ✓ **Lyfja** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Pharmacy chain with hand, electric and hands-free pumps from Chicco, Lansinoh, Medela and MAM (about 4,900-39,400 kr), in branches and webshop. · [lyfja.is/verslun/modir-og-barn/brjostagjof/brjostapumpur](https://www.lyfja.is/verslun/modir-og-barn/brjostagjof/brjostapumpur/) · **Discount:** Vöggugjöf Lyfju: free gift pack for expectant parents incl. a 4,000 kr 66°Norður voucher (sold out when checked) · *Branches incl. Akureyri; free delivery over 9.900 kr.*
- ✓ **Apótekarinn** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Pharmacy chain selling Lansinoh electric, hand and hands-free pumps and the MAM Move wearable pump, plus nipple shields and accessories. · [apotekarinn.is/en/verslun/modir-og-barn/brjostagjof-og-medganga](https://www.apotekarinn.is/en/verslun/modir-og-barn/brjostagjof-og-medganga) · **Discount:** Apótekarinn+ loyalty programme (benefits not shown on page) · *About 28 stores incl. Akureyri (Glerártorg, Hrísalundur); Lyf og heilsa now redirects here.*
- ✓ **Tvö Líf** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Maternity and nursing specialist whose electronics range includes breast pumps, bottle warmers and sterilisers; Lansinoh and Medela-style accessories. · [tvolif.is](https://tvolif.is/) · *Álfheimar 74 (Glæsibær).*
- ✓ **Bíum Bíum** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Baby store with a breast pump and bottle-warmer category: Perla hands-free, Bella Twin, Camino and other pumps (about 16,000-32,000 kr). · [biumbium.is/collections/brjostapumpur-pelahitarar](https://biumbium.is/collections/brjostapumpur-pelahitarar) · *Ármúli 38 plus webshop; brand filter shows Bibs and Neno; no rental mentioned.*
- ✓ **Dótabúðin (Chicco Ísland)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Chicco storefront in Kópavogur selling Chicco breast pumps, nursing accessories and the Boppy nursing pillow. · [dotabudin.is/pages/chicco](https://dotabudin.is/pages/chicco) · **Discount:** Dótaklúbburinn: 5% store credit; free delivery over 15,000 kr · *Grænatún 1, Kópavogur; chicco.is redirects here.*
- ✓ **Barnið Okkar** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Baby and children's webshop (also wholesale) with a breast pump and baby-scale category alongside prams, car seats and pacifiers. · [barnidokkar.is](https://barnidokkar.is) · **Discount:** Sale and clearance sections; no club seen · *Banner says the Hlíðasmári store is closing but the business continues.*
- ✓ **aha.is** (Vettvangur/app · Netverslun) — Icelandic online marketplace listing pumps and pumping accessories (Lansinoh hands-free pump pack, Medela Freestyle Flex shield, Easy Express pumping bra) from partner shops. · [cdn.aha.is/lansinoh-handfrjalsi-pumpupakkinn](https://cdn.aha.is/lansinoh-handfrjalsi-pumpupakkinn) · *Search hits only; home page returned 403 when fetched.*
- ○ **Ninekids** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Children's goods shop in Kópavogur with a Brjóstagjöf (breastfeeding) category linking to Lansinoh nursing products. · [ninekids.is](https://www.ninekids.is) · **Discount:** Stofn (Sjóvá) members: 10% off car seats; separate page for insurance-company discounts · *Bæjarlind 6, Kópavogur. Pumps not named on the page checked.*

### G2-03 · Leiga á brjóstapumpum — Breast-pump rental (often called 'mjaltavélar')

**Problem:** Hospital-grade pump without buying one  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3

*Market:* Móðurást on Laugavegur is the only business seen renting hospital-grade pumps (Medela Symphony and Ardo, 350 kr per 24 h, own pump set required). Lyfja, Apótekarinn and Lyfjaval pages show pump sales only and no rental, baby shops such as Fífa, Nine Kids and Undrabörn appear only as pump sellers, and the Keflavík and Akureyri pharmacy leads stay unverified.

- ✓ **Móðurást** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Rents Medela Symphony and Ardo breast pumps (350 kr per 24 h) and infant scales (450 kr per 24 h); renters must own their own pump set. · [modurast.is/brjostapumpuleiga](https://modurast.is/brjostapumpuleiga) · *Laugavegur 178, Reykjavík; hours Mon-Fri 11-17, Sat 12-15. Has rented Medela pumps for decades.*
- ○ **Lyfja Reykjanesbær (Keflavík)** (Keðja · Suðurnes) — Listed by an older breastfeeding blog (about 10 years old) as renting Medela Symphony pumps; current Lyfja pages show pump sales but no rental. · [lyfja.is/um-lyfju/verslanir](https://www.lyfja.is/um-lyfju/verslanir/) · *Unverified lead: call the branch. Krossmóum 4, Reykjanesbær.*
- ○ **Apótekarinn Akureyri** (Keðja · Akureyri) — Listed by the same older blog as renting Medela Symphony pumps; current Apótekarinn pages show no rental. · [apotekarinn.is/verslanir](https://www.apotekarinn.is/verslanir) · *Unverified lead. Stores at Glerártorg and Hrísalundur, Akureyri.*

### G2-06 · Ungbarnablöndur og þurrmjólk — Infant formula (incl. allergy / specialised formula)

**Problem:** Where to buy formula and which one  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Krónan and Lyfja (specialised formula only) were verified earlier; Hagkaup, Krambúðin and Bónus are seen only second-hand (Wolt listings, a 2018 receipt) and aha.is on its own product pages. Specialised allergy formula runs through two healthcare suppliers (Icepharma, Fastus) and Nestlé's brand site shows NAN. Nettó, Heimkaup, Costco and Fjarðarkaup almost certainly stock formula but show no formula page to cite.

### G2-13 · Barnadiskar, smekkir og matarbúnaður — Baby tableware, bibs & silicone feeding sets

**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

- ✓ **Fífa** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Baby shop with Mushie silicone suction plates, bowls, spoons and bibs, Done by Deer spoon sets, snack cups and trainer cups. · [fifa.is/categories/heimilid/matartiminn](https://fifa.is/categories/heimilid/matartiminn) · **Discount:** Insurance customers (e.g. Sjóvá Stofn): 20% off car seats · *Faxafen 8, Reykjavík; webshop ships nationwide.*

### G2-14 · Snarl og millimál fyrir smábörn — Toddler snacks & finger foods

**Problem:** Healthy snacks on the go  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Toddler snacks are sold mainly through grocers and importers: Krónan (Millimál) and Innnes (Organix) plus Hagkaup's Barnamatur category, the aha.is delivery platform and HiPP's distributor OJK-ÍSAM (Mini Stars/Mini Dinos snacks, fruit pouches); Bónus, Nettó, Heimkaup, Fjarðarkaup and Costco showed no verifiable toddler-snack category, and no Icelandic toddler-snack producer was found.

Brands not itemised.*

- ○ ⚠ **Hagkaup** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Grocery and department-store chain with a Barnamatur webshop category: Ella's Kitchen fruit pouches (skvísur), Semper meals from 6 months and other baby foods. · [verslun.hagkaup.is/product-category/barnavara/barnamatur](https://verslun.hagkaup.is/product-category/barnavara/barnamatur/) · *Category seen via search result on its own domain; the page returned 503 when fetched. **Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1

## G3 · Vagnar, bílstólar og barnabúnaður — Strollers, car seats & baby gear

### G3-01 · Barnavagnar — Prams / newborn bassinet strollers

**Problem:** What to push a newborn in (lying flat)  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11

*Market:* About ten specialist baby shops sell prams, each tied to a few brands (Bugaboo at Petit, Cybex at Nine Kids, Silver Cross at Móðurást and I Am Happy, Emmaljunga and Maxi-Cosi at Fífa, Joolz at Mía); no dominant chain, so the slot is genuinely contested.

- ✓ **Barnið okkar** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir innanlands)) — Webshop with a dedicated prams category (Barnavagnar) plus strollers, car seats, rain covers and sheepskin footmuffs. · [barnidokkar.is](https://barnidokkar.is) · **Discount:** Sale, warehouse-sale and clearance sections; no parent club seen. · *Brands seen: Bass Next, Coss, Leclerc, Lionelo, Tula. The Hlíðasmári shop is closing but the online store and warehouse continue; wholesale arm too.*
- ✓ **Fífa** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Baby shop with Emmaljunga 3-in-1 pram sets, a Maxi-Cosi carrycot, Maxi-Cosi Oxford sets, a Britax Tira Style model, rain covers and car-seat adapters. · [fifa.is](https://fifa.is) · **Discount:** 20% off car seats in store for Sjóvá Stofn members; 'insurance price' about 20% below list for insurance customers; 15% off Clippasafe safety products (Stofn) · *One shop in Reykjavík. Also lists Stokke, Thule and BabyBjörn.*
- ✓ **Petit** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Bugaboo pram systems (Fox 5, Butterfly 2, Donkey 6 mono/duo/twin) plus Bugaboo car seats and accessories. · [petit.is](https://petit.is) · *Dedicated Bugaboo page; Maxi-Cosi, Ergobaby and Voksi also in the brand list.*
- ✓ **Nine Kids** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur) + netverslun) — Cybex prams and stroller systems with foldable cot options (Talos S Lux, Balios S Lux, Avi Spin), rain covers and footmuffs. · [ninekids.is](https://www.ninekids.is) · **Discount:** 10% off car seats and bases for Sjóvá, Vörður and VÍS customers (not prams). · *Cybex-only stroller range; showroom in Kópavogur plus webshop.*
- ✓ **I Am Happy** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + netverslun) — Silver Cross prams, carrycots and pram units, travel systems and accessories such as footmuffs and cup holders. · [iamhappy.is](https://iamhappy.is) · **Discount:** Vildarklúbbur loyalty club with member offers (amount not stated). · *Also Voksi products.*
- ✓ **Móðurást** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Laugavegur baby shop selling Silver Cross strollers (Cove seen) and car seats alongside breast-pump rental and baby goods. · [modurast.is](https://modurast.is) · **Discount:** 'Vörur á tilboði' sale section; no parent discount stated. · *Also Snuz Pod5 and Medela pump rental.*
- ✓ **Mía** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Webshop for Joolz Geo3, Day5 and Aer strollers with accessories such as sibling footboards and upper-cot adapters. · [miaverslun.is](https://miaverslun.is) · *Newborn suitability not stated on the page; clearance of up to 80% seen.*
- ✓ **Dótabúðin (Chicco Ísland)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur) + netverslun) — Toy shop with a large Chicco section including prams and strollers (kerrur og barnavagnar), car seats and carriers. · [dotabudin.is](https://www.dotabudin.is) · **Discount:** Loyalty scheme giving 5% store credit. · *Mainly a toy shop; Chicco Ísland section.*

### G3-12 · Notaðar barnavörur og barnamarkaðir — Second-hand baby goods & kids' markets (e.g. Barnaloppan)

**Problem:** Baby gear is expensive and outgrown fast  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11

*Market:* Barnaloppan (reportedly moved in 2026 to Köllunarklettsvegur, next to Góði hirðirinn), Gullið mitt and Bland.is lead; also found are Góði hirðirinn (SORPA), Hertex (Salvation Army, Akureyri too), Aftur nýtt (Akureyri, with webshop), Fjarðabásar in Reyðarfjörður, the Visteyri, Kaupx and Barnamarkaðurinn online markets and Lindex's trade-in scheme; no verified kids' market in Hafnarfjörður or Suðurnes.

- ✓ **Barnaloppan** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Second-hand children's market with rented stalls: clothes, toys, prams, strollers and car seats; sellers set their own prices. · [barnaloppan.is](https://barnaloppan.is) · **Discount:** Mailing list: offers plus a monthly draw for a 14-day stall rental. · *Reykjavík only; stalls rented from one week; gift cards sold.*
- ✓ **Gullið mitt** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Second-hand shop with rented booths selling used clothes for children and adults. · [gullidmitt.is](https://gullidmitt.is) · *Kópavogur; baby gear not confirmed, mostly clothing.*
- ✓ **Bland.is** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — National online classifieds with a Barnavörur category (strollers and chairs, cots and beds, toys). · [bland.is](https://www.bland.is) · *Platform, not a shop; no parent offer seen.*
- ✓ **Góði hirðirinn** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — SORPA's reuse market with about 1,000 listed second-hand items, including toys, children's clothing, baby carriers and cradles at low prices. · [godihirdirinn.is](https://godihirdirinn.is/) · *Run by SORPA (municipal waste company), not a private shop; Barnaloppan reportedly moved next door in March 2026 (MBL).*
- ✓ **Hertex** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurnes) — Second-hand shops run by the Salvation Army in Reykjavík, Akureyri (Hrísalundur) and Reykjanesbær selling used clothing, books and household goods. · [hertex.is](https://hertex.is/) · *Run by Hjálpræðisherinn (NGO); children's items not itemised on the site, so the kids' range is unconfirmed.*
- ✓ **Aftur nýtt** (Verslun · Akureyri + netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Akureyri second-hand store and large webshop where sellers rent racks or shelves from 10 days; stocks children's items, toys, clothing and furniture. · [afturnytt.is](https://afturnytt.is/) · *Sunnuhlíð, Akureyri; items sell in the shop and the webshop. Best Akureyri match for a kids' market found.*
- ✓ **Fjarðabásar** (Verslun · Austurland (Reyðarfjörður)) — Stall-rental resale shop in Reyðarfjörður where children's clothing from newborn to 15 years and toys make up a large share of stock; stalls from 8,000 kr per two weeks. · [fjardabasar.is](https://fjardabasar.is/) · *Open Tuesday to Saturday; new items go into stalls every Tuesday.*
- ✓ **Visteyri** (Vettvangur/app · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Eco marketplace run by Visteyri ehf. for used goods with a Börn section for clothing, shoes and baby products; buyers pay on site and items ship nationwide. · [app.visteyri.is](https://app.visteyri.is/) · *visteyri.is redirects to app.visteyri.is.*
- ✓ **Kaupx** (Vettvangur/app · Netverslun (afhending ekki tilgreind)) — Online marketplace where sellers open their own shops, with a Barnafata Markaður section for used baby and children's clothes, toys and bedtime wear. · [kaupx.is](https://kaupx.is/) · *Listings show a pickup option; operating company and delivery terms not stated on the page.*
- ○ ⚠ **Barnamarkaðurinn** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Online shop selling used children's goods at low prices, plus knitwear. · [barnamarkadurinn.is](https://barnamarkadurinn.is/) · *Seen via search result on its own domain; the site returned 503 each time it was fetched, so current activity is unconfirmed. [auto] website did not respond in the final check.*
- ○ **Lindex Ísland** (Keðja · Verslanir víða (staðsetningar ekki staðfestar)) — Clothing chain whose second-hand scheme gives store credit for used Lindex children's clothes, which are resold in selected Icelandic stores. · [dfs.is/frettir/notud-barnafot-i-skiptum-fyrir-inneign](https://www.dfs.is/frettir/notud-barnafot-i-skiptum-fyrir-inneign/) · **Discount:** Store credit for returned used Lindex children's clothing · *Seen only in a South Iceland newspaper item from February 2024; Lindex's own site blocked fetching and whether the scheme still runs is unconfirmed.*

### G3-13 · Leiga á barnavörum — Baby-gear rental (cots, strollers, seats)

**Problem:** Short-term needs, visitors, travel  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3

*Market:* Real baby-gear rental is a tiny market: Barnabílstólar (BeSafe car seats), Bílanaust (Axkid seats) and Móðurást (Medela breast pumps). No cot, stroller or travel-gear rental firm for visitors was found.

- ✓ **Barnabílstólar (Bambino ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Rents BeSafe car seats so families can swap sizes as the child grows; Icelandic partner of BeSafe. · [barnabilstolar.is](https://www.barnabilstolar.is) · *Run by Bambino ehf.; useful for visitors and growing children.*
- ✓ **Bílanaust** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Austurland (Egilsstaðir) + netverslun) — Rents Axkid car seats (rental line seen on a seat page) and sells them. · [bilanaust.is](https://bilanaust.is) · **Discount:** Stofn members: 30% off rented Axkid seats (in store). · *Rental terms not seen.*
- ✓ **Móðurást** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Rents Medela breast pumps (a service run for several decades) at its Laugavegur shop. · [modurast.is](https://modurast.is) · *Only pumps confirmed for rent; see also G2 breast-pump rental.*

### G3-14 · Barnavöruverslanir — General baby & nursery stores (one-stop shops)

**Problem:** Where to buy everything for the baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 16

*Market:* A busy market of roughly fifteen baby and nursery shops, mostly in Reykjavík and Kópavogur plus webshops; the price-comparison site Barnavörur.is lists 20 Icelandic retailers. No dedicated baby shop was found in Akureyri, so webshop delivery is the northern route.

- ✓ **Fífa** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — One-stop baby shop: prams, car seats, carriers, safety products, feeding, nursery items and clothing from Maxi-Cosi, Britax, Emmaljunga, BabyBjörn and others. · [fifa.is](https://fifa.is) · **Discount:** 20% off car seats in store for Sjóvá Stofn members; 15% off Clippasafe safety products (Stofn); 'insurance price' on car seats. · *Faxafen area, Reykjavík; Mon-Fri and Saturday opening.*
- ✓ **Nine Kids** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur) + netverslun) — Baby and children's shop: Cybex car seats and strollers, Stokke high chairs, sleep, feeding, carriers, wooden toys, nursery decor and clothing. · [ninekids.is](https://www.ninekids.is) · **Discount:** 10% off car seats and bases for Sjóvá, Vörður and VÍS customers. · *Showroom in Kópavogur plus webshop; brands incl. Cybex, Stokke, Ergo Pouch, Little Dutch, Liewood.*
- ✓ **Petit** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Reykjavík baby and kids' shop: Bugaboo strollers, Maxi-Cosi, Ergobaby, Voksi, cots, sleep bags, night lights, clothing and gifts. · [petit.is](https://petit.is) · *Shop at Ármúli plus webshop; sizes from newborn to 12 years.*
- ✓ **Móðurást** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Long-running Laugavegur baby shop: Silver Cross strollers and car seats, Snuz sleep products, mealtime and bath items, mother and pregnancy range. · [modurast.is](https://modurast.is) · **Discount:** 'Vörur á tilboði' sale section. · *Also rents Medela breast pumps.*
- ✓ **Barnið okkar** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir innanlands)) — Online baby shop and wholesaler: prams, strollers, i-Size car seats, sheepskin and wool sleeping bags, travel cots, balance bikes and helmets. · [barnidokkar.is](https://barnidokkar.is) · **Discount:** Sale, warehouse-sale and clearance sections. · *Hlíðasmári store is closing; online store and warehouse continue.*
- ✓ **Húsgagnaheimilið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Family-run Reykjavík baby shop (founded 2000): strollers, jogging and sibling strollers, car seats, cots, mattresses, nursery furniture, safety gates and bouncers. · [husgogn.is](https://husgogn.is) · **Discount:** Newsletter with early offers; no parent discount seen. · *Brands incl. Nuna, Baby Jogger, Flexa, Joie, BOB Gear.*
- ✓ **I Am Happy** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + netverslun) — Baby shop for Silver Cross prams, travel systems and car seats plus Voksi, high chairs, toys and children's clothing. · [iamhappy.is](https://iamhappy.is) · **Discount:** Vildarklúbbur loyalty club with member offers. · *Garðabær shop plus webshop.*
- ✓ **Mía** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Webshop for baby and children's products: Joolz and Doona strollers, cots, changing tables, clothing, toys and maternity items. · [miaverslun.is](https://miaverslun.is) · **Discount:** Allt að 80% clearance seen; no parent discount. · *Brands incl. Joolz, Doona, Jellycat, Nobodinoz.*
