## G4 · Svefn og barnaherbergi — Sleep & nursery

### G4-01 · Svefnráðgjöf fyrir ungbörn og smábörn — Infant & toddler sleep consultants

**Problem:** Baby will not sleep, parents exhausted  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3

*Market:* Three private sleep-consulting services exist: Svefnráðgjöf.is (Hafnarfjörður or phone), the online-only Mín líðan (ages 0-7) and the Sofa Borða Elska course brand (4 months-2 years); all work remotely, none is based in Akureyri, and public clinics (Landspítali, health-centre nurses in G21) are the alternative.

- ✓ **Svefnráðgjöf.is** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður) + símaráðgjöf) — Sleep consulting for ages 0-5 (older children welcome): review of sleep habits and a simple tailored plan, in person in Hafnarfjörður or by phone. · [svefnradgjof.is](https://svefnradgjof.is) · *Sole-practitioner service; online booking; 30-minute and longer sessions (twins and complex cases); price list dated February 2024.*
- ✓ **Mín líðan** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (fjarviðtöl)) — Online psychology service offering video sleep consultations for parents of children aged 0-7: assessment interview, follow-up sessions and a sleep diary. · [minlidan.is/fraedsla/svefnthjalfun-barna](https://www.minlidan.is/fraedsla/svefnthjalfun-barna/) · *Video only, so usable from Akureyri and abroad; assessment interview listed at 17,000 kr; also offers family counselling.*
- ✓ **Sofa Borða Elska** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netnámskeið og símtöl)) — Infant and child sleep-consulting brand run by a certified consultant: online course plus group or one-on-one calls for ages 4 months to 2 years. · [sofabordaelska.is](https://www.sofabordaelska.is/) · *Packages by age band (0-3 months, 4-24 months, 2-4 years, siblings) seen in search results; site blocks automated fetching, so the offering is confirmed only via search hits on its own domain.*

### G4-02 · Barnarúm, vöggur og ferðarúm — Cots, cribs, bedside bassinets & travel cots

**Problem:** Where baby sleeps  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 12

*Market:* Cots and travel beds are sold by most baby shops (Petit with Sebra and CamCam, Húsgagnaheimilið, Epal, Móðurást's Snuz Pod5, Dótabúðin's Chicco Next2Me) plus JYSK for budget travel cots; IKEA's baby bed range was not confirmed.

- ✓ **Petit** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Sebra bedside cribs and Baby & Jr. expandable beds, moses baskets, CamCam baby nests, mattresses and cot accessories. · [petit.is](https://petit.is) · *Reykjavík shop and webshop.*
- ✓ **Epal** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Hveragerði + netverslun) — Cribs (Barnarúm) with canopies, mobiles and bedding in its children's sleep and care department. · [epal.is](https://www.epal.is) · *Five stores incl. Skeifan, Kringlan, Smáralind; Sebra among brands.*
- ✓ **Húsgagnaheimilið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Cot categories: Rimlarúm, hliðarúm and vöggur (bedside cribs), Húsrúm and Kojur & Rúm, plus mattresses from 40x90 cm. · [husgogn.is](https://husgogn.is) · *Reykjavík shop and webshop; Flexa among brands.*
- ✓ **Móðurást** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Snuz Pod5 listed under cribs and mattresses at the Laugavegur baby shop. · [modurast.is](https://modurast.is) · *Product type not described on the page.*
- ✓ **Dótabúðin (Chicco Ísland)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur) + netverslun) — Chicco Next2Me co-sleeper beds and travel cots in a Kópavogur toy shop with webshop. · [dotabudin.is](https://www.dotabudin.is) · **Discount:** Loyalty scheme giving 5% store credit.
- ✓ **Barnið okkar** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir innanlands)) — Travel cots, bed rails and a co-sleeper category in the 'Sofum' (sleep) range. · [barnidokkar.is](https://barnidokkar.is) · **Discount:** Sale and clearance sections. · *Webshop with warehouse; some cots made to order (4-6 weeks).*
- ✓ **Bíumbíum** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Cribs and canopy beds (himnasængur) plus bedding among its sleep products. · [biumbium.is](https://biumbium.is) · *Reykjavík shop and webshop.*
- ✓ **Mía** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Cots (Vöggur), changing tables and bedding. · [miaverslun.is](https://miaverslun.is) · *Webshop.*
- ✓ **I Am Happy** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + netverslun) — Silver Cross Slumber travel cot (ferðarúm). · [iamhappy.is](https://iamhappy.is) · **Discount:** Vildarklúbbur loyalty club with member offers. · *Garðabær shop plus webshop; only one cot seen.*
- ✓ **JYSK (áður Rúmfatalagerinn)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Selfoss + Reykjanesbær + netverslun) — Foam cot mattresses and the VODSKOV travel cot in its Barnavara range. · [jysk.is](https://jysk.is) · *Stores incl. Akureyri; some items online-only or limited stock.*
- ✓ **Nine Kids** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur) + netverslun) — Foldable cot included in Cybex pram bundles and a JetKids CloudSleeper travel mattress. · [ninekids.is](https://www.ninekids.is) · *No standalone cot listed.*
- ○ **IKEA Ísland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + netverslun) — Children's sleep range (beds and bedding) at the Kauptún store and webshop. · [ikea.is](https://www.ikea.is) · *Cots themselves not confirmed on the page.*

- ✓ **Epal** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Hveragerði + netverslun) — Children's bedding (Rúmföt), canopy beds and nursery textiles. · [epal.is](https://www.epal.is) · *Five stores plus webshop.*

### G4-05 · Hvítur hávaði, næturljós og svefnhjálp — White-noise machines, night lights & sleep aids

**Problem:** Settling and keeping baby asleep  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Sleep aids are an add-on for baby and design shops: Petit (Moonie white-noise bears with night light), Regnboginn (night lights, Snuza monitors), DIMM, Epal and IKEA (children's lamps), and a deals webshop selling a white-noise device; Cloud B soothers appear at Bíumbíum and Barnið okkar.

- ✓ **Petit** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Moonie humming bear and bunny with night light (white-noise range) plus a night-light lamp collection. · [petit.is](https://petit.is) · *Reykjavík shop and webshop.*
- ✓ **Ofurkaup / barnasvefn.is** (Netverslun · Netverslun (heimsending innanlands)) — Deals webshop selling a white-noise machine with night light for babies (26 sounds, timer) via its barnasvefn.is page. · [barnasvefn.is](https://barnasvefn.is) · *Run by Ofurkaup ehf., Kópavogur; promotional price; home delivery within Iceland.*
- ✓ **Regnboginn verslun** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur) + netverslun) — Night lights and Snuza baby-movement products alongside FirstBIKE and toys. · [regnboginnverslun.is](https://regnboginnverslun.is) · **Discount:** Regnbogaklúbburinn mailing list (offers first). · *Kópavogur shop and webshop.*
- ✓ **DIMM** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Lamps and night lights for children's rooms in a design shop. · [dimm.is](https://dimm.is) · **Discount:** Free shipping over 15,000 kr; mailing list. · *Reykjavík shop and webshop.*
- ✓ **Epal** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Hveragerði + netverslun) — Children's lamps, mobiles and music boxes in its nursery and sleep departments. · [epal.is](https://www.epal.is) · *Five stores plus webshop.*
- ✓ **IKEA Ísland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + netverslun) — Lighting for children range at the Kauptún store and webshop. · [ikea.is](https://www.ikea.is) · *Not baby-specific.*
- ✓ **Minimo** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Lighting within its nursery and bedroom range. · [minimo.is](https://minimo.is) · *Faxafen shop plus webshop.*
- ○ **Bíumbíum** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Cloud B listed among brands (sleep soothers) in a Reykjavík children's shop. · [biumbium.is](https://biumbium.is) · *Products not seen on the page.*
- ○ **Barnið okkar** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir innanlands)) — Cloud-B listed among brands on its front page. · [barnidokkar.is](https://barnidokkar.is) · *Products not seen on the page.*

### G4-06 · Myrkvunargardínur og rúllugardínur — Blackout curtains & blinds

**Problem:** Midnight sun and bright summer nights ruin sleep  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 12

*Market:* Blackout blinds are sold ready-made by IKEA, JYSK, ILVA and Bauhaus (60 blackout roller blinds, nationwide delivery) and made to measure by Álnabær (also Akureyri), Z-brautir, Gardínur ehf., Sólar, Myrkvun, Móðins and Vogue (Reykjavík and Akureyri); Myrkvi alters and fits ready-made curtains; Húsasmiðjan and BYKO showed no blinds category on their sites.

- ✓ **Álnabær** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes + Akureyri) — Custom curtains and blackout roller blinds (myrkvunargardínur), blackout Pli-Sol and Twinlight, with measuring and installation service. · [alnabaer.is](https://www.alnabaer.is) · *Stores in Reykjavík, Reykjanesbær and Akureyri; no family offer seen.*
- ✓ **JYSK (áður Rúmfatalagerinn)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Selfoss + Reykjanesbær + netverslun) — Ready-made blackout roller blinds (Darker, Bolga, Falster, Hopen, Fonno thermo) in many sizes, plus ready-made curtains. · [jysk.is](https://jysk.is) · *Stores incl. Akureyri; some sizes sold out online.*
- ○ **IKEA Ísland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + netverslun) — Curtains range at the Kauptún store and webshop. · [ikea.is](https://www.ikea.is) · *Blackout items not confirmed on the page.*
- ○ **ILVA** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + Akureyri + Selfoss + netverslun) — Curtains (Gardínur) in its textile range. · [ilva.is](https://ilva.is) · *Stores in Garðabær, Selfoss and Akureyri; blackout items not confirmed.*
- ✓ **Bauhaus Ísland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — DIY and home store with 60 blackout roller blinds among 80 ready-made roller blinds (Debel, Kirsch), widths 90-160 cm, delivered nationwide. · [bauhaus.is/heimilid-og-geymslan/heimilid/gardinur/rullugardinur](https://www.bauhaus.is/heimilid-og-geymslan/heimilid/gardinur/rullugardinur) · *Ready-made sizes only on the page; made-to-measure and installation not stated. One store, in Reykjavík.*
- ✓ **Gardínur ehf.** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Made-to-measure roller blinds with blackout fabrics, pleated blinds with blackout options and slat blinds; measuring and installation mainly in the capital area. · [gardinur.is](https://gardinur.is/) · *Online price calculator, quote after measuring; free consultation on screen curtains; no Akureyri service mentioned.*
- ✓ **Z-brautir og gluggatjöld** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + sendir um allt land) — Made-to-measure 100% blackout roller blinds and strip blinds, plus blackout pleated blinds and sewn curtains from its own workshop; measuring and installation offered. · [z.is/myrkvunargardinur](https://www.z.is/myrkvunargardinur/) · *Shop and workshop at Faxafen, Reykjavík; page says products are sent countrywide, Akureyri delivery not specifically stated.*
- ✓ **Vogue fyrir heimilið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri (Glerártorg) + netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Home store with a curtains section: made-to-measure pleated blinds from your measurements (measuring visit on request) and roller blinds in screen, blackout and standard fabrics. · [vogue.is](https://vogue.is/) · **Discount:** 10% off first purchase for mailing-list sign-ups · *Shops in Reykjavík (Síðumúli) and Akureyri; blackout and made-to-measure detail seen in a search result on its own domain, as the curtains page itself returned 404 when fetched.*
- ✓ **Sólar gluggatjöld** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Icelandic producer of curtains and roller blinds since 1946, made to measure with a blackout option; staff can measure at your home. · [solar.is/voruflokkur/2-rullugardinur](https://solar.is/voruflokkur/2-rullugardinur) · *Showroom at Ármúli, Reykjavík; pages did not render when fetched, details seen in search results on its own domain; delivery outside the capital area not stated.*
- ✓ **Myrkvun** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (mæling og uppsetning) + netverslun) — Authorised Blind Screen dealer: made-to-measure window covers with full blackout or 50% light filtering, plus roller, slat and pleated blinds; self-install or fitting. · [myrkvun.is/is](https://www.myrkvun.is/is) · **Discount:** Measuring fee of 8,000 kr is credited toward the purchase · *Installation only mentioned for the capital area; showroom and Akureyri service not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Myrkvi Gardínuþjónusta** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Curtain service that shortens, narrows and mounts ready-made curtains from JYSK, IKEA and Bauhaus and can measure your windows or buy for you. · [myrkvi.is](https://www.myrkvi.is/) · *Workshop in Reykjavík with visits by arrangement; blackout products not named on the page; price list on site.*
- ✓ **Móðins Gluggatjöld** (Verslun · Ekki tilgreint á vef) — Made-to-measure pleated, roller and slat blinds with measuring, optional installation, free non-binding quotes and a typical lead time of 2-5 weeks. · [modins.is](https://modins.is/) · *Page does not mention blackout fabrics or its location, so ask about blackout options.*

- ✓ **JYSK (áður Rúmfatalagerinn)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Selfoss + Reykjanesbær + netverslun) — Children's furniture (Barnahúsgögn) and changing-table mattresses. · [jysk.is](https://jysk.is) · *Stores incl. Akureyri.*
- ✓ **ILVA** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + Akureyri + Selfoss + netverslun) — Children's room (Barnaherbergi) category with children's beds and bean-bag cushions. · [ilva.is](https://ilva.is) · *Stores in Garðabær, Selfoss and Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Epal** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Hveragerði + netverslun) — Children's furniture (Barnahúsgögn), lamps, storage boxes, rugs and posters from Sebra, Kids by Friis, Ferm Living and others. · [epal.is](https://www.epal.is) · *Five stores plus webshop.*

- ✓ **Minimo** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Nursery and bedroom items: furniture, lighting, cushions, rugs and storage. · [minimo.is](https://minimo.is) · *Faxafen shop plus webshop.*

### G4-09 · Barnvæn málning og efni án eiturefna — Child-safe paint & low-emission materials

**Problem:** Painting the nursery safely  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3

*Market:* Three paint suppliers clearly offer Nordic Swan (Svansmerkt) interior paint: Slippfélagið (says it poses no risk to infants or allergy sufferers), Málning (Kópal, Svanurinn category, solvent-free Glitra 10) and Flügger (Dekso AÏR allergy-friendly); none markets specifically to nurseries.

- ✓ **Slippfélagið** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + endursöluaðilar víða um land) — Svansmerkt (Nordic Swan) paint range; its page says the paint is solvent-free and poses no risk to infants, pregnant women or asthmatics. · [slippfelagid.is](https://www.slippfelagid.is) · *Main store in Reykjavík plus resellers around the country; Akureyri store listed on its site.*
- ✓ **Málning hf. (Kópal)** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt (sölustaðir um land)) — Kópal indoor paints with a Svansmerkt product category; Kópal Glitra 10 is odourless and solvent-free. · [malning.is](https://www.malning.is) · *Which indoor paints carry the Swan label not itemised on the page.*
- ✓ **Flügger Ísland** (Verslun · Landið allt (verslanaleit á vef) + netverslun) — Svansmerkt indoor paints (Dekso 1 Ultramat, Dekso AÏR, Perform 5) and an allergy-friendly wall paint. · [flugger.is](https://flugger.is) · *Click-and-collect and store finder on site.*

### G4-11 · Rúm og dýnur fyrir foreldra — Parents' mattresses & beds (sleep quality)

**Problem:** Poor sleep and back pain, co-sleeping set-ups  
**Stig:** Foreldrarnir · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

*Market:* Parents' beds and mattresses are well served: Svefn & heilsa, Betra Bak and Dorma all have Reykjavík and Akureyri stores; Rekkjan sells health beds; JYSK, IKEA and ILVA cover the mid and budget segments. None showed any new-parent offer.

- ✓ **Dorma (GER verslanir ehf.)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri) — Beds, mattresses (Simba, Sealy, MLILY), bedding and furniture from GER verslanir ehf. · [dorma.is](https://dorma.is) · *Stores in Smáratorg, Holtagarðar and Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Rekkjan** (Verslun · Netverslun + verslun (staðsetning ekki staðfest)) — Health beds and mattresses (Kensa, Royal, LS), mattress toppers, duvets and pillows. · [rekkjan.is](https://rekkjan.is) · *Opening-hours page exists; address not captured.*
- ✓ **JYSK (áður Rúmfatalagerinn)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Selfoss + Reykjanesbær + netverslun) — Mattresses and beds (Dýnur og rúm), duvets and pillows. · [jysk.is](https://jysk.is) · *Stores incl. Akureyri.*
- ✓ **IKEA Ísland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + netverslun) — Beds, bed frames and mattresses at the Kauptún store and webshop. · [ikea.is](https://www.ikea.is)
- ✓ **ILVA** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + Akureyri + Selfoss + netverslun) — Bed frames (Luna, Wonderland) and Luna mattresses in 140, 160 and 180 cm. · [ilva.is](https://ilva.is) · *Stores in Garðabær, Selfoss and Akureyri.*

- ✓ **ILVA** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + Akureyri + Selfoss + netverslun) — Children's beds (Barnarúm) category under its children's room range. · [ilva.is](https://ilva.is) · *Stores in Garðabær, Selfoss and Akureyri; products not itemised.*

- ✓ **Epal** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Hveragerði + netverslun) — Canopy beds (Himnasængur) and cribs in its children's sleep department. · [epal.is](https://www.epal.is) · *Five stores plus webshop.*

## G5 · Föt, skór og íslensk barnahönnun — Clothing, shoes & Icelandic kids' design

### G5-01 · Nýburaföt og heimferðarsett — Newborn clothing & going-home outfits

**Problem:** First clothes for the hospital and home  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11

*Market:* Newborn clothing is sold by independent baby boutiques in Reykjavík (Petit, Bíumbíum, Fífa) and by fashion chains in Smáralind/Kringlan (Mayoral, KappAhl, Lindex, Name It, H&M); no one dominates.

- ✓ **Petit** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Reykjavík children's boutique with going-home (heimferð), newborn and christening collections; Danish labels such as Konges Sløjd and MarMar; sizes from 56, premature range too. · [petit.is](https://petit.is) · **Discount:** Seasonal sale items (e.g. 15% off some outerwear) · *Ármúli 23. Also Bugaboo, Maxi-Cosi, Ergobaby, Jellycat. Sells only in its own store and webshop.*
- ✓ **Bíumbíum** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Reykjavík children's shop with a dedicated Nýburinn (newborn) collection, baby sets, bodysuits, wool and organic pieces, plus prams, car seats and nursery goods. · [biumbium.is](https://biumbium.is) · **Discount:** 10% insurance discount on BeSafe car seats (all domestic insurers) · *Ármúli 38. Brands MINI A TURE, WHEAT, HUTTEliHUT, MoonBoon, BeSafe. Same-day dispatch if ordered before noon.*
- ✓ **Fífa** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Large baby-goods store with a clothing category (Reima, Minymo, Hust & Claire) alongside prams, car seats, feeding and nursery products. · [fifa.is](https://fifa.is) · **Discount:** 20% off car seats for customers of selected insurers; periodic clothing sales · *Faxafen 8. Also Maxi-Cosi, Britax, Stokke, Emmaljunga, BabyBjörn, Mushie.*
- ✓ **Mayoral Iceland** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Smáralind) + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Spanish children's wear chain with a 0-18 month range (all-in-ones, sets, gift ideas) plus infant shoes sizes 15-25. · [mayoraliceland.is](https://mayoraliceland.is) · **Discount:** Customer club sign-up; free shipping over 10,000 kr · *Store in Smáralind per mall directory; also 6-36 month ranges.*
- ✓ **KappAhl Ísland** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Smáralind) + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Swedish family fashion chain with a Newbie newborn range (0-18 months), a separate premature-baby section, kids' and maternity clothing. · [kappahl.is](https://kappahl.is) · **Discount:** KappAhl club: 20% off first order plus points · *Store in Smáralind per mall directory; Akureyri store not confirmed.*
- ✓ **As We Grow** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnartorg) + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Icelandic knit and woven label with children's ranges for 0-36 months in alpaca and other natural fibres; shop and studio at Hafnartorg. · [aswegrowiceland.com](https://aswegrowiceland.com) · **Discount:** 10% off first order via newsletter; free shipping over 25,000 ISK · *Tryggvagata 23. Gender-neutral designs; also sells to adults.*

### G5-06 · Barnaskór og sokkar — Baby & toddler shoes and socks

**Problem:** First shoes and cold feet  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11

*Market:* First shoes are sold by sports retailers (Dansport, H verslun), boutiques (Petit, Bíumbíum, Fífa), Mayoral and Hagkaup; no dedicated children's shoe specialist found.

- ✓ **Varma** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Icelandic wool factory founded 1926 making blankets, sweaters, hats and gloves in Iceland, with a children's clothing category. · [varma.is](https://varma.is) · **Discount:** Outlet section; free shipping over 18,000 kr · *Store locator on site; children's items not visible when checked.*

## G6 · Bleyjur, böð og húðumhirða — Diapers, bath & skincare

### G6-01 · Bleyjur — Disposable diapers

**Problem:** Thousands of them, and the cost adds up  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 15

*Market:* Supermarkets and pharmacies dominate. Krónan, Lyfja, Heimkaup, Costco and Móðurást were found first; added Bónus (Bambo Nature Nordic Swan diapers, Barnabónus new-parent gift bag), Kjörbúðin (free baby gift with diapers), Apótekarinn, Lyfjaval, RV, Lindsay (Huggies) and aha.is. Nettó, Hagkaup and Fjarðarkaup are listed as unconfirmed (only older price surveys show diapers; their sites list none online).

- ✓ **Krónan** (Keðja · Landið allt (Akureyri included) + Netverslun (heimsending)) — Supermarket chain selling Nordic-Swan-labelled Grön Balance diapers (newborn to size 6 plus pull-ups) and baby wipes; online ordering and home delivery. · [kronan.is/svansvottadar-bleiur](https://kronan.is/svansvottadar-bleiur) · **Discount:** Prices on diaper range frozen ('Fryst verð'); no parent club found · *Newborn pack 28 pcs 529 kr. Home delivery covers capital area and many places in North, East and South.*
- ✓ **Lyfja** (Keðja · Landið allt + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Pharmacy chain with a Bleyjusvæði (nappy area): diapers, wipes and nappy-rash cream, plus a wider Móðir og barn range; webshop. · [lyfja.is](https://www.lyfja.is) · **Discount:** 'Vöggugjöf Lyfju' newborn gift mentioned on site; free shipping over 9,900 kr · *45 pharmacies and branches nationwide per site. Webshop diaper filter showed only Grön Balance (mini, 28 pcs 1,099 kr).*
- ✓ **Heimkaup** (Netverslun · Netverslun (heimsending)) — Icelandic online store with home delivery; its product sitemap lists Coterie premium diapers (sizes 1-3) and wipes alongside a Baby & Kids category. · [heimkaup.is](https://www.heimkaup.is) · *Mainstream diaper brands (Pampers, Huggies) not seen in the sitemap; confirm range. Homepage showed drinks and bundles.*
- ○ **Costco Iceland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun) — Membership warehouse with a Barnavörur & leikföng (baby products & toys) category; large packs are the usual draw for diapers. · [costco.is](https://costco.is) · **Discount:** Membership required for warehouse savings · *Diaper range not confirmed on the page fetched.*
- ✓ **Móðurást** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Mother-and-baby shop with a 'Bleyjur, koppar og bleyjufötur' (nappies, potties and nappy bins) category; cloth nappies seen. · [modurast.is](https://modurast.is) · *Laugavegur 178 per Wolt listing; webshop categories and products updated Oct 2026. Disposable diaper brands not itemised.*
- ✓ **Bónus** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Discount supermarket chain whose site presents Bambo Nature diapers (Nordic Swan and EU Ecolabel certified) under 'our products', newborn to 16+ kg, plus swim diapers. · [bonus.is/um-bonus/vorurnar-okkar/bambo-nature-bleiur](https://bonus.is/um-bonus/vorurnar-okkar/bambo-nature-bleiur/) · **Discount:** Barnabónus: free gift bag for expecting and new parents (babies up to 3 months) with full-size products and samples, collected in a Bónus store after online sign-up; its page also mentions discount terms · *Diaper pages are undated (a news post appears to be from about 2019) and give no prices; the online shop only sells a gift-card top-up, so confirm the current range in store.*
- ✓ **Kjörbúðin (Samkaup)** (Keðja · Akureyri + Norðurland + Austurland + Suðurnes + Suðurland + Vesturland) — Samkaup's small-town supermarket chain (16 stores) that gives new parents a free baby gift (vöggugjöf) containing Änglamark diapers, wipes and nappy cream. · [kjorbudin.is/voggugjof](https://kjorbudin.is/voggugjof/) · **Discount:** Free vöggugjöf for babies under 3 months (diapers, wipes, nappy cream, baby oil), requested on the website; eligibility depends on municipality · *Page lists the gift contents, not a separate diaper shelf range. Bambino Mio and Eco Naps. · [taubleyjur.is](https://taubleyjur.is) · **Discount:** Periodic campaigns (e.g. 15-40% in a Singles Day promotion) · *Álfabakki 6, Reykjavík, weekdays 11-17. - ✓ **Móðurást** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Mother-and-baby shop selling BORA cloth nappies within its nappies, potties and nappy-bins category. · [modurast.is](https://modurast.is) · *Laugavegur 178 per Wolt listing; webshop categories and products updated Oct 2026.*

### G6-03 · Bleyjuáskrift og heimsending — Diaper subscription & delivery

**Problem:** Never running out at 3 a.m.

- ✓ **Krónan** (Keðja · Landið allt (Akureyri included) + Netverslun (heimsending)) — Supermarket chain with online ordering (Snjallverslun and app) and home delivery to the capital area and many places in the North, East and South. · [kronan.is](https://kronan.is) · *Stocks Nordic-Swan-labelled diapers and wipes (Grön Balance). No subscription mentioned on page.*
- ✓ **Lyfja** (Keðja · Landið allt + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Pharmacy chain whose webshop sells diapers, wipes and nappy cream and offers delivery; free shipping over 9,900 kr. · [lyfja.is](https://www.lyfja.is) · **Discount:** Free shipping over 9,900 kr · *Drive-through hatches at several branches.*
- ✓ **Lyfjaval** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes + Suðurland + Netverslun) — Pharmacy chain with a Parents and Children category, webshop and home delivery (Heimsending); drive-through hatches open 24/7 at Hæðasmári. · [lyfjaval.is](https://www.lyfjaval.is) · *Branches in Kópavogur, Reykjavík, Reykjanes and Selfoss; baby range not itemised.*

## G7 · Heilsa barna — Child health

### G7-01 · Barnalæknar á einkastofum — Private paediatricians

**Problem:** Baby is unwell and you want a quick appointment  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3

*Market:* Essentially one private paediatric practice (Domus barnalæknar, Kópavogur, open every day of the year, already listed); second pass adds Læknastöðin (Glæsibær: 1 paediatrician + 1 paediatric surgeon + ENT) and Heilsuklasinn (paediatric cardiologist); the paediatric society's member list shows most members at Landspítali, SAk or other public units (18 at Domus barnalæknar); no private paediatric practice confirmed in Akureyri (SAk children's ward is public, Læknastofur Akureyrar at Glerártorg has no paediatrics; one society member lists a private Akureyri address, unverified).

- ✓ **Domus barnalæknar (Barnalæknaþjónustan ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Paediatric specialist centre in Urðarhvarf, Kópavogur: dozens of paediatricians incl. sub-specialists, online booking for weekday daytime visits plus evening/weekend on-call service every day of the year. · [barnalaeknardomus.is](https://barnalaeknardomus.is/) · *Moved from Domus Medica to Urðarhvarf 8 in Jan 2022; also the main private after-hours service for children (see G7-02) and has allergy/infectious-disease sub-specialists (see G7-07). Old domain barnavaktin.is redirects here.*
- ✓ **Læknastöðin (Glæsibæ)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private specialist centre in Glæsibær with about 40 specialists, incl. a paediatrician and a paediatric surgeon; stresses good service for families with children. · [laeknastodin.is](https://laeknastodin.is) · *Doctor list shows 1 paediatrician, 1 paediatric surgeon and 12 ENT doctors; clinic news item says the referral requirement for children's specialist visits was abolished; overlaps with the Orkuhúsið cooperation (musculoskeletal).*
- ✓ **Heilsuklasinn (sérfræðilæknar, Bíldshöfða)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Health cluster at Bíldshöfði whose specialist doctors include a paediatric cardiologist; same group runs the private Höfða health centres with infant and pregnancy care. · [klasinn.is/serfraedilaeknar](https://klasinn.is/serfraedilaeknar/) · *Specialist level only (paediatric cardiology; homepage also names paediatric allergy/immunology), not a general walk-in paediatrician; group also runs Heilsugæslan Höfða (see G7-04) and Medicus (workplace health).*

### G7-02 · Læknavakt og kvöld- og helgarþjónusta — After-hours & weekend doctors

**Problem:** Fever at 11 p.m.  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3

*Market:* Capital area: Læknavaktin (evenings, weekends, home visits) and Domus paediatric on-call (both already listed), plus Tannlæknavaktin for dental emergencies; the private health centres only run a one-hour afternoon duty (Höfða, Urðarhvarfi, Kirkjusandi). Outside the capital (incl. Akureyri/HSN, SAk, HSS) after-hours care is the public 1700 line and on-call clinics, so no private buyer; no private English-language home-visit doctor found.

- ✓ **Læknavaktin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Out-of-hours doctor service for the capital area: reception weekday evenings, weekends and holidays, plus an evening/weekend home-visit doctor service. · [laeknavaktin.is](https://laeknavaktin.is/) · *Hours per island.is: reception 17-22 weekdays, 09-22 weekends/holidays; home visits 17-23:30 weekdays, 08-23:30 weekends. Site blocks non-Icelandic IPs (403), so verified only via search hit and island.is.*
- ✓ **Domus barnalæknar - bráða- og vaktþjónusta** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Paediatricians on call for sick children in Urðarhvarf, Kópavogur: weekdays 17:00-21:00, weekends 09:30-14:00, open every day of the year. · [barnalaeknardomus.is/vaktthjonusta](https://barnalaeknardomus.is/vaktthjonusta/) · *Same company as G7-01 (Barnalæknaþjónustan, formerly barnavaktin.is). Older sources give slightly different hours, so check before visiting.*
- ✓ **Tannlæknavaktin ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Dental emergency clinic in Reykjavík open evenings (to 22:00 Mon-Thu) and weekends; handles tooth injuries, severe toothache and oral infections. · [tannlaeknavaktin.is](https://tannlaeknavaktin.is/) · *Dentist on call; dental emergencies only (not fever/illness); phone booking required and location varies by day; hours as stated on its own (old-style) site; also relevant to G7-09 for children's tooth injuries.*

### G7-03 · Fjarlækningar og netlæknir — Online doctor / telemedicine

**Problem:** Quick advice from home  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 2

*Market:* Private telemedicine still nascent: Fjarlækningar (questionnaire-based consults for common problems, HSU pilot patients 18+) and Kara Connect are the only private players found; no Icelandic private online paediatrician; public chat/phone advice via Heilsuvera and 1700; Lyfja offers digital psychology (in its app) and nursing services, not doctors and Doktor.is is only a health-information portal with written Q&A.

- ✓ **Fjarlækningar** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt (netþjónusta)) — Icelandic online doctor service: pick a common complaint, answer a doctor-designed questionnaire and get a reply or e-prescription within about two hours, daily 10:00-22:00. · [fjarlaekningar.is](https://fjarlaekningar.is/) · *Adults 18+ with electronic ID only, so useful for the parents themselves, not children. Pilot with HSU (Vestmannaeyjar, Uppsveitir) is free to those patients; wider rollout planned.*
- ○ ⚠ **Kara Connect** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt (netþjónusta)) — Icelandic-founded video-consultation and practice platform used by health, welfare and education professionals (e.g. therapists, dietitians) to see clients remotely. · [karaconnect.com](https://karaconnect.com/) · *B2B platform: parents meet it via the clinician they book, not as a stand-alone online doctor. Relevant also to G7-14 and G7-16. [auto] website did not respond in the final check.*

### G7-04 · Einkareknar heilsugæslustöðvar — Privately operated health centres

**Problem:** Choosing a clinic for the family  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* Complete picture found: 19 capital-area stations, 15 run by public HH and 4 private (Höfða, Salahverfi, Urðarhvarfi/Heilsuvernd, Kirkjusandi), plus Höfða Suðurnesjum in Reykjanesbær; second pass adds Urðarhvarfi and Kirkjusandi. Akureyri/North Iceland is served by the public HSN; no private station found outside the capital area and Reykjanesbær.

- ✓ **Heilsugæslan Höfða (Heilsuklasinn)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Privately run health centre at Bíldshöfði, Reykjavík: family doctors, nurses, pregnancy care, infant and toddler care, psychology, afternoon same-day slot and travel vaccinations. · [hgh.is](https://hgh.is/) · *Part of the Heilsuklasinn group (klasinn.is) with paediatric allergy and cardiology specialists, physio, psychology and dietitians on site (see G7-07, G7-13, G7-14, G7-16). Operator name not stated on page. Also listed as: Heilsugæslan Höfða Suðurnesjum.*
- ✓ **Heilsugæslan Salahverfi (Salus)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Privately run health centre in Salahverfi, Kópavogur: family doctors, nurses and midwives, pregnancy care, infant and toddler care, psychology for children and adults, parent education. · [salus.is](https://www.salus.is/) · *Linked to the Heilsuklasinn group; open 08-17 with a 16-17 afternoon same-day slot. Operator company not named on page.*
- ✓ **Heilsugæslan Kirkjusandi** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Privately run health centre in Reykjavík with pregnancy care, home-visit infant monitoring with nurse and paediatrician check-ups, school health and psychology. · [hgkirkjusandi.is](https://hgkirkjusandi.is/) · *Named by Vísir (2026) as one of the four private capital-area stations; formerly Heilsugæslan Lágmúla; walk-in síðdegisvakt Mon-Thu 16-17; Sameind runs the lab.*
- ✓ **Heilsugæslan Urðarhvarfi (Heilsuvernd)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Private health centre in Kópavogur run by Heilsuvernd: GP and nurse care, pregnancy care, infant and toddler care, vaccinations, psychology and school health. · [heilsugaesla.hv.is](https://heilsugaesla.hv.is/) · *Operated by Heilsuvernd (also nursing homes and workplace health); doctors' síðdegisvakt 16-17 on weekdays only for the station's own registered patients; one of the four private capital-area stations (Vísir 2026).*

### G7-05 · Apótek og netapótek — Pharmacies & online pharmacies (baby medicines & supplies)

**Problem:** Baby medicines, formula, thermometers  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 14

*Market:* Big market: three chains (Lyfja, Apótekarinn, Lyfjaval) plus about a dozen independents, most with online ordering or home delivery. Lyfja already runs a free new-parent gift (Vöggugjöf Lyfju) - a ready-made warm lead. 14 listed; more small pharmacies exist (the Medicines Agency lists all).

- ✓ **Lyfja** (Keðja · Landið allt (m.a. Akureyri, Egilsstaðir, Ísafjörður, Húsavík)) — Pharmacy chain, 45 pharmacies and branches, with a Móðir og barn range (nappies, breast pumps, bottles, children's medicines and vitamins), webshop, app and home delivery. · [lyfja.is](https://www.lyfja.is/) · **Discount:** Free 'Vöggugjöf Lyfju' gift pack for expectant parents and babies up to 3 months (incl. a 66°Norður voucher; showed sold out); free delivery over 9,900 kr. · *Already sponsors/partners on a new-parent gift; strongest incumbent for this slot.*
- ✓ **Apótekarinn** (Keðja · Landið allt (m.a. Glæsibær, Miklabraut, Urðarhvarf, Suðurfell, Vesturlandsvegur, Reykjanes, Selfoss.*
- ✓ **Lyfjaver** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Low-price pharmacy and online pharmacy at Suðurlandsbraut with a Foreldri og barn category and free home delivery over 9,900 kr. · [lyfjaver.is](https://www.lyfjaver.is/) · **Discount:** Vildarklúbbur loyalty club (details not shown) · *Approved online pharmacy per Lyfjastofnun.*

### G7-06 · Heimapróf og mælitæki — Thermometers, pulse oximeters, nasal aspirators, baby scales & home tests

**Problem:** Checking fever, breathing, weight at home  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Thermometers, oximeters, nasal care and home tests are sold mainly by the pharmacy chains (Lyfja, Apótekarinn, Lyfjaval), plus ELKO (Beurer oximeter, first-aid kit) and a Chicco stockist. The independent pharmacies in G7-05 also stock basic mælar. No dedicated baby-health-device specialist found; medical-device wholesalers (Fastus, Icepharma, Medor) are B2B only.

- ✓ **Lyfja** (Keðja · Landið allt (m.a. Akureyri) + netverslun) — Pharmacy chain with a Sjálfspróf og mælar range: thermometers, pulse/oxygen-saturation meters, blood-pressure meters, pregnancy and ovulation tests, plus nasal drops and teething items. · [lyfja.is](https://www.lyfja.is/) · **Discount:** Free 'Vöggugjöf Lyfju' gift pack for expectant parents/babies up to 3 months (when in stock) · *Same company as G7-05 entry; also Heilsuhúsið now sits under Lyfja.*
- ✓ **Apótekarinn** (Keðja · Landið allt (m.a. Akureyri) + netverslun) — Pharmacy chain (Lyf og heilsa ehf.) with an Aðhlynning range covering Mælar og sjálfspróf, nose/throat/ear care and heat/cold products, plus a Móðir og barn section. · [apotekarinn.is/adhlynning](https://www.apotekarinn.is/adhlynning) · **Discount:** Apótekarinn+ loyalty programme · *Same company as G7-05 entry.*
- ✓ **Lyfjaval** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Reykjanes + Selfoss + netverslun) — Discount pharmacy chain with a Sjálfspróf og mælar category, a Foreldrar og börn category and home delivery. · [lyfjaval.is](https://www.lyfjaval.is/) · *Same company as G7-05 entry.*
- ✓ **ELKO** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + netverslun) — Electronics chain whose health range includes Beurer pulse and oxygen-saturation meters, nebulisers, bathroom scales and a Red Cross first-aid kit. · [elko.is/voruflokkar/ymis-taeki-182](https://elko.is/voruflokkar/ymis-taeki-182) · *Thermometers and baby scales not seen in the category pages checked; also relevant to baby monitors (G9-02).*
- ✓ **Dótabúðin** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur) + netverslun) — Toy and baby shop in Kópavogur and webshop stocking the Chicco range, including thermometers, nasal aspirators and breast pumps. · [dotabudin.is/pages/chicco](https://dotabudin.is/pages/chicco) · **Discount:** Dótaklúbburinn gives 5% store credit (not parent-specific) · *Chicco is the main baby brand there; also lists a safety gate (G9-01).*

### G7-07 · Ofnæmispróf og ofnæmislæknar barna — Allergy testing & allergists

**Problem:** Rash, food-allergy fears  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 2

*Market:* Paediatric allergy is concentrated at Domus barnalæknar (paediatric allergy and immunology specialists, ages 0-17). Heilsuklasinn lists general allergy/immunology specialists but no child-specific allergy service. Other private allergy options (Landspítali's allergy clinic is public) were not found; no private allergy-testing lab found.

- ✓ **Domus barnalæknar - ofnæmislækningar barna** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Paediatric allergy and immunology specialists for ages 0-17: asthma, eczema, hay fever and pet allergy, food allergy and intolerance, drug allergy and hives. · [barnalaeknardomus.is/sergreinar-laekninga](https://barnalaeknardomus.is/sergreinar-laekninga/) · *Same company as G7-01; page does not detail which tests (skin-prick etc.) are offered.*
- ✓ **Heilsuklasinn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Health cluster at Bíldshöfði, Reykjavík with specialists in allergy and immunology and in allergy and pulmonology, plus a child cardiologist, physio, psychology and dietitians on site. · [klasinn.is/serfraedilaeknar](https://klasinn.is/serfraedilaeknar/) · *Allergy specialists not stated to be paediatric; same group as Heilsugæslan Höfða and Salahverfi (G7-04).*

### G7-08 · Faðernispróf og erfðapróf — Paternity & DNA tests

**Problem:** Establishing paternity  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 0 (+2 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* No private Icelandic paternity or consumer DNA-test seller found (Sameind, deCODE and Matís list no such service); Landspítali's pathology department does both court-ordered and private paternity tests (payment up front) and its prenatal diagnosis unit offers NIPT, so this is effectively a public monopoly; paternity declarations themselves go through sýslumaður and Þjóðskrá.

- ✓ **Landspítali - Meinafræðideild (faðernis- og skyldleikarannsóknir)** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt (prófað í Reykjavík)) — Hospital pathology department that performs DNA tests to confirm or exclude paternity, on court or commissioner request or as private cases paid up front by individuals. · [island.is/en/o/landspitali/pathology-department/paternity-and-kinship-testing](https://island.is/en/o/landspitali/pathology-department/paternity-and-kinship-testing) · *Public and apparently the only provider in Iceland (no private lab or home-kit seller found); private cases need one responsible person and consent forms; mother's sample improves accuracy; not a realistic ad buyer.*
- ✓ **Landspítali - Fósturgreiningardeild (NIPT fósturerfðaskimun)** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt (Reykjavík)) — Hospital prenatal diagnosis unit offering NIPT, a blood test screening for chromosomal conditions such as Down's syndrome; genetic counselling in pregnancy also offered. · [island.is/en/o/landspitali/prenatal-diagnosis/non-invasive-prenatal-testing-nipt](https://island.is/en/o/landspitali/prenatal-diagnosis/non-invasive-prenatal-testing-nipt) · *Public; covers the genetic-test side of this category rather than paternity; no private clinic offering NIPT turned up in searches; price list is published on the unit's pages.*

### G7-09 · Barnatannlæknar — Paediatric dentists

**Problem:** First teeth and dental habits  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 14

*Market:* Large market: every general dentist can be a child's registered home dentist and Sjúkratryggingar pays 100% of children's treatment (annual visit fee, 3,500 kr), so any discount would be on extras; first pass found Brosið and Tannval, second pass adds 12 clinics with child-dentistry pages or family service (Kópavogur, Reykjavík, Garðabær, Vestmannaeyjar, and Akureyri/Siglufjörður in the North); no stand-alone paediatric-dentist practice found; many more clinics exist (Hafnarfjörður, Suðurnes, Suðurland) but the dental association's directory tannsi.is is blocked; Tannlæknavaktin is the emergency service.

- ✓ **Brosið tannlæknastofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Dental clinic in Hlíðasmári with a dedicated children's page: advises a first visit no later than age 3 to build trust, plus preventive dentistry. · [brosid.is/born](https://www.brosid.is/born/) · *General and preventive dentistry; no paediatric specialist stated.*
- ✓ **Tannlæknastofan Tannval** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Family dental clinic at Grensásvegur treating patients of all ages, often whole families, with regular check-ups and prevention. · [tannval.is](https://tannval.is/) · *No separate children's page seen; price list available on site.*
- ✓ **Tannir tannlæknastofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Family dental clinic in Hlíðasmári with a children's page (first visit around age 2-3, fissure sealants) plus guidance on teeth in pregnancy and infancy. · [tannir.is](https://www.tannir.is) · *Separate pages for infants (Ungbörn) and dental care in pregnancy; also orthodontics; weekday hours; links to Tannlæknavaktin for emergencies.*
- ✓ **Hlýja Tannlæknastofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Glæsibær) + Vestmannaeyjar) — One of the country's largest dental clinics (Glæsibær and Vestmannaeyjar) with a dedicated children's dentistry service, child-friendly reception and first visit around age two. · [hlyja.is](https://hlyja.is) · *Specialists in all fields under one roof (incl. orthodontics, periodontics, prosthetics); page says all dentists treat children; stickers and prize cabinet for kids.*
- ✓ **Clinica tannlæknastofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Kópavogur clinic for all ages with a children's dentistry page: first visit around age three, fluoride varnish, fissure sealants and a reward for every child. · [clinica.is](https://clinica.is) · *Bæjarlind; also orthodontics and cosmetic dentistry; cooperates with specialist dentists.*
- ✓ **Lindarbros** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Bæjarlind dental clinic with a children's dentistry page: relaxed first visits, TV in the chair, a reward after each visit; first visit at age 2-3. · [lindarbros.is](https://lindarbros.is) · *Address on its site is the same as Tannlind's (Bæjarlind 12) but it is a separate practice.*
- ✓ **Tannlæknastofan Turninn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Smáratorg clinic with a children's page: first visit around age three (about 15 minutes), brushing and pacifier advice, positive approach to build trust. · [tannlaeknastofan.is](https://tannlaeknastofan.is) · *Based in the Turninn building at Smáratorg; weekday hours; 'Börn' page under Meðferðir.*
- ✓ **Krýna** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Fellsmúli clinic offering general dentistry for all ages plus specialists, with a children's dentistry page: prevention from the first tooth, first visit at 2-3 years. · [kryna.is](https://www.kryna.is) · *Also Invisalign, implants and aesthetic dentistry; recently reopened at Fellsmúli 26.*
- ✓ **Tannlæknastofan Heilar tennur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Faxafen clinic treating children and adults, with a 'Tannlækningar fyrir börn' page (first visit around 2.5 years, brushing and toothpaste advice). · [heilartennur.is](https://heilartennur.is) · *Lift in the building; teeth whitening; price list online.*
- ✓ **Tannlind** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Bæjarlind clinic offering all general dentistry for the whole family plus oral and maxillofacial surgery, prosthetics and orthodontic services. · [tannlind.is](https://tannlind.is) · *No dedicated children's page seen; same building address as Lindarbros.*
- ✓ **Tanntorg** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær)) — Garðatorg clinic providing all general dentistry for every age group, with a warm-service focus and a minimal-intervention philosophy. · [tanntorg.is](https://www.tanntorg.is) · *Team includes an oral-medicine specialist; no dedicated children's page seen; weekday hours.*
- ✓ **BR Tannlæknar** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri dental clinic (Kaupangur) offering general dentistry, implants and children's dentistry; states children's treatment is covered by Sjúkratryggingar apart from a visit fee. · [brtannlaeknar.is](https://www.brtannlaeknar.is/) · *Site quotes an older 2,500 kr visit fee (the national fee is now 3,500 kr); also root canals and whitening; North Iceland lead.*
- ✓ **Tannlæknastofan á Glerártorgi (tannsiakureyri.is)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Glerártorg clinic in Akureyri whose service list includes a separate children's dentistry (barnatannlækningar) page, plus cosmetic dentistry, white fillings and implants. · [tannsiakureyri.is](https://www.tannsiakureyri.is/) · *Trades under a name containing the owners' first names, so listed by its domain; second floor of Glerártorg; North Iceland lead.*
- ✓ **Tannklínikin ehf.** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Siglufjörður) — Dental clinic in Akureyri (Hofsbót) and Siglufjörður offering all general dentistry for the whole family, with clear-aligner orthodontics and specialist referrals. · [tannklinikin.is](https://www.tannklinikin.is/) · *No children-specific page seen; Siglufjörður branch open Wed-Thu only; Akureyri weekdays 8-16 (Fri to 14); North Iceland lead.*

### G7-10 · Augnlæknar og sjóntækjafræðingar — Eye exams & children's glasses

**Problem:** Squint, vision checks  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6

*Market:* Four optician chains/shops with online sight-test booking plus two private eye clinics, all in the capital area (and Reykjanesbær for Optical Studio). None of the pages shows a dedicated children's range or paediatric squint service, so that must be asked; no Akureyri optician or eye clinic could be verified.

- ✓ **Optical Studio** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Optician chain with sight tests (online booking) and glasses in Smáralind, Kringlan, Hafnartorg and Reykjanesbær. · [opticalstudio.is](https://opticalstudio.is/) · *Children's glasses and discounts not mentioned on the page; no Akureyri shop.*
- ✓ **Eyesland gleraugnaverslun** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Optician with three shops (Glæsibær, Grandi, Spöngin), online booking of sight tests and designer frames and lenses. · [eyesland.is](https://eyesland.is/) · *Children's range not mentioned; no Akureyri shop.*
- ✓ **Prooptik** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kringlan)) — Optician in Kringlan with online booking of sight tests, glasses and lenses. · [prooptik.is](https://prooptik.is/) · *Single shop; children's glasses not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Augastaður** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Mjódd, Hafnarfjörður)) — Optician with shops in Mjódd and Hafnarfjörður: glasses, lenses, sports and safety glasses, sight tests with online booking. · [augastadur.is](https://www.augastadur.is/) · *Children's glasses and Akureyri not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Sjónlag augnlæknastöð** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Glæsibær)) — Private eye clinic offering all ophthalmology sub-specialties in one place; glasses measurement only as part of laser-surgery screening. · [sjonlag.is](https://www.sjonlag.is/) · *Page does not say whether children or squint are treated; ask directly.*
- ✓ **Augljós augnlæknastöð** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Glæsibær)) — Eye clinic known for laser surgery that also offers general ophthalmology and sight tests. · [augljos.is](https://augljos.is/) · *Children and squint not mentioned on the page.*

### G7-11 · Heyrnarmælingar og heyrnarþjónusta — Hearing tests & hearing care

**Problem:** Delayed speech, repeated ear infections  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Four private hearing-aid/hearing-test providers in the capital area; none states a children's service on its page. Children's hearing (newborn screening, speech and language) is handled by the public Heyrnar- og talmeinastöð, which is being folded into the health-centre system. No Akureyri provider verified.

- ✓ **Heyrnartækni** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + heimsóknir á landsbyggðina) — Hearing clinic in Síðumúli: hearing tests and ear examinations, hearing-aid fitting, one-week trial and an online hearing test. · [heyrnartaekni.is](https://www.heyrnartaekni.is/) · *Oticon aids; audiologist also visits rural health centres; children not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Heyrn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Selfoss/Höfn heimsóknir) — Hearing tests and consultations, hearing aids on loan, hearing protection, ear moulds and an accessories webshop; clinic in Kópavogur. · [heyrn.is](https://heyrn.is/) · *Children not mentioned on the page.*
- ✓ **Heyrnarstöðin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kringlan)) — Hearing station in Kringlan offering free hearing tests, hearing aids (Beltone, Rexton), loan aids, accessories and batteries. · [heyrnarstodin.is](https://www.heyrnarstodin.is/) · **Discount:** Free hearing tests · *Children not mentioned on the page.*
- ✓ **Heyrðu heyrnarþjónusta (Heilsuklasinn)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Bíldshöfði)) — Hearing diagnosis and counselling inside Heilsuklasinn: hearing tests, Phonak hearing aids with free three-week trial, tinnitus and hyperacusis treatment. · [klasinn.is/onnur-fagthjonusta/heyrdu-heyrnarthjonusta](https://klasinn.is/onnur-fagthjonusta/heyrdu-heyrnarthjonusta/) · **Discount:** Free three-week hearing-aid trial · *Own site heyrdu.is blocks automated access; children not stated.*
- ✓ **Heyrnar- og talmeinastöð Íslands** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — State institute for hearing and speech: newborn hearing screening, children's hearing impairment, language development and clefts; its services are being moved to the public health-centre system (HH). · [hti.is/index.php/is](https://hti.is/index.php/is/) · *Public body and the main provider for children's hearing, so not a slot buyer; listed for market context.*

### G7-12 · Talmeinafræðingar og málþroski — Speech therapists

**Problem:** Late talking  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1

*Market:* Only one business-level provider verified (Trappa, a Reykjavík company placing speech therapists, OT and psychologists with families and schools). Demand far exceeds supply: official reports cite waits of up to four years and an average 17 months at private practices, and the state is piloting speech therapy in schools. The professional association (talmein.is) publishes no clinic directory, so many individual practices are not captured.

- ✓ **Trappa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes + Suðurland + fjarþjónusta) — Company connecting children and parents with specialists in speech and language therapy, language-development delays, stuttering and pronunciation, plus behaviour counselling and OT; in-person and online. · [trappa.is](https://trappa.is/) · *Founded 2014, at Skipholt in Reykjavík; bookings via the Kara Connect marketplace; also relevant to G7-13 and G7-14.*

### G7-13 · Iðjuþjálfun og sjúkraþjálfun barna — Paediatric occupational & physical therapy

**Problem:** Motor-development concerns  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Child therapy is dominated by the non-profit Gló Æfingastöð (physio and OT for infants upwards) plus a few private multi-therapist businesses (Trappa, Lífsbrunnur, Heilsuklasinn). The physiotherapists' association directory loads dynamically, so many small physio clinics are not captured.

- ✓ **Gló Æfingastöð** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Háaleitisbraut) + landið allt) — Non-profit early-intervention and rehabilitation centre for children and young people, mainly physiotherapists and occupational therapists; parents of newborns can apply directly with developmental concerns. · [glofelag.is](https://glofelag.is/) · *Formerly Æfingastöðin (aefingastodin.is now redirects). Run by the Gló association; referral needed for children over 2. Main specialist provider, but non-profit.*
- ✓ **Trappa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes + Suðurland + fjarþjónusta) — Company placing specialists with families and schools, including occupational therapy, behaviour counselling and speech therapy for children; in-person and online. · [trappa.is](https://trappa.is/) · *Same company as G7-12 and G7-14.*
- ✓ **Lífsbrunnur ehf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (fjarþjónusta)) — Occupational therapy for all ages delivered by secure video: daily-life skills, school and learning support for children, sensory-processing assessment and parent coaching. · [lifsbrunnur.is/service/idjuthjalfun](https://lifsbrunnur.is/service/idjuthjalfun/) · *Remote-only; no physical clinic address on the page.*
- ✓ **Heilsuklasinn - iðjuþjálfun og sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Bíldshöfði)) — Health cluster offering occupational therapy for children and adults and physiotherapy (Stígandi) including pregnancy swimming and post-birth mothers' training. · [klasinn.is/onnur-fagthjonusta](https://klasinn.is/onnur-fagthjonusta/) · *Physio page does not mention treating children; OT page does. Same group as G7-04.*

### G7-14 · Barnasálfræðingar og atferlisráðgjöf — Child psychologists & behaviour advice

**Problem:** Behaviour, anxiety, sleep problems  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* A few business-level providers verified: a child-and-teen psychology practice (Sálstofan), a baby/child sleep consultancy (Svefnráðgjöf.is), Trappa, and a general psychology practice with child expertise. Many individual psychologists practise under their own names and were not listed; the large anxiety clinic (Kvíðameðferðarstöðin) is adult-focused.

- ✓ **Svefnráðgjöf.is** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður) + símaráðgjöf um allt land) — Licensed sleep consultancy for babies and children aged 0-5: tailored sleep plans, in-person at Lífsgæðasetrið in Hafnarfjörður or by phone, plus lectures and a blog. · [svefnradgjof.is](https://www.svefnradgjof.is/) · *Directly matches the 'sleep problems' need; prices not shown on page.*
- ✓ **Sálstofan** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Psychology practice for children and young people: assessment, therapy and counselling for behaviour problems, anxiety, sleep problems, ADHD, autism and trauma, working with the family. · [salstofan.is](https://salstofan.is/) · *Page mentions children and young people generally, not toddlers specifically; at Hlíðasmári, Kópavogur.*
- ✓ **Trappa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes + Suðurland + fjarþjónusta) — Company matching children and parents with specialists in behaviour counselling, psychological counselling, speech therapy and OT, in person or online. · [trappa.is](https://trappa.is/) · *Same company as G7-12 and G7-13.*
- ✓ **Sálfræðingarnir** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + fjarþjónusta) — Multidisciplinary psychology practice in Reykjavík (since 2016) with expertise in psychopathology of children, youth and adults; video sessions via Kara Connect. · [salfraedingarnir.is](https://salfraedingarnir.is/) · *Parent counselling and behaviour work not listed on the page; confirm directly.*

**Problem:** Colic, reflux, torticollis, feeding trouble
