*Market:* Two chiropractic clinics and one osteopathy practice verified as treating infants, plus an infant-massage studio and Domus barnalæknar's medical colic service as neighbouring options. No Akureyri provider found; the osteopaths' association runs a 'find an osteopath' directory (osteopatia.is) that lists more individual practitioners.

- ✓ **Líf Kírópraktík** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur) + Suðurland (Selfoss)) — Chiropractic clinic marketed to the whole family: gentle care for babies with stiffness, fussiness and breastfeeding difficulty, plus children incl. sleep problems. · [lifkiro.is](https://www.lifkiro.is/) · *Single return visit listed at 7,500 kr; clinics in Kópavogur and Selfoss.*
- ✓ **Kírópraktorstöðin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Chiropractic clinic at Sogavegur whose practitioners treat children of all ages, with experience from a breastfeeding clinic run with midwives. · [kiro.is](https://www.kiro.is/) · *No dedicated infant page; colic, reflux and torticollis not named; no Akureyri location.*
- ✓ **Osteópati.is** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Osteopathy practice at Suðurlandsbraut stating osteopathy helps infants, children and adults. · [osteopati.is](https://osteopati.is/) · *Infant-specific conditions (colic, reflux) not described on the page.*
- ✓ **Domus barnalæknar - ungbarnakveisa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Specialised paediatric service for colic: fussy babies under about 3-4 months who cry a lot get assessed by paediatricians. · [barnalaeknardomus.is/serhaefd-thjonusta](https://barnalaeknardomus.is/serhaefd-thjonusta/) · *Medical, not manual therapy; same company as G7-01.*
- ✓ **Nálar & nudd** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + fjarkennsla) — Infant-massage classes (private, two babies or by video) with materials and oil; the page says it eases colic and gas and aids bonding and sleep. · [nalarognudd.is](https://www.nalarognudd.is/) · *Gift vouchers sold online; also pregnancy massage. Neighbouring category to manual therapy.*

### G7-16 · Næringarfræðingar og næringarráðgjöf barna — Paediatric dietitians & nutrition advice

**Problem:** Picky eating, allergies, growth  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 2

*Market:* No dedicated paediatric dietitian business could be verified. Options found are medical (Domus barnalæknar paediatric gastroenterology and nutrition, allergy, growth/endocrine) and general dietitian services inside Heilsuklasinn. Most dietitians in Iceland work as individuals or in health centres; the dietitians' association directory was not reachable.

- ✓ **Domus barnalæknar - meltingar- og næringarlækningar barna** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Paediatric specialists in gastroenterology, hepatology and nutrition, plus allergy (food allergy and intolerance) and endocrinology for growth concerns, ages 0-17. · [barnalaeknardomus.is/sergreinar-laekninga](https://barnalaeknardomus.is/sergreinar-laekninga/) · *Same company as G7-01; medical rather than dietitian-led.*
- ✓ **Heilsuklasinn - næringarfræðingur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Bíldshöfði)) — Dietitian consultation, support and education on a healthy relationship with food, within the Heilsuklasinn health cluster. · [klasinn.is/onnur-fagthjonusta](https://klasinn.is/onnur-fagthjonusta/) · *Page does not say whether children are seen; same group as G7-04.*

### G7-17 · Skyndihjálp og slysavarnir barna — Infant first-aid, CPR & child-safety courses

**Problem:** Choking, falls, burns  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Tiny market: Sjóvá (free course with Miðstöð slysavarna barna), the Red Cross and Skyndihjálparskólinn (mostly groups and workplaces), plus the newly found private Bráðaskólinn (4-hour infant course, also in English, held at Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur) and one unverified Reykjavík provider; libraries and health centres run only occasional free talks, and no Akureyri provider was found.

- ✓ **Sjóvá - öryggi ungra barna á heimilinu** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hátún, Reykjavík); netútgáfa fyrirhuguð) — Insurer-sponsored free 90-minute course for parents of children aged 0-3: tour of a fully equipped apartment, home safety and car-seat choice, with a Sjóvá gift pack of safety products. · [sjova.is/en/sjova/information/media-hub/news/2025/safety-of-young-children-at-home-free-safety-course-for-parents-of-children-aged-0-3](https://www.sjova.is/en/sjova/information/media-hub/news/2025/safety-of-young-children-at-home-free-safety-course-for-parents-of-children-aged-0-3) · **Discount:** Free course plus gift package; Stofn members also get 20% on car seats and 15% on Clippasafe at Fífa · *Run with Miðstöð slysavarna barna; Icelandic and English sessions; online version for outside the capital planned. Already a sponsor in this niche, so a competitor/benchmark rather than a likely slot buyer.*
- ✓ **Skyndihjálparskólinn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Private provider of first-aid courses in Iceland; page content is script-rendered, so course list and any infant or parent course could not be read. · [skyndihjalparskolinn.is](https://skyndihjalparskolinn.is/) · *Only the page title was readable ('Skyndihjálparnámskeið á Íslandi'); verify that infant/child courses exist.*
- ✓ **Rauði krossinn - Slys og veikindi barna** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt (deildir), m.a. Akureyri) — Four-hour in-person course for parents and carers covering child accidents and illness: CPR, bleeding, burns, poisoning, fractures, seizures and allergic reactions. · [raudikrossinn.is/verkefni/skyndihjalp](https://www.raudikrossinn.is/verkefni/skyndihjalp/) · *About 13,200 kr per person; the Akureyri course listed was general first aid, not the child course. Non-profit, not a slot buyer.*
- ✓ **Bráðaskólinn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Private first-aid school: 4-hour infant first-aid course for parents and expectant parents (CPR, choking, allergies, fever, burns), also offered in English. · [bradaskolinn.is/products/skyndihjalp-og-brad-veikindi-barna](https://www.bradaskolinn.is/products/skyndihjalp-og-brad-veikindi-barna) · **Discount:** Gift vouchers sold (from 5,000 kr); the birth-centre page suggests checking union (stéttarfélag) subsidies · *Course held in the hall of Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur; five dates this season, all shown as sold out or unavailable at check; also professional courses for health staff and a first-aid products webshop; priced 13,600 kr.*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Private midwife-run birth centre (pregnancy care, births, breastfeeding counselling, physiotherapy, courses) that hosts Bráðaskólinn's infant first-aid course in its own hall. · [faedingarheimilid.is/skyndihjalp](https://www.faedingarheimilid.is/skyndihjalp) · *Listed as host and likely partner for this slot rather than as the course provider; overlaps with the pregnancy and birth categories; its page shows an older price (12,900 kr) than Bráðaskólinn's current 13,600 kr.*
- ○ **Fyrstahjálp GiR** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík, Brautarholt)) — Small Reykjavík provider listed in business directories for 'Börn og skyndihjálp', first-aid courses aimed at parents and carers of children. · [findglocal.com/IS/Reykjav%C3%ADk/542808152427031/Fyrstahj%C3%A1lp-GiR---B%C3%B6rn-og-skyndihj%C3%A1lp,-fyrir-foreldra-og-a%C3%B0standendur-barna](https://www.findglocal.com/IS/Reykjav%C3%ADk/542808152427031/Fyrstahj%C3%A1lp-GiR---B%C3%B6rn-og-skyndihj%C3%A1lp,-fyrir-foreldra-og-a%C3%B0standendur-barna) · *Only third-party directory listings seen (own site not found; directory page itself blocked by Cloudflare); verify that it is still active before treating it as a competitor.*

## G8 · Heilsa og vellíðan foreldra — Parents' health & wellbeing

### G8-01 · Grindarbotnsþjálfun og endurhæfing eftir fæðingu — Pelvic-floor & postnatal rehab physiotherapy

**Problem:** Leaks, pain, recovery after birth  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Solid cluster of physiotherapy clinics with women's-health or pelvic-floor expertise in the capital area, one in Akureyri, plus a pelvic-floor device webshop and a private birth centre. No clear leader. Landspítali also runs a public pelvic-floor outpatient clinic (not listed).

- ✓ **Gáski sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physiotherapists specialising in pregnancy, pelvic girdle pain, urinary leakage and postnatal problems; also pregnancy aquanatal classes. First six sessions need no GP referral. · [gaski.is/en/thjonusta/medgongutengd-vandamal-grindarbotn](https://gaski.is/en/thjonusta/medgongutengd-vandamal-grindarbotn/) · *Clinics at Mjódd and Bolholt (booking form also offers Mosfellsbær); runs medgongusund.is*
- ✓ **Styrkur sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physiotherapy and training during pregnancy and after birth, treatment of pelvic girdle pain and pelvic floor problems, plus group training and a gym room. · [styrkurehf.is/kvenheilsa](https://styrkurehf.is/kvenheilsa/) · *Reykjavík; a third-party article also names a 'Mömmuform' course here (not confirmed on own site)*
- ✓ **Stígandi sjúkraþjálfun (Heilsuklasinn)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physiotherapy clinic offering pelvic-floor physiotherapy, pregnancy and postpartum care, pelvic girdle and lower-back treatment, and pregnancy swimming courses. · [stigandi.is](https://www.stigandi.is) · *Reykjavík (Heilsuklasinn health cluster)*
- ○ ⚠ **TÁP sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Kópavogur physiotherapy clinic with women's-health focus; has a dedicated page on urinary leakage after birth and weak pelvic floor. · [tap.is/thvagleki-eftir-faedingu-veikur-grindarbotn](https://tap.is/thvagleki-eftir-faedingu-veikur-grindarbotn/) · *Kópavogur per search snippet. Website returned a server error on 17 Nov 2026, so existence confirmed only via search hit and a partner clinic's page - recheck before use*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur - sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private birth centre in Reykjavik hosting a women's-health and obstetric physiotherapist who sees women during pregnancy and after birth, with a research focus on pelvic floor health. · [faedingarheimilid.is/sjukrathjalfun](https://www.faedingarheimilid.is/sjukrathjalfun) · *Reykjavík; physio service is run by TÁP-affiliated specialist; birth centre itself also relevant to G21-01*
- ✓ **Partera** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Pelvic-floor training with HIFEM electromagnetic technology, aimed at bladder control, less leakage and regaining strength after pregnancy and birth; bookable sessions and cards. · [partera.is/pelvic-health/for-women](https://www.partera.is/pelvic-health/for-women) · *Hafnarfjörður; also men's programme*
- ✓ **Efling sjúkraþjálfun (Efling ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri physiotherapy clinic whose staff are trained in women's health: pelvic floor and pelvic girdle problems before and after pregnancy, plus a postnatal group class. · [eflingehf.is/thjonusta](https://eflingehf.is/thjonusta/) · *Akureyri; unrelated to the trade union Efling; postnatal class 'Þjálfun eftir meðgöngu'*
- ✓ **Sjúkraþjálfun Íslands** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Large physiotherapy clinic with a dedicated women's-health team (Kvenheilsuteymi) alongside individual treatment and group training. · [sjukratjalfun.is](https://www.sjukratjalfun.is/) · *Sites: Kringlan (3rd floor) and Orkuhúsið, Kópavogur; a third-party article also mentions a mother's training course (not confirmed on own site)*
- ✓ **Píkusport (Grindarbotnsþjálfinn)** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Icelandic-language webshop for an app-connected pelvic-floor trainer for postnatal recovery, leakage and prolapse prevention; 30-day money-back guarantee. · [pikusport.is](https://pikusport.is/) · *Company address and delivery terms not shown on the page*

### G8-02 · Kvensjúkdómalæknar og kvenheilsa — Gynaecologists & women's-health clinics (postnatal checks, contraception)

**Problem:** Checks and contraception after birth  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4 (+2 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Private gynaecology sits in a few Reykjavik/Kópavogur practices: Kvensjúkdómalæknir.is and Domus Læknar (both Hlíðasmári, Kópavogur), Lækning Lágmúla and Kvenheilsuklíníkin, plus public Heilsubrú; Læknastöðin (Álfheimar) lists no gynaecologists. No private gynaecologist confirmed in Akureyri: Sjúkrahúsið á Akureyri's maternity ward is the main women's-health provider in North Iceland and visiting gynaecologists serve HSN units in Húsavík and Sauðárkrókur.

- ✓ **Kvensjúkdómalæknir.is** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Private gynaecology clinic near Smáralind: contraception counselling, IUD insertion and removal, smears, incontinence and prolapse assessment, menopause advice. Online booking only. · [xn--kvensjkdmalknir-7lb1tpe.is](https://www.xn--kvensjkdmalknir-7lb1tpe.is/) · *Near Smáralind, Kópavogur; works under a Sjúkratryggingar agreement; postnatal checks not explicitly listed; IUDs sold at about pharmacy price*
- ✓ **Lækning Lágmúla** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Specialist-doctor practice (since 1996) with several gynaecologists among a dozen specialities; appointments by phone or email. · [laekning.is](https://laekning.is/) · *Reykjavík; page does not list contraception or postnatal checks specifically*
- ✓ **Kvenheilsuklíníkin (Klíníkin Ármúla)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Women's-health clinic with gynaecologists offering gynaecological operations including endometriosis surgery, alongside the Brjóstaklíníkin breast service. · [klinikin.is](https://klinikin.is) · *Reykjavík; contraception and postnatal checks not mentioned on page*
- ✓ **Heilsubrú (Heilsugæsla höfuðborgarsvæðisins)** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Public women's-health service of the capital-area health centres: women's-health team with gynaecologists and midwives, contraception counselling clinic opened July 2024, advice for expectant mothers. · [island.is/en/o/hh/heilsubru](https://island.is/en/o/hh/heilsubru) · *Public body, content partner rather than advertiser; island.is returns 403 to WebFetch so only seen in search results*
- ✓ **Domus Læknar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Large private gynaecology and surgery practice at Hlíðasmári with about ten gynaecologists; all doctors hold Sjúkratryggingar contracts and a lab is on the same floor. · [domuslaeknar.is](https://domuslaeknar.is) · *Hlíðasmári 17, 2nd floor, near Smáralind; separate from Kvensjúkdómalæknir.is (Hlíðasmári 9); site has contraception information but does not itemise postnatal checks; booking via contact page or switchboard.*
- ✓ **Sjúkrahúsið á Akureyri - Fæðingadeild** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Public maternity ward in Akureyri covering antenatal care, birth and examinations of women's reproductive organs; the main women's-health provider in North Iceland. · [island.is/s/sak/faeding-og-kvensjukdomar](https://island.is/s/sak/faeding-og-kvensjukdomar) · *Public hospital, a content partner rather than an advertiser; no private gynaecology practice in Akureyri confirmed; visiting gynaecologists serve HSN units in Húsavík and Sauðárkrókur.*

### G8-03 · Mömmuleikfimi og líkamsrækt með barn — Mum-and-baby fitness (postnatal classes, stroller workouts)

**Problem:** Getting fit again with a baby in tow  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

*Market:* World Class is the broadest option (mum classes in Reykjavik, Hafnarfjörður, Akranes and Akureyri); several physio-led clinics run dedicated postnatal groups and CrossFit Reykjavík has a babies-welcome class. Smaller independent studios probably exist but could not be searched.

- ✓ **World Class** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Vesturland) — WorldFit Mömmur strength classes and Pilates Mömmur for expectant and new mothers, babies welcome, booked in the app; 9,800 kr per month add-on to a World Class card. · [worldclass.is/klubbar/worldfit/mommur](https://www.worldclass.is/klubbar/worldfit/mommur) · **Discount:** 5% family discount on cards for people at the same address; membership freeze considered around pregnancy and birth · *WorldFit Mömmur at Laugar, Tjarnarvellir, Akranes and Skólastígur Akureyri; Pilates Mömmur at Laugar Mon/Wed/Fri 9:30 with a child space in the hall*
- ✓ **CrossFit Reykjavík** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Mömmu CrossFit: scalable workout classes for expectant and new parents, infants welcome, Mon/Wed/Fri 9:30, led by a coach certified in exercise during and after pregnancy. · [crossfitreykjavik.is/hvad-er-i-bodi/mommu-crossfit](https://crossfitreykjavik.is/hvad-er-i-bodi/mommu-crossfit/) · *Reykjavík; needs a CFR subscription; box also has staffed childcare hours (see G8-04)*
- ✓ **Vivus** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physio-founded training centre with Mömmuþrek group training and online strength training for mothers, in person and remote. · [vivus.is](https://vivus.is) · *Reykjavík; page gives no schedule or price for Mömmuþrek*
- ✓ **Stígandi sjúkraþjálfun (Heilsuklasinn)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physiotherapy clinic running Mömmuþjálfun, a postnatal training group for mothers after pregnancy, alongside pregnancy swimming and water training. · [stigandi.is/namskeid](https://www.stigandi.is/namskeid) · *Reykjavík; prices on its Gjaldskrá page*
- ✓ **Efling sjúkraþjálfun (Efling ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri physiotherapy clinic with a group class 'Þjálfun eftir meðgöngu' (training after pregnancy) plus women's-health physiotherapy. · [eflingehf.is/thjonusta](https://eflingehf.is/thjonusta/) · *Akureyri; not the trade union Efling*
- ✓ **Styrkur sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physiotherapy and training during pregnancy and after birth, plus group training; a third-party article names a 'Mömmuform' course here. · [styrkurehf.is/kvenheilsa](https://styrkurehf.is/kvenheilsa/) · *Reykjavík; Mömmuform not confirmed on own site*
- ○ **Sjúkraþjálfun Íslands** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physiotherapy clinic with group training and a women's-health team; a third-party article lists a mothers' training course here. · [sjukratjalfun.is](https://www.sjukratjalfun.is/) · *Kringlan and Orkuhúsið Kópavogur; mothers' course not seen on own site*

### G8-04 · Líkamsræktarstöðvar með barnagæslu — Gyms with childcare

**Problem:** Exercising without a sitter  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* Few gyms advertise staffed childcare: World Class (4 stations), CrossFit Reykjavík and Sporthúsið's Reykjanesbær branch (Krílabær). CrossFit Katla's ForeldraFit lets parents bring babies into class instead of childcare. Hreyfing, Hress, Katla Fitness stations, GYM heilsa and Bjarg (Akureyri) show none on their sites; smaller regional gyms still unchecked.

- ✓ **World Class** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurland + Vesturland) — Largest gym chain; staffed childcare (barnagæsla) at Laugar and Egilshöll (max 1.5 hours per child, single visit or 15/30-visit cards); unstaffed kids' corners elsewhere; mum classes with babies welcome. · [worldclass.is/stodvar-og-sundlaugar/laugar](https://www.worldclass.is/stodvar-og-sundlaugar/laugar) · **Discount:** 5% family discount on cards for people at the same address; membership freeze considered around pregnancy and birth · *Laugar: Mon-Fri 08:30-14:00 and 16:00-19:30, Sat 09:00-14:00; Egilshöll: Mon-Thu 16:30-19:30; site shows 4 childcare stations in total (other two not identified); no childcare seen at Skólastígur and Strandgata (Akureyri), Smáralind or Kringlan; FAQ says cardholders get free access - check terms*
- ✓ **CrossFit Reykjavík** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — CrossFit box with childcare hours on weekday afternoons and weekends, plus a babies-welcome Mömmu CrossFit class. · [crossfitreykjavik.is](https://crossfitreykjavik.is/) · *Reykjavík; childcare Mon-Thu 16:00-19:00, Fri 15:30-18:00, Sat 09:00-12:00, Sun 10:00-12:00*
- ✓ **Sporthúsið (Krílabær, Reykjanesbær)** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes) — Gym and CrossFit club whose Reykjanesbær branch runs the Krílabær childcare for its youngest customers with set opening hours. · [sporthusid.is/um-sporthusid](https://www.sporthusid.is/um-sporthusid) · *Krílabær hours Mon-Fri 08:15-13:15 and 16:15-18:45, weekends mornings; Kópavogur branch shows no childcare on site*
- ✓ **CrossFit Katla (Katla Fitness)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — CrossFit box in the Katla Fitness chain whose ForeldraFit lets parents on leave train with their babies in the hall instead of finding a sitter. · [crossfitkatla.is](https://crossfitkatla.is) · **Discount:** Katla Fitness family subscription for four at 19,960 kr/month; Foreldra CrossFit membership 18,200 kr/month · *No staffed childcare - babies join the class. ForeldraFit taught Mon/Wed/Fri 10:10 at Lambhagi plus open gym Tue/Thu (changes from 1 Sept 2026). Katla Fitness stations: Holtagarðar, Lambhagi, Faxafen, Urðarhvarf, Tjarnarvellir, Salalaug, Katrínartún (formerly Reebok Fitness).*

### G8-05 · Jóga, núvitund og slökun fyrir foreldra — Yoga, mindfulness & relaxation for parents

**Problem:** Stress and overload  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 12

*Market:* Parent-relevant yoga: Jógasetrið (mum-and-baby), Yogaljós, Yogahúsið in Hafnarfjörður (pregnancy and mums' yoga) and, in Akureyri, Ómur Yoga & Gongsetur (pregnancy yoga, gong relaxation); relaxation-led options are Yoga Shala, YogaLíf, Hot Yoga Iceland, Kramhúsið and the Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó hub. No parent-specific mindfulness provider found; Akureyri has few studios and none other than Ómur could be verified.

- ✓ **Jógasetrið** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Yoga studio with pregnancy yoga, Mömmujóga (mums and babies from 6-8 weeks, 8-week course), Jóga Nidra relaxation, plus online packs and a couples course. · [jogasetrid.is](https://www.jogasetrid.is) · *Reykjavík; next Mömmujóga course starts 11 Jan 2027 Mon/Wed 10:15; booked via Abler; prices on its Verðskrá page*
- ✓ **Yoga Shala Reykjavík** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Yoga studio offering Yoga Nidra deep relaxation, Yin/restorative, healing and gentle fascia yoga classes suited to tired, stressed parents; no parent-specific class listed. · [yogashala.is](https://www.yogashala.is) · *Reykjavík; heated-room classes too*
- ✓ **Endurheimt heilsumiðstöð** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Health centre running a women's relaxation class (Kvenheilsa - Slökun) with gentle movement and yoga nidra for stress and exhaustion, plus a women's exercise class. · [endurheimt.is/product/kvenheilsa-slokun](https://endurheimt.is/product/kvenheilsa-slokun/) · *Reykjavík; Tuesdays 12:00-13:00; payable with a physiotherapy referral*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private birth centre offering pregnancy and mother yoga, birth preparation and breastfeeding courses, acupuncture, massage and family counselling. · [faedingarheimilid.is/home](https://www.faedingarheimilid.is/home) · *Reykjavík; also free Tuesday lunchtime meet-up for parents of young children (see G8-15)*
- ✓ **DAO Nuddstofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Massage studio that also lists mindfulness (núvitund) courses; page gives no detail on content or audience, so not parent-specific. · [dao.is](http://dao.is/) · *Reykjavík; see also G8-10 for massage*
- ✓ **Yogaljós** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Pregnancy yoga classes plus listed mother-and-baby yoga, relaxation and meditation, and a 4-week 'Birth without fear' yoga and birth-preparation course starting 4 January 2027. · [yogaljos.is](https://yogaljos.is) · *Classes held at Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur; schedule and prices not on page; postnatal classes not described*
- ✓ **YogaLíf** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Yoga and Pilates studio near Smáralind with restorative yoga, yin and yoga nidra deep relaxation and gentle evening classes; drop-ins and courses. · [yogalif.is](https://yogalif.is) · *Hlíðasmári 17, same building as Domus Læknar; open Mon-Fri 6-20 and weekends; no pregnancy or mum-and-baby yoga listed.*
- ✓ **Hot Yoga Iceland** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Long-running hot yoga studio with a public timetable, yoga teacher training and retreats; a heated-room stress outlet, no parent-specific classes seen. · [hotyoga.is](https://www.hotyoga.is) · *Contact page gives the venue only as World Class (station not stated); timetable and prices could not be read.*
- ✓ **Ómur Yoga & Gongsetur** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri yoga and gong studio offering pregnancy yoga, pregnancy exercises in water, yoga nidra, restorative yoga and gong relaxation; classes booked online. · [hafyoga.is](https://www.hafyoga.is) · *Lyngholt 20, Akureyri; booking via Abler shop; also HAF Yoga teacher training (listed event in Hveragerði); some site content dates from 2024 - confirm current timetable.*
- ✓ **Yogahúsið** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Hafnarfjörður's oldest yoga studio, five teachers: yoga nidra, yin, kundalini, soft flow, plus pregnancy yoga and mums' yoga as listed by its host centre. · [yogahusid.is](https://yogahusid.is) · *Based in Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó, Suðurgata 41; pregnancy and mums' yoga appear on the stjo.is tenant page, own site shows no class list; lunchtime yoga nidra events running Nov 2026.*
- ✓ **Kramhúsið** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Central Reykjavík dance and movement house whose timetable includes yoga, Pilates and low-impact fitness; general adult classes, no parent-baby sessions listed. · [kramhusid.is](https://www.kramhusid.is) · *Skólavörðustígur 12; mostly a dance timetable, so only a loose fit for parent relaxation.*
- ✓ **Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Hafnarfjörður wellbeing centre housing a yoga studio, yoga nidra and relaxation sessions, counselling and parent services such as birth education under one roof. · [stjo.is](https://stjo.is) · *Suðurgata 41; hub of independent practitioners (Yogahúsið, Míró yoga nidra, Ljósa, Móðurafl, Hlöðuloftið, Hugarsetur, Tengslamat); weekly events seen for 17-19 Nov 2026.*

### G8-06 · Sálfræðiþjónusta fyrir foreldra — Psychologists for parents (postnatal depression, anxiety)

**Problem:** Baby blues, postnatal depression, parental anxiety  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 10 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* General psychology practices can serve new parents but none was found advertising postnatal-depression treatment on its own site. Capital area: Hlöðuloftið (children and parents), Sálfræðingarnir, Hugarsetur plus Samskiptastöðin, KMS and Sálfræðistofan; online: Mín líðan and the Kara Connect platform. Akureyri/North: METIS Sálfræðistofa and the public HSN service, which states special emphasis on pregnant women and parents of infants. The sal.is directory lists individuals and was not used.

- ✓ **Sálfræðistofan** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Psychology practice offering individual therapy for depression and anxiety, parenting counselling, and family and couples therapy; postnatal depression not named specifically. · [salfraedistofan.is](https://www.salfraedistofan.is) · *Reykjavík; prices not shown on page*
- ✓ **Samskiptastöðin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Clinic with psychologists offering psychotherapy, couples and family services, and a course for parents of young children. · [samskiptastodin.is](https://www.samskiptastodin.is) · *Address not shown on page; course page https://samskiptastodin.is/namskeid-fyrir-foreldra-ungra-barna/ has only a title*
- ✓ **Kvíðameðferðarstöðin (KMS)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Specialist anxiety clinic with individual and group therapy for worry, panic, health anxiety, OCD and trauma-related problems; relevant to parental anxiety. · [kms.is](https://www.kms.is) · *Reykjavík; no postnatal-specific programme or pricing seen*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private birth centre offering birth debriefing and family counselling alongside midwifery, breastfeeding support and courses for new parents. · [faedingarheimilid.is/home](https://www.faedingarheimilid.is/home) · *Reykjavík; counselling details not described on the page*
- ✓ **Mín líðan** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (fjarmeðferð, líka Akureyri og Norðurland)) — Icelandic online psychology service: 10-hour CBT programme by written messages, 50-minute video sessions, a stress course and family counselling; suits parents who cannot travel. · [minlidan.is](https://www.minlidan.is) · **Discount:** Many unions subsidise the online therapy (10 hours cost 39,900 ISK per the site) · *Run by Mín líðan ehf. with Embætti landlæknis permission; focus on depression, anxiety, social anxiety and low self-esteem; no postnatal-specific programme seen.*
- ✓ **Hlöðuloftið - sálfræðistofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Psychology practice for children and parents with parent-training support, EMDR trauma therapy and animal-assisted psychotherapy; Sjúkratryggingar contract makes referred visits subsidised. · [hloduloftid.com](https://www.hloduloftid.com/) · *Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó, moving to Þykkvabær from 2026/27 per site; 50-minute interview 25,000 ISK; waiting list 6-8 months; child-focused rather than postnatal.*
- ✓ **Sálfræðingarnir** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + fjarþjónusta) — Reykjavík psychology clinic offering individual and couples therapy for anxiety, depression, trauma and attachment problems, with video sessions for people outside Reykjavík. · [salfraedingarnir.is](https://www.salfraedingarnir.is) · *Engjateigur 9; remote sessions via Kara Connect; team includes child and adolescent specialists; no postnatal-specific page.*
- ✓ **METIS Sálfræðistofa** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland (fjarþjónusta)) — Akureyri psychology practice offering psychotherapy and assessments for children, adults, parents and families, plus courses and remote sessions. · [metis.is](https://www.metis.is) · *Kaupangur building, Mýrarvegur, Akureyri; a private practice serving North Iceland; no postnatal-specific page.*
- ✓ **Hugarsetur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Psychology practice in Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó offering psychological therapy including EMDR trauma treatment. · [hugarsetur.is](https://hugarsetur.is/) · *Suðurgata 41; site lists no postnatal or parenting-specific service.*
- ✓ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Norðurlands (HSN) - sálfélagsleg þjónusta** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Public psychology service in North Iceland for children, adults and families with special emphasis on pregnant women and parents of infants; in person or remote. · [island.is/s/hsn/salfelagsleg-thjonusta](https://island.is/s/hsn/salfelagsleg-thjonusta) · **Discount:** Visit fee only 1,000 ISK; free for children · *Referral from a health or welfare professional is required; public body rather than advertiser, but the main option for Akureyri and North Iceland parents.*
- ○ ⚠ **Kara Connect** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt (fjarþjónusta)) — Booking platform for one-to-one sessions with therapists and coaches, in person or online, used by Icelandic practices for remote sessions; no postnatal listing seen. · [karaconnect.com](https://karaconnect.com) · *Site has an Icelandic-language option but does not state where the company is based; employer-paid wellbeing model; Sálfræðingarnir names it for remote sessions. [auto] website did not respond in the final check.*

### G8-07 · Parameðferð og samskiptaráðgjöf — Couples therapy & relationship counselling

**Problem:** Strain on the relationship  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Couples and family therapy is offered by a few Reykjavik-area practices plus the church's low-cost family service; no couples-specialist brand or regional (Akureyri) provider confirmed.

- ✓ **Samskiptastöðin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Psychologist-led clinic listing couples and marriage therapy (para- og hjónameðferð), family services and a course for parents of young children. · [samskiptastodin.is/einstaklingar/#para-og-hjonamedferd](https://samskiptastodin.is/einstaklingar/#para-og-hjonamedferd) · *Price list on its Verðskrá page; address not shown*
- ✓ **Sálfræðistofan** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Psychology practice offering family and couples therapy for people struggling with communication and close relationships, plus parenting counselling. · [salfraedistofan.is/thjonusta/fjolskyldu-og-paramedferd](https://salfraedistofan.is/thjonusta/fjolskyldu-og-paramedferd/) · *Reykjavík*
- ✓ **Fjölskylduþjónusta kirkjunnar** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Church-run counselling with one-hour cohabitation, family, grief and pastoral interviews; open to people across Iceland. · [kirkjan.is/salgaeslu-og-fjolskylduthjonusta](https://kirkjan.is/salgaeslu-og-fjolskylduthjonusta) · *Run from Háteigskirkja parish hall, Reykjavík; page does not give prices; also reachable via local parishes*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private birth centre listing family counselling among its services for new parents. · [faedingarheimilid.is/home](https://www.faedingarheimilid.is/home) · *Reykjavík; no detail on couples work*

### G8-08 · Uppeldisráðgjöf og foreldranámskeið — Parenting coaching & courses

**Problem:** Not knowing what you are doing  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 10

*Market:* Few dedicated 0-3 parenting-course businesses: Samskiptastöðin (Gottman course), Fæðingarheimilið, Sálfræðistofan and Bráðaskólinn, plus birth/infant course providers Ljósa, Móðurafl and 9 mánuðir in Hafnarfjörður/Kópavogur, and Hlöðuloftið (parent training) and Tengslamat (attachment work). The free parenting courses (Heilsugæslan, Reykjavík city, Þroska- og hegðunarstöð) are public; unions reimburse fees (e.g. Ljósa). Akureyri: only METIS and the public HSN; no private parenting course found.

- ✓ **Samskiptastöðin - Að verða foreldri** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Gottman-based course for expectant parents and parents of children up to 3 on couple relationship, teamwork and understanding children's feelings; babies under 6 months welcome. · [samskiptastodin.is/namskeid-fyrir-foreldra-ungra-barna](https://samskiptastodin.is/namskeid-fyrir-foreldra-ungra-barna/) · **Discount:** Most unions contribute to the course fee (per page) · *Reykjavík; in person; page appears outdated (mentions autumn 2022) - confirm dates; also couples therapy*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Birth centre course programme: three-evening 'Nýtt upphaf' birth and first-days course, hypnobirthing, mother yoga, infant first aid and a baby-development movement class. · [faedingarheimilid.is/namskeid](https://www.faedingarheimilid.is/namskeid) · **Discount:** Many unions reimburse course fees; payments can be split; gift cards valid for all courses · *Reykjavík; 'Nýtt upphaf' 39,900 ISK per couple; courses also in English and Polish; yoga via Yogaljós, first aid via Bráðaskólinn, baby class with Hreyfiland*
- ✓ **Sálfræðistofan** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Psychology practice offering parenting counselling alongside child and adolescent treatment and family therapy; courses mentioned but not listed. · [salfraedistofan.is/thjonusta/medferd-barna-og-unglinga-uppeldisradgjof](https://salfraedistofan.is/thjonusta/medferd-barna-og-unglinga-uppeldisradgjof/) · *Reykjavík; geared more to children with emotional or behavioural difficulties than to babies*
- ✓ **Bráðaskólinn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Infant first aid and acute-illness course for parents (choking, CPR, fever, allergies) in Icelandic and English, 13,600 ISK; gift vouchers available. · [bradaskolinn.is](https://bradaskolinn.is) · **Discount:** Discounts announced first to its mailing list · *Venue not stated on the page; course also hosted at Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur; see also G21-06*
- ✓ **Ljósa (Ljósmæðraþjónustan ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Midwife-led practice running birth education in Icelandic and English (on-site and online), bump meet-ups and parent mornings, plus ultrasound and acupuncture. · [ljosa.is](https://ljosa.is) · **Discount:** Birth education 19,990 ISK per couple; most unions reimburse all or part (per site) · *Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó, Hafnarfjörður; breastfeeding counselling announced as coming soon; booking at ljosa.is/boka; see also G8-15.*
- ✓ **Móðurafl** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Birth preparation and coaching, doula support and 'Nýtt upphaf' postpartum life coaching on wellbeing, relationships and self-image for new parents. · [modurafl.is](https://modurafl.is) · **Discount:** Free 25-minute introductory conversation · *Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó, Suðurgata 41; prices and dates not on the page; overlaps with doula and birth-education categories.*
- ✓ **9 mánuðir** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Independent family health centre with childbirth education, breastfeeding, twin-pregnancy and infant-massage courses, plus ultrasound, midwife support, massage and acupuncture. · [9manudir.is](https://9manudir.is) · *Hlíðasmári 10, Kópavogur; reception weekdays 8:30-15:30; also lists fertility treatment; gynaecology and postnatal checks not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Hlöðuloftið - sálfræðistofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Psychology practice for children and parents listing parent training (CPS) and support for parents and professionals alongside ADHD assessments and EMDR therapy. · [hloduloftid.com](https://www.hloduloftid.com/) · *Parent-training page text not readable; St. Jó listing mentions CPS parent training; Sjúkratryggingar contract; waiting list 6-8 months; aimed at children with behavioural difficulties rather than babies.*
- ✓ **Tengslamat** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður) + Suðurland (Selfoss)) — Attachment-focused practice offering attachment assessments plus individual, couple and family interviews for children and parents, and training for professionals. · [tengslamat.is](https://tengslamat.is) · *Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó and Bankinn vinnustofa, Selfoss; no parent course described on the site, so more family counselling than infant-parenting course.*
- ✓ **METIS Sálfræðistofa** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland (fjarþjónusta)) — Akureyri psychology practice serving parents and families with psychotherapy, assessments, training and workshops; remote sessions available. · [metis.is](https://www.metis.is) · *Kaupangur building, Mýrarvegur, Akureyri; no infant-parenting course described - the only Akureyri provider seen with parent and family services.*

### G8-09 · Bak-, háls- og axlarvandi foreldra — Physio, chiropractic & massage for parents' back/neck pain

**Problem:** Carrying a baby hurts  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 13

*Market:* Large, fragmented market: dozens of physiotherapy clinics in the capital area, one chiropractor found, few outside Reykjavik. Listed the 13 seen; most clinics have no specific parent offer. Physio costs are partly covered by Sjúkratryggingar and unions' sick funds.

- ✓ **Sjúkraþjálfun Íslands** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Large physiotherapy clinic with specialist teams for neck and back, shoulder, hand and elbow, knee and hip, plus a women's-health team and group training. · [sjukratjalfun.is](https://www.sjukratjalfun.is/) · *Kringlan (3rd floor) and Orkuhúsið, Kópavogur*
- ✓ **Gáski sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physiotherapy since 1988 with FAQ pages on back pain, neck-related headaches and shoulder problems, a gym, and pregnancy swimming. No referral needed for first six sessions. · [gaski.is](https://gaski.is/) · *Mjódd and Bolholt, Reykjavík*
- ✓ **Styrkur sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physiotherapy clinic with focus on musculoskeletal problems and movement control, individual treatment, group training and a gym room. · [styrkurehf.is](https://styrkurehf.is/) · *Reykjavík*
- ✓ **Stígandi sjúkraþjálfun (Heilsuklasinn)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Individual physiotherapy with an evidence-based approach, plus group training, water training and pregnancy swimming. · [stigandi.is](https://www.stigandi.is) · *Reykjavík; page does not list specific conditions*
- ✓ **Vivus** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physio-founded centre offering assessment, treatment and rehab plans in person or online, plus group and remote training including programmes for mothers. · [vivus.is](https://vivus.is) · *Reykjavík; online physiotherapy available*
- ✓ **Sjúkraþjálfunin Sporthúsinu** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Physiotherapy diagnosing and treating musculoskeletal problems with movement analysis, advice and massage; works with doctors and chiropractors on site. · [sporthusid.is/thjonusta/sjukrathjalfunin-sporthusinu](https://www.sporthusid.is/thjonusta/sjukrathjalfunin-sporthusinu) · *Kópavogur (Sporthúsið building); site sjukrasport.is*
- ✓ **Kírópraktorstofa Íslands** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Chiropractic clinic using digital X-ray to find spinal problems behind neck, upper-back and lower-back pain, with exercise plans; treatment starts the same day. · [sporthusid.is/thjonusta/kiropraktorstofa-islands](https://www.sporthusid.is/thjonusta/kiropraktorstofa-islands) · *Sporthúsið, Kópavogur; no pregnancy-specific wording on page*
- ✓ **Hress sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Physiotherapy clinic at the Hress gym: examination and diagnosis, strength training and shockwave therapy; booking online or by phone. · [sjukrahress.is](https://www.sjukrahress.is/) · *Hafnarfjörður; page does not name specific back or neck conditions*
- ✓ **Ásmegin sjúkraþjálfun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Physiotherapy for musculoskeletal problems and sports injuries, group training and supervised water exercise in Ásvallalaug pool. · [asmegin.net](https://www.asmegin.net/) · *Hafnarfjörður (inside Ásvallalaug pool); 24-hour cancellation rule with fees*
- ✓ **Sjúkraþjálfarinn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Two Hafnarfjörður sites: one with child physiotherapy facilities and one focused on musculoskeletal and sports physiotherapy. · [sjukrathjalfarinn.is](https://www.sjukrathjalfarinn.is/) · *Details from a search snippet of its own site; page text did not load in WebFetch*
- ✓ **Sjúkraþjálfun Reykjavíkur / Garðabæjar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Physiotherapy at two sites with exercise and group classes, golf training and cooling and resistance products. · [srg.is/sjukrathjalfun-gardabaejar](https://srg.is/sjukrathjalfun-gardabaejar/) · *Reykjavík and Garðabær sites; page lists no specific conditions*
- ✓ **Efling sjúkraþjálfun (Efling ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri physiotherapy clinic offering general physiotherapy, sports rehabilitation, electrotherapy and lymphatic drainage plus group classes. · [eflingehf.is/thjonusta](https://eflingehf.is/thjonusta/) · *Akureyri; unrelated to the trade union Efling*
- ✓ **Endurheimt heilsumiðstöð** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Health centre with physiotherapy, lifestyle medicine, chronic-pain reception and musculoskeletal exercise classes, plus a women's exercise class. · [endurheimt.is/product/kvenheilsa-slokun](https://endurheimt.is/product/kvenheilsa-slokun/) · *Reykjavík*

No parent-specific discounts seen; spa cards and gift vouchers are the common offers. Independent massage studios are numerous and only a few were checked.

- ✓ **Nuddstofan Hress** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Massage room at the Hress gym offering 60-minute classic oil massage (13,000 kr) and deep-tissue massage (15,000 kr); online booking via Noona; sauna access included. · [hress.is/nuddstofan-hress](https://www.hress.is/nuddstofan-hress/) · *Hafnarfjörður; no pregnancy massage listed*

- ✓ **Sjóböðin Hvammsvík (Hvammsvík Hot Springs)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Eight sea-side hot pools in Hvalfjörður about 56 km from Reykjavik, with a bistro; 10-visit cards and gift cards; open daily 10:30-20:30. · [hvammsvik.com/is](https://hvammsvik.com/is) · **Discount:** 10-visit card · *No massage or treatments listed; 30% promotion seen on 11.11*

### G8-12 · Snyrtistofur og húðvörur fyrir mömmur — Beauty salons & skincare for mums

**Problem:** Feeling like yourself again  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

*Market:* Three Icelandic skincare brands (one with a dedicated mother-and-child line), two day spas with facials, and many beauty salons that are visible only through the Noona booking directory. Large market; beauty salons were not checked individually.

- ✓ **Villimey** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun) — Organic Icelandic herbal skincare marketed as safe for mother and child: facial oil, healing ointment, nappy-rash ointment, a Mother & Child gift pack and a 'Bumbu Galdur' bump product. · [villimey.is](https://villimey.is) · *Webshop in Icelandic and English; has a Sölustaðir (stockists) page; Bumbu Galdur and gift pack not detailed on page*
- ✓ **Taramar** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt) — Icelandic skincare from seaweed and medicinal herbs (serum, night cream, cleansing oil) free of hormone-disrupting substances; webshop with home or Dropp delivery. · [taramar.is](https://www.taramar.is) · **Discount:** Vildarklúbbur loyalty club: 3,000-point welcome credit and 4% points on web purchases · *Sold at Hagkaup (Smáralind, Kringlan, Garðabær, Akureyri), Íslandsapótek and Taste of Iceland; no pregnancy-specific claims*
- ✓ **Sóley Organics** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt) — Organic Icelandic skincare (face, body, hair, hands) with Eygló moisturiser and Græðir healing balm; webshop with free shipping over 15,000 kr and refill stores. · [soleyorganics.is](https://soleyorganics.is) · *Head office in Reykjavík; stockist locator on site; no maternity range*
- ✓ **Laugar Spa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Day spa with facials, foot and hand care, waxing, lash and brow tinting, massage and organic Laugar Spa skincare; gift vouchers. · [laugarspa.is](https://laugarspa.is/) · *Laugardalur, Reykjavík; part of World Class group*
- ✓ **Hreyfing Spa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Spa with massage and spa treatments, couples treatments and clay treatment; multi-visit cards cheaper than single visits. · [hreyfing.is/hreyfing-spa/medferdir](https://www.hreyfing.is/hreyfing-spa/medferdir/) · **Discount:** Spa and massage cards about 10-15% below single prices · *Glæsibær, Reykjavík*
- ○ **GLOW Snyrtistofa Hafnarfjörður** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Beauty salon in Hafnarfjörður seen in the Noona booking directory; treatments and parent offers not checked. · [noona.is/companies/hairstyling/hafnarfjordur](https://noona.is/companies/hairstyling/hafnarfjordur) · *Seen only in directory*
- ○ **Emilía Beauty Studio** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes) — Beauty studio in Reykjanesbær seen in the Noona booking directory; treatments and parent offers not checked. · [noona.is/companies/hairstyling/reykjanesbaer](https://noona.is/companies/hairstyling/reykjanesbaer) · *Seen only in directory*

### G8-13 · Vítamín og bætiefni fyrir foreldra — Vitamins & supplements for parents

**Problem:** Iron, vitamin D, folate, energy  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6

*Market:* Pharmacy-led market: Lyfja (41 pharmacies nationwide, with a free 'Vöggugjöf' baby gift for expectant parents) and Apótekarinn (28 stores, merged with Lyf og heilsa) dominate; independents and webshops add a few more. Heilsuhúsið's shop has closed and its range now sits with Lyfja.

- ✓ **Lyfja** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Pharmacy chain with a 'Meðganga & brjóstagjöf' vitamin category, supplements for mothers and children, breastfeeding and nappy ranges; 41 pharmacies in 31 towns including Akureyri. · [lyfja.is](https://www.lyfja.is) · **Discount:** Free Vöggugjöf Lyfju baby gift for expectant parents and babies up to 3 months (includes a 4,000 kr voucher for 66°Norður); currently sold out, mailing list for restock · *Also owns Heilsuhúsið range; store list at lyfja.is/um-lyfju/verslanir; one gift per baby Also listed as: Heilsuhúsið (Lyfja).*
- ✓ **Apótekarinn** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Pharmacy chain with 28 stores, vitamin brands Better You, Probi and Jörth and a 'Móðir og barn' category; Apótekarinn+ members get fixed discounts. · [apotekarinn.is](https://www.apotekarinn.is) · **Discount:** Apótekarinn+ loyalty: 10% off skin and cosmetic products, 15% off Gamla apótekið products; no parent-specific benefit seen · *Lyf og heilsa's site redirects here; store list on /verslanir page; 20% November offers on Better You and Probi seen*
- ✓ **Lyfjaver** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun) — Low-price pharmacy and online pharmacy selling vitamins and supplements, skincare and baby and mother products; free delivery over 9,900 kr. · [lyfjaver.is](https://www.lyfjaver.is) · **Discount:** Vildarklúbbur loyalty club (benefits not described on page) · *Reykjavík pharmacy plus online pharmacy (netapótek); delivery area not stated*
- ✓ **Reykjavíkurapótek** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Independent pharmacy selling vitamins, supplements, cosmetics and nursing-care products; free home delivery of medicines. · [reyap.is](http://www.reyap.is/) · *Reykjavík; no baby-specific range described*
- ✓ **Garðs Apótek** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Independent pharmacy selling vitamins, supplements and nursing supplies; home delivery in the capital area and postal delivery nationwide through its Appótek web pharmacy. · [gardsapotek.is](https://www.gardsapotek.is) · *Reykjavík; web pharmacy appotek.is*
- ✓ **Eylíf** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Icelandic-made supplements including 'Smoother Skin & Hair', joints, bones, gut and liver lines; webshop with subscriptions and gift cards. · [eylif.is](https://www.eylif.is) · **Discount:** 20% off subscriptions; free Dropp delivery over 7,500 kr · *No pregnancy or postpartum products listed; stockists not shown*

### G8-14 · Næringarráðgjöf og mataræði eftir fæðingu — Postnatal nutrition coaching

**Problem:** Eating well while breastfeeding and sleep-deprived  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1

*Market:* No clinic or brand specialising in postnatal or breastfeeding nutrition could be confirmed; dietitians in Iceland mostly trade under personal names (excluded by the brief) and the web search budget ran out before brand-name searches. Only a general nutrition-education site is listed; check the Heilsuklasinn building and pharmacy chains' mother-and-child ranges (G8-13) as adjacent options.

- ✓ **Heilsubankinn** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt (netnámskeið)) — Icelandic health-education site with articles on nutrition, supplements and recipes and online courses on energy, pain and sleep, including a free introductory course and a 16-week programme. · [heilsubankinn.is](https://www.heilsubankinn.is) · **Discount:** Free introductory online course · *Not postnatal-specific; no individual nutrition counselling or prices shown on page*

### G8-15 · Foreldrahópar og mömmumorgnar — Parent groups & meet-ups

**Problem:** Isolation, wanting other parents  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4 (+3 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Parent meet-ups are mostly free parish foreldramorgnar, library and community-house mornings, and the non-profit Opni leikskóli Memmm (Reykjavík and Hafnarfjörður); private operators are Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur (free Tuesday lunch meet-up), Ljósa in Hafnarfjörður (bump meet-ups, parent mornings) and mum-and-baby yoga studios. Akureyri: parish foreldramorgnar at Glerárkirkja and a Memmm trial at Amtsbókasafnið in winter 2026. Facebook-based mömmuhópar cannot be listed.

- ✓ **Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur - Hittumst í hádeginu** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Free Tuesday 11:30-13:00 gathering for all parents of young children at the private birth centre, alongside courses, yoga and breastfeeding support. · [faedingarheimilid.is/home](https://www.faedingarheimilid.is/home) · **Discount:** Meet-up is free · *Reykjavík; fathers' course also offered*
- ✓ **Jógasetrið - Mömmujóga** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — 8-week mum-and-baby yoga course for babies aged 6-8 weeks, a regular way for new mothers to meet; also pregnancy yoga and a couples course. · [jogasetrid.is](https://www.jogasetrid.is) · *Reykjavík; next course starts 11 Jan 2027, Mon/Wed 10:15*
- ✓ **Hallgrímskirkja - Foreldramorgnar** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Wednesday 10:00-12:00 parent-and-baby mornings in the choir basement with a sing-along at 11:00, guest speakers and refreshments; open to grandparents too. · [hallgrimskirkja.is/foreldrarmorgnar](https://www.hallgrimskirkja.is/foreldrarmorgnar) · *Parish activity, not an advertiser; page does not state if free*
- ✓ **Akureyrarkirkja / Glerárkirkja - Foreldramorgnar** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri) — Thursday 10:00-12:00 parent-and-infant mornings in winter (September-May) at Glerárkirkja, with talks on parenting, baby games and singing. · [akureyrarkirkja.is/is/safnadarstarf/foreldramorgnar](https://www.akureyrarkirkja.is/is/safnadarstarf/foreldramorgnar) · *Parish activity, not an advertiser; cost not stated*
- ✓ **Samskiptastöðin - Að verða foreldri** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Group course for expectant parents and parents of children up to 3, babies under 6 months welcome, with other parents in the same stage. · [samskiptastodin.is/namskeid-fyrir-foreldra-ungra-barna](https://samskiptastodin.is/namskeid-fyrir-foreldra-ungra-barna/) · **Discount:** Most unions contribute to the course fee (per page) · *Reykjavík; page may be outdated, confirm dates*
- ✓ **Opni leikskóli Memmm** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík, Hafnarfjörður)) — Free weekly open play mornings with coffee and a sing-along for children 0-5 and their parents or carers at libraries and a community house. · [memmm.is](https://memmm.is) · **Discount:** Free for all families · *Non-profit association, no sign-up needed (2026 education-prize recognition; partners Reykjavík and Hafnarfjörður): Hafnarfjörður library Mon/Thu, Aflagrandi Tue, Gerðuberg Wed; ran Akureyri parent mornings at Amtsbókasafnið in winter 2026 (not on the current schedule).*
- ✓ **Ljósa (Ljósmæðraþjónustan ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Midwife-led practice hosting bump meet-ups for expectant mothers and parent mornings for mothers on leave and fathers, with midwives on site. · [ljosa.is](https://ljosa.is) · *Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó, Hafnarfjörður; no schedule or price for the meet-ups on the page; also sells birth education (see G8-08).*

## G9 · Öryggi og eftirlit — Safety & monitoring

### G9-01 · Barnahlið, læsingar og hornavarnir — Safety gates, locks & corner guards

**Problem:** Crawling and climbing starts  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11

*Market:* Fífa is the main specialist, but gates and locks are also sold by Húsgagnaheimilið (about 20 gates plus corner, socket and cupboard guards), the DIY chains BYKO, Húsasmiðjan and BAUHAUS, JYSK, IKEA and Heimkaup (Clippasafe locks); Draumaland's site did not load, and Akureyri is covered only by the chains.

- ✓ **Fífa barnavöruverslun** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Faxafen) + netverslun) — Baby store with a full Öryggi range: safety gates, cupboard, drawer, fridge, toilet and oven-knob locks, socket covers, table-corner guards and a home safety starter pack. · [fifa.is/categories/oryggi](https://fifa.is/categories/oryggi) · **Discount:** 15% off Clippasafe safety products for Stofn (Sjóvá) and Vörður customers; 20% off car seats · *Also sells Neonate baby monitors (see G9-02); one store at Faxafen 8; insurance-company discount page on site.*
- ✓ **IKEA Ísland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær) + netverslun) — IKEA's Patrull child-safety range: socket covers, door stoppers and slide guards, plus an 'Öryggi heimilisins' information page. · [ikea.is/is/search/?q=patrull](https://www.ikea.is/is/search/?q=patrull) · *Only three Patrull items found in the web search; gates and corner guards not seen. Delivers countrywide via webshop.*
- ✓ **Dótabúðin (Chicco)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur) + netverslun) — Toy and baby shop and Chicco's Icelandic outlet; Chicco safety category includes a safety gate (Öryggisgrind). · [dotabudin.is/pages/chicco](https://dotabudin.is/pages/chicco) · **Discount:** Chicco: 20% off car seats and accessories for Vörður customers; Dótaklúbburinn 5% credit · *chicco.is redirects here; only a gate seen in safety range.*
- ✓ **Barnið okkar** (Netverslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Baby store and wholesaler (since 2014) with Öryggisvörur and Öryggishlífar categories, play pens (Leikgrindur) and the Clippsafe brand, plus prams, car seats and nursery items. · [barnidokkar.is](https://barnidokkar.is/) · **Discount:** Sale sections and mailing-list offers; no loyalty club seen · *Gates, locks and corner guards not named individually on the page; a Hlíðasmári shop was mentioned as closing while the business continues.*
- ✓ **Húsgagnaheimilið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Family-run baby store (since 2000) with a large Öryggið range: about 20 safety gates plus cupboard, door, window, socket and table-corner guards (Dreambaby). · [husgogn.is/collections/barnaoryggisvorur](https://husgogn.is/collections/barnaoryggisvorur) · *Store at Fossaleynir, Reykjavík; free delivery nationwide via Dropp/Eimskip; also sells baby monitors (see G9-02); trades as Húsgagnaheimilið - Barnaheimilið ehf.*
- ✓ **BYKO** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurnes + netverslun) — DIY chain with a Barnavörur > Öryggi barna section; sells a no-drill child safety gate (74-81 cm, two-way opening, for children up to 24 months). · [byko.is/vara/barnaoryggishlid-346077](https://byko.is/vara/barnaoryggishlid-346077) · *Stores in Kópavogur, Reykjavík (Grandi), Akureyri (Óðinsnes) and Suðurnes; category pages load dynamically so the rest of the child-safety range was not visible.*
- ✓ **Húsasmiðjan** (Keðja · Landið allt (m.a. Akureyri) + netverslun) — Building-supplies chain with a Barnaöryggi category under Öryggisvörur; a 91 cm hardwood safety gate (about 10,400 kr) was listed, alongside child car seats. · [husa.is/fatnadur-og-oryggisvara/oryggisvorur/barnaoryggi](https://www.husa.is/fatnadur-og-oryggisvara/oryggisvorur/barnaoryggi/) · *The gate page seen in search was sold out/unavailable at check and category products load dynamically; stores incl. Akureyri, Selfoss, Reykjanesbær, Ísafjörður, Egilsstaðir.*
- ✓ **BAUHAUS Ísland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Home-improvement store with a Barnaöryggisvörur category and the Dreambaby brand, incl. a 140 cm stair/doorway safety gate (about 14,600 kr). · [bauhaus.is/heimilid-og-geymslan/oryggisvorur/barnaoryggisvorur](https://www.bauhaus.is/heimilid-og-geymslan/oryggisvorur/barnaoryggisvorur) · *Single store at Lambhagavegur, Reykjavík; ships nationwide; category lists load dynamically so the full range was not visible.*

- ✓ **Heimkaup** (Netverslun · Netverslun (heimsending; svæði ekki staðfest)) — Online store with a child-safety (Öryggisvörur barna) section selling Clippasafe cupboard and drawer locks, screw-on and adhesive versions. · [heimkaup.is](https://heimkaup.is/) · *Confirmed only through search hits on its own domain (the shop pages load dynamically); delivery area not checked; also lists Chicco reflective stickers (see G9-07).*
- ○ ⚠ **Draumaland** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Baby webshop with a full Clippasafe safety range: self-closing stair gate (73-96 cm), cupboard, drawer, fridge and toilet locks, table-corner guards. · [draumaland.is/collections/clippasafe](https://www.draumaland.is/collections/clippasafe) · *Site returned 503/TLS errors on 17 Nov 2026 so it may be down or closed; it still has an active product feed on the Já.is price search and cached search pages - verify before contacting.*

Wi-Fi 1080p), Eufy E21 and Nedis monitors. · [ht.is/heilsu-og-snyrtitaeki/barnavorur/barnapiur.html](https://ht.is/heilsu-og-snyrtitaeki/barnavorur/barnapiur.html) · *Stores in Reykjavík (Suðurlandsbraut), Reykjanesbær, Selfoss, Akureyri (Glerártorg) and Egilsstaðir; prices about 5,000-40,000 kr; the Philips Avent video monitor showed as sold out.*
