### G9-03 · Reykskynjarar og slökkvitæki — Smoke detectors & fire extinguishers

**Problem:** Fire safety at home  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* One home-focused specialist with an existing insurer discount (ÓGER Eldvarnamiðstöðin, 20% for Stofn members), one extinguisher service firm in Reykjanesbær, and the security companies Securitas, Nortek and Öryggismiðstöðin selling detectors/extinguishers. DIY chains (BYKO, Húsasmiðjan) surely stock detectors but their webshops could not be read; the Fire Safety Alliance (Eldvarnabandalagið) is a non-profit information body.

- ✓ **ÓGER Eldvarnamiðstöðin** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Fire-safety shop at Sundaborg and webshop: extinguishers, standalone and interconnected smoke, heat, gas and water detectors, fire blankets, first-aid kits and rescue ladders for homes and summer houses. · [eldvarnamidstodin.is](https://eldvarnamidstodin.is/) · **Discount:** 20% off home safety products (smoke detectors, extinguishers) for Stofn (Sjóvá) members · *Domain oger.is redirects here; no Akureyri outlet listed.*
- ✓ **Eldvarnir ehf.** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes + landið allt (heimsóknir)) — Extinguisher sales, annual inspections, service contracts and fire-safety training from a workshop in Reykjanesbær; recommends a 6 L water extinguisher, fire blanket and smoke detectors for homes. · [eldvarnir.is](https://www.eldvarnir.is/) · *Stated 50 years in business; no webshop (quote form); smoke detector sales not confirmed.*
- ✓ **Securitas** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (Reykjavík, Akureyri, Austurland, Suðurland, Reykjanes)) — Security company whose webshop has a fire and safety category: smoke detectors from about 1,350 kr, powder extinguishers, fire blankets and extinguishing spray. · [securitas.is/vefverslun](https://www.securitas.is/vefverslun) · *Also sells alarms and cameras (see G9-04); branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Nortek** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + netverslun) — Security and fire-protection supplier with fire alarm systems, extinguishers and emergency lighting for homes and businesses, plus a webshop. · [nortek.is](https://www.nortek.is/) · *Stores in Reykjavík (Fossaleynir) and Akureyri (Tryggvabraut); smoke detectors not named on the page.*
- ✓ **Öryggismiðstöðin** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (aðsetur í Kópavogi)) — Security company offering extinguishers and fire hoses and linking smoke detectors to its 24/7 monitored systems. · [oryggi.is](https://www.oryggi.is/) · *Smoke detectors for homes not listed as a product on the page; HQ in Kópavogur.*

### G9-04 · Öryggiskerfi og eftirlitsmyndavélar — Home alarm systems & cameras

**Problem:** Peace of mind  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Three security companies dominate (Securitas, Öryggismiðstöðin, Nortek), two of them with Akureyri presence; a small electrician-run supplier (Raf-Bræður/alarm.is) and ELKO's smart-home range cover the do-it-yourself end. No parent- or insurer-specific discounts seen on the pages.

- ✓ **Securitas** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (Reykjavík, Akureyri, Austurland, Suðurland, Reykjanes)) — Home alarm package Heimavörn+ with app control and 24/7 monitoring, panic button, summer-house alarm, and a webshop with indoor/outdoor cameras and doorbells. · [securitas.is/heimili](https://www.securitas.is/heimili/) · *Webshop sells Alarm.com Wi-Fi cameras (about 10,000-70,000 kr), smoke detectors and extinguishers (see G9-03); branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Öryggismiðstöðin** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (aðsetur í Kópavogi)) — Home security (Heimilisöryggi) with 24-hour monitoring and response, camera systems, GPS emergency button, extinguishers and fire hoses. · [oryggi.is](https://www.oryggi.is/) · *Branches beyond Kópavogur not shown on the page.*
- ✓ **Nortek** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + netverslun) — Intrusion alarms, cameras and Ajax smart devices (water shut-off valve, air-quality sensor) with an online package builder for home security. · [nortek.is](https://www.nortek.is/) · *Stores in Reykjavík and Akureyri; webshop at nortek.is/vefverslun.*
- ✓ **Raf-Bræður (alarm.is)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netverslun)) — Licensed electrical contractor selling security systems, GSM monitoring units and 4G cameras, with a webshop and general electrical work. · [alarm.is](https://www.alarm.is/) · *Branches/locations not shown on the page.*
- ✓ **ELKO** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + netverslun) — Smart-home range with security cameras, video doorbells and smart locks for self-installation. · [elko.is/voruflokkar/snjallheimili-11](https://elko.is/voruflokkar/snjallheimili-11) · *Stores in Reykjavík, Keflavík and Akureyri.*

### G9-05 · Mygluskoðun, rakamælingar og loftgæði — Mould inspection, humidity & air-quality testing

**Problem:** Mould and damp are a major worry in Icelandic homes  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 18

*Market:* Bigger than first found: besides Verkvist, Verkís and EFLA there are specialist mould/air-quality firms (Disact, Mygluprof.is, Zetor, MT Ísland), COWI (ex-Mannvit), remediation contractors (HiH, Björninn) and many property-condition inspectors selling moisture and mould checks; mostly capital-area based, regional offices mainly at the engineering houses; Og Synir and Wabisabi sites seen in search no longer load.

- ✓ **Verkvist** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík)) — Indoor-environment consultancy specialised in moisture damage and mould: inspections, mould and material sampling, humidity measurements and air-quality measurements, with a home (Heimili) price list. · [verkvist.is](https://www.verkvist.is/) · *Orders via a service-request email link; no Akureyri office shown; has also written mould/moisture memos for Reykjavík city buildings.*
- ✓ **Verkís** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (skrifstofur í Reykjavík og Akureyri)) — Engineering firm with a service page on moisture damage and mould: building inspections, moisture measurement, mould assessment, indoor air-quality evaluation and repair advice. · [verkis.is/thjonusta/byggingar/husasott-mygla](https://verkis.is/thjonusta/byggingar/husasott-mygla/) · *Service page is general (not aimed specifically at home owners); regional offices include Akureyri.*
- ○ **EFLA** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Engineering consultancy that has done moisture and mould investigations of concrete walls and school buildings for Reykjavík city, including material sampling. · [efla.is](https://www.efla.is/) · *Seen only in Reykjavík city report PDFs, not on its own site; whether it serves private home owners is unconfirmed.*
- ✓ **Disact** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Indoor-climate firm: moisture and mould surveys (raka- og mygluúttekt), Mycometer mould analysis, Airbeld continuous air-quality monitoring, ActivePure air purifiers and mould treatment for homes. · [disact.is](https://disact.is/) · *Says it serves homes, companies and public bodies nationwide; also sells treatment and air-purifier solutions, so a likely buyer for both inspection and air-quality angles.*
- ✓ **Mygluprof.is** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint (líklega Höfuðborgarsvæðið)) — Mould-testing service: dust DNA mould tests, moisture measurements and limited visual inspection, with a report on species found, occupant risk assessment and an air-quality grade. · [mygluprof.is](https://mygluprof.is/) · *Appears to be run by the consultancy Ýmir ráðgjöf; has a price list page; service area not stated.*
- ✓ **Zetor** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint (líklega Höfuðborgarsvæðið)) — Construction firm offering mould inspections and moisture measurements plus project management and repairs after mould or moisture damage; also condition surveys and housing-association services. · [zetor.is/mygluskodun](https://www.zetor.is/mygluskodun) · *Zetor ehf.; also a builder for new-home owners; service area not stated.*
- ✓ **MT Ísland** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Kópavogur damage-restoration firm (Midtfyns Totalservice Island): mould and moisture inspection with thermal camera, Mycometer sampling, dehumidifier and air-purifier rental, disinfection and asbestos removal. · [fynstotalservice.dk/island](https://www.fynstotalservice.dk/island/) · *MT Ísland ehf., 18 staff, serves individuals, companies and housing associations; inspection about 36,000 kr (max 2 h, ex VAT); website sits on a Danish .dk domain.*
- ✓ **COWI á Íslandi (áður Mannvit)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (sex starfsstöðvar)) — Engineering firm's indoor-climate service: moisture readings with thermal camera, material and DNA dust sampling, and 1-2 week loggers for humidity, CO2, TVOC and PM2.5. · [cowi.com/innivist-og-mygla](https://www.cowi.com/innivist-og-mygla/) · *Page is aimed at companies and institutions; home-owner service not stated; mannvit.is now redirects to COWI Iceland.*
- ✓ **Fasteignaskoðun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Property-condition inspectors offering 'rakamælingar/mygluskoðun' (moisture and mould inspection) alongside condition surveys, cost estimates and court valuations, with an illustrated report. · [fasteignaskodun.is](https://www.fasteignaskodun.is/) · *Price list stated to apply to the capital region.*
- ✓ **Fagsýn** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint (líklega Höfuðborgarsvæðið)) — Condition-survey firm for buyers and owners: moisture readings with optional thermal imaging, leak and moisture analysis, and sampling when microbial growth is suspected. · [fagsyn.is](https://www.fagsyn.is/) · *Serves individuals, companies and institutions; area not stated.*
- ✓ **Vernd og viðhald** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint (líklega Höfuðborgarsvæðið)) — Condition-survey and maintenance-planning firm with a dedicated 'Raka- eða mygluskoðun' service, buy/sell inspections and maintenance advice. · [verndogvidhald.is/astandsskodun](https://www.verndogvidhald.is/astandsskodun) · *Also runs courses and publishes property-purchase and maintenance checklists.*
- ✓ **Sérskoðun.is** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint (líklega Höfuðborgarsvæðið)) — Property-inspection firm offering home air-quality measurement (humidity, temperature, CO2, VOC, particles, about a week, with report), plus sampling and condition surveys. · [serskodun.is/thjonusta/loftgaeda-maeling](https://serskodun.is/thjonusta/loftgaeda-maeling/) · *Serves individuals, companies and housing associations; also building supervision.*
- ✓ **A Fagmenn** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint (líklega Höfuðborgarsvæðið)) — Property-inspection company offering moisture and mould inspections with moisture meters and thermal camera, quick mould test or sampling, and oral or written report. · [afagmenn.is/raka-og-mygluskodun](https://www.afagmenn.is/raka-og-mygluskodun/) · *A Fagmenn ehf.; published price list (base fee 75,000 kr without report, 24,800 kr hourly); travel surcharge outside the capital region.*
- ✓ **Eikarfell** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint (líklega Höfuðborgarsvæðið)) — Roofing and property-inspection firm offering condition surveys with moisture measurement and thermal imaging, buy/sell inspections and security/risk inspections. · [eikarfell.is/astandsskodun](https://eikarfell.is/astandsskodun/) · *Also does roof repairs; moisture measurement can be booked alone.*
- ✓ **Matsmaður** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint) — Property inspector (condition surveys, rental inspections, cost estimates) with a moisture page; also inspects for mould fungus and issues reports usable in purchase or sale. · [matsmadur.is](https://matsmadur.is/) · *Small operator trading as 'Matsmaður - Skoðunarmaður fasteigna'; the same business also appears under matfasteigna.is; area not stated.*
- ✓ **Frumherji** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint (Frumherji er með starfsstöðvar víða um land)) — Large inspection company whose property-inspection service covers visual condition surveys and rental inspections, with moisture measurement when signs of damp are seen. · [frumherji.is/fasteignaskodun](https://frumherji.is/fasteignaskodun/) · *Moisture checks are secondary to the general condition survey, so not a mould specialist; the property-inspection locations were not listed (its other divisions inspect vehicles and installations nationwide).*
- ✓ **HiH** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kópavogur)) — Contractor offering mould cleaning and removal for homes, plus moisture measurements, mould inspection advice and preparation of mould samples or tests. · [hih.is/portfolio-item/mygluthrif](https://hih.is/portfolio-item/mygluthrif/) · *HiH ehf., Kópavogur; general building contractor (roof cleaning, painting, carpentry) where mould is one service line.*
- ✓ **Björninn Verktakar** (Þjónusta · Ótilgreint) — Building contractor offering mould inspection, moisture measurement, mould removal, repair of moisture damage and ventilation checks; quotes after consultation. · [bjorninnverktakar.is/mygluhreinsun](https://bjorninnverktakar.is/mygluhreinsun/) · *Service area and sampling not stated; the page loaded only through the fetch tool, not plain curl.*

### G9-06 · Hitastýrð blöndunartæki og brunavarnir í vatnslögnum — Thermostatic mixer taps & scald protection (plumbers)

**Problem:** Geothermal hot water can scald a small child  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* No company markets scald protection to parents. Bathroom-fittings retailers (Tengi with Kópavogur, Akureyri and Selfoss shops; Ísleifur webshop) and plumbing contractors (AH Pípulagnir, Lagnir.is) could install or sell thermostatic mixers, but no page confirmed a thermostatic range. DIY chains (BYKO, Húsasmiðjan, BAUHAUS) exist but their product pages could not be read.

- ✓ **Tengi** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Selfoss) — Specialist retailer of bathroom and kitchen fittings and mixer taps with a plumbing department offering pipe materials and advice; shops in Kópavogur, Akureyri and Selfoss. · [tengi.is](https://www.tengi.is/) · *Thermostatic taps not named on the page; brands seen include Mera, Vola, Quooker; webshop requires an application.*
- ✓ **Ísleifur (Fagkaup)** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Webshop for bathroom and kitchen fittings, mainly AXOR: shower sets, basin mixers and rails; some product names include 'thermo select'. · [isleifur.is](https://www.isleifur.is/) · *Part of Fagkaup ehf.; thermostatic function implied by product names but not stated.*
- ✓ **AH Pípulagnir** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Garðabær)) — Plumbing contractor in Garðabær: new installations, repairs, underfloor heating, radiator and district-heating piping, with an online work-request form. · [pipulagnir.is](https://pipulagnir.is/) · *Mixer taps and scald protection not mentioned on the page.*
- ✓ **Lagnir.is** (Þjónusta · Suðurland (Selfoss)) — Plumbing contractor and materials seller in Selfoss serving the main South Iceland municipalities, with a price list and work-request form. · [lagnir.is](https://lagnir.is/) · *Thermostatic mixers not named on the page.*

### G9-07 · Endurskin og vetraröryggi — Reflectors, lights & winter safety for strollers and kids

**Problem:** Dark winters and icy paths  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 17 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Still no stroller-reflector specialist, but many mainstream sellers carry child reflective gear: kids' outdoor brands (ZO•ON, Cintamani, Icewear, 66°Norður), tags and vests at Epal, A4, Motif, BYKO, Húsasmiðjan and Heimkaup, bike shops (Hjólasprettur, Vaskur) and Akureyri stroller-bag shop Kara Kids; insurers and NGOs give away reflectors, and no Icelandic seller of kids' ice grips was found.

- ✓ **Örninn Reiðhjólaverslun** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Faxafen) + netverslun) — Bike shop with a winter selection of lights, reflectors and reflective vests, child bike seats and trailers (Thule), and studded tyres including for children's bikes. · [orninn.is](https://orninn.is/) · *Store at Faxafen 8, the same address as the Fífa baby store; no Akureyri branch seen; stroller-specific lights not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Endurskin.is** (Netverslun · Netverslun (Ísland)) — Icelandic webshop for CE-certified reflective tags (endurskinsmerki), including custom-printed reflectors for gifts and fundraising. · [endurskin.is](https://endurskin.is/) · *Children's or stroller products not named on the page; delivery terms not shown.*
- ✓ **Lífland** (Keðja · Landið allt (m.a. Akureyri)) — Retailer and wholesaler (horse, pet and farm supplies) selling LED visibility lights in four colours for about 550 kr, visible up to 500 m, with children listed among the uses, plus a safety-vest section. · [lifland.is/en/online-store/men/safety-vests/index/endurskin/led-ljos-gummi-fjorir-litir](https://www.lifland.is/en/online-store/men/safety-vests/index/endurskin/led-ljos-gummi-fjorir-litir) · *Stores in Reykjavík (Korputorg), Akureyri, Borgarnes, Blönduós, Hvolsvöllur and Selfoss; prams not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Barnið okkar** (Netverslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Baby store and wholesaler (since 2014) selling stroller winter accessories: sheepskin footmuffs and stroller gloves, rain covers for many stroller brands, plus safety products. · [barnidokkar.is](https://barnidokkar.is/) · **Discount:** Sale sections and mailing-list offers; no loyalty club seen · *Reflectors and stroller lights not listed; page mentions a Hlíðasmári shop closing without the business shutting down.*
- ✓ **Krabbameinsfélagið - vefverslun** (Samtök/opinbert · Netverslun (Ísland)) — The Cancer Society's webshop sells Bookman reflectors (about 950 kr) that clip or tie onto clothing, backpacks, strollers and prams. · [vefverslun.krabb.is/products/endurskinsmerki-barna](https://vefverslun.krabb.is/products/endurskinsmerki-barna) · *Non-profit webshop, not a slot buyer; listed because it is a visible reflector seller for strollers.*
- ✓ **ZO•ON** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Selfoss + netverslun) — Icelandic outdoor brand whose kids' Hoppa rain jacket and trousers carry reflective details on the front, back and legs; free delivery over 15,000 kr. · [zo-on.is/products/bleyta-regnjakki-krakka-copy](https://zo-on.is/products/bleyta-regnjakki-krakka-copy) · *Own stores in Reykjavík, Akureyri and Selfoss; site search for 'endurskin' returned ten products; stroller-specific reflectors not sold.*
- ✓ **Cintamani** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Faxafen) + netverslun) — Icelandic outdoor brand with kids' and infant outerwear; its site search for 'endurskin' returns dozens of children's items, e.g. rain gear with reflective strips. · [cintamani.is/voruflokkur/kids](https://www.cintamani.is/voruflokkur/kids/) · *Store at Faxafen, Reykjavík; also sold through Heimkaup; stand-alone reflectors or lights not seen.*
- ✓ **Icewear** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun + verslanir (staðsetning ekki staðfest)) — Icelandic outdoor brand with children's jackets, hats and gloves that carry reflective details, e.g. a winter parka with reflectors on sleeve and hood. · [icewear.is/is-IS/born](https://icewear.is/is-IS/born) · *Site sits behind a bot check so only search snippets of its pages were readable; store locations not verified.*
- ✓ **66°Norður** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun + verslanir (staðsetning ekki staðfest)) — Icelandic outerwear brand that sells a 5 x 4.5 cm reflector tag (about 690 kr) and has Kids and Infants outerwear ranges. · [66north.com/is/endurskinsmerki-5x4/p/L87112](https://www.66north.com/is/endurskinsmerki-5x4/p/L87112) · *Premium price tier; product page itself did not load for us, listing seen via search; store locations not checked.*
- ✓ **Epal** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Design store selling Bookman reflective tags, clip-on magnetic reflectors and reflective stickers for clothes, bags, bikes and helmets in a back-to-school range, about 1,200-1,550 kr. · [epal.is/collections/i-skolann](https://www.epal.is/collections/i-skolann) · *Stores at Skeifan, Laugavegur and Kringlan; same Bookman reflectors as the Krabbameinsfélagið entry.*

## G10 · Heimilið og heimilistæki — Home & appliances

### G10-01 · Þvottavélar og þurrkarar — Washers & dryers (appliance stores)

**Problem:** Mountains of laundry  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Five established appliance retailers/agents found (ELKO, Rafha, Heimilistæki, Smith & Norland, Ormsson); HT and ELKO cover Akureyri. Mostly general promos, no baby-specific offers seen.

- ✓ **ELKO** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Electronics and large-appliance chain with a washing machines category; parents can compare and order washers and dryers online or in store. · [elko.is](https://www.elko.is) · *Part of Festi group; stores in capital area, Akureyri and Keflavík airport (per press sources, not re-checked on site); also sells robot vacuums and smart-home gear.*
- ✓ **Rafha** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Home-appliance store with a laundry range: front-loading washers, washer-dryers, condenser dryers and drying cabinets; delivery offered. · [rafha.is](https://www.rafha.is) · *Samsung, AEG and Siemens seen; shop on Suðurlandsbraut, Reykjavík; also kitchen design (Kvik).*
- ✓ **Heimilistæki (HT)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurnes + Suðurland + Austurland) — Appliance retailer with a washers and dryers category and stores in five towns, so parents outside Reykjavík can also buy locally. · [ht.is](http://ht.is/) · *Stores in Reykjavík, Reykjanesbær, Selfoss, Akureyri (Glerártorg) and Egilsstaðir; also sells Sonos and small appliances.*
- ✓ **Smith & Norland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + sending utan svæðis) — Importer and showroom for Siemens, Bosch and Gram washing machines; offers delivery, removal of the old appliance and a long warranty. · [sminor.is](https://www.sminor.is) · *Siemens, Bosch, Gram, NEFF, Gaggenau; showroom at Nóatún 4, Reykjavík; five-year warranty on large appliances noted on site.*
- ✓ **Bræðurnir Ormsson** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Long-established appliance importer-retailer with washer, washer-dryer and heat-pump dryer ranges from AEG, Electrolux and Samsung. · [ormsson.is](https://ormsson.is/) · *Own site blocks automated fetching; offering seen in its own-domain product catalogue files. Store at Lágmúli, Reykjavík.*

### G10-02 · Rakatæki, rakaeyðar og lofthreinsitæki — Humidifiers, dehumidifiers & air purifiers

**Problem:** Dry winter air, damp, allergies  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

*Market:* Appliance chains (HT, Rafha, ELKO) carry humidifiers, dehumidifiers and HEPA purifiers; DIY chains sell moisture absorbers; Hljóðfærahúsið is a surprising Venta humidifier stockist; no specialist air-quality shop found.

- ✓ **Heimilistæki (HT)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurnes + Suðurland + Austurland) — Dedicated humidifier and air purifier category with brands such as Medisana, Wilfa, Canvac and Woods; stores in five towns incl. Akureyri. · [ht.is/heilsu-og-snyrtitaeki/rakataeki-og-lofthreinsitaeki.html](https://ht.is/heilsu-og-snyrtitaeki/rakataeki-og-lofthreinsitaeki.html) · *Also sells washers and dryers (G10-01) and small appliances.*
- ✓ **Rafha** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Air-quality range: humidifiers, dehumidifiers (e.g. Xiaomi, Domo, Medisana) and air purifiers for damp or dry homes. · [rafha.is/products/loftgaedi](https://rafha.is/products/loftgaedi) · *Store on Suðurlandsbraut, Reykjavík; also heating and ventilation range.*
- ✓ **ELKO** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Electronics chain selling air purifiers and dehumidifiers (e.g. Woods hybrid unit) and a blog guide on improving indoor air at home. · [elko.is/vorur/woods-hybrid-lofthreinsitaeki-og-rakathettir-ad20g-391311/703783](https://elko.is/vorur/woods-hybrid-lofthreinsitaeki-og-rakathettir-ad20g-391311/703783) · *Stores incl. Akureyri (Furuvellir).*

### G10-09 · Gæludýr og nýtt barn — Pets & new baby (dog trainers, pet shops, vets)

**Problem:** Preparing the dog or cat for a baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

*Market:* Several pet-shop chains (Dýrabær with Akureyri store, Dýraríkið, Gæludýr.is, Lífland), two dog schools and a vet hospital found; none advertises a dog-and-baby programme yet, so the slot is an open niche.

- ✓ **Dýraríkið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Pet shop since 1979 with food, toys, cages and live small animals, plus dog grooming and nationwide web delivery. · [dyrarikid.is](https://dyrarikid.is) · **Discount:** Club discount (details not shown). · *Stores in Reykjavík (Holtagarðar) and Selfoss.*
- ✓ **Gæludýr.is** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Pet webshop for dogs, cats, fish, rodents and birds with a dog grooming salon, free delivery and vet-reviewed advice articles. · [gaeludyr.is](https://gaeludyr.is) · *Has a new-puppy checklist article but no baby content.*
- ✓ **Lífland** (Keðja · Landið allt (m.a. Akureyri) + Netverslun) — Country-supply chain with a pets category in its webshop and stores around the country, including Akureyri. · [lifland.is](https://lifland.is) · *Stores in Reykjavík (Korputorg), Akureyri, Borgarnes, Blönduós, Hvolsvöllur, Selfoss; dog and cat range not confirmed on front page.*
- ✓ **Hundaskólinn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Dog school in Hafnarfjörður with 9-session puppy and basic courses for whole families, obedience courses and private lessons. · [hundaskolinn.is](https://hundaskolinn.is) · **Discount:** Many unions reimburse course fees; discount on dog licence fee noted. · *No dog-and-baby course listed; could add one.*
- ✓ **Gallerí Voff** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Dog school in Mosfellsdalur since 1991 with attention-training courses, private lessons and special training. · [hundaskoli.is](https://hundaskoli.is) · *Contact via Facebook per site; no baby-prep course listed.*

## G11 · Fjármál og tryggingar — Finance & insurance

### G11-01 · Barnatryggingar — Children's accident & illness insurance

**Problem:** What if something happens to the child  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6

*Market:* Four insurers (Sjóvá, VÍS, TM, Vörður) sell child accident/illness policies from age one month; Landsbankinn (TM) and Arion (Vörður) distribute. Sjóvá also has a standalone pregnancy insurance. Verna stopped selling insurance in Jan 2026.

- ✓ **Sjóvá-Almennar tryggingar hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Child insurance for babies from one month old (future-income protection, critical illness, care benefit) plus a one-off pregnancy insurance for expecting mothers. · [sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/life-and-health/child-insurance](https://www.sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/life-and-health/child-insurance) · **Discount:** Stofn members get 10% off child insurance; pregnancy insurance buyers get partner discounts (Móðurást, Lyfja, 9 mánuðir, Kírópraktor Lindir). · *Child policy about 1,079 ISK/month; pregnancy insurance 30,000 ISK one-off, buyable until week 32. Stofn also gives car-seat discounts via Fífa, Bílanaust, AB varahlutir, Nine Kids. Branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **VÍS tryggingar** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Combined child insurance (accident, critical illness, life, care benefit) from one month old; also group accident cover for children with childminders and at school. · [vis.is/en/child-insurance](https://vis.is/en/child-insurance/) · **Discount:** Customer discount page lists child car-seat deals (Chicco, Nine Kids, Undrabörn, M Design, Barnabílstólar rental); Íslandsbanki customers with VÍS home insurance get loyalty terms. · *Has an 'Eignast barn' (having a baby) page and a dagforeldrar group accident product; VÍS advisors sit in some Íslandsbanki branches. Office in Akureyri.*
- ✓ ⚠ **TM (Tryggingamiðstöðin)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Child insurance from one month old paying disability, death and hospital daily-allowance benefits; can run to age 25 and renews automatically. · [tm.is/en/family/childinsurance](https://www.tm.is/en/family/childinsurance) · **Discount:** Fjölskylduleið bundle (child insurance plus home and other policies) gives up to 10% claim-free refund; Landsbankinn customers earn 1% Aukakrónur on premiums. · *Congenital conditions excluded; also sold through Landsbankinn branches and app (TM and Landsbankinn merged their Akureyri branch in 2025).*
- ✓ **Vörður tryggingar hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Two child policies, one needing no health screening; covers disability from illness or accident, type 1 diabetes and psychological help after trauma. · [vordur.is/tryggingar/lif-og-heilsa/barnatryggingar](https://www.vordur.is/tryggingar/lif-og-heilsa/barnatryggingar) · **Discount:** Customers get discounts on safety products via Vörður's discount page; Arion customers with Vörður home insurance can get up to 7% claim-free refund. · *Sold directly and through Arion banki; cover from one month old to 18, running to age 26. Branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Landsbankinn hf. (tryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Bank branches and app sell TM's child insurance and life cover as registered insurance agent, bundled in the Fjölskylduleið family package. · [landsbankinn.is/tryggingar/fjolskyldan](https://www.landsbankinn.is/tryggingar/fjolskyldan) · **Discount:** Vild/Aukakrónur: 1% Aukakrónur back when paying TM premiums by linked card; Fjölskylduleið up to 10% claim-free refund. · *Distributor for TM, not an insurer itself; branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Arion banki hf. (tryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Sells Vörður's two child insurance products (with and without health assessment) plus life and critical-illness cover through its branches and app. · [arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/tryggingar/tryggingar-lif-og-heilsa](https://www.arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/tryggingar/tryggingar-lif-og-heilsa) · **Discount:** Arion fríðindi members with Vörður home insurance can get up to 7% claim-free refund. · *Distributor under a service agreement with Vörður; branch in Akureyri.*

### G11-02 · Líf- og sjúkdómatryggingar foreldra — Parents' life & critical-illness insurance

**Problem:** Protecting the family's income  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6

*Market:* Same four insurers sell parents' life and critical-illness cover; banks Landsbankinn (TM) and Arion (Vörður) distribute. Children are usually covered automatically under a parent's policy (small death/illness benefit). VÍS lets cover be raised after a birth without a health assessment.

- ✓ **Sjóvá-Almennar tryggingar hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Life and critical-illness insurance for parents; children aged 3 months to 18 are covered automatically under a parent's critical-illness policy at half the benefit. · [sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/life-and-health/critical-illness-insurance](https://www.sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/life-and-health/critical-illness-insurance) · **Discount:** Stofn members get 10% off life and critical-illness insurance. · *25 listed illnesses in five categories; includes a fertility-preservation benefit for the insured or their child.*
- ✓ **VÍS tryggingar** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Life insurance to age 75 and critical-illness cover in five categories; sum insured can be raised when the family grows without a special health assessment. · [vis.is/en/life-and-critical-illness-insurance](https://vis.is/en/life-and-critical-illness-insurance/) · **Discount:** Online calculator tailored to families; VÍS discount page has partner offers; loyalty terms for Íslandsbanki customers holding VÍS home insurance. · *Child death benefit of ISK 2 million included in the life policy; free psychologist visits after a critical-illness payout.*
- ✓ ⚠ **TM (Tryggingamiðstöðin)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Life insurance (to age 75, tax-free lump sum) and critical-illness insurance with child illness cover; part of the Fjölskylduleið family bundle. · [tm.is/fjolskyldan/liftrygging/liftr-upplysingaskjal](https://www.tm.is/fjolskyldan/liftrygging/liftr-upplysingaskjal) · **Discount:** Fjölskylduleið: up to 10% claim-free refund plus access to TM's Velferðartorg wellbeing portal; Aukakrónur 1% back for Landsbankinn card payers. · *Also pays a death benefit if a child under 18 dies.*
- ✓ **Vörður tryggingar hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Life insurance for ages 18-62 (valid to 75, worldwide) and critical-illness cover; payout goes to spouse and children by default. · [vordur.is/lif-og-heilsa/liftrygging](https://vordur.is/lif-og-heilsa/liftrygging) · **Discount:** Customers get discounts on safety products via Vörður's discount page; Arion fríðindi tie-in. · *Pays up to ISK 2,000,000 if a child of the insured dies; sold directly and via Arion banki.*
- ✓ **Landsbankinn hf. (tryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Registered insurance agent selling TM life insurance (ages 18-64, valid to 75) and illness cover through branches and the app. · [landsbankinn.is/tryggingar/fjolskyldan/liftrygging](https://www.landsbankinn.is/tryggingar/fjolskyldan/liftrygging) · **Discount:** Vild/Aukakrónur: 3,000 loyalty points for taking out insurance and 1% Aukakrónur back on premiums paid by linked card. · *Distributor for TM, not an insurer itself.*
- ✓ **Arion banki hf. (tryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Sells Vörður life, critical-illness and disability insurance, including a cheaper life and illness package for ages 18-39. · [arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/tryggingar/tryggingar-lif-og-heilsa](https://www.arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/tryggingar/tryggingar-lif-og-heilsa) · **Discount:** Arion fríðindi loyalty tiers (tied to Vörður insurance and Frjálsi pension savings). · *Distributor under a service agreement with Vörður.*

### G11-03 · Fjölskyldu- og heimilistryggingar — Family & home insurance

**Problem:** Home contents and liability  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

*Market:* Four insurers sell home/contents + liability + family leisure-accident packages; banks Landsbankinn (TM), Arion (Vörður) and Íslandsbanki (VÍS) distribute. Sjóvá, VÍS, TM, Vörður, Landsbankinn and Arion have Akureyri offices; bundles unlock claim-free refunds and loyalty tiers.

- ✓ **Sjóvá-Almennar tryggingar hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Home insurance covering contents, liability (including damage by children under 10) and optional family leisure-time accident cover for spouse and children. · [sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/home-and-family/home-insurance](https://www.sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/home-and-family/home-insurance) · **Discount:** Stofn members get 10% off home insurance, claim-free refund and partner discounts (e.g. 20% on car seats at Fífa); deductible choice cuts premium up to 20%. · *Stofn entry requires a home policy plus two other insurance types; branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **VÍS tryggingar** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Home insurance bundling contents, liability, leisure-time accident, trauma counselling and legal-expense cover for the whole family; optional comprehensive contents and travel cover. · [vis.is/en/home-insurance](https://vis.is/en/home-insurance/) · **Discount:** VÍS discount page has partner deals (Chicco 20% car seats, Fífa, Nine Kids, Bílstólaleigan); Íslandsbanki customers with VÍS home insurance get Diamond loyalty terms. · *Formerly sold as 'F plús'; covers unmarried children living in the household; office in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **TM (Tryggingamiðstöðin)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Heimatrygging TM2, TM3, TM4: contents, liability and leisure accident cover; the top tier also pays for childcare during a hospital stay. · [tm.is/fjolskylduleid](https://www.tm.is/fjolskylduleid) · **Discount:** Fjölskylduleið bundle (home tier 2-4 plus two more policies) gives up to 10% claim-free refund and TM's Velferðartorg service; Aukakrónur for Landsbankinn card payers. · *Also sold through Landsbankinn; merged TM/Landsbankinn branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Vörður tryggingar hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Heimilisvernd 1-4: contents and liability for the household, tiers 2-4 add family accident cover; tier 3 is the popular family policy. · [vordur.is/en/insurance/family-and-property/home-protection-2](https://www.vordur.is/en/insurance/family-and-property/home-protection-2) · **Discount:** Customer discount page (Afslættir) has a 'Barnið' section with Chicco 20% on car seats, Fífa, Nine Kids 10% and Bílstólaleigan 30%; Arion customers up to 7% claim-free refund. · *Also sold via Arion banki; branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Landsbankinn hf. (tryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Sells TM's Heimatrygging and the Fjölskylduleið family package through branches, website and app. · [landsbankinn.is/tryggingar/fjolskyldan](https://www.landsbankinn.is/tryggingar/fjolskyldan) · **Discount:** Vild loyalty points, 1% Aukakrónur on insurance premiums paid by linked card, Fjölskylduleið refund up to 10% when claim-free. · *Distributor for TM; Aukakrónur has 200+ partners.*
- ✓ **Arion banki hf. (tryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Sells Vörður home and family insurance alongside banking; customers register for Arion fríðindi to get claim-free refunds. · [arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/tryggingar/tryggingar-lif-og-heilsa](https://www.arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/tryggingar/tryggingar-lif-og-heilsa) · **Discount:** Arion fríðindi: up to 7% refund on Vörður home insurance when claim-free for customers who bank with Arion. · *Distributor under a service agreement with Vörður; same-premises branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Íslandsbanki hf. (VÍS tryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netbanki og app) + Höfuðborgarsvæðið (VÍS ráðgjafar)) — Partnership with VÍS: home insurance bought through the bank app, with VÍS advisors in selected branches. · [islandsbanki.is/en/milestone/islandsbanki-and-vis](https://www.islandsbanki.is/en/milestone/islandsbanki-and-vis) · **Discount:** Home insurance with VÍS plus an active Íslandsbanki card gives automatic Diamond loyalty terms and personalised Fríða offers. · *Home insurance earns 25 loyalty points; VÍS advisors seen at a branch in Kópavogur.*

### G11-04 · Barnasparnaður og barnareikningar — Children's savings accounts

**Problem:** Saving for the child's future  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* Three big banks plus three regional savings banks. Landsbankinn (Framtíðargrunnur plus Sprotarnir children's programme with a 5,000 ISK newborn gift) and Arion (Young Saver account, locked to 18) have dedicated child savings; Íslandsbanki and the savings banks only show general savings accounts. indó is 18+ only.

- ✓ **Landsbankinn hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Sprotarnir programme for children 0-8 with Framtíðargrunnur savings account locked until age 18, standing orders, free Sproti learning app; accounts and cards for older kids. · [landsbankinn.is/en/personal/service-and-advice/young-customers/sprotarnir](https://www.landsbankinn.is/en/personal/service-and-advice/young-customers/sprotarnir) · **Discount:** Newborns of Varðan/Náman members can get a 5,000 ISK gift paid into a Framtíðargrunnur account; Aukakrónur/Vild loyalty for parents. · *Account paying the highest deposit rate at the time; Vasapeningar card for ages 9-18. Branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Arion banki hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Young Saver Account: inflation-indexed child savings until 18, opened by guardians in the Arion app; also funds for children and a free first debit card. · [arionbanki.is/en/individuals/savings-options/savings-accounts](https://www.arionbanki.is/en/individuals/savings-options/savings-accounts) · **Discount:** Arion fríðindi loyalty tiers for parents; savings goals reward children with cinema or ice-cream gift cards. · *Family view in the app shows children's finances; 'Having a Baby' article on the site. Branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Íslandsbanki hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netbanki og app)) — Online sign-up lets parents open savings accounts for themselves and their children; Loyalty Savings account pays higher rates with loyalty status. · [islandsbanki.is/is/vara/sparnadur/velkomin-i-hopinn](https://www.islandsbanki.is/is/vara/sparnadur/velkomin-i-hopinn) · **Discount:** Íslandsbanki loyalty service (Fríða offers, points for savings and funds) for parents. · *No dedicated child savings product seen on the site; confirm before offering a child-specific slot.*
- ✓ **Smári Sparisjóður** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland + Vestfirðir) — Savings bank (merger of Höfðhverfinga and Strandamanna) with savings accounts and regular savings plan for individuals; branches in Akureyri, Grenivík and Hólmavík. · [spar.is/smari-sparisjodur](https://www.spar.is/smari-sparisjodur) · *No child-specific account seen on site; Lífsval supplementary pension product. Akureyri office. Also listed as: Sparisjóður Þingeyinga. Also listed as: Sparisjóður Austurlands.*

### G11-05 · Fjárfestingar og sjóðir fyrir börn — Investment funds for kids

**Problem:** Longer-term saving  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Only Stefnir (Arion) has a dedicated children's fund-savings page; Landsbréf, Íslandssjóðir, Íslensk verðbréf and Akta are general fund houses where parents can invest for a child but no child product was seen. Kvika showed no retail child offering.

- ✓ **Stefnir hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netþjónusta)) — Children's fund savings: guardians set up monthly or one-off fund purchases in a child's name via the Arion app; no purchase fee and no minimum. · [stefnir.is/sjodir/vidskipti-med-sjodi-stefnir/sparnadur-barna](https://www.stefnir.is/sjodir/vidskipti-med-sjodi-stefnir/sparnadur-barna) · **Discount:** Confirmation-age matching contribution (6,000 ISK on a 30,000 ISK deposit, plus 5% above, 2026 offer); Arion group benefits. · *Arion subsidiary; child can follow the savings from age 9, trades alone at 18.*
- ✓ **Landsbréf hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri (via Landsbankinn)) — Landsbankinn's fund company: equity, bond and mixed funds with monthly subscriptions without purchase commission; parents can arrange funds for children through the bank. · [landsbref.is](https://www.landsbref.is) · **Discount:** 25% off purchase commission when buying in the app; Landsbankinn pays a 6,000 ISK confirmation-age contribution for deposits in funds. · *No child-specific fund seen; children's funds are set up by contacting Landsbankinn.*
- ✓ **Íslandssjóðir hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (via Íslandsbanki app)) — Íslandsbanki's fund company with 23 retail funds; monthly subscriptions from 5,000 ISK with no purchase cost through online banking. · [islandssjodir.is](https://www.islandssjodir.is) · **Discount:** 25% off purchase cost in the app; loyalty-service points for fund subscriptions. · *No child-specific offering seen on site.*
- ✓ **Íslensk verðbréf hf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri) — Independent fund and asset manager with fund subscriptions for small regular amounts; offices in Reykjavík and Akureyri. · [iv.is](https://iv.is) · **Discount:** Reduced brokerage fees on subscription savings and fund investments for VÍS customers. · *No child-specific savings product seen on site.*
- ✓ **Akta sjóðir hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netþjónusta)) — Fund and asset manager serving individuals, families, companies and professional investors with a range of funds. · [akta.is](https://www.akta.is) · *No monthly-savings or child-specific product seen on site.*

### G11-06 · Lífeyris- og fjármálaráðgjöf fyrir fjölskyldur — Pension & family financial planning

**Problem:** Money planning after the baby arrives  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 14

*Market:* Mandatory pension saving is by law, so the real competition is for séreign (supplementary) savings, advice and member loans. About ten open or regional pension funds plus three banks and Aurbjörg; Stapi has an Akureyri office. More closed or occupational funds exist (bankamanna, FÍA, Rangæinga, bænda, Vestmannaeyja, SL).

- ✓ **Gildi lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund open to all; pays spouse and child pensions, offers five supplementary-savings investment options and bookable advice appointments. · [gildi.is](https://www.gildi.is/) · *Office in Reykjavík; anyone may pay in.*
- ✓ **Lífeyrissjóður verzlunarmanna (LV)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund with child and spouse pensions, supplementary savings, advisor bookings, guidance on family changes and a loan payment pause during parental leave. · [live.is](https://www.live.is/) · *Office in Reykjavík; open to VR-covered employees and anyone who can choose a fund.*
- ✓ **Frjálsi lífeyrissjóðurinn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund focused on supplementary pension savings, including tilgreind séreign; savings can be put toward a home loan; education videos and FAQ. · [frjalsi.is](https://www.frjalsi.is/) · **Discount:** Supplementary savings count toward Arion fríðindi loyalty tiers. · *Linked to Arion banki; announced mergers with Lífeyrissjóður bænda and LTFÍ.*
- ✓ **Almenni Lífsverk lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund paying child pension at twice the legal minimum, with disability cover, seven séreign investment options and online advisor appointments. · [almenni-lifsverk.is](https://www.almenni-lifsverk.is/) · *Open to anyone who can choose a fund.*
- ✓ **Birta lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund with child pension, supplementary savings (including toward a home loan) and bookable advice conversations. · [birta.is](https://www.birta.is/) · *Membership partly tied to certain unions or lack of mandatory membership elsewhere.*
- ✓ **Stapi lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Norðurland + Austurland (skrifstofa á Akureyri)) — Regional pension fund for wage earners in North and East Iceland with child and spouse pension, séreign options, loans and bookable advice. · [stapi.is](https://www.stapi.is/) · *Offices in Akureyri and Neskaupstaður.*
- ✓ **Festa lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes + Vesturland + Suðurland) — Regional pension fund with lifelong, spouse, child and disability pension, a separate supplementary savings fund, calculators and member loans. · [festa.is](https://festa.is/) · *Offices in Reykjanesbær, Akranes and Selfoss.*
- ✓ **Íslenski lífeyrissjóðurinn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Independent pension fund run day-to-day by Landsbankinn; mandatory and supplementary savings with 2% employer match, applied for in the Landsbankinn app. · [landsbankinn.is/islenski-lifeyrissjodurinn](https://www.landsbankinn.is/islenski-lifeyrissjodurinn) · *Operating fee 0.22% a year; open to those who can choose a fund.*
- ✓ **Lífeyrissjóður starfsmanna ríkisins (LSR)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund for state employees with child and spouse pension, séreign with no fees plus 2% employer match, member mortgages and advisors. · [lsr.is](https://www.lsr.is/) · *Public-sector members only; reception in Reykjavík.*
- ✓ **Brú lífeyrissjóður starfsmanna sveitarfélaga** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund for municipal employees offering accident pension, supplementary savings, division of pension rights between spouses and bookable conversations. · [lifbru.is](https://www.lifbru.is/) · *Office in Kópavogur; V-deild is open to all.*
- ✓ **Landsbankinn hf. (fjármálaráðgjöf)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Bank advice by phone or branch on savings goals, home financing and pensions; guardians can sign for children remotely; family sharing view in the app. · [landsbankinn.is/en/personal/service-and-advice/welcome-to-landsbankinn](https://www.landsbankinn.is/en/personal/service-and-advice/welcome-to-landsbankinn) · **Discount:** Vild/Aukakrónur loyalty programme; Fjölskylduleið insurance refund up to 10%. · *Page does not mention parental-leave specific help.*
- ✓ **Arion banki hf. (fjármálaráðgjöf)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Having-a-baby guide: build a reserve, review insurance, child savings and advice meetings in person, by phone or in English. · [arionbanki.is/en/individuals/family/having-a-baby](https://www.arionbanki.is/en/individuals/family/having-a-baby) · **Discount:** Arion fríðindi loyalty tiers; up to 7% Vörður claim-free refund. · *Branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Íslandsbanki hf. (ráðgjöf)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netbanki og app)) — Life-milestone guides (including having children), calculators, a financial-education section and bookable advisor appointments. · [islandsbanki.is/is/grein/timamot](https://www.islandsbanki.is/is/grein/timamot) · **Discount:** Loyalty service with Fríða offers and VÍS insurance terms. · *No parent-specific article seen.*
- ✓ **Aurbjörg ehf.** (Vettvangur/app · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Personal-finance app: daily budget overview, comparisons of savings accounts and mortgages, refinancing and pension optimisers. · [aurbjorg.is](https://aurbjorg.is) · *Aurbjörg Plús subscription 14,990 ISK a year; no family plan seen.*

### G11-07 · Heimilisbókhald og fjárhagsáætlun í fæðingarorlofi — Household budgeting during parental leave

**Problem:** Income drops during leave  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 12

*Market:* Leave-specific help comes from the three big banks (halved mortgage payments during leave) and pension funds LV, Lífbrú and Almenni (loan pauses that name parental leave); Frjálsi offers general 12-month deferrals. Budgeting tools are Aurbjörg and indó savings jars (Meniga is B2B only); regional savings banks show no leave-specific offer.

- ✓ **Arion banki hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Having-a-baby page: build a reserve before income drops, reduce mortgage payments by up to half during parental leave, and book advice meetings (English available). · [arionbanki.is/en/individuals/family/having-a-baby](https://www.arionbanki.is/en/individuals/family/having-a-baby) · **Discount:** Arion fríðindi loyalty tiers; no new-parent discount mentioned. · *Unpaid mortgage amount is added to the principal; branch in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Lífeyrissjóður verzlunarmanna (LV)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund offering a payment pause on member loans during parental leave, plus advisor bookings and guidance on changes in the family. · [live.is](https://www.live.is/) · *Loan pause 'Greiðsluhlé í fæðingarorlofi' listed under loans; terms not detailed on the page.*
- ✓ **Aurbjörg ehf.** (Vettvangur/app · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Budgeting app showing income, spending and what remains each month, plus savings, mortgage and refinancing tools. · [aurbjorg.is](https://aurbjorg.is) · *Aurbjörg Plús costs 14,990 ISK a year; gift cards available.*
- ✓ **Landsbankinn hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Bank advice by phone or branch; savings goals and family sharing view in the app help plan household money on a lower income. · [landsbankinn.is/en/personal/service-and-advice/welcome-to-landsbankinn](https://www.landsbankinn.is/en/personal/service-and-advice/welcome-to-landsbankinn) · **Discount:** Vild/Aukakrónur loyalty programme. · *No parental-leave specific page found.*
- ✓ **Íslandsbanki hf.** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netbanki og app)) — Calculators, regular-saving guide and bookable advisors under its life-milestones section, which names having children as a time to review finances. · [islandsbanki.is/is/grein/timamot](https://www.islandsbanki.is/is/grein/timamot) · **Discount:** Loyalty service with Fríða offers. · *No parental-leave specific article seen.*
- ✓ **Lífbrú (BRÚ lífeyrissjóður)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund for municipal employees; borrowing members on parental leave can request a 3-12 month loan-payment deferral with a Parental Leave Fund statement. · [lifbru.is/is/lan/breytingar-a-lani/frestun-a-greidslum-1](https://www.lifbru.is/is/lan/breytingar-a-lani/frestun-a-greidslum-1) · *Deferral form lists parental leave as a reason; terms per the fund's loan rules.*
- ✓ **Almenni lífeyrissjóðurinn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund offering a payment pause (greiðsluhlé) on member loans that lists parental leave among its reasons; advisers can be booked online. · [almenni-lifsverk.is/lanabreytingar/greidsluerfidleikar](https://www.almenni-lifsverk.is/lanabreytingar/greidsluerfidleikar) · *Pause granted up to six months at a time; study, illness and job change are also listed.*
- ✓ **Frjálsi lífeyrissjóðurinn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund whose loan page offers partial or full payment deferral for up to 12 months and lower payments when income drops. · [frjalsi.is/lan](https://www.frjalsi.is/lan/) · *Parental leave not named as a reason; general deferral rules only.*
- ✓ **indó sparisjóður** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (aðeins í appi)) — App-only savings bank with debit card, customisable savings jars (sparibaukar) with goals and shared group jars; no branches. · [indo.is](https://indo.is) · *No parental-leave content seen; needs electronic ID and age 18+.*
- ✓ **Sparisjóðurinn (Sparisjóður Austurlands, Sparisjóður Þingeyinga, Smári sparisjóður)** (Þjónusta · Austurland + Norðurland + Strandir) — Shared site of three regional savings banks with online banking and loan portals for individuals; no parental-leave or budgeting content seen. · [spar.is](https://www.spar.is/) · *Smári is the merger of Sparisjóður Höfðhverfinga and Sparisjóður Strandamanna (June 2026 news item); ask about leave-related payment reductions.*
- ✓ **Stapi lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland + Austurland) — Pension fund with Akureyri and Neskaupstaður offices; its FAQ explains pension contributions during parental leave and optional supplementary savings. · [stapi.is/is/um-sjodinn/spurt-og-svarad/nyjir-sjodfelagar](https://www.stapi.is/is/um-sjodinn/spurt-og-svarad/nyjir-sjodfelagar) · *No leave-specific loan pause seen; has a payment-difficulty section under loans.*
- ✓ **Auratal (Aurabankinn)** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt (á netinu)) — Icelandic personal-finance education brand with free explainers on household money, investing and mortgages, short videos and a mailing list. · [auratal.is](https://auratal.is/) · *Founded 2023; no budgeting tool or parental-leave content seen.*

### G11-08 · Skattframtal og barnabætur — Tax return & child-benefit help

**Problem:** Claiming benefits correctly  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 16

*Market:* Many small bookkeeping firms prepare individual returns, mostly in the capital area; regional: AÓ Bókhald and Enor (Akureyri), Skrifstofuþjónusta Austurlands (Egilsstaðir), Bókhaldsstofa Vesturlands (Stykkishólmur, countrywide), Grimma (Suðurnes). Individual-return fees seen about 39,000-54,000 kr excl./incl. VAT; none mention barnabætur, since Skatturinn pre-fills the return and sets the benefit. Big-four firms focus on companies and were left out.

- ✓ **Enor ehf.** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland + Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Accounting and advisory firm listing tax-return preparation (skattframtalsgerð) and tax advice; offices in Akureyri, Húsavík and Reykjavík. · [enor.is](https://www.enor.is) · *Services mostly aimed at companies; confirm that private returns and barnabætur checks are offered before approaching.*
- ✓ **Framtalsþjónustan** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Tax-return service for individuals, couples, cohabiting partners, contractors and estates; also tax advice, replies to tax authority queries and inheritance-tax reports. · [framtalsthjonustan.is/index.php/skattframtal](https://framtalsthjonustan.is/index.php/skattframtal/) · *Office in Ármúli, Reykjavík; no mention of barnabætur or parental leave.*
- ✓ **Skatt bókhaldsstofa ehf** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Bookkeeping firm preparing individual and contractor tax returns for a fixed fee (about 54,000 kr incl. VAT for the 2027 filing season). · [skatt.is/portfolio-item/skattframtaleinstaklinga](https://skatt.is/portfolio-item/skattframtaleinstaklinga/) · *Cheaper rate (12,500 kr) for young people living with parents; Kópavogur office.*
- ✓ **Métis bókhald & ráðgjöf ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Bookkeeping firm with a published price list for individual tax returns: base fee 39,000 kr, or 63,000 kr when pay is self-calculated (excl. VAT). · [metisbokhald.com/ver%C3%B0skr%C3%A1](https://www.metisbokhald.com/ver%C3%B0skr%C3%A1) · *Hafnarfjörður firm.*
- ✓ **AÓ Bókhald ehf** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri bookkeeping firm preparing tax returns for individuals, married couples and cohabiting partners, plus companies and sole traders. · [aobokhald.is/skattskil](https://aobokhald.is/skattskil/) · *Office in Akureyri (Vaðlatún).*
- ✓ **Skrifstofuþjónusta Austurlands** (Þjónusta · Austurland) — East Iceland office-services firm preparing tax returns for individuals and companies, plus bookkeeping, payroll and business consulting. · [skrifa.is](https://www.skrifa.is/) · *Based in Egilsstaðir; says it works from five locations in the East.*
- ✓ **Bókhaldsstofa Vesturlands** (Þjónusta · Vesturland + landið allt (fjarþjónusta)) — Accounting firm preparing tax returns for individuals with and without their own business; serves clients anywhere in Iceland. · [bokvest.is](https://bokvest.is/) · *Office in Stykkishólmur.*
- ○ ⚠ **Grimma** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes) — Family-run bookkeeping service making tax returns for companies and individuals, with personal advice on filing. · [grimma.is](https://grimma.is/) · *Office at Ásbrú, Reykjanesbær; site resets plain HTTP clients such as curl but loads in a browser-style fetch. [auto] website did not respond in the final check.*
- ✓ **3 Skref ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Bookkeeping and consulting firm preparing tax returns for individuals with or without a business, plus company accounts. · [3skref.is](https://www.3skref.is/) · *Office in Reykjavík (Stangarhylur).*
- ✓ **Bókarinn minn (X-us Viðskiptalausnir ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Bookkeeping service that also prepares individuals' tax returns, with or without business activity. · [bokarinnminn.is/thjonusta](https://bokarinnminn.is/thjonusta/) · *Office in Reykjavík (Álfabakki).*
- ✓ **Bókhald.is** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Bookkeeping service whose list includes making tax returns for individuals, alongside bookkeeping in common accounting systems. · [bokhald.is](https://bokhald.is/) · *Office in Skeifan, Reykjavík.*
- ✓ **Endurskoðun.is** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Accounting and audit firm in Hafnarfjörður handling tax returns for companies and individuals, plus bookkeeping, payroll and advice. · [endurskodun.is/thjonusta.html](https://www.endurskodun.is/thjonusta.html) · *Office at Fjarðargata, Hafnarfjörður.*

### G11-09 · Raðgreiðslur og greiðsludreifing — Instalment payments / buy-now-pay-later

**Problem:** Paying for large baby purchases  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Two dedicated pay-later apps (Netgíró, Pei) plus bank credit-card payment splitting (Landsbankinn up to 36 months, Arion up to 18). Íslandsbanki credit cards exist but instalment terms were not verified. Aur and Síminn Pay showed no consumer instalments.

- ✓ **Netgíró ehf.** (Vettvangur/app · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Buy-now-pay-later app used online and in stores; purchases collect on one monthly account that can be split into several payments. · [netgiro.is](https://www.netgiro.is) · *Monthly fee 695 ISK in months of use; thousands of partner stores, no baby stores named on the page.*
- ✓ **Pei (Greiðslumiðlun ehf.)** (Vettvangur/app · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Pay-later service with 14-60 day interest-free deferral and instalment plans up to several years for purchases of 30,000 ISK or more. · [pei.is](https://www.pei.is) · *Over 1,500 merchants incl. BYKO, Húsgagnahöllin, Hekla; no baby stores named.*
- ✓ **Landsbankinn hf. (greiðsludreifing)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Credit-card bills can be split into up to 36 monthly instalments in the app or via the bank, for large or unexpected expenses. · [landsbankinn.is/en/personal/cards/credit-cards](https://www.landsbankinn.is/en/personal/cards/credit-cards) · **Discount:** Credit cards earn 2-5 Aukakrónur per 1,000 ISK spent, usable at 200+ partners. · *Annual card fee from 3,700 ISK; Plúskort has no annual fee.*
- ✓ **Arion banki hf. (greiðsludreifing)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Credit-card payment distribution lets cardholders spread the balance over up to 18 months, minimum 30,000 ISK and 10% of the bill. · [arionbanki.is/en/individuals/individuals-cards/credit-cards](https://www.arionbanki.is/en/individuals/individuals-cards/credit-cards) · **Discount:** Arion fríðindi loyalty tiers. · *Up to 10 distributions in total; fees follow the bank's price list.*
- ○ **Íslandsbanki hf. / Kreditkort** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netbanki og app)) — Bank credit cards (kreditkort.is redirects to Íslandsbanki's card pages); instalment option likely but not confirmed on the pages reached. · [islandsbanki.is/is](https://www.islandsbanki.is/is) · **Discount:** Loyalty service with Fríða offers. · *Card page returned an error; verify instalment terms directly.*

### G11-10 · Fríðindakerfi og afsláttarklúbbar — Loyalty & discount clubs for families

**Problem:** Saving money on everything  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Every large bank and insurer runs a loyalty or partner-discount scheme; Sjóvá's Stofn, VÍS and Vörður list child car-seat and baby-product partners (Chicco, Fífa, Nine Kids, Undrabörn, Bílstólaleigan). Aukakrónur is the biggest payment-card scheme (200+ partners). Einkaklúbburinn and Moggaklúbburinn were only seen in search listings.

- ✓ **Stofn (Sjóvá)** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Sjóvá customer club: 10% off insurance, yearly claim-free refund, free roadside assistance and partner discounts, including child car seats and children's theatre tickets. · [sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/stofn-loyalty-program/offers-and-benefits](https://sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/stofn-loyalty-program/offers-and-benefits) · **Discount:** 20% car seats at AB varahlutir, Bílanaust (Axkid) and Fífa, 10% Nine Kids; Fífa Clippasafe 15%; Lotta summer shows 2-for-1, children 2 and under free. · *Needs a home policy plus two other insurance types; Akureyri partner Brimborg (tires).*
- ✓ **Aukakrónur / Vild (Landsbankinn)** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Landsbankinn's reward currency earned on card spending and spendable at over 200 partners; Vild levels add extra rewards and loan perks. · [landsbankinn.is/en/personal/cards-and-payments/aukakronur](https://landsbankinn.is/en/personal/cards-and-payments/aukakronur) · **Discount:** 1 Aukakróna = 1 ISK at partners; 1% back on TM premiums; Fjölskylduleið refund up to 10%. · *About 75,000 participating customers; new Vild reward system announced June 2026; TM joined as partner Jan 2026.*
- ✓ **Arion fríðindi** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt + Akureyri) — Arion group loyalty tiers rewarding customers who use bank, Vörður insurance, Frjálsi pension and Stefnir funds; register in the Arion app. · [arionbanki.is/en/articles/arion-rewards](https://www.arionbanki.is/en/articles/arion-rewards) · **Discount:** Up to 7% claim-free refund on Vörður home insurance; better terms at higher tiers. · *Einkaklúbburinn app (free for Arion customers) is a separate discount club.*
- ✓ **Íslandsbanki loyalty service (Fríða)** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt (app)) — App-based points programme with five tiers; more products and savings raise status and unlock Loyalty Savings rates and personalised Fríða offers. · [islandsbanki.is/en/landing/services/our-loyalty-services](https://www.islandsbanki.is/en/landing/services/our-loyalty-services) · **Discount:** VÍS home insurance earns 25 points and keeps 'Diamond' terms on VÍS insurance. · *No family-specific benefits described on the page.*
- ✓ **VÍS afslættir og gjafir** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt + Akureyri) — VÍS customer partner discounts activated in the app, including child car seats, car-seat rental, helmets, tires and home-safety products. · [vis.is/en/discounts-and-gifts](https://vis.is/en/discounts-and-gifts) · **Discount:** Child-seat deals with Chicco, Nine Kids, Undrabörn, M Design (Junama) and Barnabílstólar rental; reduced fees at Íslensk verðbréf. · *Partners include Akureyri's Höldur (car wash); gifts are safety items, none child-specific.*

### G11-N1 · Meðgöngutrygging — Pregnancy & birth insurance *(new — added during research)*

**Problem:** What if there are complications in pregnancy or at birth  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1

*Market:* Only Sjóvá sells a standalone pregnancy insurance (one-off 30,000 ISK, buy until week 32); no other insurer's equivalent was found. Its partner perks (Móðurást, Lyfja, 9 mánuðir, Kírópraktor Lindir) show who else courts expecting parents.

- ✓ **Sjóvá-Almennar tryggingar hf. (Meðgöngutrygging)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt + Akureyri) — One-off pregnancy insurance for mother and baby: hospital stays, complications such as emergency C-section, congenital conditions found in year one, and trauma therapy. · [sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/life-and-health/pregnancy-insurance](https://www.sjova.is/en/insurance/individuals/life-and-health/pregnancy-insurance) · **Discount:** Buyers get partner discounts: Móðurást 15-25% on baby clothes and feeding products, Lyfja 15% baby products, 9 mánuðir 15% on 3D ultrasounds and classes, Kírópraktor Lindir 25%. · *Benefits 500,000-1,500,000 ISK; same price for multiple births and high-risk pregnancies; takes effect in week 17; free home-safety gift while supplies last.*

## G12 · Lögfræði og réttindi — Legal & rights

### G12-01 · Fjölskyldulögfræði: forsjá, umgengni og meðlag — Family law (custody, access, child support)

**Problem:** Separation and custody questions  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* Four firms confirmed on their own pages: OPUS (child law with custody, contact and child support), Pacta (custody and divorce advice; Reykjavík, Akureyri, Blönduós), Sókn (Egilsstaðir) and Lögfræðistofa Reykjavíkur (family matters, no custody wording). Found by direct site checks only (web search budget ran out); many more Icelandic law firms exist, most big ones focus on business clients. Public sýslumenn handle statutory mediation and custody decisions (G21).

- ✓ **OPUS lögmenn ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Law firm with a child-law practice covering custody, contact, residence, child support and paternity, plus divorce, property division and dissolution of cohabitation. · [opus.is/einstaklingar](https://www.opus.is/einstaklingar/) · *Also a dedicated personal-injury page; Icelandic, English, Polish and Romanian site versions.*
- ✓ **Pacta lögmenn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Norðurland) — Law firm with an individuals section offering advice on custody matters, divorce and property-division agreements. · [pacta.is/logfraedithjonusta-fyrir-einstaklinga](https://pacta.is/logfraedithjonusta-fyrir-einstaklinga/) · *Offices in Reykjavík, Akureyri and Blönduós; employment law not listed for individuals.*

*Market:* Many firms advertise wills to private clients, often with published fixed fees of roughly 50,000-76,000 kr (JURALIS, Bonafide, Cicero, ESJA, Porto, Lögfræðiþjónusta Hafnarfjarðar); regional: Lögmenn Suðurlandi (Selfoss), Lögmenn Norðurlandi and Almenna lögþjónustan (Akureyri), Delikt (Akranes), plus Pacta and Sókn from the first pass. None mention naming a guardian for children; many more firms exist and business-law firms (LEX, Landslög, LOGOS) were left out.

### G12-04 · Sáttamiðlun — Family mediation

**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1

*Market:* Only Sáttamiðlun ehf. in Reykjavík was confirmed as a private mediation provider with a couple, divorce and custody mediation service. Sýslumenn (district commissioners) run the statutory mediation in custody disputes (public, G21); some individual mediators exist but are not listed here. Web search budget ran out, so smaller providers may be missing.
