### G12-05 · Fæðingarorlof og réttindi á vinnumarkaði — Parental-leave & employment-rights law

**Problem:** Employer disputes around leave  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Few firms advertise employment law for employees. Sókn (Egilsstaðir), LEX and Lagastoð list employment law (LEX and Lagastoð mainly for business clients) and VR (union) publishes members' parental-leave rights incl. return to work and dismissal protection. Fæðingarorlofssjóður is public (G21). Web search budget ran out.

- ○ ⚠ **Sókn Lögmannsstofa ehf.** (Þjónusta · Austurland (Egilsstaðir)) — East Iceland law firm listing employment law and labour-market matters among its practice areas. · [sokn.is/index.php/starfssvidh](https://www.sokn.is/index.php/starfssvidh) · *Parental leave not named explicitly; single office in Egilsstaðir. [auto] website did not respond in the final check.*
- ✓ **LEX lögmannsstofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland (Selfoss)) — Law firm listing employment and staff law (vinnuréttar- og starfsmannamál) alongside damages, insurance and property law; offices in Reykjavík and Selfoss. · [lex.is](https://www.lex.is) · *Employment law is filed under business services, so mainly employer-side; no parental-leave wording.*
- ✓ **Lagastoð lögfræðiþjónusta** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Law firm with an Employment practice area that says it advises individuals, families, businesses and public bodies. · [lagastod.is](https://www.lagastod.is) · *Office in Reykjavík; practice list is mostly corporate and no parental-leave wording.*
- ✓ **VR stéttarfélag** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Union explaining members' parental-leave rights: return to former job, protection from dismissal and accrued service rights; members can contact VR with questions. · [vr.is/kjaramal/orlofsrettur/faedingarorlof-og-foreldraorlof](https://www.vr.is/kjaramal/orlofsrettur/faedingarorlof-og-foreldraorlof/) · *Union for commerce and office workers; page does not describe dispute support; sjúkrasjóður has a 'Veikindi og slys barna' page.*

### G12-06 · Íslenskunám, túlkun og þýðingar fyrir erlenda foreldra — Icelandic courses, interpreting & translation for foreign parents

**Problem:** Language barrier in healthcare and paperwork  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Many Icelandic-course providers (lifelong-learning centres around the country plus private schools); Mímir, Múltí Kúltí, Tin Can Factory, Retor and Saga Akademía are the main private ones, SÍMEY covers Akureyri. None advertise parent-specific classes or childcare. No commercial interpreting agency was verified: insured patients get free healthcare interpreting via clinics (public) and certified translators are individuals.

- ✓ **Mímir símenntun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + landið allt (fjarnám)) — Adult-education centre with a 'Study Icelandic' course category; on-site classes in Reykjavík and some distance learning. · [mimir.is](https://www.mimir.is) · *Course details were not visible on the page reached; no childcare mentioned.*
- ✓ **Múltí Kúltí** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + landið allt (netnám)) — Language centre with Icelandic courses on site in Reykjavík and Kópavogur and online, in 7-week terms. · [multikulti.is](https://multikulti.is/) · **Discount:** Courses fully sponsored for job seekers and refugees registered with Vinnumálastofnun and for Reykjavíkurborg employees. · *Level 2 course listed at 59,500 ISK; no parent or childcare options mentioned.*
- ✓ **The Tin Can Factory (Dósaverksmiðjan)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + landið allt (netnám)) — Icelandic school with levels 1-8 (A1-B2), speaking courses and online options; placement test before enrolling. · [dosaverksmidjan.is/en/home-en](https://dosaverksmidjan.is/en/home-en/) · **Discount:** Larger unions usually reimburse at least 75% of course fees; unemployed students can use two courses a year billed to Vinnumálastofnun. · *Also teaches other languages; no childcare mentioned.*
- ✓ **Retor fræðsla** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — School offering Icelandic for Polish speakers and Icelandic for English speakers in Kópavogur; evening opening hours. · [retor.is/namskeid](https://retor.is/namskeid/) · *Format, levels and prices not stated on the page.*
- ✓ **Saga Akademía** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes + landið allt (fjarnám)) — Ministry-accredited Icelandic courses for foreigners in small groups, intensive or private, in Reykjanesbær and by Teams or Zoom. · [sagaakademia.is/en/home-page](https://www.sagaakademia.is/en/home-page/) · *Also teaches English, Spanish and Polish; prices on a linked page.*
- ✓ **SÍMEY - Símenntunarmiðstöð Eyjafjarðar** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri adult-education centre listing 'Íslenska sem annað mál' (Icelandic as a second language) among its courses. · [simey.is](https://www.simey.is) · *Prices and union funding not shown on the page; Akureyri.*
- ✓ **MSS - Miðstöð símenntunar á Suðurnesjum** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes) — Reykjanesbær adult-education centre with an Íslenskunámskeið (Icelandic courses) section on its site. · [mss.is](https://www.mss.is) · *Courses not detailed on the page reached; tuition fees on a separate page; unions may fund courses.*
- ✓ **Fræðslunetið - símenntun á Suðurlandi** (Þjónusta · Suðurland) — Icelandic for foreigners in five levels (Íslenska 1-5, A1 to B1); offices in Selfoss, Hvolsvöllur and Höfn. · [fraedslunetid.is/islenska-fyrir-utlendinga-2](https://fraedslunetid.is/islenska-fyrir-utlendinga-2/) · *Course towns, online format and prices not stated.*
- ✓ **Austurbrú** (Þjónusta · Austurland) — East Iceland development and learning centre with an 'Icelandic as a second language' course category; office in Egilsstaðir. · [austurbru.is/namsflokkur/islenska-sem-annad-mal](https://austurbru.is/namsflokkur/islenska-sem-annad-mal/) · *Details of locations and prices not shown on the page reached.*
- ✓ **LÓA Language School** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netnám)) — Online Icelandic for beginners (Íslenska 1 and 2), tutoring and private lessons, plus free mini-courses. · [loalanguageschool.is/products](https://www.loalanguageschool.is/products) · *Íslenska 1 and 2 cost 256 EUR each; all products showed as out of stock when checked.*
- ○ **Fræðslumiðstöð Vestfjarða** (Þjónusta · Vestfirðir) — Westfjords learning centre in Ísafjörður listed by the Multicultural Centre as an Icelandic-course provider. · [frmst.is](https://www.frmst.is/) · *Not checked on its own site; listed on the Fjölmenningarsetur course directory.*
- ✓ **Icelandic Online (Háskóli Íslands)** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt (netnám)) — Free online Icelandic courses run by the University of Iceland, including a Survival course for beginners; paid tutor-supported PLUS course. · [icelandiconline.com](https://icelandiconline.com) · *University service, not a buyer; no parent-specific content found.*

## G13 · Húsnæði og flutningar — Housing & moving

### G13-01 · Fasteignasölur — Real-estate agents (upsizing)

**Problem:** The flat is too small now  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 15

*Market:* Very large market: about 150 agencies are listed on fasteignir.visir.is. These 15 are well-known ones with a working own site; many more exist (e.g. Kjöreign, Fasteignamarkaðurinn, Torg, Fasteignamiðstöðin, and Akureyri agencies such as Hvammur, Eignaver, Nordik, FS Fasteignir). Upsizing families are a core customer group; several agencies advertise free valuations.

- ✓ **Lind fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur agency selling homes and new builds; free, no-obligation valuation for families wanting to sell and move to a bigger home. · [fastlind.is](https://fastlind.is/) · *Office in Bæjarlind, Kópavogur; fee schedule published online; sells properties across the capital area*
- ✓ **Ás fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Hafnarfjörður agency trading since 1988; sells flats, houses and new builds, including shared-equity-loan developments, for families upsizing. · [as.is/soluskra](https://www.as.is/soluskra) · *Office on Fjarðargata, Hafnarfjörður*
- ✓ **Eignamiðlun** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Established Reykjavik agency with a large sales register of flats, houses and summer houses; valuation and full buying/selling service. · [eignamidlun.is](https://www.eignamidlun.is/) · *Office on Grensásvegur, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Trausti fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavik agency with published fee schedule and free no-obligation valuation; sells homes in the capital area and elsewhere. · [trausti.is](https://trausti.is/) · *Office in Vegmúli, Reykjavik; fee page states commission for exclusive sales*
- ✓ **Borgir fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland) — Agency founded in 1994 selling homes across Iceland, with many listings in the south and the capital area; promotes fair commission. · [borgir.is/soluskra](https://www.borgir.is/soluskra/) · *Office on Suðurlandsbraut, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Domusnova fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland + Vesturland) — One of the larger agencies; sells homes in Reykjavik, Kópavogur, Mosfellsbær, Selfoss and elsewhere, with valuation service for upsizing families. · [domusnova.is](https://www.domusnova.is) · *Head office Hlíðasmári, Kópavogur; branches in Selfoss and Akranes*
- ✓ **RE/MAX Ísland** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland) — Franchise agency with free valuations and listings from Reykjavik to Selfoss and Þorlákshöfn, including new builds and open-house schedule. · [remax.is](https://www.remax.is) · *Office in Skeifan, Reykjavik; international brand*
- ○ ⚠ **Miklaborg fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavik agency with sales register, new-build sales and free valuations for owners selling an outgrown flat. · [miklaborg.is](https://www.miklaborg.is) · *Office on Hallgerðargata, Reykjavik [auto] website did not respond in the final check.*
- ✓ **Valhöll fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Agency founded in 1995 with listings in Reykjavik, Kópavogur, Mosfellsbær, Hafnarfjörður and Reykjanesbær; buying, selling and valuations. · [valholl.is](https://www.valholl.is) · *Offices in Síðumúli, Reykjavik and Reykjanesbær; new owners since 2023*
- ✓ **Hraunhamar fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Hafnarfjörður agency selling homes, commercial units and new builds; valuations and help with shared-equity loans (hlutdeildarlán). · [hraunhamar.is](https://www.hraunhamar.is) · *Office on Bæjarhraun, Hafnarfjörður*
- ✓ **Gimli fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland + Vesturland) — Reykjavik agency selling flats, houses, new builds, plots and summer-house lots in the capital area and regional towns. · [gimli.is](https://www.gimli.is) · *Office in Skipholt, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Aldir fasteignir** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur agency with over 20 licensed agents, formed by merging Landmark and Heimili; full property buying and selling service. · [aldirfasteignir.is](https://www.aldirfasteignir.is/) · *Landmark fasteignamiðlun and Heimili fasteignasala now redirect to this site; office in Hlíðasmári, Kópavogur*
- ✓ **Betri Stofan fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + landið allt) — Reykjavik agency selling homes and commercial property with valuations and rental brokerage; listings span the capital area, Akureyri and regions. · [betristofan.is](https://www.betristofan.is) · *Office in Borgartún, Reykjavik; also rental brokerage*
- ✓ **Húsaskjól fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland) — Kópavogur agency, member of the real-estate agents association; sells homes in the capital area and South Iceland, with valuations. · [husaskjol.is](https://www.husaskjol.is) · *Office in Hlíðasmári, Kópavogur*
- ✓ **Byggð fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri agency selling flats, houses, townhouses and commercial space in Akureyri, Eyjafjörður and East Iceland. · [byggd.is](https://www.byggd.is) · *Office in Skipagata, Akureyri; one of several Akureyri agencies*

### G13-02 · Húsnæðislán og fjármögnun — Mortgages & financing

**Problem:** Affording a bigger place  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 14 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Strong parent hook: all three big banks (Landsbankinn, Íslandsbanki, Arion) and LIVE pension fund advertise lower or deferred mortgage payments during parental leave. Many pension funds and three Sparisjóðurinn savings banks also lend. Others seen on comparison site but not listed: SL lífeyrissjóður, Lífeyrissjóður bankamanna, EFÍA.

- ✓ **Landsbankinn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Largest bank; mortgages with first-time-buyer terms and a parental-leave option that halves the monthly mortgage payment for up to 12 months. · [landsbankinn.is/einstaklingar/lan-og-heimildir/leidin-ad-nyju-heimili](https://www.landsbankinn.is/einstaklingar/lan-og-heimildir/leidin-ad-nyju-heimili) · **Discount:** Parental leave: monthly mortgage payment can be halved for up to 12 months; payment-cap option · *Also offers greiðsluþak (fixed payment for 12 months) for non-indexed loan customers*
- ✓ **Íslandsbanki** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Mortgages for buying or refinancing; first-time buyers get up to 90% loan-to-value and no origination fee; payments can be deferred during parental leave. · [islandsbanki.is/is/flokkur/lan/husnadislan](https://www.islandsbanki.is/is/flokkur/lan/husnadislan) · **Discount:** Mortgage payments can be deferred up to 12 months during parental leave; first-time buyer terms · *Needs payment plan from the Parental Leave Fund; green mortgage with 0.10% rate discount*
- ✓ **Arion banki** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Mortgages incl. first-time-buyer loans up to 85% of value with no arrangement fee, lower payments during parental leave and a payment holiday. · [arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/lan/ibudalan](https://www.arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/lan/ibudalan/) · **Discount:** Payments cut by up to half during parental leave; 12-month payment holiday; no arrangement fee for first-time buyers · *Mortgage life insurance via Vörður; green mortgages*
- ✓ **Auður (Kvika banki)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (stafrænt)) — Digital brand of Kvika banki with online savings accounts and a housing-loan section; app-style alternative lender for families financing a bigger home. · [audur.is](https://www.audur.is/) · *Mortgage terms not shown on the page I fetched; verify product details*
- ✓ **Lífeyrissjóður verzlunarmanna (LIVE)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Large pension fund giving members mortgages and a payment pause on loans during parental leave. · [live.is](https://www.live.is/) · **Discount:** Payment pause of 3-12 months on fund loans during parental leave for members in good standing · *Loan eligibility checked on member pages*
- ✓ **Gildi lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund offering members favorable housing loans in several variants, depending on the borrower's needs. · [gildi.is](https://www.gildi.is/) · *Open to members who have paid into the fund*
- ✓ **Birta lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund with member mortgages (sjóðfélagalán), published rates, lending rules and a loan calculator. · [birta.is](https://www.birta.is/)
- ✓ **Frjálsi lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund with indexed member loans; members can use private pension savings toward housing loans. · [frjalsi.is](https://www.frjalsi.is/)
- ✓ **Stapi lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland + Austurland) — Pension fund with a member-loan section covering loan options, rules and fees; offices in Akureyri and Neskaupstaður. · [stapi.is](https://www.stapi.is/) · *Natural fit for the North and East Iceland edition*
- ✓ **LSR lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund for public-sector staff offering members property loans with flexible terms, a loan calculator and payment assessment. · [lsr.is](https://www.lsr.is/)
- ✓ **Almenni lífeyrissjóðurinn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Pension fund lending to members against real estate, indexed and non-indexed, up to 85% for a first home. · [almenni-lifsverk.is](http://almenni-lifsverk.is/) · *almenni.is redirects to this site*
- ○ **Festa lífeyrissjóður** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes + Suðurland + Vesturland) — Pension fund with offices in Reykjanesbær, Akranes and Selfoss and a loans menu; listed as a mortgage lender on a comparison site. · [festa.is](https://www.festa.is/) · *Mortgage terms not confirmed on its own page*
- ✓ **Sparisjóður Þingeyinga (Sparisjóður Suður-Þingeyinga)** (Þjónusta · Norðurland) — Savings bank in Þingeyjarsýsla with three mortgage products incl. a first-time-buyer loan; branches Laugar, Húsavík and Mývatn. · [spar.is/sparisjodur-s-thingeyinga/einstaklingar/lan/ibudalan](https://www.spar.is/sparisjodur-s-thingeyinga/einstaklingar/lan/ibudalan) · **Discount:** No arrangement fee for first-home purchase · *Part of the Sparisjóðurinn brand Also listed as: Smári sparisjóður.*
- ✓ **Stofn ráðgjöf** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Specialist advisory firm for property financing; helps buyers structure the mortgage for a bigger home. · [stofn.is](https://www.stofn.is) · *Page is brief; services page not read*
- ✓ **HMS - hlutdeildarlán** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Public housing agency running interest-free shared-equity loans for first-time buyers of new homes, with monthly application rounds. · [hms.is/frettir/hlutdeildarlan-fest-i-sessi-rymri-leid-fyrir-tekjulaegri-fyrstu-kaupendur](https://hms.is/frettir/hlutdeildarlan-fest-i-sessi-rymri-leid-fyrir-tekjulaegri-fyrstu-kaupendur) · **Discount:** Shared-equity loan of up to 25-35% of price, no interest or repayments until sale · *Public body; also HMS payment-assessment guidance*

### G13-03 · Leiguíbúðir og leigumiðlun — Rental housing & agencies

**Problem:** Finding a family-friendly rental  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 10 (+2 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Mix of one large private landlord (Ívera, ex-Heimstaden), housing cooperatives (Búseti, Búfesti), income-tested non-profits and many licensed agents who also broker rentals (government licence list shows dozens). A few extra rental-broker agencies exist on the licence list (Kjöreign, FRÓN, Eignastofan, Procura, Eignaver in Akureyri and others) but no own site was seen. WebSearch was unavailable for this category, so coverage relies on direct site checks.

- ✓ **Ívera íbúðafélag** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (m.a. Akureyri, Reykjavík, Reykjanesbær, Selfoss)) — Largest private long-term landlord (took over from Heimstaden); 1-5 room flats in many towns, in-house maintenance team and personal customer service. · [ivera.is](http://www.ivera.is/) · *No family-specific offer seen; 3-5 room units suit families; heimstaden.is redirects here*
- ✓ **Búseti húsnæðissamvinnufélag** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Housing cooperative: members get lifelong occupancy rights (búseturéttur) in apartments, a secure alternative to renting or buying for families. · [buseti.is](https://www.buseti.is) · *Also on the government list of licensed rental brokers*
- ✓ **Búfesti hsf.** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri housing cooperative offering apartments with occupancy rights (búseturéttur), membership, monthly housing fee and maintenance service. · [bufesti.is](https://www.bufesti.is) · *Office at Baldursnes, Akureyri*
- ✓ **Bjarg íbúðafélag** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Non-profit renting long-term homes to lower-income working families; income limit rises per child under 20; waiting list with yearly renewal. · [bjargibudafelag.is](https://www.bjargibudafelag.is) · *Must be ASÍ or BSRB union member; not-for-profit, built by the unions*
- ✓ **Félagsbústaðir** (Samtök/opinbert · Reykjavík) — Reykjavik's social rental housing company with family apartments for households under income and asset limits; apply via neighbourhood centre. · [felagsbustadir.is](https://www.felagsbustadir.is) · *Municipal and income-tested, so narrow audience*
- ○ **Leigufélagið Bríet** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (óstaðfest)) — Rental company named on its own site; page content was minimal, so offering and areas were not confirmed. · [briet.is](http://briet.is/) · *Verify scope before approaching*
- ✓ **Félagsstofnun stúdenta (Stúdentagarðar)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Student housing for students and their families in various flat sizes, with three preschools on site at the student gardens. · [fs.is](https://www.fs.is) · *Only for university students; also runs campus bookshop and cafés*
- ✓ **Betri Stofan fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + landið allt) — Agency that also offers rental brokerage alongside sales and valuations; search filters cover capital area, Akureyri and regions. · [betristofan.is](https://www.betristofan.is) · *Same company as in G13-01*
- ○ **Domusnova fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland + Vesturland) — Large agency on the government list of licensed rental brokers; handles tenancy brokering besides sales. · [domusnova.is](https://www.domusnova.is) · *Rental brokerage seen only on the licence list*
- ○ **Lind fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur agency listed among licensed rental brokers; can broker tenancies as well as sales. · [fastlind.is](https://fastlind.is/) · *Rental brokerage seen only on the licence list*
- ○ **Húsaskjól fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Agency on the licensed rental-broker list; handles lettings alongside sales in the capital area. · [husaskjol.is](https://www.husaskjol.is) · *Rental brokerage seen only on the licence list*
- ○ **Byggð fasteignasala** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri agency on the licensed rental-broker list for North Iceland; sells and can broker lettings. · [byggd.is](https://www.byggd.is) · *Rental brokerage seen only on the licence list*

### G13-04 · Flutningaþjónusta — Movers

**Problem:** Moving house with a baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 16

*Market:* Many small van and mover firms plus two nationwide freight lines; this pass added eleven (Propack, Flutningslausnir, Toppflutningar, several van firms, and Akureyri-based SBN and Fast Parts). No parent or family discounts seen anywhere; local Akureyri, Austurland and Vestfirðir movers still thin.

- ✓ **Nýja Sendibílastöðin hf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + landið allt) — Van and truck transport coordinated for households: moves from a single room upward, pianos, heavy items and general freight; price by driver estimate. · [sendibilar.is](https://www.sendibilar.is) · *Independent drivers; reference price list published; based in Knarrarvogur, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Sendibílastöðin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + landið allt) — Household moves from one room upward, piano and safe transport and freight with vans up to 19 pallets; local and nationwide. · [sendibilastodin.is](https://www.sendibilastodin.is) · *Independent drivers on a base tariff; no discounts listed*
- ✓ **Samskip** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Large freight company listing household and vehicle moving (búslóða- og bílaflutningar) among its transport services, domestic and abroad. · [samskip.is](https://www.samskip.is) · *Service details not shown on the front page; also Faroe Islands and Europe*
- ✓ **Eimskip** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — International household moves by sea (full container or loose cargo) for people moving to or from Iceland; free quote and volume calculator. · [eimskip.is/thjonusta/buslodaflutningar](https://www.eimskip.is/thjonusta/buslodaflutningar/) · *No packing service; insurance mandatory; domestic trucking under Flytjandi*
- ○ **Flytjum** (Þjónusta · Óstaðfest) — Moving company whose site headline reads 'moving made simple'; services and areas not shown on the page I could read. · [flytjum.is](https://www.flytjum.is) · *Page content minimal; confirm household-moving offering and area*
- ✓ **Pökkun & Flutningar ehf. (Propack)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Landið allt) — Established household mover: packing, moves within Iceland and abroad, heated storage and an online volume calculator; quotes by form. · [propack.is](https://www.propack.is/) · *Smiðshöfði 1, Reykjavík; founded 1981; also art transport; no published prices or discounts.*
- ✓ **Flutningslausnir ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland (Þorlákshöfn) + Landið allt) — Household moves with box trucks and crew: published hourly rates and fixed-price packages by crew size, packing, apartment and estate clearances. · [flutningslausnir.is/verdskra_page](https://www.flutningslausnir.is/verdskra_page) · *Reykjavík and Þorlákshöfn; 'Framúrskarandi fyrirtæki 2025' badge; the Búslóð.is household-move site (buslod.is) appears to be run by the same firm; newsletter offers only.*
- ✓ **Toppflutningar ehf.** (Þjónusta · Óstaðfest) — General household and goods moving for individuals and companies; small and large lift trucks; quotes for moves of any size. · [toppflutningar.is](http://www.toppflutningar.is/) · *Service area not stated on the site (footer 2026); no prices or discounts listed.*
- ✓ **Sendibílaþjónustan ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Landið allt) — Van and lift-truck service for household and piano moves, waste runs and moving cleaning; published vehicle rates from 10,000 kr excl. VAT; free quotes. · [flutningabilar.is](https://flutningabilar.is/) · *Breiðvangur, Hafnarfjörður; forwards goods to freight terminals for delivery around the country; footer and images dated 2026.*
- ✓ **Flutningaþjónustan ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Mosfellsbær) + Landið allt) — Mover since 2004 with four service tiers for individuals, from driver only to full move with crew; piano moves; published price list. · [flutningathjonustan.is](https://www.flutningathjonustan.is/) · *Desjamýri, Mosfellsbær; says it moves goods anywhere in Iceland; also business staffing and distribution.*
- ✓ **Sendibílar Norðurlands (SBN)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Van and freight firm in Akureyri and Reykjavík: fixed-price quotes for moves and loads, weekly Reykjavík-Akureyri runs, packing guide and price list. · [sbn.is](https://www.sbn.is/) · *Runs Reykjavík to Akureyri on Thursdays and back on Fridays; owned by A 534 ehf.; site images date from 2021 but footer shows 2026.*
- ✓ **Sendibílastöðin Flutningar ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Landið allt) — Reykjavík van firm with lift vans, a household-belongings lift, container truck and crane truck; says it serves most regions. · [flutningur.net](https://www.flutningur.net/) · *Appears separate from Sendibílastöðin hf. (sendibilastodin.is); site footer shows 2023 so confirm it is still active; no discounts.*
- ✓ **Skutla (Sendibílaþjónustan Skutla)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland + Suðurnes + Vesturland) — Van service for small household moves, home deliveries, waste runs and porter work, with trips to Selfoss, Hveragerði, Keflavík and Akranes. · [skutla.com](https://skutla.com/) · *Delivery offer from 10,000 kr; page undated (booking notice has no year, vehicle photo 2023), so check it is active.*
- ✓ **SG Sendingar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Vesturland + Suðurland) — Small van and freight firm founded 2023 with a published price list; runs to Akranes, Borgarnes and Selfoss; extra workers for moves on request. · [sgsendingar.is](https://www.sgsendingar.is/) · *Household moves not a headline service; price list effective Feb 2025; footer 2026.*
- ✓ **Leit Leið** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Transport and courier firm with a household-moving (búslóðaflutningar) page; same-day deliveries in the capital area and custom quotes per job. · [leitleid.is](https://leitleid.is/) · *Pricing is per job; moving is one of several services; footer 2026.*
- ✓ **Fast Parts - Flutningar** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Weekly freight between depots in Akureyri and Hafnarfjörður for pallets and furniture at 15,000 kr per cubic metre, pickup and home delivery included. · [fastparts.is/flutningar](https://fastparts.is/flutningar/) · *Fast Parts ehf. is a car-parts supplier and freight is a side service; page has no date; heavy items need notice.*

### G13-05 · Geymslur og geymsluhúsnæði — Storage units

**Problem:** Baby gear piling up  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 2

*Market:* Only two self-storage operators confirmed (both capital area/Suðurnes); a third site (Geymslusvæðið in Hafnarfjörður) says it has stopped renting out space. No operator found in Akureyri; more small ones may exist but WebSearch was unavailable.

- ✓ **Geymslur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Self-storage rooms for individuals and businesses in over 100 sizes from 1 m2 upward, from 30-day leases; 24-hour access at three sites. · [geymslur.is](https://www.geymslur.is) · *Sites in Reykjavik (Fiskislóð, Tunguháls) and Reykjanesbær; no promotions seen*
- ✓ **Geymsla Eitt (Geymsla 1)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Self-storage units from 2 to 17 m2 suited to household belongings, 24/7 video surveillance, use your own lock; open daily. · [geymsla1.is](https://geymsla1.is/) · **Discount:** Advertises up to 50% discount offers (details not shown on page) · *Single site at Steinhella, Hafnarfjörður; geymslaeitt.is redirects here*

### G13-06 · Málarar og iðnaðarmenn fyrir barnaherbergi — Painters & tradespeople for a nursery makeover

**Problem:** Getting the room ready in time  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Mix of one-stop renovation contractor, painters' association directory, three paint brands/shops and three building-supply chains with renovation discount schemes. Individual painting firms are numerous and were not reachable without WebSearch; the association directory lists 109 master painters.

- ✓ **Aðalfagmenn ehf. (Fagmenn.is)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Renovation company with painters, plumbers and carpenters under one roof; single contact, project management and cost estimates for a nursery makeover. · [fagmenn.is](https://www.fagmenn.is) · *Serves small repairs up to larger changes; area beyond capital region not stated*
- ✓ **Málarameistarafélagið** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Association of 109 licensed master painters with a member directory, request-a-quote form and guidance on interior painting. · [malarar.is](https://www.malarar.is) · *Trade association, not a painting firm; could sponsor on behalf of members*
- ✓ **Málning hf.** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + landið allt) — Icelandic paint maker; water-thinnable, odourless, solvent-free interior paint (Kópal Glitra) and a find-a-professional link for painters. · [malning.is](https://www.malning.is) · *Head office and shop in Kópavogur; colour cards for exterior; no children's-room range seen*
- ✓ **Slippfélagið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Paint shop with full colour range, colour cards, decorative paint, wallpaper and painting tools; hosts in-store colour advice sessions. · [slippfelagid.is](https://www.slippfelagid.is) · *Store in Skútuvogur, Reykjavik; mailing list for offers; Kalklitir chalk paint*
- ✓ **Flügger Ísland** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Paint webshop and stores with interior paints, magnetic and chalkboard paint, wallpaper, colour cards and inspiration; free delivery over 10,000 ISK. · [flugger.is](https://www.flugger.is) · *Pro section for trade customers; click and collect; store finder on site*
- ✓ **Húsasmiðjan** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Building-supply chain with parquet, tiles, fittings, tool rental and a renovation discount programme (Framkvæmdakjör) for people planning home projects. · [husa.is](https://www.husa.is) · **Discount:** Framkvæmdakjör: up to 30% off selected products for planned renovations · *Paint range not shown on the page I fetched*
- ✓ **BYKO** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Building-supply chain with BYKO PLÚS loyalty programme, renovation terms and a construction loan up to 3 million ISK for individuals. · [byko.is](https://www.byko.is) · **Discount:** BYKO PLÚS members get monthly offers and special renovation terms · *Head office in Kópavogur; store list on site; paint not shown on page fetched*
- ✓ **BAUHAUS Ísland** (Keðja · Landið allt (heimsending)) — Large DIY store with paint colour mixing, tools, lighting, flooring, price matching and nationwide delivery to home or service point. · [bauhaus.is](https://www.bauhaus.is) · *Store in Lambhagavegur, Reykjavik; 365-day return policy*

### G13-07 · Sérsmíði og innréttingar — Custom joinery & fitted furniture (kids' rooms, storage)

**Problem:** Making space in a small home  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* Only four relevant sellers verified: two kitchen/wardrobe cabinet makers, IKEA with kids' storage and a design service, and a small construction joinery. Independent custom-joinery workshops (sérsmíði) are many but could not be discovered without WebSearch; Kvik's old domain now belongs to an unrelated union.

- ○ ⚠ **Brúnás Innréttingar** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Austurland + sendir um land) — Cabinet maker with kitchen cabinets, bathroom vanities, wardrobe solutions and laundry-room cabinets; showrooms in Reykjavik and Egilsstaðir, ships nationwide. · [brunas.is](https://www.brunas.is) · *Production and head office in Egilsstaðir; custom joinery and kids' furniture not mentioned on page [auto] website did not respond in the final check.*
- ✓ **INNlifun** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Made-to-order kitchen cabinets designed to fit each customer's space, using German IMPULS cabinets; showroom in Reykjavik. · [innlifun.is](https://innlifun.is/) · *Kitchens only on page seen; alno.is redirects here*
- ✓ **IKEA Ísland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Wardrobe systems, home storage and children's room furniture, with interior designers who plan rooms in store or by video call. · [ikea.is](https://www.ikea.is) · *No family club or discount seen on page; not custom-made but fitted-storage systems*
- ✓ **Trésmiðja Heimis** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Suðurland) — Joinery in Þorlákshöfn building summer houses, guest houses, decks and portable classrooms; page has an interior-fittings (innréttingar) section; moves houses nationwide. · [tresmidjan.is](https://www.tresmidjan.is) · *More construction than fitted furniture; check whether they do household fitted furniture*

## G14 · Dagvistun og umönnun — Childcare

### G14-01 · Dagforeldrar — Registered childminders (dagforeldrar)

**Problem:** The gap between parental leave and preschool  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3 (+4 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Dagforeldrar are self-employed individuals, so only organisers exist: municipal registers (Reykjavík and Akureyri listed; Kópavogur, Hafnarfjörður, Garðabær, Mosfellsbær, Vogar, Múlaþing and others publish their own), three private directories/platforms (Dagmamma.is, Dagforeldri.is, Nanna) and two Reykjavík associations (Barnið, Barnavistun) that have no websites.

- ✓ **Dagmamma.is** (Vettvangur/app · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes + Suðurland + Norðurland (Akureyri)) — Private online directory of dagforeldrar by region with search, guides on subsidies and regulations, and a registration form for childminders; about 100 listings. · [dagmamma.is](https://dagmamma.is) · *Operating company not named on the site. Roughly 40 listings in Reykjavík, 50 in Hafnarfjörður, 13 in Akureyri. Natural platform for a parent-facing ad slot.*
- ✓ **Reykjavíkurborg - Allir dagforeldrar í Reykjavík** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Official register of dagforeldrar by neighbourhood showing working hours and whether places are free (updated 16 Nov 2026); parents contact childminders directly. · [reykjavik.is/allir-dagforeldrar](https://reykjavik.is/allir-dagforeldrar) · **Discount:** City subsidises day-parent fees (higher from 18 months); municipal, not a discount · *Municipal register, not a buyer. Other municipalities (Kópavogur, Hafnarfjörður, Mosfellsbær, Reykjanesbær, Garðabær) have similar lists.*
- ✓ **Akureyrarbær - Dagforeldrar** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri) — Akureyri municipal page and list of dagforeldrar with hours and availability, fee schedule from 1 April 2025, fee subsidies and home-care grants while waiting for a place. · [akureyri.is/skola-og-fristundastarf/dagforeldrar](https://www.akureyri.is/skola-og-fristundastarf/dagforeldrar) · **Discount:** Municipal fee subsidy and home-care grant (heimgreiðslur) for children over 12 months waiting for a place · *About 12 childminders at 11 locations per the municipality (2022 figure); applicants need legal residence in Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Nanna (dagnanna.is)** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Platform linking parents and dagforeldrar: search by postcode or town, apply to several childminders at once, and read a daily diary of sleep, meals and play. · [dagnanna.is](https://dagnanna.is/) · *Operator not named on the site and no launch date shown; childminders also get a waiting list and group diary, so it could advertise to both sides.*
- ✓ **Dagforeldri.is** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Directory of currently operating dagforeldrar in Iceland, so parents can find a childminder with an open place. · [dagforeldri.is](https://dagforeldri.is/) · *Seen in several search results on its own domain; the site sits behind a bot check and could not be opened, so operator and details are unconfirmed.*
- ○ ⚠ **Barnavistun - félag dagforeldra** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Association of childminders founded in 1993 that works in its members' interests; a channel to reach many dagforeldrar at once. · [facebook.com/groups/135356273184754](https://www.facebook.com/groups/135356273184754/) · *Facebook group only, no website found; registered as an association in Reykjavík (business directory); Reykjavík city documents name it beside Barnið.*
- ○ **Barnið - félag dagforeldra í Reykjavík** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík childminders' association (founded 2011) that city officials meet when changing childminder rules; a channel to reach many dagforeldrar at once. · [gulusidurnar.is/fyrirtaeki/barnidfelag-dagforeldra-i-reykjavik-6302110930](https://gulusidurnar.is/fyrirtaeki/barnidfelag-dagforeldra-i-reykjavik-6302110930/) · *No website found; the link is its business-directory listing (shown as an active association); a Facebook group of the same name exists but its activity is unclear.*

### G14-02 · Ungbarnaleikskólar og ungbarnadeildir — Privately run nurseries for infants

**Problem:** Childcare from 6–12 months  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Four independent infant nurseries exist in Reykjavík (Ársól, Krílasel, Lundur, Sólgarður) plus several independent preschools with infant units; all are tied to Reykjavík service agreements. Outside Reykjavík private infant care is rare and the municipalities dominate. Sólgarður is open only to children of University of Iceland students.

- ✓ **Ungbarnaleikskólinn Ársól (Skólar ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Independent health-focused infant nursery in Reykjavík run by Skólar ehf., for children under 3 (city lists it among four independent infant nurseries). · [skolar.is/um-okkur](https://www.skolar.is/um-okkur/) · *Skólar ehf. also runs five health preschools (Kór in Kópavogur, two Urriðaból in Garðabær, Skógarás in Reykjanesbær, Krókur in Grindavík); Ársól page itself not reachable.*
- ✓ **Ungbarnaleikskólinn Krílasel** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — One-unit infant nursery run by Krílasel ehf. in Seljahverfi, Breiðholt, opened 2019; mostly funded through a service agreement with Reykjavík city. · [krilasel.is](https://www.krilasel.is/) · *Prioritises children with legal residence in Reykjavík; other municipalities only if places are free.*
- ✓ **Sólgarður (Félagsstofnun stúdenta)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — HighScope infant preschool for ages 9 months to 3 years, 115 places, 7:45-16:00; run by the student services foundation. · [solgardur.fs.is](https://solgardur.fs.is/) · *Only for children of University of Iceland students; sister preschool Mánagarður takes ages 1-6. Also listed as: Mánagarður (Félagsstofnun stúdenta).*
- ○ **Lundur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Independently run infant nursery in Laugardalur (Kleppsgarðar), listed by Reykjavík among its four independent infant nurseries. · [reykjavik.is/lundur](https://reykjavik.is/lundur) · *Own website not found; seen only on the city's page.*
- ○ **Barnaheimilið Ós** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Independent preschool near the city centre with an infant unit (ungbarnadeild); founded by parents in 1973 and funded via agreement with Reykjavík. · [reykjavik.is/os](https://reykjavik.is/os) · *Own site barnaheimilidos.is linked from the city page but did not load; age of infant unit not confirmed.*
- ○ **Leikskólinn Vinaminni** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private play-based preschool in Breiðholt (Asparfell) with four departments; the city lists it as having an infant unit. · [vinaminni.is](https://vinaminni.is) · *Minimum age not stated on own site.*
- ○ **Leikskólinn Sælukot** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Neo-humanist preschool at Þorragata, Reykjavík, with yoga, meditation and a vegetarian kitchen; the city lists an infant unit. · [saelukot.is](https://saelukot.is) · *Minimum age not stated on own site.*
- ○ **Leikskólinn Bakkakot (LFA ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Independent three-unit preschool in Grafarvogur run by LFA ehf.; the city lists an infant unit. · [lfa.is/bakkakot](https://www.lfa.is/bakkakot/) · *LFA also runs Korpukot and Fossakot; minimum age not stated on own site. Also listed as: Leikskólinn Korpukot (LFA ehf.).*
- ○ **Leikskólinn Askja (Hjallastefnan)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Hjallastefnan preschool in Hlíðar that the city lists as independent with an infant unit. · [hjallastefnan.is/skolar](https://hjallastefnan.is/skolar/) · *Hjallastefnan also runs 14 other preschools, incl. Hólmasól in Akureyri.*

### G14-03 · Einkareknir leikskólar og uppeldisstefnur — Private preschools & pedagogies (Hjallastefnan, Montessori, Waldorf …)

**Problem:** Finding the right preschool  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 13

*Market:* A sizeable independent preschool sector: Hjallastefnan (15 preschools nationwide, incl. Akureyri) and Skólar ehf. (6 health preschools) are the big chains, with LFA, Framtíðarfólk and student foundation FS as mid-size operators; Waldorf (Reykjavík, Kópavogur), Neo-humanist, Christian (KFUM) and parent-run schools add pedagogy variety. No Montessori preschool confirmed.

- ✓ **Hjallastefnan** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Pedagogy with gender-aware 'Hjalli' method; 15 preschools incl. Hólmasól (Akureyri), Ásar, Hnoðraholt, Vífilsholt (Garðabær), Hjalli (Hafnarfjörður), Akur and Gimli (Reykjanesbær). · [hjallastefnan.is/skolar](https://hjallastefnan.is/skolar/) · *Also primary schools and a webshop; preschools in Reykjavík, Selfoss, Þorlákshöfn, Vestmannaeyjar, Borgarnes, Ísafjörður, Skagaströnd.*
- ✓ **Skólar ehf. (heilsuleikskólar)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Six 'health preschools' built on the Heilsustefna ('healthy soul in a healthy body'): Kór, Ársól, two Urriðaból, Skógarás and Krókur. · [skolar.is/um-okkur](https://www.skolar.is/um-okkur/) · *Founded 2000; largest operator in Samtök heilsuleikskóla, second-largest in the independent schools association.*
- ✓ **LFA ehf. - Leikskóli fyrir alla** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Operator of three independent preschools: Bakkakot and Korpukot (both in Grafarvogur, 112 Reykjavík) and Fossakot. · [lfa.is](https://www.lfa.is) · *Run under the tagline 'Leikskóli fyrir alla'.*
- ✓ **Framtíðarfólk ehf. - Leikskólinn Áshamar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Six-unit preschool in Hamranes, Hafnarfjörður, with a slow-living 'hæglæti' approach and its own learning areas. · [framtidarfolk.is](https://www.framtidarfolk.is/) · *The city lists two independent preschools in Hafnarfjörður, apparently Áshamar and Hjalli.*
- ✓ **Félagsstofnun stúdenta - Sólgarður og Mánagarður** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Two HighScope preschools run by the student foundation: Sólgarður (9 months-3 years, student parents only) and Mánagarður (ages 1-6, open to all). · [fs.is/leikskolar](https://www.fs.is/leikskolar/) · *Apply through the Vala system.*
- ✓ **Waldorfskólinn Sólstafir - leikskólinn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Waldorf (Rudolf Steiner) preschool open 8:00-17:00 with strong daily and seasonal rhythm, run by a non-profit school foundation. · [waldorf.is/leikskolinn](https://waldorf.is/leikskolinn/) · *Opening-hour options of 8 hours; fees and application pages on site.*
- ✓ **Waldorfskólinn Lækjarbotnum - leikskólinn Ylur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Waldorf preschool Ylur and primary school in Lækjarbotnar, Kópavogur, with bus service; follows Steiner pedagogy. · [waldorfskolinn.is](https://waldorfskolinn.is/) · *The city lists two independent preschools in Kópavogur (this one and Skólar ehf.'s Kór).*
- ✓ **Leikskólinn Sælukot** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Neo-humanist preschool run by Ananda Marga's women's wing: yoga, meditation, vegetarian food cooked on site, care for animals and nature. · [saelukot.is](https://saelukot.is) · *Four departments; at Þorragata in Reykjavík.*
- ✓ **Vinagarður leikskóli KFUM og KFUK** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Christian association-run preschool at Holtavegur with four departments (Ungagarður, Grísagarður, Kópagarður, Uglugarður). · [vinagardur.is](https://www.vinagardur.is/) · *Operated by the KFUM og KFUK youth associations.*
- ✓ **Leikskólinn Laufásborg** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Independent preschool in central Reykjavík working in the spirit of Hjallastefnan; chess, French and kitchen-garden projects. · [laufasborg.is](https://laufasborg.is) · *Listed by Hjallastefnan as run independently by its manager.*
- ✓ **Leikskólinn Vinaminni** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Privately run play-based preschool in Breiðholt with four departments; motto 'play holds wisdom'. · [vinaminni.is](https://vinaminni.is) · *Independent school with city service agreement.*
- ○ **Leikskólinn Skerjagarður** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Small independent preschool at Bauganes in Skerjafjörður, listed by Reykjavík among its independent preschools. · [reykjavik.is/skerjagardur](https://reykjavik.is/skerjagardur) · *Own site skerjagardur.is did not load.*
- ○ **Barnaheimilið Ós** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Parent-founded independent preschool near the city centre, with an infant unit, funded via Reykjavík service agreement. · [reykjavik.is/os](https://reykjavik.is/os) · *Own site barnaheimilidos.is did not load.*

### G14-04 · Barnapíur og kvöldpössun — Babysitters & evening sitters

**Problem:** Date night and emergencies  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3

*Market:* Babysitters are individuals, so only intermediaries exist: babysitter.is, the Babysitting.is agency (Allt Mitt Barnapössun) and the international Babysits platform; sitters otherwise advertise in Facebook groups and on Bland.is, and tourist-focused services (e.g. Iceland Babysitter) are not parent-resident options.

- ✓ **babysitter.is** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Icelandic online platform ('Find babysitting you can trust') for booking a babysitter in a few minutes. · [babysitter.is](https://babysitter.is/) · *Single-page app; operator, regions and prices not visible without JavaScript; check it is live before approaching.*
- ✓ **Babysitting.is (Allt Mitt Barnapössun)** (Þjónusta · Óstaðfest) — Babysitting agency with contracted sitters for evenings, weekends, hotel and event sitting; parents book via form; published price list from 3,000-4,000 kr per hour. · [babysitting.is](https://babysitting.is/) · *Describes itself as the first agency of its kind in Iceland; 3-hour minimum, 3,000 kr booking fee, sitter paid on site; regions not stated; footer 2026.*
- ✓ **Babysits (babysits.is)** (Vettvangur/app · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes + Suðurland + Akureyri) — Online platform where parents search sitter profiles and arrange an intro meeting; free to start with a paid premium tier; Iceland pages cover Reykjavík area, Selfoss and Akureyri. · [en.babysits.is](https://en.babysits.is/) · *Operated by Babysits B.V., an international company, with an English-language Iceland site; sitter profiles seen mainly in Reykjavík, Kópavogur and Hafnarfjörður; likely a weak advertiser for a local book.*

### G14-05 · Au pair og barnfóstrur — Au pair & nanny agencies

**Problem:** Full-time help at home  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1

*Market:* Au pair help comes mostly through international platforms (AuPair.com, AuPairWorld, not listed); one Icelandic agency, Nínukot, places au pairs aged 18-25 with host families; no Icelandic nanny (barnfóstra) agency found, nannies being individuals.

- ○ ⚠ **Nínukot** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Icelandic au pair agency for host families: application, matching, video interviews, arrival paperwork help and a two-month guarantee; placement fee 89,900 kr. · [ninukot.is/au-pair](https://ninukot.is/au-pair/) · *Fee charged only if a placement happens; host family also provides room, board and weekly pocket money; also runs programmes abroad (US camp, au pair USA and Germany); footer 2026. [auto] website did not respond in the final check.*

### G14-06 · Barnagæsla á staðnum — Drop-in childcare (stores, gyms, events, play rooms)

**Problem:** Short breaks while you shop or train  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

*Market:* Drop-in minding is rare: gyms (World Class, Hress, CrossFitReykjavik, Norður in Akureyri, Stöðin in Ísafjörður) and two play areas (Kringlan's Ævintýraland, IKEA's Småland for ages 3-7); gyms such as Hreyfing, Sporthúsið, Katla Fitness and GYM heilsa do not advertise it; childcare hours, ages and prices were unreadable on the two regional gym sites.

- ✓ **World Class - barnagæsla** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Staffed child-minding while parents train at Breiðholt, Egilshöll, Laugar and Tjarnarvellir; unstaffed kids' corner in Mosfellsbær; any age on weekdays, max 1.5 hours per visit. · [worldclass.is/barnagaesla](https://www.worldclass.is/barnagaesla) · **Discount:** 440 kr single visit; 15- and 30-visit cards; kids' corner free for cardholders · *Weekend minimum age 2; staff do not change nappies.*
- ✓ **Hress heilsurækt - barnagæsla** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Hafnarfjörður gym with a child-minding room from age 3 months (TV, toys, baby chairs, walkers); babies can sleep in prams outside; max 1 hour 15 minutes. · [hress.is](https://www.hress.is/) · *Limited hours (summer hours listed: Mon/Wed evenings, Saturday morning).*
- ✓ **CrossFitReykjavik** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — CrossFit gym at Faxafen 12 with child-minding hours on weekday afternoons and weekend mornings. · [crossfitreykjavik.is](https://crossfitreykjavik.is/) · *Barnagæsla times listed on the front page footer.*
- ✓ **Ævintýraland Kringlunnar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Play world in Kringlan for ages 3-9 with ball pits and play kitchen; children can be left while parents shop; under-3s with an adult. · [kringlan.is/aevintyraland](https://www.kringlan.is/aevintyraland) · **Discount:** Sibling prices and 10-visit card 13,300 kr; under-3s 1,090 kr · *Hourly entry from 1,690 kr; birthday parties available.*
- ✓ **IKEA Ísland - Småland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Play forest at the store entrance for children aged 3-7 while parents shop; IKEA's English page calls it a free supervised play area. · [ikea.is/is/smaland](https://www.ikea.is/is/smaland) · *Kauptún 4, Garðabær; time limit and rules not stated on the Icelandic page; the same retailer also sells potties and nursery goods.*
- ✓ **Norður Akureyri (líkamsrækt)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri gym with a dedicated barnapössun page, open gym, drop-in tickets and two free trial sessions. · [nordurak.net/new-page](https://nordurak.net/new-page) · **Discount:** Offers for members page; two free trial sessions for newcomers · *Tryggvabraut 22, Akureyri; childcare hours, ages and price could not be read on the page; 2026 timetable published.*
- ✓ **Stöðin heilsurækt (Ísófit ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Vestfirðir) — Ísafjörður gym with group hall and weights room that publishes a dedicated barnapössun (childcare) page; cards sold online. · [stodinheilsuraekt.is/barnapossun](https://stodinheilsuraekt.is/barnapossun/) · *Run by Ísófit ehf. in a former dairy building; childcare hours, ages and price not readable on the page; weekday hours 05:45-20:00.*

## G15 · Leikur, þroski og afþreying — Play, development & activities

### G15-01 · Ungbarnasund — Baby swimming classes

**Problem:** Water confidence and bonding  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 10 (+2 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Baby swimming is delivered mainly by small private instructor brands that rent pool time (Reykjavík, Hafnarfjörður, Kópavogur, Garðabær, Mosfellsbær, Reykjanesbær, Akureyri and a few rural towns), co-ordinated by the teachers' association Busli, which lists about 15 providers. No chain or market leader; public pools mainly host them. Reykjavík's own 2026 baby-swim survey page could not be fetched.

- ✓ **Sundskóli Hörpu** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Baby swimming (beginners and continuation) plus swim school for ages 1-6, at Mörkin (Reykjavík) and Suðurbæjarlaug (Hafnarfjörður); online booking. · [sundskolihorpu.is](https://www.sundskolihorpu.is/) · *Next courses start 16-17 January 2027.*
- ✓ **Sundskóli Sóleyjar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Baby swimming for ages 2-12 months, child swimming 1-6 years and adult courses at Hrafnista Boðaþing, Kópavogur; courses booked in webshop. · [sundskoli.is](https://sundskoli.is/) · *Course price about 32,000 kr for infants 2-6 months.*
- ✓ **Ungbarnasund Erlu** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Baby swimming courses for children from 3 months to 2.5 years at Suðurbæjarlaug, Hafnarfjörður, with online booking and timetable. · [ungbarnasunderlu.is](https://ungbarnasunderlu.is/)
- ✓ **Ungbarnasund hjá Fabio** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Vesturland) — Baby swimming at Reykjalundur (Mosfellsbær) and the pool in Borgarnes; sign-up and songs on site. · [sundhjafabio.is](https://www.sundhjafabio.is/)
- ✓ **Þjálfun í vatni** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes) — Baby swimming, swim school, water aerobics and pregnancy/mums water training in the Akurskóli pool, Reykjanesbær. · [thjalfunivatni.is](https://www.thjalfunivatni.is/) · *Also pregnancy and mums water training, 4-week course 18,000 kr.*
- ○ **Ungbarnasund Heiðu** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Baby swimming in the warm children's pool at Hátún 12, Reykjavík. · [instagram.com/ungbarnasundheidu](https://www.instagram.com/ungbarnasundheidu) · *Listed by Busli; own page not seen.*
- ○ ⚠ **Ungbarnasund Hrundar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Baby swimming in the rehabilitation pool at Grensás, Reykjavík. · [facebook.com/ungbarnasundhrundar](https://www.facebook.com/ungbarnasundhrundar) · *Listed by Busli; own page not seen.*
- ○ **Ungbarnasund Snorra** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Baby swimming at Æfingastöðin (Háaleitisbraut and Hátún 12), Reykjavík. · [ungbarnasund.is/ungbarnasundnamskeid](https://www.ungbarnasund.is/ungbarnasundnamskeid) · *Listed by Busli; registration by phone.*
- ○ **Ungbarnasund Bjargar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Baby swimming in the Sjálandsskóli pool, Garðabær. · [instagram.com/ungbarnasundbjargar](https://www.instagram.com/ungbarnasundbjargar) · *Listed by Busli; own page not seen.*
- ○ ⚠ **Sundskóli Marínar** (Þjónusta · Austurland) — Baby and child swimming in the pool at Fáskrúðsfjörður. · [facebook.com/sundskolimarinar](https://www.facebook.com/sundskolimarinar) · *Listed by Busli; own page not seen.*
- ○ ⚠ **Sundfélag ÍBV** (Samtök/opinbert · Suðurland) — Swim club offering baby swimming in the Vestmannaeyjar sports centre; no fixed schedule because the pool is only heated occasionally. · [facebook.com/SundfelagIBV](https://www.facebook.com/SundfelagIBV) · *Sports club (non-profit); listed by Busli.*
- ✓ **Busli - félag ungbarnasundkennara** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Association of baby swimming teachers (founded 1994) running ungbarnasund.is with a nationwide list of providers and teacher training. · [ungbarnasund.is](https://www.ungbarnasund.is/) · *Directory of about 15 providers; useful first contact for the slot.*

### G15-02 · Ungbarnanudd og ungbarnajóga — Baby massage & baby yoga

**Problem:** Colic relief and bonding  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Baby massage and baby yoga are taught by a few small studios and independent instructors: ungbarnanudd at Nálar & nudd and Dharma Heilsa, mömmujóga at Jógasetrið, Yogaljós and Faðmur; no pure baby-yoga studio and no Akureyri provider found, and library one-off talks and Facebook-only instructors are not listed.

- ✓ **Nálastungur & nudd (ungbarnanudd)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Massage clinic in Bolholt running baby-massage courses and private sessions where parents learn to massage; claims better bonding and sleep, relief from colic and gas. · [nalarognudd.is](https://www.nalarognudd.is/) · *Also pregnancy massage, acupuncture and distance teaching; gift vouchers available.*
- ✓ **Jógasetrið - Mömmujóga** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík yoga studio (since 2002) with a mums-and-babies yoga course to rebuild strength after birth while bonding with the baby. · [jogasetrid.is/jogatimar/mommujoga](https://jogasetrid.is/jogatimar/mommujoga/) · *Also pregnancy yoga, couples courses and kids' yoga.*
- ✓ **Faðmur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Hafnarfjörður wellness practice teaching mömmujóga with babies: core and pelvic-floor work, songs, play and infant massage, currently as private or small-group sessions. · [fadmur.is/mommujoga](https://fadmur.is/mommujoga/) · *Suggested from about 6-8 weeks after birth until the baby crawls; also pregnancy yoga and swimming and hypnotherapy; a class was once listed at Lífsgæðasetur St. Jó, Hafnarfjörður; blog post dated Aug 2026; no price on page.*
- ✓ **Yogaljós** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík yoga company with weekly Tuesday mömmujóga for new mothers and babies: gentle yoga, pelvic-floor breathing, relaxation and a toy-equipped room with pram garden. · [yogaljos.is/medgonguyoga](https://yogaljos.is/medgonguyoga/) · *Taught at Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur (the birth centre); also pregnancy yoga and a fear-free birth course; footer says 2022 but posts mention 2027 courses; no baby massage or baby yoga.*
- ✓ **Dharma Heilsa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík massage and beauty clinic (Dharma ehf.) that teaches baby massage in private sessions and mothers' groups, alongside pregnancy massage. · [dharmaheilsa.is/fraedsla-radgjof](https://dharmaheilsa.is/fraedsla-radgjof/) · *Tunguháls 19, shares the building with Samkennd Heilsusetur; no prices or dates on the page, posts suggest it is active in 2026; no baby yoga.*

### G15-03 · Tónlistarnámskeið fyrir ungbörn og foreldra — Baby & toddler music classes

**Problem:** Stimulation and getting out of the house  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1

*Market:* Tónagull (running since 2004) is the specialist for baby and toddler music classes; no competing commercial provider was found. Music schools checked (Allegro Suzuki, Tónmenntaskóli, Tónlistarskólinn á Akureyri) show no baby or toddler classes on their start pages, and Kramhúsið's start page lists none either.

- ✓ **Tónagull - Tónlist með ungum börnum** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Music classes for babies and toddlers with parents (ages about 2 months to 4 years), 45-minute classes, six-week courses; also Polish and Ukrainian courses and a songbook. · [tonagull.is](https://www.tonagull.is/) · **Discount:** Siblings free, one price per family; gift vouchers available · *Held at Grensáskirkja hall in Reykjavík; Icelandic course 29,500 kr; trains its own teachers; Akureyri not mentioned.*

### G15-04 · Krílaleikfimi og hreyfinámskeið — Toddler gym & movement classes

**Problem:** Energy and motor skills  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5 (+3 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Hreyfiland is the one dedicated parent-and-baby movement studio; the rest comes from dance schools (Ballettskóli Eddu Scheving, STEPS in Akureyri, Danskompaní in Reykjanesbær), the Skopp trampoline park's 0-5 sessions and non-profit gymnastics clubs (Gerpla, Fylkir, KA in Akureyri) - many other clubs run toddler groups too but are not listed. Akureyri has STEPS (commercial) and KA's krílahópar; no commercial parent-baby gym found in North Iceland.

- ✓ **Hreyfiland** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Parent-child movement classes for babies (Snillingafimi), toddlers (Hreyfifimi) and preschoolers, in a purpose-built play gym; also pregnancy/breastfeeding workout classes. · [hreyfiland.is](https://hreyfiland.is/) · *Location not stated on the pages read; online shop for courses, gift cards not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Gerpla - Kríli og Bangsar** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur gymnastics club's toddler programme for ages 18 months to 5 years; autumn 2026 registration via Abler. · [gerpla.is/deildir/krili-og-bangsar](https://www.gerpla.is/deildir/krili-og-bangsar/) · *Sports club (non-profit); registration opens on Abler each term.*
- ✓ **Fylkir - Ungbarnafimi** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Gymnastics division of the Reykjavík club with 'Ungbarnafimi' for ages 1-2 attending with parents, 45 minutes on Saturdays. · [fylkir.is/fimleikar](https://www.fylkir.is/fimleikar) · *Sports club (non-profit).*
- ✓ **STEPS Dancecenter (Akureyri)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri dance school with Kríladans for ages 2-5 (ballet technique, play and fairy-tale themes, show at Hof) and a separate 4-week class for 18-24-month-olds with parents. · [steps.is/kr%C3%ADladans](https://www.steps.is/kr%C3%ADladans) · *Sunnuhlíð; sign-up via Abler; the 4-week toddler course was listed at 8,000 kr; also classes for older children and teens.*
- ✓ **Ballettskóli Eddu Scheving** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Ballet school with a weekly 30-minute '2 ára ballett' class where a parent attends (max 12 children), plus a preschool class for ages 3-6, at three venues. · [bsch.is/namskeid/2-ara-ballett](https://bsch.is/namskeid/2-ara-ballett/) · *Venues: Skipholt (Reykjavík), Kópavogur and Grafarvogur; 40,200 kr for 10 weeks; new courses start 11 January 2027; registration via xps.is.*
- ✓ **Danskompaní (Reykjanesbær)** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes) — Reykjanesbær dance school accepting children from age 2 in its 'Ævintýrahópar' (ages 2-3), one of 19 groups; registration through Sportabler. · [danskompani.is](https://www.danskompani.is/) · *Class times, price and whether parents attend were not stated on the pages read; a 'Fjölskyldutímar' (family classes) event is listed.*
- ✓ **Skopp - krakkatímar 0-5 ára** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur trampoline park with special toddler sessions for ages 0-5 on weekends, plus a daily FjölskylduSkopp price so families can jump together. · [skoppisland.is](https://www.skoppisland.is/) · **Discount:** FjölskylduSkopp: families jump together every day at a better price · *Same company as the indoor-playground entry; Dalvegur, Kópavogur; toddler sessions seen as weekend mornings 10-12; prices not read.*
- ✓ **KA - Fimleikadeild (krílahópar)** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri) — Gymnastics division of KA Akureyri (founded 2023) with krílahópar for children born 2021-2024, 45-60 minute sessions attended with a parent. · [ka.is/fimleikar/hopar](https://www.ka.is/fimleikar/hopar) · *Non-profit club division; registration via Abler; the only Akureyri club toddler gymnastics group confirmed (listed because of the North Iceland focus).*

### G15-05 · Listsmiðjur og skynjunarleikur fyrir smábörn — Art & sensory-play classes

**Problem:** Creative play  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 0 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* No commercial art or sensory-play class for under-3s was found. The only verified lead is the Akureyri Art Museum's family tours with art workshop (public, not toddler-specific). Sensory 'baby sensory'-type franchises and toddler art studios may exist but did not appear in direct domain checks.

- ✓ **Listasafnið á Akureyri - fjölskylduleiðsögn og listasmiðja** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri) — Akureyri Art Museum holds family guided tours combined with an art workshop; not specific to toddlers. · [listak.is/is/frettir/frettasafn/fjolskylduleidsogn-og-listasmidja](https://www.listak.is/is/frettir/frettasafn/fjolskylduleidsogn-og-listasmidja) · *Public museum; check for toddler-friendly dates.*

### G15-06 · Táknmál og snemmbær tjáning — Baby sign language & early communication

**Problem:** Communicating before speech  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* No dedicated baby-sign business exists; early signing in Iceland runs on Tákn með tali (TMT). Mimi Saga sells a self-paced online family course, the state diagnostic centre RGR runs one-day TMT courses with half-price family places, municipalities publish TMT dictionaries and SHH (public) teaches Icelandic Sign Language mainly to deaf families. No Akureyri or North Iceland provider found; speech-therapy clinics were not found as parent-course sellers.

- ✓ **Mimi Saga (Málörvun með Mími)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (netnámskeið)) — Online family course in Tákn með tali for parents and caregivers of young children: basic signs, language-stimulation tips and light assignments, self-paced. · [malorvun-med-mimi.teachable.com](https://malorvun-med-mimi.teachable.com/) · *Course 17,900 kr with instalment option; works anywhere in Iceland including Akureyri; page does not state a minimum age for the child.*
- ✓ **Ráðgjafar- og greiningarstöð ríkisins (RGR) - Tákn með tali** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — State diagnostic centre running a 7-hour Tákn með tali course in Reykjavík, mainly for professionals but open to family members; the page says TMT is often used with children from birth. · [rgr.is/is/fraedsla-og-namskeid/namskeid/namskeid-a-naestunni/takn-med-tali-0926](https://www.rgr.is/is/fraedsla-og-namskeid/namskeid/namskeid-a-naestunni/takn-med-tali-0926) · **Discount:** Family members pay half price (15,750 kr vs 31,500 kr) · *Public body, not an advertiser; the listed date (28 September 2026) has passed, so check rgr.is for new dates; minimum 13 participants.*

### G15-07 · Leikgrindur, leikmottur og þroskamottur — Playpens, play mats & baby gyms

**Problem:** A safe play space  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 12

*Market:* Well served: about ten Icelandic baby/toy shops list playpens and play mats as a category (mostly Kópavogur stores plus webshops), and Hagkaup carries sensory play mats. No clear leader; barnavorur.is (a price-comparison site for 20 Icelandic baby shops) can be used to compare. Akureyri: no dedicated baby shop found, only Hagkaup and online shops.

- ✓ **Miniplay.is** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Kópavogur shop and webshop with a 'Leikgrindur og leikmottur' category plus Pikler climbing sets and quality wooden toys. · [miniplay.is](https://miniplay.is/) · *Store at Tónahvarf, Kópavogur; 100-day return period; ships nationwide with Dropp.*
- ✓ **Húsgagnaheimilið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Baby shop with playpens, travel playpens and play mats/mattresses alongside cots, strollers and safety products; free shipping nationwide. · [husgogn.is](https://husgogn.is/)
- ✓ **Barnið okkar** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Kópavogur baby shop (Hlíðasmári) specialising in Lionelo strollers and car seats, with a playpen category. · [barnidokkar.is](https://barnidokkar.is/)
- ✓ **Bíumbíum** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Kids' clothing and baby goods shop with a 'Leikgrindur & leikmottur' collection; very large range (over 3,000 products). · [biumbium.is](https://biumbium.is/) · *Brands include BeSafe, Bibs, Angulus and Babydan.*
- ✓ **Petit** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Icelandic baby shop (strollers, seats, clothes, toys) listing playpens and play mats among 2,700 products. · [petit.is](https://petit.is/) · *Brands include Bugaboo and B.box; site shows a bot-check page to scripts.*
- ✓ **Dótabúðin.is** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Toy and baby webshop with 'Leikteppi og leikgrindur' and developmental toys under its 'Fyrstu árin' (first years) section. · [dotabudin.is](https://dotabudin.is/) · *Chicco, Smoby and Zuru ranges.*

### G15-10 · Barnabækur og bókaklúbbar — Children's books & book subscriptions

**Problem:** Reading from birth  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 8

*Market:* Icelandic children's books are sold by the big publisher-retailer Forlagið (with a 0-5 years section and book clubs), small children's publishers (Bókabeitan, Setberg, Ugla, Salka) and chains (A4 with 557 children's titles, Penninn Eymundsson, Hagkaup). Storytel offers a Kids Mode subscription. No dedicated baby book-subscription box was found. Public libraries are the main free reading-from-birth provider but are municipal.

- ✓ **Forlagið vefverslun og bókabúð** (Netverslun · Landið allt + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Largest publisher's webshop with a children's books section for 0-5 years, audiobooks, e-books for children and 'Bókaklúbbar' (book clubs). · [forlagid.is](https://www.forlagid.is/) · *Ships nationwide; also sells audiobooks and e-books for children and teens.*
- ✓ **Bókabeitan** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Children's and youth book publisher ('Töfraheimur barnabókanna') with educational material and extras on the site. · [bokabeitan.is](https://bokabeitan.is/)
- ✓ **Setberg** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Publisher of children's books since 1952 with a webshop and gift cards. · [setberg.is](https://setberg.is/)
- ✓ **Salka** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Publisher and bookshop at Hverfisgata with a children's section 'Fyrir þau yngstu' (for the youngest) and preschool-age books. · [salka.is](https://www.salka.is/)
- ✓ **Ugla útgáfa** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Publisher with a webshop offering classic children's and youth books alongside crime fiction and non-fiction. · [uglautgafa.is](https://uglautgafa.is/)
- ✓ **A4** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Stationery chain with 557 children's books and play books in its toy section. · [a4.is/fyrir-barni/leikfong.html](https://a4.is/fyrir-barni/leikfong.html) · *Stores in Skeifan, Kringlan, Smáralind and Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Storytel Ísland** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Audiobook and e-book subscription with a children-only Kids Mode; family plan for 2-6 accounts. · [storytel.com/is](https://www.storytel.com/is) · **Discount:** 3-day free trial; Family plan from 3,990 kr/month · *Over 1 million titles in Icelandic, English and other languages.*
