### G15-12 · Innileikjagarðar og leiksvæði innanhúss — Indoor playgrounds

**Problem:** Winter days with nowhere to go  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6 (+2 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Few true indoor playgrounds: Kringlan's Ævintýraland, Skopp (the Rush Iceland domain now redirects to it; it also runs 0-5 sessions) and Barnaland at Smárabíó; Fjölskylduland (Dugguvogur) has closed permanently. Free options fill the gap: Harpa's Hljóðhimnar sound-play space, the Memmm open preschool (a non-profit, free, ages 0-5) and library children's departments; parent guides also mention play areas in Smáralind and a VáVá Barnaklúbbur in Reykjavík that could not be verified. Akureyri has no indoor play centre (only the library's weekly Foreldramorgnar, an indoor skatepark and a climbing gym) and the cafés with play areas (Laundromat Café) are in G15-13.

- ✓ **Ævintýraland Kringlunnar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Play world in Kringlan with ball-pit castles, boats, play kitchen and a quiet book room; ages 3-9, under-3s with an adult, under-1s free. · [kringlan.is/aevintyraland](https://www.kringlan.is/aevintyraland) · **Discount:** Siblings cheaper; 10-visit card 13,300 kr; under-3s 1,090 kr; Wednesday Kúmen meal includes a 30-min invitation · *Entry from 1,690 kr per hour; birthday parties.*
- ✓ **Skopp (Skoppísland)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Trampoline park in Kópavogur with BarnaSkopp for ages 0-5 (with a parent): trampoline, small climbing wall, obstacle course, treehouse; also family sessions and birthdays. · [skoppisland.is](https://www.skoppisland.is/) · *Dalvegur 10-14, Kópavogur; 60 or 90 minute visits; prices on separate page.*
- ✓ **Smárabíó - Barnaland** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kids' area (Barnaland) in the Smárabíó entertainment zone beside lasertag, arcade and birthday rooms in Smáralind. · [smarabio.is/skemmtisvaedi](https://www.smarabio.is/skemmtisvaedi) · *Details of Barnaland (ages, price) not given on the page read.*
- ✓ **Harpa - Hljóðhimnar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Free sound-and-music play space on Harpa's ground floor where children and their families sit in the conductor's seat, hear lullabies and explore instruments; children must be accompanied. · [harpa.is/en/hljodhimnar](https://www.harpa.is/en/hljodhimnar) · **Discount:** Free entry · *Open when the building is open; access may be limited to about 30 people at peak times; Harpa also runs a family programme with events for children.*
- ✓ **Memmm - Opni leikskólinn** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Non-profit open preschool: free drop-in play, coffee and a song session for families with children aged 0-5 and an adult, no registration needed. · [memmm.is/opni-leikskolinn](https://memmm.is/opni-leikskolinn) · **Discount:** Free for all families · *Sessions at Hafnarfjörður library, Aflagrandi (Vesturbær) and Gerðuberg library (Breiðholt); a Visit Akureyri listing also mentions Memmm sessions at Akureyri library, but memmm.is does not list Akureyri.*
- ✓ **Amtsbókasafnið á Akureyri (Foreldramorgnar)** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri) — Akureyri's municipal library holds free Foreldramorgnar every Tuesday 10-12 in its children's department, where parents meet over coffee while the children play. · [akureyri.is/amtsbokasafnid](https://www.akureyri.is/amtsbokasafnid) · **Discount:** Free, coffee provided · *Public library; the page does not mention a dedicated play area or toys; Memmm Akureyri guests have led singing and sensory sessions on alternating Tuesdays (Visit Akureyri listing).*
- ○ **Smáralind (leiksvæði)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur shopping centre that parent and travel guides say has free, padded open play areas for toddlers; its own pages confirm free pram loans. · [smaralind.is](https://www.smaralind.is/) · *Play area not confirmed on smaralind.is pages read (only pram loans and family events); same building as Barnaland at Smárabíó and near Skopp.*
- ○ ⚠ **VáVá Barnaklúbbur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Indoor children's club at Drafnarfell, Reykjavík, listed in a parent guide to places for 0-2-year-olds; an aggregator describes climbing structures, slides and a ball pit. · [heimilisvefurinn.is/hvadgeteggertmedbarninuifaedingarorlofinu2526](https://heimilisvefurinn.is/hvadgeteggertmedbarninuifaedingarorlofinu2526/) · *No own website found; only secondary listings (heimilisvefurinn.is, an aggregator), so verify it is still open before approaching.*

### G15-13 · Barnvæn kaffihús og veitingastaðir — Family-friendly cafés & restaurants

**Problem:** Getting out of the house with a baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 13

*Market:* No directory exists, but parent guides name a handful of Reykjavík-area cafés with play corners, and the Laundromat Café and Nauthóll verify with their own kids' pages; the Akureyri side is Greifinn and Bautinn (children's menus), Verksmiðjan (family restaurant at Glerártorg) and Sykurverk, plus the Daladýrð petting-zoo café in Fnjóskadalur. A parent guide also names Kaffi Áslákur, Mathús Garðabæjar, Kastalakaffi, Spilakaffi and Klambrar Bistro, and Kaffi Kú near Akureyri appears in a family guide, but none could be checked on an own page.

- ✓ **Kúmen (Kringlan)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Restaurant on Kringlan's third floor; Wednesday meals include a 30-minute voucher for the Ævintýraland play world (ages 3-9). · [kumen.is](https://kumen.is/) · **Discount:** Wednesday meal includes 30 min in Ævintýraland · *Open until 20:00 every evening.*
- ✓ **Greifinn** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri family restaurant with a separate children's menu (Barnamatseðill) and lunch deals. · [greifinn.is](https://www.greifinn.is/)
- ✓ **Bautinn** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri restaurant (since 1971) at Hafnarstræti with a dedicated children's menu and online booking. · [bautinn.is](https://bautinn.is/) · *Also serves Pizzasmiðjan pizzas.*
- ✓ **Króníkan** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Pizzeria and café inside Gerðarsafn art museum in Kópavogur; Visit Reykjavík lists it as family-friendly, and the museum has an outdoor play area. · [kronikan.is](https://kronikan.is/) · *Family-friendly label from visitreykjavik.is, not from the café's own page.*
- ✓ **The Laundromat Café** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Downtown Reykjavík café-restaurant with a dedicated children's play area downstairs, a separate kids' menu and high chairs; it says families are welcome. · [thelaundromatcafe.is/bornin](https://www.thelaundromatcafe.is/bornin) · *Austurstræti 9; the page lists no changing table or other baby facilities; also on the 'places for 0-2 year olds' lists of parent guides.*
- ✓ **Nauthóll** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Restaurant at Nauthólsvegur by the Nauthólsvík outdoor-recreation area in Reykjavík with a 2026 children's menu (Barnamatseðill) on its site; also brunch and an event hall. · [nautholl.is](https://nautholl.is/) · *Own site confirms the children's menu only; play corner and high chairs not mentioned; Nauthólsvík is a popular family outing area.*
- ✓ **Verksmiðjan (Glerártorg)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Family restaurant at Glerártorg in Akureyri, labelled family-friendly by Visit Akureyri; open daily 11:00-21:00 (21:30 on Fridays and Saturdays) with plenty of parking. · [verksmidjan.is](https://verksmidjan.is/) · *Own site calls it a 'fjölskylduveitingastaður' but does not mention a kids' menu, play area or high chairs.*
- ✓ **Daladýrð (Café Animal)** (Þjónusta · Norðurland (Fnjóskadalur, near Akureyri)) — Petting zoo 15 minutes from Akureyri with 'Café Animal' where families sit among hens and turkeys, plus a kids' playground with hay, bobby cars, sandbox and trampolines. · [daladyrd.is](https://daladyrd.is/) · **Discount:** Children aged 1-2 free (adults 1,900 kr, ages 3-12 1,500 kr) · *Seasonal and animal-based, so winter opening is unclear (hours are given on Instagram); also fits the family activities and zoos category.*
- ○ **Kaffi Laugalækur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Brunch and lunch café at Laugarnesvegur near Laugardalur that guides describe with a kids' play corner and high chairs; its own site shows menus and opening hours. · [kaffilaugalaekur.is](https://www.kaffilaugalaekur.is/) · *Child features come from two guides and a parent guide, not from the café's own pages; listed among cafés with children's areas for ages 0-2.*
- ○ **Arna ís- og kaffibar (Eiðistorg)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Seltjarnarnes)) — Ice-cream and coffee bar at Eiðistorg, Seltjarnarnes, listed in a tourism directory with a spacious children's play area and in a parent guide for ages 0-2. · [arna.is](https://www.arna.is/) · *arna.is covers Arna's dairy products and did not mention the café or play area; confirm the café's own channels.*
- ○ **Kaffi Dalur (Farfuglaheimilið Dalur)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Café at the HI Reykjavík Dalur hostel in Laugardalur described by a tourism directory as a family café with a play area; hostel page lists family rooms, cots and a café and bar. · [hostel.is/en/hostels/hi-reykjavik-dalur](https://www.hostel.is/en/hostels/hi-reykjavik-dalur) · *Play area stated only in the ferdalag.is directory and a parent guide, not on the hostel page; close to the Family Park and zoo.*
- ○ **Sykurverk (Akureyri)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri café on Strandgata serving crêpes, baked goods and food, open daily 11:30-20:00; a local family guide describes a children's corner. · [sykurverk.is](https://www.sykurverk.is/) · *Children's corner is from a family guide, not the café's own page; the guide also mentions Kaffi Hús and Orðakaffi (library building) as parent-friendly in Akureyri.*
- ○ **Hamborgarafabrikkan** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Burger restaurant chain that a family-restaurant guide says has its own kids' menu; the chain's site is JavaScript-only and could not be read. · [fabrikkan.is](https://www.fabrikkan.is/) · *Kids' menu, high chairs and any play corner unverified on an own page; branches (including Akureyri or Hafnarfjörður) not checked.*

### G15-14 · Fjölskylduafþreying, söfn og dýragarðar — Family activities, museums & zoos

**Problem:** Weekend outings with small children  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5 (+2 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Several family attractions: Reykjavík family park and zoo (municipal; free under 6), Whales of Iceland (free 0-6), Perlan, Lava Centre in South Iceland, and Daladýrð petting zoo near Akureyri (free 1-2 years). Leikhópurinn Lotta tours children's musicals in summer. Akureyri's museum is free for children. Very few attractions are aimed at under-3s specifically.

- ✓ **Fjölskyldu- og húsdýragarðurinn** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík's family park and zoo with farm animals; free entry for children 0-5, annual cards for families. · [mu.is](https://mu.is/) · **Discount:** Children 0-5 free; annual cards · *Municipal park run by Reykjavík.*
- ✓ **Whales of Iceland** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Whale museum at Grandi, Reykjavík, with life-sized models and interactive exhibits; open daily 10:00-17:00. · [whalesoficeland.is](https://www.whalesoficeland.is/) · **Discount:** Children 0-6 free; family ticket 8,000 kr; family multipass · *Fiskislóð 23-25.*
- ✓ **Perlan** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Interactive nature museum in Reykjavík with hands-on exhibits, volcano show and glacier experience; visitor reviews on the site praise it for children. · [perlan.is](https://perlan.is/)
- ✓ **Daladýrð** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Petting zoo in Fnjóskadalur, about 20 minutes from Akureyri, with many baby animals in spring and summer. · [daladyrd.is](https://daladyrd.is/) · **Discount:** Free for ages 1-2; ages 3-12 1,500 kr · *Brúnagerði, route 833; opening hours on Instagram.*
- ✓ **Leikhópurinn Lotta** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Outdoor children's musicals touring Iceland each summer (summer musical 'Ljóti andarunginn'), with shows in Elliðaárdalur and recordings on disc and streaming. · [leikhopurinnlotta.is](https://www.leikhopurinnlotta.is/) · **Discount:** Stofn members (Sjóvá): buy one ticket, get one free; children 2 and under free · *Stofn offer seen on Sjóvá's page.*
- ✓ **LAVA Centre** (Þjónusta · Suðurland) — Interactive volcano and earthquake exhibition with a family ticket for interactive tours. · [lavacentre.is](https://lavacentre.is/) · **Discount:** Family pack 13,000 kr · *South Iceland.*
- ✓ **Minjasafnið á Akureyri** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri) — Akureyri museum and the Nonni house, daily 13-16; free for children; also a toy house open for groups. · [minjasafnid.is](https://www.minjasafnid.is/) · **Discount:** Free for children · *Public-sector or non-profit museum.*

### G15-15 · Hljóðbækur, tónlist og streymi — Audiobooks, music & streaming

**Problem:** Something to do during night feeds  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Few Icelandic-owned options: Storytel (Kids Mode, family plan) and Forlagið's audiobooks cover reading aloud; Sýn offers a one-month free SÝN+ subscription to new parents on parental leave, a ready-made new-parent hook; Síminn has Sjónvarp Símans Premium with Icelandic children's content. KrakkaRÚV is the free public option. Music streaming is dominated by foreign services (not listed).

- ✓ **Sýn (SÝN+ / Stöð 2)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — TV and streaming provider with the SÝN+ app; new parents on parental leave get a free one-month SÝN+ subscription ('betra fæðingarorlof'). · [syn.is](https://www.syn.is/) · **Discount:** One month free SÝN+ for new parents in parental leave · *Sign-up form on the front page; Sýn also sells internet and TV packages.*
- ✓ **Storytel Ísland** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Audiobook and e-book subscription with a children-only Kids Mode and Icelandic titles; family plan for 2-6 accounts. · [storytel.com/is](https://www.storytel.com/is) · **Discount:** 3-day free trial; Family plan from 3,990 kr/month · *Over 1 million titles; also a student plan.*
- ✓ **Forlagið - hljóðbækur** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Publisher's webshop selling Icelandic audiobooks and e-books for children and teens plus printed books for ages 0-5. · [forlagid.is](https://www.forlagid.is/)
- ✓ **Síminn - Sjónvarp Símans Premium** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Telecom with Sjónvarp Símans Premium streaming and news items on Icelandic-language children's content. · [siminn.is/sjonvarp](https://www.siminn.is/sjonvarp) · *Article 'Barnaefni á íslensku' (October 2026) on the site.*
- ✓ **KrakkaRÚV** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Public broadcaster's free children's streaming section with stories, music and educational programmes for the whole family. · [ruv.is/krakkaruv](https://www.ruv.is/krakkaruv) · **Discount:** Free · *Public service; not a buyer.*

### G15-N1 · Barnaafmæli, skírnarveislur og veisluskraut — Birthday and christening parties: venues and party supplies *(new — added during research)*

**Problem:** Planning the first birthday or a christening party  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Party venues around Reykjavík/Kópavogur are plentiful (Skopp, Ævintýraland, Smárabíó, Gerpla's party hall for about 90 guests) and Partyland is the party-supply specialist; Skopp customers get 20% off at Partyland. Most venues cater for ages 3+, so first-birthday and christening hosts mainly need a hall and decorations.

- ✓ **Partyland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Party-supply shop in Holtagarðar with themed decorations, balloons and tableware; webshop in Icelandic and English. · [partyland.is](https://partyland.is/) · **Discount:** 20% off party goods and balloons for customers who book a birthday at Skopp · *Open every day.*
- ✓ **Skopp (Skoppísland)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Trampoline-park birthday parties in Kópavogur for children, plus kids' sessions; guests bring their own cake. · [skoppisland.is/is/gardurinn/afmaeli](https://www.skoppisland.is/is/gardurinn/afmaeli) · **Discount:** 20% off at Partyland when booking a birthday · *Dalvegur 10-14, Kópavogur; glitter and confetti not allowed.*
- ✓ **Ævintýraland Kringlunnar - afmæli** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Two-hour birthday parties in Kringlan's play world for ages 3-9 with 8-24 guests; food from home or Domino's and Boom Boom boxes. · [kringlan.is/aevintyraland](https://www.kringlan.is/aevintyraland) · *Tableware rental 2,000-2,500 kr; candles not allowed.*
- ✓ **Smárabíó - afmæli og skemmtisvæði** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Cinema in Smáralind offering birthday packages that combine cinema, lasertag, arcade and Barnaland. · [smarabio.is/skemmtisvaedi/afmaeli](https://www.smarabio.is/skemmtisvaedi/afmaeli) · *Mainly for school-age children.*
- ✓ **Gerpla - veislusalur** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Gymnastics club's party hall at Versalir, Kópavogur, seating about 90, rented per day; suitable for christenings and larger family parties. · [gerpla.is/veislusalur](https://www.gerpla.is/veislusalur/) · *Hire about 95,000 kr per day; club hall (non-profit).*
- ✓ **Skemmtigarðurinn Gufunesi** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Activity park in Gufunes with a children's section for birthdays and holiday courses; more for school-age than toddlers. · [skemmtigardur.is](https://skemmtigardur.is/) · *Barnaafmæli page on site; courses for ages 6-13.*

### G15-N2 · Barnasýningar og leikhús fyrir yngstu — Children's shows, theatre & puppetry for toddlers *(new — added during research)*

**Problem:** A first cultural outing with a small child  
**Stig:** 1–3 ár · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Several Icelandic children's entertainers sell tickets directly: Skoppa og Skrítla (Skrítla ehf.), the touring Brúðubíllinn puppet theatre, Leikhópurinn Lotta's summer musicals, Sirkus Íslands, and the two big theatres with children's shows. Most are seasonal or touring, which suits a three-month coupon slot.

- ✓ **Skoppa og Skrítla (Skrítla ehf.)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Children's entertainment brand with an online shop (book, board game, backpack, merchandise) and live holiday parties, e.g. at Hof in Akureyri. · [skoppaogskritla.is](https://skoppaogskritla.is/) · *Operating company Skrítla ehf.; show dates not read from own site.*
- ✓ **Brúðubíllinn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Travelling puppet theatre with shows for preschools, town festivals and families; can be booked across the country; programme for 2026 and a shop. · [brudubillinn.is](https://brudubillinn.is/)
- ✓ **Leikhópurinn Lotta** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Outdoor children's musicals touring Iceland each summer, with shows at Lottutún in Elliðaárdalur and recordings on disc and streaming. · [leikhopurinnlotta.is](https://www.leikhopurinnlotta.is/) · **Discount:** Stofn members (Sjóvá): buy one ticket, get one free; children 2 and under free · *Stofn offer seen on Sjóvá's page.*
- ✓ **Sirkus Íslands** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Icelandic circus selling tickets online for a family circus show (5,900 kr per ticket). · [sirkus.is](https://sirkus.is/) · *Show 'Skinnsemi 2026'.*
- ✓ **Borgarleikhúsið - barnasýningar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — City theatre with children's productions on the small stage (e.g. 'Pétur og kötturinn Brandur') and a Christmas family show on the main stage. · [borgarleikhus.is](https://www.borgarleikhus.is/) · *Gift cards and theatre cards sold.*
- ✓ **Þjóðleikhúsið** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — National Theatre with children's and family productions (next big show 'Dýrin í Hálsaskógi') and a youth programme. · [leikhusid.is](https://leikhusid.is/) · *State theatre.*

## G16 · Bíll og ferðalög — Car & travel

### G16-01 · Fjölskyldubílar — New family cars (dealers)

**Problem:** Need a bigger, safer car  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Iceland's new-car market is run by a dozen or so importers/dealers, each holding several brands; nine of the main ones are listed. Brands not listed separately are covered by these dealers (e.g. Lexus under Toyota, Cupra under Hekla). No parent-specific discounts seen; Akureyri is served directly by Brimborg and Toyota.

- ✓ **Brimborg** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri) — Dealer for Ford, Volvo, Mazda, Citroën, Peugeot, Opel and Polestar; has a seven-seater category plus loans, leasing, trade-in and test drives. · [brimborg.is](https://www.brimborg.is) · *Brands: Ford, Volvo, Mazda, Citroën, Peugeot, Opel, Polestar; also used cars, rental and EV charging; Akureyri showroom at Tryggvabraut*
- ✓ **Bílaumboðið Askja** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Dealer for Mercedes-Benz, Kia, Honda and smart with electric models carrying the 400,000 kr EV subsidy; also used cars, parts and workshop. · [askja.is](https://www.askja.is) · *Brands: Mercedes-Benz (incl. vans), smart, Kia, Honda; head office Krókháls, branch in Reykjanesbær*
- ✓ **BL** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Importer/dealer for Land Rover, Hyundai, MG, Nissan, Hongqi and Subaru; EV offers with energy-fund grant; used cars via Bílaland. · [bl.is](https://www.bl.is) · *Brands: Land Rover, Hyundai, MG, Nissan, Hongqi, Subaru (subaru.is is BL); BMW also mentioned; no Akureyri showroom listed*
- ✓ **Hekla** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Dealer for Audi, Volkswagen, Škoda, Mitsubishi and GWM ORA, including 7-seat electric Škoda Peaq; financing page and used cars. · [hekla.is](https://www.hekla.is) · *Brands: Audi, Volkswagen, Škoda, Mitsubishi, GWM ORA; head office Þorraholt, Garðabær*
- ✓ **Toyota á Íslandi** (Heildsali/umboð · Landið allt (Garðabær, Akureyri, Reykjanesbær, Selfoss)) — New Toyotas from Yaris and Corolla to RAV4 and Land Cruiser; financing, KINTO long-term leasing, Toyota insurance and a 3-year service programme. · [toyota.is](https://www.toyota.is) · *Dealers in Kauptún, Akureyri, Reykjanesbær and Selfoss*
- ✓ **Bílabúð Benna** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Official dealer for Porsche and KGM (SsangYong) with showrooms, used cars, workshop and parts; prices cut after krona strengthened. · [benni.is](https://www.benni.is) · *Brands: Porsche, KGM; showrooms at Krókháls*
- ✓ **Ísband** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Dealer for Jeep, RAM, Fiat, Alfa Romeo and Leapmotor EVs; leasing, car loans, used-car deals and workshop. · [isband.is](https://www.isband.is) · *Brands: Leapmotor, Jeep, RAM, Fiat, Fiat Professional, Alfa Romeo; new-car showroom in Mosfellsbær*
- ✓ **Vatt** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Electric-car dealer for BYD, Maxus and Aiways, including the seven-seat BYD Tang family car; financing, workshop and parts. · [vatt.is](https://www.vatt.is) · *Brands: BYD (byd.is), Maxus, Aiways; OMODA, JAECOO and EXLANTIX planned; Skeifan, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Suzuki bílar** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + söluaðilar um land) — Suzuki importer with compact cars to family SUVs (Vitara, S-Cross, Across plug-in hybrid); financing section and price list. · [suzuki.is](https://www.suzuki.is/) · *Skeifan, Reykjavik; dealer-finder page, regional dealers not checked*

### G16-02 · Notaðir bílar og bílasölur — Used-car dealers

**Problem:** Cheaper upsizing  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 22

*Market:* Big market: the bilasolur.is dealer directory lists 82 used-car dealers (about 60 in the capital area). These 22 were opened and confirmed, with the five Akureyri dealers and regional ones included; many more exist (e.g. Bílamiðstöðin, Toppbílar, Sparibíll, Saga Motors, 100 bílar, K2 bílar, Bílafang, Bílás in Akranes). No parent-specific discounts seen; most advertise financing up to 100%.

- ✓ **Bílaland (BL)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — BL's used-car dealer with many makes (Audi, BMW, Toyota, Volvo, Tesla, Hyundai), consignment cars and an own financing page. · [bilaland.is](http://www.bilaland.is) · *Krókháls 7, Reykjavik; linked to importer BL*
- ✓ **Askja notaðir bílar** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Importer Askja's used-car sales of pre-owned cars, hybrids, EVs and vans, including consignment sales. · [notadir.is](https://www.notadir.is/) · *Krókháls, Reykjavik and Reykjanesbær*
- ✓ **Toyota Betri notaðir bílar** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Toyota's used-car dealer selling used Toyota, Lexus and other makes, with filters by price, year, mileage and fuel type and consignment cars. · [notadir.toyotakauptuni.is](http://notadir.toyotakauptuni.is/) · *Kauptún, Garðabær*
- ✓ **Bílabúð Benna notaðir bílar** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Used cars including 4x4s and electric vehicles from the Porsche/KGM importer; advertises a low-interest financing promotion. · [notadir.benni.is](https://notadir.benni.is/) · *Promotion advertised: 4.9% interest on financing; second site at Njarðarnes (Bb, betri bílar)*
- ✓ **Bílasala Reykjavíkur** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Inspected used cars with on-site financing via Arion, Ergo, Lykill and Landsbankinn; takes trade-ins and sells your car for you. · [br.is](http://www.br.is) · *Bíldshöfði, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Bílahöllin** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Used and some new cars including electric and hybrid models; listings show importer warranty where it still applies. · [bilahollin.is](http://www.bilahollin.is) · *Bíldshöfði, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Stóra bílasalan** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Large used-car dealer with many brands and fuel types, financing through partner banks and lenders, and a vehicle-registration page for sellers. · [stora.is](http://www.stora.is) · *Kletthálsi, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Borgarbílasalan** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Used cars plus motorcycles, ATVs, trailers and boats; financing through partner banks including 100% financing on some listings. · [borgarbilasalan.is](http://www.borgarbilasalan.is) · *Funahöfði, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Bílalind** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Used cars, SUVs, vans and pickups with financing of up to 100% through several banks and lenders. · [bilalind.is](http://www.bilalind.is) · *Kletthálsi, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Netbílar** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Used cars, SUVs, vans, electric and hybrid models with financing of up to 100% over five years. · [netbilar.is](http://www.netbilar.is) · *Núpalind, Kópavogur*
- ✓ **Bílamarkaðurinn** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur used-car dealer for cars, vans, caravans and trailers with a financing page and sell-your-car form; some cars under warranty. · [bilamarkadurinn.is](http://www.bilamarkadurinn.is) · *Smiðjuvegur, Kópavogur*
- ✓ **Bílaborg** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Dealer with used and new cars and several financing partners (Lykill, Landsbankinn, Ergo, Arion banki). · [bilaborg.is](http://www.bilaborg.is) · *Smiðshöfði, Reykjavik*
- ✓ **Hertz bílasala** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Used cars from Hertz's former rental fleet, mostly under maker's warranty, otherwise a Hertz warranty; financing and trade-ins offered. · [hertzbilasala.is](https://www.hertzbilasala.is) · *Hertz warranty up to 12 months or 15,000 km; Selhella, Hafnarfjörður*
- ✓ **Höldur Bílasala** (Verslun · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri dealer selling new and used cars across many price ranges; part of the Höldur group that also runs Bílaleiga Akureyrar. · [holdurbilasala.is](https://www.holdurbilasala.is/) · *Þórsstígur, Akureyri; financing and warranty not described on page*
- ✓ **Bílasala Akureyrar** (Verslun · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri dealer selling used and new vehicles including BYD models and imports; considers trade-ins on some listings. · [bilak.is](http://www.bilak.is) · *Freyjunes, Akureyri*
- ✓ **BB bílar (betri bílar)** (Verslun · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri used-car dealer advertising financing, including 90-100% loans on some cars. · [bbbilar.is](http://bbbilar.is) · *Gránufélagsgata, Akureyri*
- ✓ **Bílaríki** (Verslun · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri vehicle dealer with filters by year and mileage, selling used cars as well as motorcycles, trailers and tractors. · [bilariki.is](http://www.bilariki.is) · *Lónsbakki, Akureyri; financing not described on page*
- ✓ **Toyota Akureyri** (Verslun · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Toyota dealer in Akureyri selling new, special-offer and used Toyotas. · [toyotaakureyri.is](http://www.toyotaakureyri.is) · *Baldursnes, Akureyri; also in G16-01 under Toyota á Íslandi*
- ✓ **Bílasala Selfoss** (Verslun · Suðurland) — Selfoss dealer selling used and new cars with financing and vehicle-registration pages. · [bilasalaselfoss.is](http://www.bilasalaselfoss.is) · *Hrísmýri, Selfoss*
- ✓ **Bílakjarninn** (Verslun · Suðurnes) — Reykjanesbær used-car dealer with vans, SUVs, hybrids and electric cars and a financing section. · [bilakjarninn.is](http://www.bilakjarninn.is) · *Njarðarbraut, Reykjanesbær*
- ✓ **Bílasala Reykjaness** (Verslun · Suðurnes) — Reykjanesbær dealer for used and new cars, vans, trucks and motorcycles, with financing and a register-your-vehicle form. · [kefbilar.is](https://kefbilar.is/) · *Holtsgata, Reykjanesbær*
- ✓ **Bílasala Austurlands** (Verslun · Austurland) — Egilsstaðir dealer for new and used Toyota and Askja cars, consignment sales and an attached repair workshop. · [bva.is](http://www.bva.is) · *Miðás, Egilsstaðir; Ellingsen dealer*

### G16-03 · Bílaleiga, rekstrarleiga og bílalán — Car rental, leasing & car loans

**Problem:** Financing a family car  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11

*Market:* Three groups: lenders (the big banks, Lykill, Ergo), car rental firms with long-term/monthly options (Höldur, Avis, Hertz, Blue) and dealers with own leasing (Brimborg, Toyota/KINTO, Ísband). Many tourist-oriented rental firms exist but were left out as they target visitors; Sixt and KINTO sites could not be loaded. No parent-specific terms seen.

- ✓ **Landsbankinn - bílalán** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Car loans up to 80% of price over up to 8 years, free early repayment in the app, and green-loan discounts for electric cars. · [landsbankinn.is/einstaklingar/lan-og-heimildir/bilalan](https://www.landsbankinn.is/einstaklingar/lan-og-heimildir/bilalan) · **Discount:** Green loan: 50% off arrangement fee and up to 0.6 percentage-point rate discount for electric cars · *Same bank as in G13-02 (parental-leave mortgage relief)*
- ✓ **Arion banki - bílalán** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Car loans for new and used cars, caravans and trailers up to 80% of price over up to 7 years; arranged through licensed dealers. · [arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/lan/bilalan](https://www.arionbanki.is/einstaklingar/lan/bilalan/) · **Discount:** Better interest rates when buying an electric car · *Same bank as in G13-02; leasing handled by the bank's car team*
- ✓ **Ergo (Íslandsbanki)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Car loans for new and used vehicles via dealers, up to 85% of price over up to 8 years, 100% for cars with a track record, no early-repayment fee. · [ergo.is](https://www.ergo.is) · *Íslandsbanki's car-finance brand*
- ✓ **Lykill** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Car loans for individuals plus financing for caravans, motorhomes and other vehicles; discounts on green loans for electric, hydrogen and plug-in hybrid cars. · [lykill.is](https://www.lykill.is) · **Discount:** Green-loan discounts for low-emission cars · *Individual leasing not described on page*
- ✓ **Höldur - Bílaleiga Akureyrar** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + landið allt) — Akureyri-based car rental with short-term, monthly and long-term leasing (langtímaleiga), vans and rentals abroad; also runs a car dealership. · [holdur.is](https://www.holdur.is) · *Offices at Tryggvabraut and Akureyri Airport; sister firm Höldur Bílasala (G16-02)*
- ✓ **Hertz Ísland** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (Akureyri, Reykjavik, Keflavík og víðar)) — Rental across 11 locations with full-size cars and 7-17 seat passenger vans suited to families; economy to 4x4, electric and hybrid. · [hertz.is](https://www.hertz.is) · *Child-seat rental not stated on page; used cars via Hertz bílasala (G16-02)*
- ✓ **Avis Ísland** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Rentals of passenger cars, jeeps and electric cars with short-term, monthly and long-term options. · [avis.is](https://www.avis.is) · *Head office in Holtagarðar, Reykjavik; whether long-term is open to private customers not stated*
- ✓ **Blue Car Rental (Blue bílaleiga)** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes + Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Rental with Keflavík Airport station, long-term rentals for businesses and used-car sales with staff-inspected vehicles. · [blue.is](https://www.blue.is) · *Long-term rental shown for businesses; used cars also in G16-02 directory*
- ✓ **Brimborg - fjármögnun og leiga** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri) — Dealer offering car loans, hire purchase (kaupleiga), operating lease (rekstrarleiga), trade-in valuation and car insurance with its new and used cars. · [brimborg.is](https://www.brimborg.is) · *Same company as in G16-01; branches in Reykjavik and Akureyri*
- ✓ **Toyota á Íslandi - KINTO og fjármögnun** (Heildsali/umboð · Landið allt) — Financing for individuals and companies plus KINTO long-term leasing and Toyota insurance. · [toyota.is](https://www.toyota.is) · *Same company as in G16-01; kinto.is itself could not be loaded*
- ✓ **Ísband - leiga og bílalán** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Dealer offering operating lease (rekstrarleiga) and car loans alongside Jeep, RAM, Fiat, Alfa Romeo and Leapmotor models. · [isband.is](https://www.isband.is) · *Same company as in G16-01*

### G16-04 · Rafbílar og heimahleðsla — EVs & home charging

**Problem:** Running costs and charging at home  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 8

*Market:* Home charging is sold mainly by energy companies (ON, Orkusalan) and Ísorka, with electrical wholesalers (Ískraft, Reykjafell) behind installers; EV dealers are listed in G16-01. Orkan and N1 pages could not be read, and many independent electricians install chargers but could not be discovered without WebSearch.

- ✓ **Orka náttúrunnar (ON)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Home charging as a monthly subscription for houses and apartment buildings with 24/7 service, plus neighbourhood and fast chargers and the ON app. · [on.is](https://www.on.is) · *Heimahleðsla ON; whether installation is included not stated on page*
- ✓ **Orkusalan - HleðsluStuð** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Home EV charging as a monthly subscription (three charger tiers) with fixed-fee installation, operation and repairs included; for Orkusalan electricity customers. · [orkusalan.is/hledslustud](https://www.orkusalan.is/hledslustud) · *Entry tier from about 1,000 kr per month; houses and summer cottages*
- ✓ **Ísorka** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Sells home charging stations for all EVs with fixed-price installation incl. HMS notification and warranty; also full solutions and personal billing for apartment buildings. · [isorka.is](https://www.isorka.is) · *Advises housing associations on charging-infrastructure grants*
- ✓ **Ískraft (Húsasmiðjan)** (Verslun · Landið allt) — Electrical supplier with a 'Hleðsla' category offering a large selection of EV charging stations. · [iskraft.husa.is](https://iskraft.husa.is/) · *Unclear whether sold to private individuals or only trade; iskraft.is redirects here*
- ✓ **Vatt** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Electric-car-only dealer for BYD, Maxus and Aiways with financing, workshop, parts and battery information; includes seven-seat family models. · [vatt.is](https://www.vatt.is) · *Same company as in G16-01*
- ✓ **Brimborg - rafbílar og hleðsla** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri) — Dealer for Volvo, Polestar, Ford and others with EV models and an EV-charging service line alongside financing and workshop. · [brimborg.is](https://www.brimborg.is) · *Same company as in G16-01*
- ✓ **Bílaumboðið Askja - rafbílar og hleðslulausnir** (Heildsali/umboð · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Kia, Mercedes-Benz and smart electric models with 400,000 kr EV subsidy on selected cars and charging solutions for electric cars. · [askja.is](https://www.askja.is) · **Discount:** 400,000 kr EV subsidy (rafbílastyrkur) on selected models · *Same company as in G16-01*
- ○ **Norðurorka** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri utility whose electricity pages link to practical information on electric cars; home-charging offering not confirmed. · [no.is](https://no.is/) · *Local utility for the North Iceland edition; check what charging services it sells*

### G16-05 · Bílaþrif og djúphreinsun — Car valeting & interior cleaning

**Problem:** Spills, crumbs and car-seat mess  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Only one verified interior-cleaning specialist (Bílaspa, seat deep cleaning with pick-up and return); the others are wash stations or exterior detailing. Many small valeting businesses exist but could not be discovered without WebSearch.

- ✓ **Bílaspa ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Valeting with seat deep cleaning, detailed interior cleaning, full cleaning with pick-up and return, ozone odour treatment and wash and wax. · [bilaspa.is/blank-2](https://www.bilaspa.is/blank-2) · *Workshop at Eldshöfði, Reykjavik; online booking; best fit for spills and car-seat mess*
- ✓ **Löður** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurnes + Vestmannaeyjar) — Car-wash chain with 24/7 contactless stations, staffed stations and self-service bays; monthly subscription for unlimited washes via the Parka app. · [lodur.is](https://www.lodur.is) · *Subscription from about 6,900 kr per month; interior cleaning not mentioned; Akureyri station*
- ✓ **Bón og Þvottastöðin** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Automatic 8-step wash with wax and water-repellent coating, hand-drying, 12-wash cards and fleet accounts. · [bon.is](https://www.bon.is) · *Grjótháls, Reykjavik; interior deep cleaning not mentioned*
- ✓ **Detail Ísland** (Þjónusta · Suðurland + landið allt (söluaðilar)) — Paint correction and Gtechniq ceramic coating plus an online shop of detailing products; resellers in Akureyri, Reykjavik and elsewhere. · [detailing.is](https://www.detailing.is) · *Exterior-focused, based in Selfoss; interior cleaning not mentioned*
- ○ **Glans bílaþvottastöð (Olís)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri) — Car-wash brand listed under Olís's car-service section at its stations; Olís has several stations in Akureyri. · [olis.is/stodvar](https://www.olis.is/stodvar) · **Discount:** Olís and ÓB loyalty options (ÓB Frelsi, Olís lykill, Icelandair points) apply at the stations · *Seen only on Olís pages; glans.is itself did not load; interior cleaning not confirmed*

### G16-06 · Ferðaskrifstofur og fjölskylduferðir — Travel agencies — family holidays

**Problem:** Planning a holiday with a baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Outbound agencies are a mid-sized market; eight verified here, with Sumarferðir and Úrval Útsýn already advertising family packages and Heimsferðir, Aventura and Trans-Atlantic offering flights from Akureyri. Vita (Icelandair group) and Terra Nova could not be loaded (errors) and are not listed, so more exist.

- ✓ **Sumarferðir** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Describes itself as a family-friendly travel agency for over 20 years; flight-and-hotel packages to Tenerife, Orlando and more, plus charter flights. · [sumarferdir.is](https://www.sumarferdir.is) · **Discount:** Family deal 'TENERIFE 2+2' from 82,500 kr and a Christmas family trip; online club for best offers · *No specific baby policy stated on page*
- ✓ **Úrval Útsýn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Long-established agency with sun, city, golf, ski and cruise holidays, including a Family Tenerife package of 8 nights. · [uu.is](https://www.uu.is) · **Discount:** Family Tenerife package from 88,900 kr for 8 nights (as advertised) · *Deposit and payment plans offered*
- ✓ **Heimsferðir** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (m.a. flug frá Akureyri)) — Sun holidays, city breaks, ski trips, Christmas and group trips, flight seats and car rental, with 24/7 customer service and trips from Akureyri. · [heimsferdir.is](https://www.heimsferdir.is) · *No family-specific offer seen on front page*
- ✓ **Aventura** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (flug frá Akureyri og Egilsstöðum)) — Sun holidays, city trips, last-minute deals, Christmas and Easter trips and charter flights; booking search takes up to 4 children per room. · [aventura.is](https://www.aventura.is) · *General promo code seen, nothing family-specific; direct flights from Akureyri and Egilsstaðir*
- ✓ **Plúsferðir** (Þjónusta · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Online agency with sun and city trips, standalone flights and flight-and-hotel packages; deposit to secure a trip and the rest paid over time. · [plusferdir.is](https://www.plusferdir.is) · *Flexible payment is useful for families; no family offer seen*
- ✓ **Trans-Atlantic** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (flug frá Akureyri og Egilsstöðum)) — Agency for city, health and special-interest trips, graduation trips and hiking tours; some departures direct from Akureyri or Egilsstaðir. · [transatlantic.is](https://www.transatlantic.is) · *Aimed more at adults; no family focus seen*
- ✓ **Bændaferðir** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Package tours led by Icelandic guides, including cruises, North American and Christmas trips and group travel; also the Hey Iceland domestic brand. · [baendaferdir.is](https://www.baendaferdir.is) · *Adult-oriented guided tours; no family policy seen*
- ✓ **Mundo** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Group trips, ready-made trips to Spain, Portugal, Morocco and Japan, Spanish courses and youth programmes for ages 13-19. · [mundo.is](https://www.mundo.is) · *Educational and group focus; no family holidays seen*
- ✓ **Hey Iceland** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Local agency booking accommodation across Iceland (hotels, farm stays, cottages) and tours such as hot springs and lava show. · [heyiceland.is](https://www.heyiceland.is) · *Brand of Bændaferðir; mainly for visitors but usable for domestic family breaks; also relevant to G16-09*

### G16-07 · Flug með ungbarn — Airlines & baby flight policies

**Problem:** Flying with a baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* Tiny market: Icelandair (international and domestic, own site blocks automated reading) and the regional Norlandair are the only scheduled Icelandic carriers found; PLAY has collapsed, Ernir's old domain now redirects to a charter broker (Air Broker Iceland) and Mýflug's site was unreachable. Adjacent players are the airport operators (Keflavík and Isavia's domestic airports incl. Akureyri), which are the only other plausible advertisers; Fríhöfnin and Flybus pages showed no baby-specific offer.

- ○ **Icelandair** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Iceland's main airline for international and domestic flights, including from Akureyri; infant fares, bassinet and baby-equipment rules not verified because site blocked. · [icelandair.com/is-is](https://www.icelandair.com/is-is/) · *Own site returns an access error to automated readers; URL seen via redirect from icelandair.is; verify baby policy pages before approaching*
- ✓ **Norlandair** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Regional airline flying from Akureyri and Reykjavik to Vestmannaeyjar, Þórshöfn, Vopnafjörður, Grímsey, Gjögur, Bíldudalur and Greenland; infants and children fly at reduced fares. · [norlandair.is](https://www.norlandair.is) · **Discount:** Infants under 2: 90% off adult fare, 20 kg baggage plus a stroller or car seat; children 2-11: 50% off · *Also charter and cargo; Loftbrú fare-discount scheme for residents mentioned*
- ✓ **Keflavík Airport (Isavia)** (Þjónusta · Suðurnes + landið allt) — State-owned airport operator with a travelling-with-children section and a children's section on its site, aiming at pleasant waits for families. · [kefairport.is](https://www.kefairport.is) · *Detail pages on baby facilities not reached; operator is state-owned*
- ✓ **Isavia Innanlandsflugvellir (Akureyrarflugvöllur o.fl.)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri + landið allt) — Isavia unit running Reykjavík, Akureyri (also international), Egilsstaðir and other domestic airports; its 2026 fee schedule exempts children under 2 from passenger and security fees. · [innanlandsflugvellir.is](http://www.innanlandsflugvellir.is/) · *State-owned; site has per-airport pages for departures, parking and services but family or baby facilities were not checked; the relevant airport for Akureyri and North Iceland parents.*

### G16-08 · Ferðatryggingar — Travel insurance

**Problem:** Cover for family trips  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 8

*Market:* Four non-life insurers sell travel cover (Sjóvá, Vörður, VÍS and TM, whose travel products show only as terms documents on its site), plus banks that bundle card-linked travel insurance (Landsbankinn and Arion via Vörður, Íslandsbanki via VÍS) and the Aur app (Kvika). The underwriter pool is small, so the banks are distributors rather than separate insurers; Verna stopped selling insurance in January 2026. Family or child cover rules were not stated on the pages read.

- ✓ **Sjóvá** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Insurer with Ferðavernd travel cover inside its home policy for short family trips, standalone Ferðatrygging and emergency assistance abroad. · [sjova.is/tryggingar/einstaklingar/ferdalog](https://www.sjova.is/tryggingar/einstaklingar/ferdalog) · **Discount:** Stofn loyalty programme (benefits seen on page are car-related: roadside assistance) · *Stofn customers are a warm lead; family/child cover details on sub-pages not read*
- ✓ **Vörður** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Travel insurance covering medical costs and emergency transport abroad, accidents and luggage; children's insurance and cancellation cover are separate products. · [vordur.is/tryggingar/ferdalog-og-ahugamal/ferdatrygging](https://www.vordur.is/tryggingar/ferdalog-og-ahugamal/ferdatrygging) · **Discount:** Arion customers can get up to 7% back on insurance with no claims (general, not travel-specific) · *Policy excludes childbirth and illness in the 9th month of pregnancy - relevant for expecting parents*
- ✓ **VÍS** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Insurer whose FAQ lists travel insurance (Ferðatryggingar) alongside child insurance and family/home contents cover; online quotes. · [vis.is/algengar-spurningar](https://www.vis.is/algengar-spurningar/) · *Travel product pages not found; details unverified*
- ✓ **TM tryggingar** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Insurer (sold through Landsbankinn too) whose terms list travel accident, cancellation, medical-costs-abroad and baggage insurance, plus card-linked travel cover and Aur Ferðatrygging. · [tm.is/skilmalar](https://www.tm.is/skilmalar) · *Main page shows home insurance and a quote form; travel products appear mainly as terms documents, so check the current product page. Landsbankinn sells TM policies and a child insurance (Barnatrygging) also valid abroad.*
- ✓ **Aur (Kvika banki) - Ferðatrygging** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Payment app selling its own travel insurance: medical costs abroad, accident, baggage, cancellation, trip interruption, delays and liability cover, bought in the app. · [aur.is/ferdatrygging](https://aur.is/ferdatrygging) · *Aur is a Kvika banki brand; TM and SOS International are named in the terms. A comparison site quoted 990 kr for the Aur Núll card (unverified); who counts as covered is not spelled out.*
- ✓ **Landsbankinn (tryggingar og kortatryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Bank selling TM's family, home and vehicle insurance, and bundling Vörður travel insurance with its General, Gold, Platinum and Premium credit cards. · [landsbankinn.is/tryggingar](https://www.landsbankinn.is/tryggingar) · **Discount:** 'Fjölskylduleið' package: free time with specialists and a refund after a claim-free year · *Card travel-insurance terms sit on vordur.is information documents; the tryggingar page does not mention travel or child insurance, but Landsbankinn's information sheets for travel accident, cancellation and child insurance name TM as insurer.*
- ✓ **Arion banki (kortatryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Bank whose credit cards (e.g. Premía, Platinum) include Vörður travel insurance in the annual card fee; holiday-tips page for cardholders. · [arionbanki.is/en/individuals/individuals-cards/credit-cards/tips-for-your-holiday](https://www.arionbanki.is/en/individuals/individuals-cards/credit-cards/tips-for-your-holiday) · **Discount:** Travel insurance included in the annual credit card fee · *Coverage varies by card; spouse and children cover not stated on the pages read; terms are on Vörður information documents.*
- ✓ **Íslandsbanki (kortatryggingar)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Bank whose credit cards carry travel and car-rental insurance; the card page says VÍS is responsible for all Íslandsbanki credit card travel insurance. · [islandsbanki.is/en/product/cards/platinum-card](https://islandsbanki.is/en/product/cards/platinum-card) · *The Platinum card page says the product is being phased out and closed to new applications; which current cards carry travel insurance and whether children are covered was not confirmed.*

### G16-09 · Fjölskylduvæn gisting innanlands — Family-friendly accommodation in Iceland (hotels, cabins)

**Problem:** Holidays at home with a baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 13

*Market:* Large, fragmented market (hundreds of hotels, guesthouses, farm stays and cabins). Thirteen of the more family-relevant or chain operators were opened; Icelandair Hotels and Hótel Edda sites would not load, and union holiday-house portals (orlofshús) were not reachable. Few operators advertise explicit baby or kids-stay-free terms on front pages; family rooms were seen at Rangá, Örk, Eldhestar, Borgarnes and Hamar.

- ✓ **Íslandshótel (Fosshótel)** (Keðja · Landið allt (Reykjavík, Norðurland, Austurland, Suðurland, Vesturland)) — Largest hotel group, with Fosshótel in Húsavík, Mývatn, East fjords, the south and west plus Reykjavik hotels; seasonal offers and restaurants. · [islandshotel.is](https://www.islandshotel.is) · **Discount:** 15% off for booking 100+ days ahead for 3+ nights in Reykjavik (general, not family) · *No family-room or kids-stay-free policy shown on front page*
- ✓ **Keahotels** (Keðja · Akureyri + Norðurland + Reykjavík + Suðurland) — Ten hotels with over 800 rooms incl. Hotel Kea in Akureyri, Siglo in Siglufjörður, Katla in Vík and Grímsborgir in Grímsnes. · [keahotels.is](https://www.keahotels.is) · *Family details are on individual hotel pages; key operator for Akureyri and North Iceland*
- ✓ **Hótel Akureyri** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Family-operated four-star hotel spread over seven houses in central Akureyri with two restaurants, café and bar. · [hotel-akureyri.com](https://www.hotel-akureyri.com/) · **Discount:** Direct booking: welcome drink and first access to free room upgrades · *hotelakureyri.is redirects here; family rooms not mentioned on front page*
- ✓ **Húsafell** (Þjónusta · Vesturland) — Hotel with 48 rooms plus cabins with kitchen and hot tub, campsite, pool, golf, playground and activities; offers a family Northern Lights adventure package. · [husafell.is](https://www.husafell.is) · **Discount:** Family and seasonal packages (e.g. 2-day Northern Lights family adventure) · *Cabins suit families with a baby (kitchen); Húsafell in Borgarfjörður*
- ✓ **Hótel Örk** (Þjónusta · Suðurland) — Hotel with very spacious family rooms, a swimming-pool area and a special-offers section. · [hotelork.is](https://www.hotelork.is) · *Hveragerði; kids-stay-free not mentioned*
- ✓ **Eldhestar** (Þjónusta · Suðurland) — Horse-riding tours from half-day to 8 days plus a hotel and guesthouse with a large family room; rides for all levels. · [eldhestar.is](https://www.eldhestar.is) · *Hveragerði area; not family-specific tours*
- ✓ **Hótel Rangá** (Þjónusta · Suðurland) — Upscale country hotel in South Iceland whose Deluxe Superior room is described as a great choice for families. · [hotelranga.is](https://www.hotelranga.is) · *Premium prices; no family-specific offers listed*
- ✓ **Hótel Borgarnes** (Þjónusta · Vesturland) — Hotel with a quadruple family room of about 35 m2 and 3-6 person apartments with kitchens; booking form accepts children 0-11. · [hotelborgarnes.is](https://www.hotelborgarnes.is) · *No child pricing or cot information on page*
- ✓ **Hótel Hamar** (Þjónusta · Vesturland) — Hotel with a family room (extra-large double bed plus sofa bed), a suite and deluxe cabins with sofa beds, plus an offers page. · [hotelhamar.is](https://www.hotelhamar.is) · *Cots and kids-stay-free not mentioned*
- ✓ **Hótel Selfoss** (Þjónusta · Suðurland) — South Iceland hotel with Superior rooms for up to three guests, plus gift cards and special offers. · [hotelselfoss.is](https://www.hotelselfoss.is) · *No dedicated family rooms seen*
- ✓ **Centerhotels** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Nine hotels in central Reykjavik incl. Þingholt apartments for cooking and sleeping; spas, hot tubs and direct-booking discounts. · [centerhotels.com](https://www.centerhotels.com) · **Discount:** Direct booking discounts of up to 30%, welcome drink and late check-out (general) · *Family policy to be checked in FAQ*
- ✓ **Hostel.is** (Þjónusta · Landið allt (m.a. Dalvík, Vík, Höfn, Seyðisfjörður)) — Network of 18 hostels, including options billed for family fun, in Reykjavik, Dalvík near Akureyri, the south, east and west. · [hostel.is](https://www.hostel.is) · **Discount:** Book-direct saving offered · *Family rooms and kids prices not listed on front page*
- ✓ **Hey Iceland** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Books handpicked accommodation across Iceland - hotels, guesthouses, farm stays, cottages and apartments - plus self-drive tours and day activities. · [heyiceland.is](https://www.heyiceland.is) · *Also listed in G16-06; farm stays and cottages suit families*

### G16-10 · Passamyndir og vegabréf fyrir börn — Passport photos & passports for babies

**Problem:** First trip abroad  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9 (+2 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Public side (Þjóðskrá, sýslumenn): the applicant, child included, is photographed at the sýslumaður, and a photographer can alternatively send a digital photo straight to the office. Commercial side: seven studios in the capital area, one in Selfoss and self-service booths in Smáralind take passport/ID photos; only Superstudio explicitly offers baby passport photos (infants under 6 months), and Krissý and Myndó also photograph newborns. No working provider found in Akureyri (Pedromyndir closed, one photographer stepped back in 2023, Hans Petersen's site shows 'store unavailable'), so Akureyri parents use the sýslumaður.

- ✓ **Þjóðskrá Íslands** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — National registry that issues Icelandic passports and ID cards; page states passports take up to 4 working days and links to application, pickup and validity pages. · [skra.is/folk/vegabref-og-onnur-skilriki/vegabref](https://www.skra.is/folk/vegabref-og-onnur-skilriki/vegabref/) · *Public body - not an advertiser; child-passport and photo rules not on the page read*
- ○ **Sýslumenn (district commissioners)** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt (Akureyri og önnur umdæmi)) — District commissioner offices where passport applications are handed in; syslumenn.is redirects to this Island.is page, which blocked automated reading. · [island.is/s/syslumenn](https://island.is/s/syslumenn) · *Public body; passport role not confirmed on a page I could read*
- ✓ **Superstudio (Snorrabraut)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík studio taking Icelandic passport photos and sending them to the district commissioner; offers baby passport photos for infants under 6 months plus newborn, maternity and family sessions. · [jonpall.is/passamyndir](https://www.jonpall.is/passamyndir) · **Discount:** 10% discount for families with three or more members (also seniors and disabled people) · *Passport photo 5,900 kr incl. six prints and a digital copy; page said booking for the service was 'currently closed' when read - confirm before approaching.*
- ✓ **Passamyndir ljósmyndastofa (Hlíðasmári)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Dedicated passport-photo studio in Kópavogur, opposite the district commissioner's office; photos for visas, driving licences, CVs and ID, weekdays 8:30-16:00. · [passamyndir7.wixsite.com/passamyndir](https://passamyndir7.wixsite.com/passamyndir) · *General price 6,500 kr (6,000 kr for pensioners and disability pensioners); page does not mention babies or Icelandic passports specifically; also on Facebook.*
- ✓ **Ljósmyndir Rutar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík (105) studio doing photos for Icelandic passports and ID cards (digital photo sent to the district commissioner), foreign passports and visas, driving licences and bank cards. · [rut.is/passamyndir](http://www.rut.is/passamyndir) · *Icelandic passport/ID photo 6,900 kr; weekdays 10-17, booking by phone or e-mail; babies not mentioned on the page.*
- ✓ **Litmynd Ljósmyndastofa (Spöngin)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Photo studio in Spöngin, Reykjavík, stating it takes passport photos for all passports and ID cards; also school photos and confirmation cards. · [litmynd.is](https://www.litmynd.is/) · *Located between Hagkaup and Domino's in Spöngin (Grafarvogur); no baby or newborn sessions mentioned; price list on a separate page.*
- ✓ **Myndó ljósmyndastofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Mosfellsbær)) — Mosfellsbær studio for passport-style photos (passports, driving licences, CVs, staff ID) with separate newborn and children photography. · [myndo.is/index.php/36-articles/108-passamyndir](http://myndo.is/index.php/36-articles/108-passamyndir) · *Þverholt, Mosfellsbær; price list on a separate page; the passport page does not describe photos of infants.*
- ✓ **Barna & fjölskylduljósmyndir (Kópavogur)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur children's and family photo studio listing passport photos next to newborn, children and family sessions, plus gift vouchers. · [ljosmyndir.net](https://www.ljosmyndir.net/) · *Prices not read (page long); a directory listing showed passport packages from about 3,990 kr to 7,990 kr with prints - unverified.*
- ✓ **Krissý Ljósmyndastúdíó** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (not stated)) — Studio for pregnancy, baby and children portraits (since 2007) that also takes passport photos and sends them straight to the district commissioner for passports. · [krissy.is/verdlisti/passamyndataka](https://krissy.is/verdlisti/passamyndataka/) · *Passport photo listed at 12,300 kr (e-mailed copy); town not stated on the pages read and the site is marked under construction.*
- ✓ ⚠ **Ljósmyndastofa Suðurlands** (Þjónusta · Suðurland) — Selfoss studio taking photos for Icelandic passports, driving licences and other ID by appointment, plus portrait and family photography. · [lydur.is/lj%C3%B3smyndastofa-su%C3%B0urlands](https://www.lydur.is/lj%C3%B3smyndastofa-su%C3%B0urlands) · *7,000 kr incl. six prints, e-mailed digital copy 1,500 kr; baby photography not mentioned on the page.*
- ○ **Passamyndir (ljósmyndakassar í Smáralind)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Two self-service passport-photo booths on Smáralind's first floor, available during mall opening hours, for quick passport-style photos. · [smaralind.is/fyrirtaeki/passamyndir](https://www.smaralind.is/fyrirtaeki/passamyndir) · *Seen only as a Smáralind tenant listing: no prices, and no word on Icelandic passports or babies; relation to the Hlíðasmári studio of the same name unclear.*

### G16-N1 · Dekk og hjólbarðaverkstæði — Tyres & car service (winter tyres, tyre changes) *(new — added during research)*

**Problem:** Keeping the family car safe on icy roads and serviced, with minimal waiting with a baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6

*Market:* Not covered by the assigned list but a real recurring need for parents: winter-tyre changes and car safety. Five tyre specialists verified (two with Akureyri branches) plus N1 (site blocked). Dealer workshops are covered under G16-01 brands. More independent tyre shops exist.

- ✓ **Dekkjahöllin** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Austurland + Suðurnes) — Tyre chain selling summer, winter and all-season tyres with tyre changing, oil service, tyre storage hotel and micro-siping. · [dekkjahollin.is](https://www.dekkjahollin.is) · *Branches in Reykjavik, Garðabær, Reykjanesbær, Akureyri (Draupnisgata) and Egilsstaðir; no loyalty club seen*
- ✓ **Sólning** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Tyre sales and fitting without appointment, tyre storage hotel, wheel alignment, micro-cutting and general car service. · [solning.is](https://www.solning.is) · **Discount:** A 20% discount banner on the front page (conditions not shown) · *Sites in Reykjavik, Kópavogur, Hafnarfjörður and Njarðvík; no Akureyri branch*
- ✓ **MAX1 Bílavaktin** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Tyre shop and car service with winter, studded and EV tyres, tyre fitting, wheel alignment, brakes, batteries and roadside assistance; online shop. · [max1.is](https://www.max1.is) · **Discount:** Sale-period offer of 33% off summer tyres and wiper blades; free wheel-alignment check with new Nokian tyres · *Workshops in Reykjavik and Hafnarfjörður; no Akureyri location seen*
- ✓ **Klettur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri) — Tyres for passenger cars and heavy vehicles with online booking for tyre changes, tyre-storage hotel, oil changes and parts store. · [klettur.is](https://www.klettur.is) · *Akureyri workshop at Goðanes; also trucks and machinery, so passenger-car focus varies by site*
- ✓ **Pitstop** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Hafnarfjörður tyre workshop offering tyre changing; further services not shown on the page read. · [pitstop.is](https://www.pitstop.is) · *Page content minimal*
- ○ **N1 hjólbarðar** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Fuel-station chain whose tyre web shop is the destination of dekk.is; offers tyres and tyre service at its stations. · [vefverslun.n1.is/voruflokkur/hjolbardar-og-tengdar-vorur](https://vefverslun.n1.is/voruflokkur/hjolbardar-og-tengdar-vorur) · *Site blocked to automated reading, so services and Akureyri branches unverified*

## G17 · Myndir, minningar og gjafir — Photos, keepsakes & gifts

### G17-01 · Nýburamyndatökur — Newborn photography

**Problem:** Capturing the first days  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 18

*Market:* Large market of mostly one-person studios (many trade under the owner's name; only brand names recorded). Typical newborn session about 40-70 thousand ISK, best within 2 weeks of birth; combined pregnancy+newborn bundles are common. Only one Akureyri studio found; more exist than listed.

- ✓ **Helga Dögg Ljósmyndir** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Cosy Reykjavík studio for newborn, pregnancy, first-year, child and family photography; newsletter for offers. · [helgadogg.is](https://www.helgadogg.is/) · *Studio at Seljavegur, Reykjavík. Also confirmation and wedding photos.*
- ✓ **Eirikur Ingi Photography** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík (Grafarvogur) studio doing newborn, infant (about 4 months), pregnancy and family sessions; gift vouchers and prints available. · [eirikuringi.is/thjonustur](https://www.eirikuringi.is/thjonustur) · **Discount:** Discount when pregnancy and newborn sessions are booked together (amount not stated) · *Studio or outdoor sessions.*
- ✓ **Rutenberg ljósmyndastofa** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík photographer offering bump and newborn shoots, family and child portraits, in or outside the studio. · [rutenberg.is/bumbumyndir-og-n%C3%BDbura](https://www.rutenberg.is/bumbumyndir-og-n%C3%BDbura) · *Studio in Ármúli, Reykjavík. Price list on site.*
- ✓ **Barna & fjölskylduljósmyndir ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur studio for baby (ungbarna), child and family portraits, with gift cards and prints. · [ljosmyndir.net](https://www.ljosmyndir.net/) · *Studio in Kópavogur; also confirmation, graduation and passport photos.*

### G17-03 · Fjölskyldumyndatökur — Family photographers

**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 18

### G17-04 · Myndabækur, albúm og prentun — Photo books, albums & prints

**Problem:** What to do with thousands of phone photos  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11

*Market:* No large dedicated photo-book specialist, but several routes: print portals Pixlar, Prentmet Oddi and Prentagram (photo books from 3,990 kr), photo shops Ljósmyndavörur and Fotomax (prints, albums, canvas), stationery chains A4 and Penninn Eymundsson (albums), and photographers bundling books with baby or family sessions (AKS, Harpa Hrund, Rut and Silja). Hans Petersen has stopped retail (closure notice, June 2025; Fotomax took over its photo services).

- ✓ **Pixlar Ljósmynda og Prentþjónusta** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík photo and print service: photo prints, albums, canvas and large-format prints, frames and an online shop. · [pixlar.is](https://www.pixlar.is/) · *Located at Suðurlandsbraut, Reykjavík; photo books not explicitly stated.*
- ✓ **Prentmet Oddi ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurland + Austurland) — Online design portal lets customers lay out photo books, cards, calendars and photo prints for printing. · [prentmetoddi.is](https://www.prentmetoddi.is/) · *Branches in Reykjavík, Selfoss, Akureyri and Egilsstaðir; site mainly targets businesses.*

- ✓ **Prentagram** (Netverslun · Netverslun) — Icelandic photo-printing webshop: small digital prints from 75 kr, retro prints, magnets, calendars, postcards and photo books from 3,990 kr. · [printem.is/pages/verd](https://printem.is/pages/verd) · *Postage included for postcards; no store address shown.*

### G17-10 · Fæðingarkort og tilkynningar — Birth announcement & thank-you cards

**Problem:** Telling family and friends  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 8

*Market:* No Icelandic printer markets birth announcements by name; the options are general printers and design shops with invitation, christening and thank-you cards (Pixel, PRENT, Garðbúinn, Pedromyndir's online card portal) alongside the four already listed. Prentmet Oddi's Egilsstaðir branch (Héraðsprent) sells framed birth-detail prints and newborn text cards; a forum thread suggests announcements are largely made on Facebook.

- ✓ **Svansprent ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur printer with occasion cards (greeting and invitation cards for all occasions), a webshop and quote requests. · [svansprent.is](https://www.svansprent.is/) · *Birth and thank-you cards not named.*
- ✓ **Prentmet Oddi ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurland + Austurland) — Online design portal for custom cards, photo books and photo prints; branches in Reykjavík, Selfoss, Akureyri and Egilsstaðir. · [prentmetoddi.is](https://www.prentmetoddi.is/) · *Mostly business-oriented; cards for individuals not described.*

- ✓ **Stafræna Prentsmiðjan ehf. (Prentun.is)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Hafnarfjörður digital printer offering invitation cards (Boðskort) and business cards. · [prentun.is](https://www.prentun.is/) · *Individual customers not explicitly mentioned.*
- ✓ **Pixel prentþjónusta** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Reykjavík print service with a webshop for photo books, greeting cards, photo prints and invitations (boðskort) in several sizes with envelopes. · [pixel.is/pages/bodskort](https://pixel.is/pages/bodskort) · *Separate business from Pixlar (different domain); birth announcements and thank-you cards not named.*

- ○ **Pedromyndir** (Netverslun · Akureyri + netverslun) — Online card portal: choose a template, photos and text for christening cards, thank-you cards, invitations and more; a proof is sent for approval. · [verslun.pedro.is/kortavefur](https://verslun.pedro.is/kortavefur/) · *Akureyri photo business; its retail page says the shop has closed ('Verslunin hefur lokað'), so confirm the card portal is still operating.*

### G17-11 · Gjafakörfur og gjafapakkar fyrir nýbura — Baby gift baskets & gift sets

**Problem:** What to give new parents  
**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Ready-made newborn gift sets are rare: Gift Reykjavík (baskets and boxes), Björk Store (Sængurgjöf set), Petit (boxed gift sets), DIMM and Lyfja's free Vöggugjöf; Bónus runs a Barnabónus newborn box, and baby shops (Minimo, Bíum Bíum, Polka) have gift collections. Florists' baskets (Garðheimar, Óskablóm) are generic gourmet, not baby-specific.

- ✓ **DIMM** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Design shop with newborn gifts (Sængurgjafir), christening gifts and feeding sets sold in gift boxes. · [dimm.is/barnid](https://www.dimm.is/barnid) · **Discount:** Mailing list announces sales and discounts; free shipping over 15,000 kr · *Store at Ármúli.*
- ✓ **Lyfja** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Pharmacy chain whose free Vöggugjöf (cradle gift) box for expectant parents and babies up to 3 months holds baby-care items and a 66°Norður voucher. · [lyfja.is/voggugjof](https://www.lyfja.is/voggugjof) · **Discount:** Free gift for expectant/new parents; 4,000 kr 66°Norður voucher inside (currently sold out, mailing list) · *One gift per child; 45 pharmacies nationwide per site header.*
- ✓ **Gift Reykjavík (Leit Leið ehf.)** (Netverslun · Reykjavík + netverslun) — Gift basket and box shop with ready-made newborn set (soft toy and rattle, 7,950 kr.), baskets for young children, a gift box for mum and build-your-own baskets. · [giftreykjavik.is/is/product-category/gjafakorfur/forsmidad](https://www.giftreykjavik.is/is/product-category/gjafakorfur/forsmidad/) · *Also has a 'Leit Verslun' where gifts can be hand-picked; delivery terms not seen.*
- ✓ **Björk Store** (Netverslun · Netverslun (Hafnarfjörður; Dropp um allt land)) — Baby-goods webshop selling a 'Sængurgjöf' set (merino hat, organic blanket, teether, 11,990 kr., about 15% below parts) and a blanket-and-teddy gift set. · [bjorkstore.is/products/saengurgjof](https://bjorkstore.is/products/saengurgjof) · **Discount:** Set priced about 15% below the individual items · *Sold out when checked; orders can be collected in Hafnarfjörður on Thursday evenings; free shipping over 12,000 kr.*
- ✓ **Petit** (Verslun · Netverslun + verslun) — Baby shop with a baby-shower gifts range of ready-made sets and boxes (MarMar, CamCam, Konges Sløjd, Liewood) from about 10,000 kr., plus christening gifts. · [petit.is/collections/baby-shower-gifts](https://petit.is/collections/baby-shower-gifts) · *Also sells memory books and christening clothes; gift wrapping 250 kr.*
- ✓ **Minimo** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Children's shop with 'Ungbarnagjafir' and 'Gjafir 0-1 árs' collections: Mushie feeding and play sets, a Filibabba memory box and e-gift cards. · [minimo.is/collections/ungbarnagjafir](https://minimo.is/collections/ungbarnagjafir) · *No ready-made newborn baskets seen; free shipping over 10,000 kr.*
- ✓ **Bíum Bíum** (Verslun · Netverslun + verslun) — Baby store with 'Babyshowergjafir' and 'Skírnar- og nafngiftargjafir' collections (soft toys, blankets, bags, memory book) across about 40 brands. · [biumbium.is/collections/babyshowergjafir](https://biumbium.is/collections/babyshowergjafir) · *No ready-made gift sets seen apart from a toy basket and boxed items.*
- ✓ **Polka** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Reykjavík) + netverslun) — Baby and children's shop with a 'Gjafaöskjur og tækifæriskort' category and feeding/gift sets, plus newborn sizes and baby nests. · [polka.is](https://polka.is/) · *Brands incl. Love To Dream, Miniware, NENO, Baby Steps.*
- ✓ **Bónus (Barnabónus)** (Keðja · Landið allt (Bónus-verslanir)) — Discount supermarket chain running Barnabónus: new parents apply online and collect a newborn box in a Bónus store. · [bonus.is/barnabonus](https://www.bonus.is/barnabonus) · **Discount:** Barnabónus newborn box (apply online, collect in store) · *mbl.is reported in April 2025 about 5,000 free boxes; page footer shows 2026 but box contents and current status not confirmed.*

### G17-12 · Gjafabréf og upplifanir fyrir foreldra — Gift vouchers & experiences for new parents

**Problem:** A treat for the parents  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 7

*Market:* Voucher issuers are plentiful: restaurant and general gift-card platforms plus shopping-centre cards, and hot-spring spas (Reykjavík area, Akureyri, Mývatn). None showed a dedicated new-parent offer; Gjafabref has a 'Fjölskyldan' persona voucher.

- ✓ **Gjafabref (Óskaskrín ehf.)** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Gift-voucher service whose one voucher works at 250+ partner shops and brands, with persona options including Fjölskyldan (family) and Parið (couple). · [gjafabref.is](https://www.gjafabref.is/) · *Partners seen include Hagkaup, Epal, 66°Norður and Orkan.*
- ✓ **Dineout gjafabréf** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Restaurant marketplace selling gift cards (10,000-20,000 kr or custom) valid at 400+ restaurants, cafés and bars. · [dineout.is](https://dineout.is/) · *A date-night treat for parents; no parent-specific offer seen.*
- ✓ **Gjafakort Smáralindar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Shopping-centre gift card (5,000-20,000 kr or custom) valid in all Smáralind shops, restaurants and services, storable in a phone wallet. · [smaralind.is/gjafakort](https://www.smaralind.is/gjafakort) · *Sold online.*
- ✓ **Gjafakort Kringlunnar** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kringlan gift card valid in its shops and restaurants, plus Bónus, Hagkaup and Sambíó; sent by SMS/email or printed. · [gjafakort.kringlan.is](https://gjafakort.kringlan.is/) · *Card in a deck costs 890 kr.*
- ✓ **Forest Lagoon (Skógarböðin)** (Þjónusta · Akureyri) — Akureyri forest-side hot-spring spa selling gift cards and season passes; children 5 and under free. · [forestlagoon.is](https://www.forestlagoon.is/) · *Admission tiers 7,490-11,210 kr.*
- ✓ **Hvammsvík Hot Springs** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Hvalfjörður sea-side hot springs selling gift vouchers, a 10-visit card, accommodation vouchers and a holiday gift basket. · [hvammsvik.com/is](https://hvammsvik.com/is) · *About 45 minutes from Reykjavík; occasional promotion banners.*
- ✓ **Earth Lagoon Mývatn (Jarðböðin)** (Þjónusta · Norðurland) — Mývatn nature baths (formerly Mývatn Nature Baths) with a gift card section on the booking site. · [earthlagoon.is](https://www.earthlagoon.is/) · *Children 5 and under free with an adult.*

## G18 · Skírn og veislur — Christening & celebrations

### G18-01 · Skírnarkjólar og hátíðarföt barna — Christening gowns & formal kids' wear

**Problem:** What the baby wears at the christening  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 9

*Market:* Dedicated christening wear is sold by Petit plus kids' and occasion shops (Minimo, Loforð, Brúðhjón, Móðurást, Polka), a few makers (Textíll, a Facebook knitter) and Akureyri's Kara Kids (general baby wear); big chains (Lindex, H&M, Zara, Name It, Ígló&Indí) could not be read, and parishes lend gowns, so the slot is contestable but not crowded.

- ✓ **Petit** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Children's clothing shop in Ármúli with a christening clothing (Skírnarfatnaður) category and dressy outfits (Spariföt) for boys and girls; webshop. · [petit.is](https://www.petit.is/) · **Discount:** Some items marked 15% off; no standing parent discount seen · *Also baby gear and nursery items.*
- ✓ **Minimo** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Children's shop and webshop with a SKÍRN category; organic-cotton Christa christening gown (12,990 kr., sizes 62-74) plus baby and kids' clothing. · [minimo.is/products/christa-skirnarkjoll-sea-salt](https://minimo.is/products/christa-skirnarkjoll-sea-salt) · *Brands incl. That's Mine, Filibabba, Fixoni, Melton, Müsli by Green Cotton, Wooly Organic; free shipping over 10,000 kr.; no parent discount seen.*
- ✓ **Loforð** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Kringlan) + netverslun) — Bridal and occasion shop with a Skírnarvörur range: christening gowns (about 20-23,000 kr.), bow ties, tights; also children's dresses and suits. · [loford.is/product-category/born/skirnarvorur](https://loford.is/product-category/born/skirnarvorur/) · *Also hires out wedding dresses; fittings by appointment.*
- ○ ⚠ **Brúðhjón** (Verslun · Netverslun + verslun (staðsetning óstaðfest)) — Bridal shop selling lace christening gowns (19,900 kr., 3-9 months, other sizes to order) and accessories; says it also hires out christening gowns. · [brudhjon.is](https://www.brudhjon.is/) · *Seen via search results on its own domain; site did not load from this session, so current activity is not re-checked. [auto] website did not respond in the final check.*

Snuz, Silver Cross.*

Love To Dream, Miniware, NENO.*
- ✓ **Kara Kids** (Verslun · Akureyri + netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Akureyri children's shop and webshop selling baby and children's clothing (dresses and skirts, wool wear) from Danish brands. · [karakids.is](https://karakids.is/) · *Only Akureyri baby-wear shop found; no dedicated christening range seen; opened in 2026 per Kaffið.is.*
- ✓ **Textíll ehf.** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Sérpantanir frá hönnuði) — Textile designer's label selling christening gowns in 100% silk with printed prayers, from stock or made to order. · [textil.is/textill](https://www.textil.is/textill) · *No webshop or prices on the page; one-designer business.*

### G18-02 · Skírnargjafir — Christening gifts

**Problem:** What to give or ask for  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6

*Market:* Gift shops and jewellers are the natural buyers: DIMM has dedicated Skírnargjafir and Sængurgjafir categories, Epal has an Epal gift-ideas page and baby section, Líf & List and the engravable-jewellery shops cover keepsakes. The Epal skírnargjafir URL exists but shows general gift ideas, not christening-labelled content.
