- ✓ **Epal hf.** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurland + netverslun) — Design retailer with a gift-ideas section (URL named skírnargjafir) and a children's range with baby furniture, bedding and gifts; gift cards. · [epal.is/vorur/gjafahugmyndir/skirnagjafir](https://www.epal.is/vorur/gjafahugmyndir/skirnagjafir) · **Discount:** Epalklúbburinn mailing list for offers; no parent discount seen · *Stores in Skeifan, Laugavegur, Kringlan, Smáralind, Hveragerði. Page read showed general gift ideas, not christening-labelled items.*
- ✓ **Líf & List** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Home and gift shop with a children's section (kids' items, children's clothing, Done by Deer and Moomin pieces), gift cards and corporate gifts. · [lifoglist.is](https://www.lifoglist.is/) · **Discount:** Product-level sales of 20-35% seen · *Store in Smáralind per the mall directory; no christening category.*
- ✓ **Petit** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Children's clothing shop with a christening clothing category, a typical gift or outfit for the day. · [petit.is](https://www.petit.is/) · **Discount:** Some items 15% off · *Store in Ármúli.*
- ✓ **Skartgripaverslunin Jens** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Jeweller with letter (name) necklaces and engravable pieces, suitable as a lasting christening gift. · [jens.is](https://www.jens.is/) · **Discount:** Vinaklúbbur Jens friends club; free shipping over 15,000 kr · *Shops in Kringlan, Smáralind and Grandagarður.*
- ✓ **AURUM** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + netverslun) — Icelandic jewellery with engravable rings and bracelets that can be personalised for a child's naming day. · [aurum.is](https://www.aurum.is/) · **Discount:** 1,500 kr off first order via mailing list; AURUM Vildarpunktar loyalty points · *Store in Bankastræti.*

### G18-03 · Nafngjöf og skírnarathafnir — Naming & baptism ceremonies (churches, Siðmennt, other communities)

**Problem:** Arranging the naming day  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1 (+2 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Only non-profits and sole practitioners provide this: under the two-NGO cap just the Þjóðkirkjan and Siðmennt stay listed (others offering naming or baptism are Fríkirkjan in Reykjavík and Hafnarfjörður, Ásatrúarfélagið, the Catholic Church and Óháði söfnuðurinn); the only private brand found is Úní, offering a pagan-style naming ceremony.

- ✓ **Þjóðkirkjan** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — National Church of Iceland; lists baptism (Skírn) among its ceremonies, performed in parish churches or homes nationwide including Akureyri. · [kirkjan.is](https://www.kirkjan.is/) · *Main provider of baptisms; booking is through the local parish. Details of the baptism page not read.*
- ✓ **Siðmennt** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Secular humanist association offering non-religious naming ceremonies (Nafngjöf) alongside civil confirmation, weddings and funerals. · [sidmennt.is](https://www.sidmennt.is/) · *Price list (Gjaldskrá) linked on site; nationwide coverage not confirmed on the page read.*
- ✓ **Úní** (Þjónusta · Óstaðfest) — Ritual practitioner offering a christening/naming ceremony ('Skírn / Nafnagjöf fyrir nýja sál') prepared with the parents, plus wedding ceremonies. · [uni.is/athafnir](https://uni.is/athafnir/) · *Sole practitioner trading under a brand name; Norse-style ritual, not a registered faith community; area and prices not stated.*

### G18-04 · Veisluþjónusta og veitingar — Catering

**Problem:** Feeding 30 guests without cooking  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11

*Market:* Many capital-area caterers and bakeries sell party platters and finger food; most pages advertise confirmations and weddings rather than christenings, so the christening angle is the pitch. Akureyri has restaurant caterers (Greifinn, Bautinn/Rub 23). More caterers exist than listed.

- ✓ **Múlakaffi veisluþjónusta** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Catering and events company since 1962 serving birthdays, confirmations and other parties from Hallarmúli, Reykjavík. · [mulakaffi.is](https://www.mulakaffi.is/) · *Linked restaurant on the same site; christenings not named.*
- ✓ **Veislulist ehf.** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Hafnarfjörður caterer: party food, finger-food tables, tapas, open sandwiches and party cakes for confirmations and graduations. · [veislulist.is](https://www.veislulist.is/) · *Closed for food orders on summer Sundays.*
- ✓ **Kokkarnir Veisluþjónusta** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Hafnarfjörður caterer with small bites, smørrebrød, combined menus and food trays; confirmation packages priced per person. · [kokkarnir.is](https://www.kokkarnir.is/) · *Packages seen are for confirmations; ask for christening menus.*
- ✓ **Kjötkompaní ehf.** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Food company with 'ready-made parties' (Tilbúnar veislur) and small-dish catering for all occasions; shops in Hafnarfjörður and Reykjavík. · [kjotkompani.is](https://www.kjotkompani.is/) · **Discount:** Newsletter subscribers hear of offers first · *Gift cards available.*

- ✓ **Fimleikafélagið Björk (Litla Björk)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Hafnarfjörður gymnastics club renting its 'Litla Björk' gym with foam pit and trampoline plus a party room for 1.5-hour children's birthdays (up to 30 people). · [fbjork.is/service-page/litla-bj%C3%B6rk-og-afm%C3%A6lissalur-1-5-klst-afm%C3%A6li-fimleikaf%C3%A9lagi%C3%B0-bj%C3%B6rk](https://www.fbjork.is/service-page/litla-bj%C3%B6rk-og-afm%C3%A6lissalur-1-5-klst-afm%C3%A6li-fimleikaf%C3%A9lagi%C3%B0-bj%C3%B6rk) · *Sports club; one adult per 10 children required; price list linked on the page is dated 2024; online booking.*
- ✓ **ARVO** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Booking marketplace for kids' birthdays: compare entertainers, bouncy castles, venues and food, send a free enquiry and receive quotes; 200+ providers listed. · [arvo.is/barnaafmaeli](https://arvo.is/barnaafmaeli) · *Page updated 17 Nov 2026; most providers are in the capital area but all regions have some listings.*
- ✓ **Outro** (Vettvangur/app · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri) — Booking platform for performers including magicians and children's entertainers; enquiry-and-quote flow, with a full refund if the artist cancels or does not show. · [outro.is/toframadur](https://outro.is/toframadur) · *Has category pages for Reykjavík and Akureyri; about nine artists listed when checked; footer 2026.*
- ✓ **Móðurskipið (Latibær-sýningar)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Entertainment agency whose catalogue includes Latibær character shows bookable from 20 minutes, with photo time, plus kindergarten and school visits on weekdays. · [modurskipid.is/skemmtanir/latibaer](https://modurskipid.is/skemmtanir/latibaer) · *Birthday parties are not named specifically on the page; prices and regions not stated; bookings by enquiry.*

## G19 · Matur og tímasparnaður — Food & convenience

### G19-01 · Matarkassar og uppskriftakassar — Meal kits

**Problem:** No time to plan and shop for dinner  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 11

*Market:* Very concentrated: Eldum rétt (Hagar-owned, about 85-90% of kit sales per the 2022 Competition Authority decision) and Einn tveir are the only true recipe-kit brands; Móðir Jörð and vegetable-box subscriptions (Matland, FoodCoop, Reykjalundur), ready-to-cook fish and meat boxes (Fiskbúðin Sundlaugavegi, Kjötkompaní, Sælkerabúðin) and ready meals (Matseðill) are the adjacent alternatives; Eldabuskan is closed for now, the Fiskur dagsins fish-kit site looks stale and Borðum rétt could not be verified.

- ✓ **Móðir Jörð** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Organic farm in Vallanes, East Iceland, selling certified organic vegetables, grain and ready foods; lists Matarkassar (food boxes) and ships home or to the nearest post office. · [modirjord.is](https://modirjord.is) · *Seasonal boxes; contents and prices not shown; not a recipe-kit service like Eldum rétt; also fits G19-06.*
- ○ **Eldum rétt** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Weekly recipe-box subscription delivered to the door with pre-portioned ingredients and recipes for family dinners. · [eldumrett.is](https://eldumrett.is) · *UNVERIFIED: site returned HTTP 403 to automated fetch on several tries (host exists); delivery areas and family boxes not checked. Confirm it is still operating.*
- ○ ⚠ **Einn tveir (áður Einn, tveir og elda)** (Netverslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Meal-kit service with recipe boxes delivered home; the former domain einntveirogelda.is redirects to einntveir.is. · [einntveir.is](https://einntveir.is/) · *UNVERIFIED: einntveir.is returned HTTP 502 on repeated tries; only the redirect from the old domain was seen. Confirm it is still operating and delivery areas.*
- ✓ **Matland (Matfélagið ehf.)** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land) + Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Online market of Icelandic growers: weekly, fortnightly or monthly vegetable-box subscription (8-9 vegetable types), home delivery in the capital area or courier and pickup points nationwide. · [matland.is/markadur/graenmeti-i-askrift](https://matland.is/markadur/graenmeti-i-askrift/) · **Discount:** 5% off for vegetable-box subscribers (5,320 kr. instead of 5,600 kr.) · *Boxes contain no recipes; capital-area delivery about 1,450 kr., courier 1,950 kr.; regional pickup points e.g. Selfoss, Ísafjörður, Siglufjörður, Egilsstaðir; also sells local lamb and beef.*
- ✓ **FoodCoop** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Weekly or one-off fruit and vegetable boxes (mixed, vegetables-only, fruit-only) delivered to homes nationwide, packed on Wednesdays and delivered next day; no recipes included. · [food-coop.is/is](https://www.food-coop.is/is) · **Discount:** Subscription discounts of 5-15% on weekly/fortnightly boxes (FAQ) · *Regular box about 10,200 kr. excl. shipping; mostly but not entirely organic; started in Seyðisfjörður in 2019, now at Grandi in Reykjavík.*
- ✓ **Reykjalundur garðyrkja** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Suðurland + Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Ecological, regenerative vegetable farm in Grímsnes selling weekly seasonal vegetable bags by subscription (CSA), delivered to the door or a local pickup point. · [reykjalundurgardyrkja.is](https://reykjalundurgardyrkja.is/) · *Run by Flight Song ehf.; 2026 autumn bag subscription advertised; also sold through Pikkoló stations; summer-autumn season only, no recipes.*
- ✓ **Fiskbúðin Sundlaugavegi** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík fishmonger selling ready-to-bake fish dishes in portions for two, four or six (oven or air fryer), e.g. teriyaki salmon, plokkfiskur, coconut-curry haddock, from about 2,750 kr. for two. · [fiskbudin.is](https://fiskbudin.is/) · *Dish of the week offer; no online ordering or delivery mentioned on its site (its dishes also appear in Pikkoló's shop).*
- ✓ **Kjötkompaní** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Butcher with three stores and a webshop selling meat boxes, grill packs with sides and ready meals; orders delivered nationwide for a small fee or collected in store. · [kjotkompani.is](https://www.kjotkompani.is) · *Order by noon the day before; pickup in Hafnarfjörður, Grandi and Bíldshöfði; contents and prices of the meat boxes not shown on the front page.*
- ✓ **Sælkerabúðin** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun) — Gourmet food shop and webshop (Reykjavík and Kópavogur) selling premium meat, meat boxes such as 'Heimapakkinn' (four dinners for four), sides, soups and ready meals, for home delivery or pickup. · [saelkerabudin.is](https://www.saelkerabudin.is/) · *Heimapakkinn (about 34,990 kr.) seen in a search hit on its own domain, not on the front page; meat-box range not itemised; no subscription seen.*
- ✓ **Matseðill (Icelandic Food Company)** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt) — Ready-to-heat dinners (rice dishes, noodles, soups, sides, sauces) made by Icelandic Food Company and sold in Krónan, for parents who want no cooking at all. · [matsedill.is](https://matsedill.is/) · *Ready meals, not raw kits; a 2022 Competition Authority decision describes Matseðill as a weekly meal-kit subscription seller, but no subscription is visible on the site now.*

### G19-02 · Heimsending á matvöru — Grocery delivery

**Problem:** Cannot easily go to the shop with a baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 15

*Market:* Krónan (delivers to Akureyri and most regions), Heimkaup, Wolt and Aha lead; Hagkaup now delivers from its own webshop and via Wolt, Nettó delivers in the capital area, Akureyri, Reykjanesbær and Selfoss, Kostur and Costco offer delivery, Krambúðin via Wolt, and Pikkoló adds 24/7 pickup stations; produce, fish and meat sellers (Matland, FoodCoop, Fisk Kompaní, Kjötkompaní) cover fresh food; Bónus and 10-11 have no online delivery.

- ✓ **Krónan Snjallverslun** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Norðurland + Austurland + Suðurland) — Online grocery shop in app and web with home delivery across the capital area and many other places, plus Pikkoló pickup stations and scan-and-go in store. · [kronan.is](https://kronan.is) · **Discount:** Heillakarfan points in the app; price freeze on 647 items to end of 2026 (general). · *26 stores incl. Akureyri; delivery zones listed by postcode in FAQ - Akureyri delivery not confirmed on front page.*
- ✓ **Heimkaup** (Netverslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Icelandic webshop with home delivery by address and offers; front page shows drinks, combo deals and everyday items. · [heimkaup.is](https://www.heimkaup.is) · *Grocery range not confirmed on the pages seen (mostly drinks/combos); delivery areas and fees not shown.*
- ✓ **Wolt** (Vettvangur/app · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri) — Delivery app for food, groceries and shops in Reykjavík and Akureyri, with fast courier delivery from local stores. · [wolt.com/en/isl/reykjavik](https://wolt.com/en/isl/reykjavik) · *Finnish-founded platform operating locally; page content is JavaScript-only so store list not readable; the task brief says Móðurást is listed on Wolt (unverified).*
- ○ **Aha** (Vettvangur/app · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Icelandic delivery platform for restaurants and shops; Subway links to it for home delivery. · [aha.is](https://aha.is) · *Site returned 403 to automated fetch; existence seen via link on subway.is (aha.is/subway). Grocery range not checked.*
- ✓ **Hagkaup** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurnes + Suðurland) — Webshop with same-day home delivery in south-west Iceland and pickup points; groceries also on Wolt (under an hour) from Skeifan, Garðabær, Spöngin and Akureyri stores. · [hagkaup.is/afhendingarmoguleikar](https://www.hagkaup.is/afhendingarmoguleikar) · **Discount:** Free delivery to pickup points on orders over 19,900 kr. · *Home delivery 1,990 kr.; Wolt partnership announced 1 Dec 2025 with 4,000+ products; also sells Eldum rétt's 'Einstakir réttir' meal kits in stores.*
- ○ **Nettó (Samkaup)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + Suðurnes + Suðurland) — Discount supermarket chain with an online store since 2017 and home delivery to the capital area, Akureyri, Reykjanesbær and Selfoss. · [netto.is](https://www.netto.is/) · *Online store and delivery areas come from the Samkaup/Heimkaup merger filing (Mar 2025) and an aha.is listing; not shown on netto.is itself, fees unconfirmed.*
- ○ **Krambúðin (Samkaup)** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Akureyri + landsbyggðin) — Convenience-store chain of about a dozen stores, including Akureyri, with small-goods delivery through Wolt from selected capital-area stores. · [krambudin.is](https://www.krambudin.is) · *Wolt delivery is stated in the Samkaup/Heimkaup merger filing (2025), not on krambudin.is; limited range.*
- ✓ **Kostur** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Discount supermarket in Garðabær and Njarðvík with a webshop and free home delivery on orders over 9,998 kr.; carries baby food and household goods. · [kostur.is](https://www.kostur.is) · **Discount:** Free home delivery over 9,998 kr. · *Delivery areas and fees for smaller orders not stated; open daily 10-20; no loyalty club seen.*
- ✓ **Costco Iceland** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Warehouse club with a same-day delivery option through Wolt for the Reykjavík-area warehouse, plus an online-only range. · [costco.is](https://www.costco.is) · *Site does not say which products are delivered or whether membership is needed; delivery handled by third-party Wolt.*

**Problem:** Too tired to cook

- ○ **Aha** (Vettvangur/app · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Icelandic food-delivery platform used by chains such as Subway for home delivery. · [aha.is](https://aha.is) · *Site returned 403 to automated fetch; seen via Subway's own page linking to aha.is/subway.*
- ✓ **Subway Ísland (Stjarnan ehf.)** (Keðja · Landið allt) — Sub chain with online ordering, home delivery via Aha and a family deal of four subs and a 2-litre drink. · [subway.is](https://subway.is) · **Discount:** Fjölskyldutilboð (family deal) seen; general, not parent-specific. · *16 restaurants across Iceland per site; Akureyri not confirmed.*

Akureyri (Undirhlíð); delivery hours (until midnight or 01:00) listed for the Skeifan store, other zones not shown.*

**Stig:** 0–3 mán · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

Supermarket chains also stock frozen family meals.

- ✓ **Veislulist** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Party kitchen and caterer with a Heimilismatur (home food) menu including a Heimaþjónusta option, plus finger food, open sandwiches and cakes. · [veislulist.is](https://veislulist.is) · *Pickup/delivery terms not read; ready meals for households not confirmed in detail.*
- ✓ **Múlakaffi** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Restaurant and catering kitchen with a weekly home-style menu and a web shop; classic Icelandic dishes like fish stew and lamb chops. · [mulakaffi.is](https://mulakaffi.is) · *Hallarmúli, Reykjavík; catering across Iceland; home delivery to households not confirmed.*
- ✓ **Fylgifiskar** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Fish shop in Kópavogur with daily ready fish dishes and sides for a complete meal in one trip, plus pre-ordered menus for pickup. · [fylgifiskar.is](https://fylgifiskar.is) · *Open weekdays only; delivery mentioned for business lunches, not households.*

### G19-06 · Heilsubúðir og lífrænar vörur — Health & organic stores

**Problem:** Healthier, cleaner products for family  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 15

*Market:* Small specialist market: Heilsuhúsið, Heilsuhornið and Móðir Jörð plus Heilsuval, Systrasamlagið, Mamma veit best and Vistvæna Búðin (Akureyri), webshops (Vistvera, FoodCoop), producers (Reykjalundur, Kaja organic, Sólheimar, BioBú) and the importer Heilsa ehf.; the well-known grocer Maður lifandi and the organic wholesaler Yggdrasill could not be verified (sites unreachable) and supermarkets' organic ranges are not listed.

- ✓ **Heilsuhúsið (Lyfja)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Health-store range of organic, vegan and gluten-free foods, vitamins, skin care and a mother-and-baby section, sold in Lyfja stores and online. · [lyfja.is/heilsuhusid](https://www.lyfja.is/heilsuhusid) · **Discount:** Vöggugjöf Lyfju baby-gift programme mentioned (details not shown); free shipping over 9,900 kr. · *Heilsuhúsið Kringlan store closed 1 February; Lyfja keeps the popular products in its stores and on heilsuhusid.is; Akureyri store not confirmed.*
- ✓ **Heilsuhornið (Ayurveda ehf.)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Reykjavík supplement and wellness shop with immune, women's health, sleep and digestion ranges, web shop and subscription discounts. · [heilsuhornid.is](https://heilsuhornid.is) · **Discount:** 10% off for joining mailing list; 25% off subscription products (general). · *Pickup at Fossháls by prior call; free DROPP shipping above a threshold.*
- ✓ **Móðir Jörð** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Certified organic farm in Vallanes, East Iceland, selling vegetables, grain, tea and ready foods, shipped countrywide. · [modirjord.is](https://modirjord.is) · *Also food boxes (see G19-01).*
- ✓ **Heilsuval (Blóm í Eggi - Heilsuvörur ehf.)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Reykjavík shop and webshop for organic, natural and vegan products, including organic baby clothing, supplements and cosmetics; free shipping on orders over 25,000 kr. · [heilsuval.is](https://www.heilsuval.is/) · *Many webshop items marked coming soon; South American foods by enquiry only.*
- ✓ **Systrasamlagið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Reykjavík natural-health shop and webshop: vitamins and supplements (incl. pregnancy supplements), Ayurveda and skincare, yoga gear, a mostly organic café and courses. · [systrasamlagid.is](https://systrasamlagid.is) · *Free shipping over 15,000 kr.; no baby-specific range seen; site active (news post dated Oct 2026).*
- ✓ **Mamma veit best ehf.** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Kópavogur health shop and wholesaler focused on natural and organic supplements and cosmetics (Acure, Four Sigmatic, Dr. Bronner's, cod-liver oil) with a family-wellbeing angle. · [mammaveitbest.is/umokkur](https://mammaveitbest.is/umokkur/) · *Weekday opening hours; delivery not mentioned on the page; no baby-specific range seen.*
- ✓ **Vistvæna Búðin** (Verslun · Akureyri + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Akureyri eco shop and webshop: eco cosmetics and personal care, cleaning products, refill bar, supplements and some groceries; baby products mentioned; free shipping from 18,000 kr. · [vistvaena.is](https://vistvaena.is/) · **Discount:** 10% off first order for newsletter sign-ups; loyalty club · *Also sells wholesale; no specific baby lines visible on the front page.*
- ✓ **Vistvera** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Webshop of plastic-free natural everyday products with sections for children, baby, pregnancy and breastfeeding, skincare and home; free home delivery over 19,000 kr. · [vistvera.is](https://vistvera.is/) · *Brands include Ruby Cup, Wuka and Care by Me; no date visible on the page, so current trading not fully confirmed.*
- ✓ **FoodCoop** (Netverslun · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Organic-leaning fruit and vegetable boxes delivered nationwide, one-off or weekly subscription, sourced mainly from organic farms in Denmark, the Netherlands and Spain. · [food-coop.is/is](https://www.food-coop.is/is) · **Discount:** Subscription discounts of 5-15% (FAQ) · *Not all produce is organic (some conventional chosen for flavour); regular box about 10,200 kr. excl. shipping.*
- ✓ **Reykjalundur garðyrkja** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Suðurland + Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Ecological, regenerative vegetable farm in Grímsnes using no synthetic pesticides or fertilisers; weekly seasonal vegetable bags by subscription, home delivery or local pickup. · [reykjalundurgardyrkja.is](https://reykjalundurgardyrkja.is/) · *Run by Flight Song ehf.; 2026 season subscription advertised; summer-autumn only.*
- ✓ **Kaja organic (Matarbúr Kaju)** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Vesturland + Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Akranes organic producer and wholesaler (vegetables, herbs, Icelandic organic pasta) that also runs the Matarbúr Kaju organic shop and Café Kaja; products also sold via Pikkoló. · [kajaorganic.com](https://www.kajaorganic.com) · *Own site shows wholesaler/manufacturer only; the Akranes shop and café come from 1819.is and news listings, hours not re-checked.*
- ✓ **Sólheimar** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Suðurland + Netverslun) — Eco-village in Grímsnes selling organic greenhouse vegetables, handmade goods and gifts in the shop Vala, with a café (Græna Kannan) and a webshop. · [solheimar.is](https://www.solheimar.is) · *Self-owned community institution; opening-hours notice on the site is partly outdated; delivery not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Heilsa ehf.** (Heildsali/umboð · Landið allt) — Importer and distributor of organic and natural food (Biona, Doves Farm, Biotta), children's vitamins, Babystart and skincare sold through pharmacies, health shops and supermarkets. · [heilsa.is](https://heilsa.is/) · *No consumer shop or prices on its site; named a 2026 Creditinfo 'Framúrskarandi fyrirtæki'.*
- ✓ **BioBú** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt) — Family-owned Icelandic organic dairy producer (organic yogurts and other dairy products) for parents wanting organic milk-based foods. · [biobu.is](https://biobu.is) · *Stockists not listed on its site; website relaunched in April 2026.*
- ✓ **Heilsuver (Lyfjaver)** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Lyfjaver's health store and webshop for organic and natural products, vitamins and supplements, essential oils, cosmetics and a 'parent and child' section; free delivery over 9,900 kr. · [lyfjaver.is/heilsuver](https://www.lyfjaver.is/heilsuver/) · **Discount:** Vildarklúbbur loyalty club (terms not seen) · *Store shares premises with Lyfjaver in Reykjavík; page footer says 2024 with some 2026 uploads; food range not stated as organic.*

### G19-07 · Kaffi og kaffihús fyrir þreytta foreldra — Coffee & cafés for tired parents

**Problem:** Sleep deprivation  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 8

*Market:* Many cafés and roasters (Te & Kaffi, Kaffitár, Reykjavík Roasters, Brauð & Co, Sandholt, Kaffifélagið, Mokka) mostly in Reykjavík; Ketilkaffi is the Akureyri find. None advertises a parent or stroller-friendly offer, so the angle is open.

- ✓ **Te & Kaffi** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Coffee roaster with eight cafés around Reykjavík and a web shop; app gives about 10% back as credit at any café. · [teogkaffi.is](https://teogkaffi.is) · **Discount:** App rewards: ~10% of each payment saved as credit (general). · *Café list not read; no Akureyri café confirmed.*
- ✓ **Kaffitár** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Roastery with web shop for coffee beans, tea and brewing gear, plus a coffee quiz; reusable-cup discount on drinks. · [kaffitar.is](https://kaffitar.is) · **Discount:** Discount on drinks when you bring your own cup (general). · *Café addresses not shown on the shop page.*
- ✓ **Reykjavík Roasters** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Netverslun (sendir um allt land)) — Specialty roaster with three cafés (Kárastígur, Brautarholt, Freyjugata), a coffee subscription and web shop - useful for tired parents wanting beans at home. · [reykjavikroasters.is](https://reykjavikroasters.is) · *Also wholesale and meeting room hire.*
- ✓ **Brauð & Co** (Keðja · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Sourdough bakery-cafés at eight addresses in Reykjavík, Kópavogur and Garðabær with pastries, catering, gift cards and an online shop. · [braudogco.is](https://braudogco.is) · *No family amenities listed on site.*
- ✓ **Sandholt** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Bakery-café on Laugavegur with daily-baked sourdough, pastries, soda and party catering. · [sandholt.is](https://sandholt.is) · *Online ordering pages looked unfinished when checked.*
- ✓ **Kaffifélagið** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Tiny coffee bar on Skólavörðustígur with Italian espresso beans, take-away coffee and home espresso machines and accessories. · [kaffifelagid.is](https://kaffifelagid.is) · *Sells machines and gift baskets as well as coffee.*
- ✓ **Mokka Kaffi** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Classic Reykjavík café on Skólavörðustígur serving espresso since 1958 with art exhibitions; open daily. · [mokka.is](https://mokka.is) · *No family information on site.*
- ✓ **Ketilkaffi** (Verslun · Akureyri) — Akureyri café on Kaupvangsstræti with all-day brunch, sourdough, specialty coffee, homemade ice cream and party catering. · [ketilkaffi.is](https://ketilkaffi.is) · *Table booking via site; no kids menu mentioned.*

### G19-08 · Mjólkurvörur og skyr — Dairy & skyr producers

**Problem:** Nutrition for the family  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Four dairy and skyr producers verified: MS (Ísey skyr, KEA skyr, Hleðsla), Arna (lactose-free), Biobú (organic) and Kjörís (ice cream). None advertises a baby or toddler range on the pages seen.

- ✓ **Mjólkursamsalan (MS)** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt) — Iceland's main dairy: skyr (Ísey, KEA), milk, yogurt, cheese, butter, lactose-free range and drinks like Hleðsla and Kókómjólk. · [ms.is](https://www.ms.is) · *HQ Reykjavík with goods pickup in Reykjavík and Akureyri; school milk day activities; no baby range shown.*
- ✓ **Ísey skyr** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt) — MS's skyr brand: high-protein fat-free skyr presented as an all-ages snack and lunchbox option. · [iseyskyr.is](https://www.iseyskyr.is) · *Brand of MS - overlap with the entry above; no kids range seen.*
- ✓ **Arna** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt) — Bolungarvík dairy making lactose-free skyr, yoghurts and Greek yoghurt from Icelandic milk, positioned for the whole family. · [arna.is](https://arna.is) · *Production in Bolungarvík, office in Reykjavík; useful for lactose-intolerant babies and parents.*
- ✓ **Biobú** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt) — Family-run organic dairy making organic yoghurts and milk products from Icelandic organic milk. · [biobu.is](https://biobu.is) · *Reykjavík (Gylfaflöt); retailers not listed on page.*
- ✓ **Kjörís** (Framleiðandi/vörumerki · Landið allt) — Hveragerði ice-cream maker with household packs, ice lollies, vegan and sugar-free ranges and a web shop. · [kjoris.is](https://kjoris.is) · *Ice cream rather than skyr/milk; fits a family treats angle.*

### G19-N1 · Fiskbúðir og kjötbúðir — Fishmongers & butchers (fresh fish, oven-ready dishes, family meat packs) *(new — added during research)*

**Problem:** Fresh fish and meat for family dinners without long prep  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 4

*Market:* Four specialist food shops verified in Reykjavík and Kópavogur; two sell oven-ready dishes by household size. No Akureyri shop checked.

- ✓ **Fiskbúðin Sundlaugavegi** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Fishmonger since 1947 selling fresh fillets and oven-ready fish dishes (teriyaki salmon, plokkfiskur and more) in two, four or six portions. · [fiskbudin.is](https://fiskbudin.is) · *Single shop in Laugarnes; pickup only, no delivery mentioned; weekly dish-of-the-week offer.*
- ✓ **Hafið Fiskverslun** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Fresh-fish retailer and processor with a shop in Kópavogur; cooking tips for boiled, grilled and fried fish. · [hafid.is](https://hafid.is) · *Also supplies school and company canteens; ready dishes and delivery not mentioned.*
- ✓ **Fylgifiskar** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Fish shop in Kópavogur with daily ready fish dishes and sides plus pre-ordered menus for pickup. · [fylgifiskar.is](https://fylgifiskar.is) · *Same entry as in G19-04; weekdays only.*
- ✓ **Kjöthöllin** (Verslun · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Specialist butcher for 80 years with ready-to-cook patties and stuffed lamb, family grill packs, bulk saving boxes, web ordering and gift vouchers. · [kjothollin.is](https://kjothollin.is) · *Shop in Skipholt, Reykjavík; delivery not stated; Christmas boxes to order.*

## G20 · Fjarskipti og orka — Telecom & energy (weak link)

### G20-01 · Internet og ljósleiðari — Internet & fibre providers

**Problem:** Home internet for video calls and streaming  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 8

*Market:* Main retail ISPs are Síminn, Nova, Sýn/Vodafone and Hringdu, plus regional Snerpa; network owners Míla, Ljósleiðarinn and Tengir (Akureyri) wholesale fibre to them. Family hooks are in mobile and TV bundles (Krakkakort, parental-leave SÝN+).

- ✓ **Síminn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Largest telecom: fibre and 5G home internet in three home bundles with TV, home phone, router and Wi-Fi amplifier, plus mobile subscriptions. · [siminn.is/pakkar](https://www.siminn.is/pakkar) · **Discount:** Free 2 GB Krakkakort with each mobile subscription; no explicit family discount seen. · *Bundles Heimilispakkinn, Þægilegi and Einfaldi; Sjónvarp Símans Premium; HQ Ármúli, Reykjavík; see G20-02 and G20-04.*
- ✓ **Nova** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Mobile and home-internet provider with fibre, the Nova Heimili bundle with an eero WiFi 7 router, Flakknet, TV and an online shop. · [nova.is](https://www.nova.is) · **Discount:** 2 fyrir 1 deals in the Nova app; under-18s get free calls/texts within Nova and 2 GB data (see G20-02). · *Shop locations not read (page links to stores list).*
- ✓ **Sýn (Vodafone)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Internet, phone and TV bundles under the Vodafone and SÝN brands; also owns Stöð 2 and radio stations Bylgjan and FM957. · [syn.is](https://syn.is/) · **Discount:** One month of free SÝN+ for parents on parental leave (register on site); 2 fyrir 1 offers. · *vodafone.is redirects to syn.is; agents network (umboðsaðilar) and webshop; warm lead via parental-leave offer.*
- ✓ **Hringdu** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Low-cost internet and mobile provider with no binding contracts; unlimited mobile from 1,990 kr/month and build-your-own home packages. · [hringdu.is](https://www.hringdu.is) · **Discount:** Vinadíll friend-referral deal; student deal; no family plan seen. · *Uses fibre of Ljósleiðarinn, Míla and Tengi; mobile on Míla/Síminn network.*
- ✓ **Snerpa** (Þjónusta · Vestfirðir) — Ísafjörður-based internet and IT provider that led fibre rollout in the Westfjords; simple unlimited fibre plan and mobile bundles. · [snerpa.is](https://snerpa.is) · **Discount:** Unlimited mobile 2,990 kr/month for up to six numbers with home internet; 5 GB kids' cards (krakkakort). · *Regional; Westfjords only.*
- ✓ **Míla** (Heildsali/umboð · Landið allt) — Wholesale network owner offering 2.5 and 10 Gb/s home fibre via retail providers, with an All-in-One install visit (router, Wi-Fi, wiring). · [mila.is](https://www.mila.is) · *Sells through Hringdu, Hringiðan, Nova, OK, Origo, Snerpa, Síminn, Sýn, Tengdu, Tölvun and iCall; not a retail seller.*
- ✓ **Ljósleiðarinn** (Heildsali/umboð · Landið allt) — Fibre network operator (owned by Orkuveita Reykjavíkur) with fibre to over 100,000 homes and a home service to connect Wi-Fi devices. · [ljosleidarinn.is](https://www.ljosleidarinn.is) · *Open network used by several ISPs; ownership may change per shareholder decision.*
- ✓ **Tengir** (Heildsali/umboð · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Akureyri-based owner of an open fibre network in Eyjafjörður (also Siglufjörður and Þórshöfn); providers like Nova, Síminn, Sýn and Hringdu sell over it. · [tengir.is](https://tengir.is) · *Not an ISP; helps users with connections and equipment; relevant for Akureyri homes.*

### G20-02 · Farsímaáskrift og fjölskyldupakkar — Mobile plans & family bundles

**Problem:** Family mobile costs  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 5

*Market:* Four national mobile operators (Síminn, Nova, Sýn/Vodafone, Hringdu) plus regional Snerpa; none sells a true family bundle on the pages read, but Síminn, Nova and Snerpa all give kids' data cards and Sýn gives parental-leave perks.

- ✓ **Síminn** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Mobile subscriptions with a free 2 GB Krakkakort for kids and home bundles that include two unlimited mobile phones and smartwatch numbers. · [siminn.is/pakkar](https://www.siminn.is/pakkar) · **Discount:** Krakkakort 2 GB free with each subscription; no family discount seen. · *Þægilegi pakkinn bundles internet, TV and two mobiles.*
- ✓ **Nova** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Mobile operator with a youth offer: under-18s get free calls and texts within Nova plus 2 GB data a month; app with 2 fyrir 1 deals. · [nova.is/farsimi](https://www.nova.is/farsimi) · **Discount:** Under-18 free Nova-to-Nova calls and 2 GB; 2 fyrir 1 in app (general). · *Plan prices on separate price-list page.*
- ✓ **Sýn (Vodafone)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Phone, internet and TV under Vodafone/SÝN with bundles, prepaid top-up (Frelsi) and an offer for parents on parental leave. · [syn.is](https://syn.is/) · **Discount:** Free month of SÝN+ for parents on parental leave. · *Family plan not seen; customer-service numbers on site not recorded.*
- ✓ **Hringdu** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Budget mobile with unlimited data, calls and texts from 1,990 kr a month, no contracts, 5G on Míla/Síminn network. · [hringdu.is](https://www.hringdu.is) · **Discount:** Vinadíll friend-referral; no family plan seen. · *Number porting possible.*
- ✓ **Snerpa** (Þjónusta · Vestfirðir) — Westfjords provider with unlimited mobile for up to six numbers when taken with home internet and 5 GB kids' cards. · [snerpa.is](https://snerpa.is) · **Discount:** 2,990 kr/month unlimited mobile per number with internet, up to six numbers (family-style); kids' cards 5 GB. · *Regional; check it sells outside Vestfirðir.*

### G20-03 · Rafmagn og orkusala — Electricity retailers

**Problem:** Higher household bills  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 6

*Market:* Several household electricity retailers: ON, Orkusalan, HS Orka, Orka heimilanna, Straumlind and Orkubú Vestfjarða; Fallorka stopped household sales from 1 January 2026. No parent-specific offers seen; Orkusalan gives moving boxes to new sign-ups.

- ✓ **Orka náttúrunnar (ON)** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Household electricity (Heimilisrafmagn ON) with a smart-meter tariff cheaper at night and weekends, plus home EV charging subscription. · [on.is](https://www.on.is) · *Part of Orkuveita Reykjavíkur group; nationwide coverage not stated on page.*
- ✓ **Orkusalan** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Household electricity retailer; sign up online in about a minute when switching or moving home and choose an energy plan. · [orkusalan.is](https://www.orkusalan.is) · **Discount:** Ten free moving boxes for sign-ups when moving (while supplies last); offered to former Fallorka customers. · *Based in Kópavogur; warm lead as new parents often move homes.*
- ✓ **HS Orka** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Supplies electricity to homes and businesses nationwide, including a Næturáskrift with free night electricity from 01:00 to 06:00. · [hsorka.is](https://www.hsorka.is) · *Night subscription pricing as shown on site.*
- ✓ **Orka heimilanna** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Nationwide household electricity with a simple price list, choice of certified renewable or cheaper uncertified energy and free, easy switching. · [orkaheimilanna.is](https://www.orkaheimilanna.is) · *Sign-up needs national ID and e-mail verification.*
- ✓ **Straumlind** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Electricity retailer with fixed or day/night prices and prepaid electricity, switching in one minute with electronic ID. · [straumlind.is](https://straumlind.is) · **Discount:** Friend offers (vinatilboð) under My Pages; prepaid 1,000 kWh bundle. · *Service area not stated on page.*
- ✓ **Orkubú Vestfjarða** (Þjónusta · Vestfirðir) — Westfjords energy company generating and distributing electricity to customers, with sign-up and price-comparison pages. · [ov.is](https://www.ov.is) · *Household retail sales implied but not stated explicitly; Westfjords only.*

### G20-04 · Sjónvarp og streymisveitur — TV & streaming services

**Problem:** Evenings at home with a baby  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Veik · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 3 (+1 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Síminn (Sjónvarp Símans Premium) and Sýn (Stöð 2/SÝN+) are the two Icelandic TV-streaming bundles; Nova resells and discounts them; RÚV is the free public broadcaster. Netflix, Disney+ and others are foreign. Sýn offers new parents a free SÝN+ month.

- ✓ **Síminn (Sjónvarp Símans)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Sjónvarp Símans Premium with HBO Max and Hayu included, Icelandic-dubbed children's shows, PIN and age limits, working with or without a set-top box. · [siminn.is/sjonvarp](https://www.siminn.is/sjonvarp) · *Premium myndlykill has built-in speaker; any provider's internet works; no family discount seen.*
- ✓ **Sýn (SÝN+ / Stöð 2)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — SÝN+ streaming subscription with children's programming, sports tiers and Stöð 2 content; stod2.is now redirects to syn.is. · [syn.is](https://syn.is/) · **Discount:** One month of SÝN+ free for parents on parental leave (register online). · *Warm lead: explicit new-parent offer.*
- ✓ **Nova (Nova sjónvarp)** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Sells SÝN+ and Sjónvarp Símans Premium packages alongside HBO Max, Viaplay, Netflix and Disney+ info; Nova home-internet customers get a discount. · [nova.is/sjonvarp](https://www.nova.is/sjonvarp) · **Discount:** 30% off the most expensive subscription for Nova home-internet customers (as stated on page). · *Reseller of others' TV content.*
- ✓ **RÚV** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Public broadcaster with TV, radio and apps for smart devices; main free source of Icelandic programming. · [ruv.is](https://www.ruv.is) · *Public company; children's content not confirmed on page read; listed only as main free provider.*

## G21 · Opinber þjónusta og samtök — Public services & NGOs (content partners, not advertisers)

### G21-01 · Fæðingar- og mæðravernd (opinber þjónusta) — Public maternity care & birth services

**Problem:** Where to be seen and where to give birth  
**Stig:** Meðganga · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 2 (+9 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Birth wards exist at Landspítali, Akranes, Ísafjörður, Akureyri, Neskaupstaður, Selfoss, Vestmannaeyjar and Reykjanesbær per the midwives' association; capital-area antenatal care runs through health centres and Landspítali's outpatient unit; the private Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur is the non-hospital alternative and home births are arranged with independent midwives. island.is (where most public bodies now live) blocks WebFetch, so several entries are unverified (nei).

- ✓ **Landspítali - Fæðingarvakt og mæðravernd** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — National hospital's birth ward in Reykjavik, with outpatient maternity care and a pregnancy and postnatal ward; page gives phone, opening hours and location. · [island.is/s/landspitali/faedingarvakt/fraedsluefni-faedingarvakt](https://island.is/s/landspitali/faedingarvakt/fraedsluefni-faedingarvakt) · *Midwives' association also lists Göngudeild mæðraverndar, Meðgöngu- og sængurlegudeild, Fósturgreiningardeild and a 'Fæðingarstofa Bjarkarinnar' in Reykjavik; island.is pages could not be fetched*
- ✓ **Heilsugæsla höfuðborgarsvæðisins - Heilsubrú** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Capital-area primary care women's-health service: midwives and gynaecologists advise expectant mothers, midwives do ultrasound scans, contraception clinic opened July 2024. · [island.is/en/o/hh/heilsubru](https://island.is/en/o/hh/heilsubru) · *Some health centres (e.g. Hlíðar, Hamraborg) offer midwife counselling; HH also runs online birth-preparation courses*
- ✓ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Vestfjarða (HVEST) - fæðingardeild og mæðravernd** (Samtök/opinbert · Vestfirðir) — Midwife-led antenatal care in Ísafjörður (7-10 visits, partners welcome) and a birth ward where expectant couples can tour the unit around 30 weeks. · [hvest.is/thjonusta/maedravernd](https://hvest.is/thjonusta/maedravernd/) · *Midwife on-call line and booking by phone; high-risk pregnancies shared with Landspítali; birth ward page hvest.is/thjonusta/faedingardeild/*
- ○ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Vesturlands (HVE)** (Samtök/opinbert · Vesturland) — Birth ward in Akranes and midwife-run antenatal care at six health centres (Akranes, Borgarnes, Grundarfjörður, Hvammstangi, Ólafsvík, Stykkishólmur). · [hve.is](https://www.hve.is) · *Per the midwives' association; hve.is redirects to island.is so own pages unread*
- ○ **Sjúkrahúsið á Akureyri (SAk)** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Regional hospital with the birth unit for North Iceland; the midwives' association lists SAk as the Akureyri birthplace. · [sak.is](https://www.sak.is) · *Own pages on island.is not readable; a head-of-obstetrics post was advertised with deadline 6 Nov 2026*
- ○ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Austurlands (HSA) - Neskaupstaður** (Samtök/opinbert · Austurland) — Austurland's birth place is the district hospital in Neskaupstaður per the midwives' association. · [hsa.is](https://www.hsa.is) · *hsa.is redirects to island.is; own pages unread*
- ✓ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Suðurlands (HSU)** (Samtök/opinbert · Suðurland) — Birth services in Selfoss and Vestmannaeyjar; physiotherapists and midwives run a musculoskeletal clinic for pregnant women as part of antenatal care across the region. · [island.is/s/hsu/frett/stodkerfismottaka-fyrir-ofriskar-konur](https://island.is/s/hsu/frett/stodkerfismottaka-fyrir-ofriskar-konur) · *News item on the pregnancy musculoskeletal clinic seen in search results (2023); birth places per midwives' association*
- ○ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Suðurnesja (HSS)** (Samtök/opinbert · Suðurnes) — Midwife-led birth service ('Ljósmæðravakt') in Reykjanesbær per the midwives' association. · [hss.is](https://www.hss.is) · *hss.is redirects to island.is; own pages unread*
- ✓ **Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private birth centre for healthy women with normal pregnancies, with continuous care through pregnancy, birth and postnatal period, breastfeeding home visits and parent courses. · [faedingarheimilid.is/home](https://www.faedingarheimilid.is/home) · **Discount:** Many unions reimburse course fees · *Reykjavík; coverage by Sjúkratryggingar not stated on page; free Tuesday parent meet-up*
- ✓ **Ljósmæðrafélag Íslands** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Midwives' association with public pages on antenatal care, midwifery services, breastfeeding counselling, home births and a list of birth places by region. · [ljosmaedrafelag.is](https://www.ljosmaedrafelag.is) · *Home-birth page lists independent midwives by region (individuals, not listed here)*
- ✓ **Heilsuvera** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Health portal run by the capital-area health service and the Directorate of Health: 'Mínar síður' to book appointments, renew prescriptions and message health staff. · [heilsuvera.is](https://www.heilsuvera.is) · *Page did not mention maternity or child-health booking specifically*

### G21-02 · Ung- og smábarnavernd (heilsugæsla) — Public child-health clinics

**Problem:** Check-ups and vaccinations  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1 (+7 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Ung- og smábarnavernd is delivered free by public health centres nationwide on one national visit schedule (home visits under 6 weeks, checks and vaccines at 6 weeks, 3, 5, 6, 8, 10, 12, 18 months, 2.5 and 4 years). Only HVEST's page could be read in full; other institutions' pages sit on island.is, which blocks WebFetch, so they are unverified (nei).

- ✓ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Vestfjarða (HVEST) - ung- og smábarnavernd** (Samtök/opinbert · Vestfirðir) — Free child health care for ages 0-4 by nurses and doctors: home visits before 6 weeks, checks at 6 weeks, 3, 5, 6, 8, 10, 12, 18 months, 2.5 and 4 years, with vaccinations. · [hvest.is/thjonusta/ung-og-smabarnavernd](https://hvest.is/thjonusta/ung-og-smabarnavernd/) · **Discount:** Free for all parents · *Sites include Ísafjörður, Patreksfjörður, Bolungarvík and others; schedule mirrors the national programme*
- ○ **Heilsugæsla höfuðborgarsvæðisins (HH)** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Capital-area network of public health centres (e.g. Hlíðar, Hamraborg, Árbær, Glæsibær, Seltjarnarnes og Vesturbær) providing primary care including child health care. · [island.is/s/hh/thjonusta-heilsugaeslustodva](https://island.is/s/hh/thjonusta-heilsugaeslustodva) · *Services page seen in search results only; child-health content not read; also runs the women's-health service Heilsubrú*
- ○ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Norðurlands (HSN)** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Public health centres across North Iceland including Akureyri; child health care not seen on the pages found. · [hsn.is](https://www.hsn.is) · *hsn.is redirects to island.is/s/hsn which blocks fetching; national schedule on HVEST page applies*
- ○ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Vesturlands (HVE)** (Samtök/opinbert · Vesturland) — Public health centres in West Iceland (Akranes, Borgarnes, Grundarfjörður, Hvammstangi, Ólafsvík, Stykkishólmur) with midwife care; child health care not read. · [hve.is](https://www.hve.is) · *hve.is redirects to island.is*
- ○ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Suðurlands (HSU)** (Samtök/opinbert · Suðurland) — Public health centres in South Iceland (Selfoss, Vestmannaeyjar and others); child health care not read. · [hsu.is](https://www.hsu.is) · *hsu.is redirects to island.is*
- ○ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Suðurnesja (HSS)** (Samtök/opinbert · Suðurnes) — Public health institution for Reykjanesbær and Suðurnes; child health care not read. · [hss.is](https://www.hss.is) · *hss.is redirects to island.is*
- ○ **Heilbrigðisstofnun Austurlands (HSA)** (Samtök/opinbert · Austurland) — Public health institution for East Iceland; child health care not read. · [hsa.is](https://www.hsa.is) · *hsa.is redirects to island.is*
- ✓ **Heilsuvera** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — Health portal with 'Mínar síður' for booking appointments and messaging health staff; medicines and vaccinations information section. · [heilsuvera.is](https://www.heilsuvera.is) · *Page did not name child health care specifically*

### G21-03 · Fæðingarorlof, barnabætur og réttindi — Parental leave, benefits & rights (agencies)

**Problem:** Applying and getting paid  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 1 (+4 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Few agencies carry this topic: Fæðingarorlofssjóður (run by Vinnumálastofnun) pays parental leave, Skatturinn pays child benefits, and the union federations explain rights. Tryggingastofnun and Sjúkratryggingar are relevant but their sites redirect to island.is, which blocks fetching, so they are not listed. Applications for leave payments must be in at least six weeks before the due date.

- ○ **Fæðingarorlofssjóður (Vinnumálastofnun)** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Parental Leave Fund run by the Directorate of Labour: written application at least six weeks before the due date, payments in arrears on the first working day of each month. · [faedingarorlof.is](https://www.faedingarorlof.is) · *Facts taken from ASÍ's labour-law site; faedingarorlof.is redirects to island.is/s/vinnumalastofnun/foreldrar, which could not be fetched*
- ✓ **Skatturinn - barnabætur** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Tax authority pays child benefits four times a year (1 Feb, 1 May, 1 June, 1 Oct), reduced above income limits; extra 130,000 kr per child under 7; calculator provided. · [skatturinn.is/einstaklingar/vaxtabaetur-og-barnabaetur/barnabaetur](https://www.skatturinn.is/einstaklingar/vaxtabaetur-og-barnabaetur/barnabaetur/) · *Also a page for benefits in the year of birth; 2026 income limits 5.844m single, 11.688m couple*
- ✓ **ASÍ - Vinnuréttarvefur (fæðingar- og foreldraorlof)** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Union federation's guide to birth leave, parental leave, payments, applications, special rules, birth grants for people outside the labour market or in full-time study, and workplace protection for pregnant workers. · [vinnurettur.asi.is/vinnurettarvefur/vinnurettur/faedingar-og-foreldraorlof](https://vinnurettur.asi.is/vinnurettarvefur/vinnurettur/faedingar-og-foreldraorlof/) · *Covers Act 144/2020 and Act 77/2022 (bereavement leave); sub-pages for each topic*
- ✓ **BSRB - fæðingar- og foreldraorlof** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Public-sector union federation's page on parental-leave rights, with links to its 18 member unions and the Klettur grants fund. · [bsrb.is/is/vinnurettur/starfsaevin/faedingar-og-foreldraorlof](https://www.bsrb.is/is/vinnurettur/starfsaevin/faedingar-og-foreldraorlof) · *Page content not read, only link seen*
- ✓ **Ísland.is** (Vettvangur/app · Landið allt) — National government portal now hosting most agencies' pages (health institutions, Vinnumálastofnun, Landlæknir, Sjúkratryggingar) and citizens' applications. · [island.is](https://island.is/) · *Returns 403 to WebFetch; seen via many search hits and redirects from agency domains*

### G21-04 · Stuðningsfélög foreldra — Parent support NGOs (preemies, twins, heart children, adoption, bereavement)

**Problem:** Finding others in the same situation  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 0 (+6 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Six established NGOs fit: heart children (Neistinn), adoption, chronically ill children (Umhyggja), grief (Sorgarmiðstöð), disability rights (Þroskahjálp) and child protection (Barnaheill). Societies for preemies, twins and parents who lose a baby (e.g. Gleym mér ei) exist but their sites did not load, so they are not listed.

- ✓ **Neistinn - styrktarfélag hjartveikra barna** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Support society (since 1995, about 500 families) for children with heart defects: parent peer support, Hjartamömmur group, events, holiday houses and a grant fund. · [neistinn.is](https://www.neistinn.is) · *Families of children and young people with heart defects*
- ✓ **Íslensk ættleiðing** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Only licensed intercountry adoption agency (since 1978) with guidance, lectures, a lending library, post-adoption follow-up, grants and member events; annual membership 5,400 kr. · [isadopt.is](https://www.isadopt.is) · *Also services for adult adoptees*
- ✓ **Umhyggja - félag langveikra barna** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Association for families of chronically ill children (about 400 members, 20 member associations): grants, counselling, holiday cabins, an apartment in Reykjavík, siblings' programme and legal advice. · [umhyggja.is](https://www.umhyggja.is) · *Cabins at Brekkuskógur and Vaðlaborgir, apartment in Borgarnes*
- ✓ **Sorgarmiðstöð** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Grief centre offering support groups, counselling, guides for grieving people, children and relatives, and events such as Children in Grief Day. · [sorgarmidstod.is](https://www.sorgarmidstod.is) · *Lífsgæðasetur, Hafnarfjörður; no separate programme for bereaved parents described on page*
- ✓ **Þroskahjálp** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Rights organisation for people with intellectual disabilities and their families, with advice, the 'Fötluð börn' information hub and family events including information in English. · [throskahjalp.is](https://www.throskahjalp.is) · *Nonprofit funded mostly by donations*
- ✓ **Barnaheill** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Child-rights NGO offering the 'Líkaminn minn' booklet for ages 0-6, an online lecture for parents on warning signs, and violence-prevention courses. · [barnaheill.is](https://barnaheill.is/) · *Save the Children Iceland; no helpline listed on page*

### G21-05 · Stéttarfélög og sjúkrasjóðir — Unions & sick funds (grants for parents)

**Problem:** Unclaimed grants and benefits  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 0 (+8 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Dozens of unions each run their own sick fund; VR is the only one seen paying an explicit birth grant (100,000 kr), Efling reimburses physiotherapy, psychologist visits and gym costs, others publish only generic sick-fund rules. Birth-centre and parenting-course providers say many unions reimburse fees, so unions are natural content partners.

- ✓ **VR (Sjúkrasjóður VR)** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Commerce and office workers' union: birth grant 100,000 kr per child born after 25 March 2026 for members taking at least 75% of parental leave, up to 250,000 kr for IVF or adoption. · [vr.is/styrkir-sjodir/sjukrasjodur-vr/styrkir](https://www.vr.is/styrkir-sjodir/sjukrasjodur-vr/styrkir/) · **Discount:** Birth grant 100,000 kr; IVF or adoption up to 250,000 kr; gym costs via VR varasjóður · *Needs 12 of last 18 months of contributions for birth grant; usable until child is 24 months; also covers illness and accidents of children (page not read)*
- ✓ **Efling stéttarfélag** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Union grants for members: physiotherapy up to 2,500 kr per visit (15 visits a year), psychologist sessions up to 10,000 kr (15 a year), health grant 23,000 kr, IVF up to 100,000 kr. · [efling.is/styrkir](https://www.efling.is/styrkir) · **Discount:** Physiotherapy, psychology and gym reimbursements (up to 75-100% of cost within caps) · *No birth grant seen on page; membership fees must have been paid for 6 months; receipts under 12 months old*
- ✓ **Sameyki stéttarfélag í almannaþjónustu** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Public-service union whose members apply via Mínar síður for sick-fund, health and prevention grants; parent-specific grants not listed on the overview page. · [sameyki.is/styrkir-og-sjodir](https://www.sameyki.is/styrkir-og-sjodir/) · *BSRB member; health-grant team handles queries*
- ✓ **Eining-Iðja** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri + Norðurland) — Union for Eyjafjörður and North Iceland with a sick fund (grants, daily allowances), education funds and a parental-leave rights page; rules published as a regulation PDF. · [ein.is](https://www.ein.is) · *Amounts only in the sick-fund regulation PDF; no birth grant seen on front page*
- ✓ **Framsýn stéttarfélag** (Samtök/opinbert · Norðurland) — Húsavík-based union with a sick fund, holiday homes and campsite grant; parent grants not stated on the front page. · [framsyn.is/framsyn/starfsreglur-sjukrasjods-framsyn](https://framsyn.is/framsyn/starfsreglur-sjukrasjods-framsyn/) · *Sick-fund rules page holds the details; also covers staff association in Húsavík*
- ✓ **BSRB** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Federation of 18 public-sector unions with a parental-leave and parental-rights information page and the Klettur grants fund. · [bsrb.is/is/vinnurettur/starfsaevin/faedingar-og-foreldraorlof](https://www.bsrb.is/is/vinnurettur/starfsaevin/faedingar-og-foreldraorlof) · *Member unions include Sameyki, Kjölur, Sjúkraliðafélag Íslands and municipal staff associations*
- ✓ **ASÍ - Vinnuréttarvefur** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Labour-law site of the national union federation explaining birth leave, parental leave, payments, applications and protection against dismissal. · [vinnurettur.asi.is/vinnurettarvefur/vinnurettur/faedingar-og-foreldraorlof](https://vinnurettur.asi.is/vinnurettarvefur/vinnurettur/faedingar-og-foreldraorlof/) · *Covers Act 144/2020 and bereavement leave after a child's death or stillbirth*
- ✓ **BHM** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Association of university-educated workers with a 'Styrkir og sjóðir' page and holiday fund; parent-specific grants not shown on front page. · [bhm.is](https://www.bhm.is) · *Details on bhm.is/styrkir-og-sjodir*

### G21-06 · Slysavarnir og skyndihjálp (félagasamtök) — Safety & first-aid NGOs

**Problem:** Free courses and safety guidance  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 2 (+4 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Two national NGOs carry the field: Slysavarnafélagið Landsbjörg (child-safety brochure, new-parent safety packages via local branches) and the Red Cross (4-hour 'Slys og veikindi barna' course in Reykjavik, Akureyri and Egilsstaðir). Fire-safety, emergency-line and insurer pages add content partners; a private infant first-aid school is the only paid-course competitor.

- ✓ **Slysavarnafélagið Landsbjörg** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — National rescue and accident-prevention NGO publishing an 'Öryggi barna' home-safety brochure (Icelandic, English, Polish); local branches hand new parents child-safety gift packs. · [landsbjorg.is/heimili-og-naersamfelag](https://landsbjorg.is/heimili-og-naersamfelag) · *Packs can include outlet covers, cabinet locks, bath thermometer, hearing protectors, sometimes a smoke detector; also available via health centres; main site landsbjorg.is*
- ✓ **Rauði krossinn á Íslandi - skyndihjálp** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — First-aid courses including the 4-hour 'Slys og veikindi barna' (child accidents and illness) for parents and carers, 13,200 kr, plus a gift voucher; also runs a national helpline and chat (Hjálparsíminn). · [raudikrossinn.is/verkefni/skyndihjalp](https://www.raudikrossinn.is/verkefni/skyndihjalp/) · *Sessions listed in Reykjavík, Akureyri and Egilsstaðir; child course run by local branches; also English courses*
- ✓ **Eldvarnabandalagið** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Fire-prevention partnership (since 2010) with home fire-safety guidance and educational material online. · [eldvarnabandalagid.is](https://www.eldvarnabandalagid.is) · *Alliance, not a seller; materials online*
- ✓ **Neyðarlínan 112** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Emergency number 112 with web chat and the 112 app for people who find it hard to call; also advice pages for parents. · [112.is](https://www.112.is) · *Web chat offered as an alternative to calling*
- ✓ **Bráðaskólinn** (Þjónusta · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Private school teaching infant first aid and acute illness (choking, CPR, fever) in Icelandic and English, 13,600 kr; gift vouchers. · [bradaskolinn.is](https://bradaskolinn.is) · **Discount:** Discounts go first to its mailing list · *Private paid alternative to the NGO courses; venue not stated; also hosted at Fæðingarheimili Reykjavíkur*
- ✓ **Sjóvá - forvarnir** (Þjónusta · Landið allt) — Insurer's prevention section with child-safety pages 'Slysavarnir barna' and 'Barnabílstólar' (car seats). · [sjova.is/forvarnir](https://www.sjova.is/forvarnir) · *Private insurer, not an NGO; page contents not read, only the link names*

### G21-07 · Bókasöfn og barnamenning — Libraries & children's culture

**Problem:** Free baby-friendly activities  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Margir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 0 (+6 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Municipal libraries are the strongest free baby-friendly option (free cards for under-18s; parent mornings in Akureyri; open play in Hafnarfjörður); the Symphony's free 'Pínulitli tónsprotinn' is the clearest baby concert. Theatres show family productions but none for babies on their front pages. Kópavogur, Garðabær and other libraries not confirmed (site errors).

- ✓ **Borgarbókasafnið** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Reykjavík city library with 8 branches (Árbær, Gerðuberg, Grófin, Kléberg, Kringlan, Spöngin, Sólheimar, Úlfarsárdalur); events listed per branch; cards free for under-18s. · [borgarbokasafn.is](https://www.borgarbokasafn.is) · **Discount:** Child and teen cards free; adults 3,300 kr per year; Menningarkort 8,700 kr also covers Listasafn Reykjavíkur and Borgarsögusafn · *No baby events on its main events page when read; check branch pages*
- ✓ **Bókasafn Hafnarfjarðar** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Library with a 'Krílahorn' for babies and toddlers, free open-play 'Opni leikskólinn Memmm' (Mon 12-14, Thu 10-12, no registration), sensory play and craft sessions; children's cards free. · [bokasafnhafnarfjardar.is](https://www.bokasafnhafnarfjardar.is) · **Discount:** Children under 18 free · *Stroller parking by the small arched entrance; storytimes listed on events page*
- ✓ **Amtsbókasafnið á Akureyri** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri) — Akureyri's main library hosts weekly Foreldramorgnar every Tuesday 10:00-12:00 in the children's department, with coffee, open to all parents. · [akureyri.is/amtsbokasafnid](https://www.akureyri.is/amtsbokasafnid) · *Card prices not on page; part of Akureyri municipality site*
- ✓ **Sinfóníuhljómsveit Íslands** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Symphony orchestra running children's series including 'Pínulitli tónsprotinn' baby concerts in Harpa (free entry), plus Litli tónsprotinn, Barnastund and Maxímús Músíkús. · [sinfonia.is](https://www.sinfonia.is) · **Discount:** Free admission to Pínulitli tónsprotinn (20 Feb session seen) · *Public cultural institution; details of other child series not read*
- ✓ **Þjóðminjasafn Íslands** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — National museum in Reykjavik with free entry for children under 18; no toddler-specific programme seen on front page. · [thjodminjasafn.is](https://www.thjodminjasafn.is) · **Discount:** Free for under-18s · *Education pages not read*
- ✓ **Menningarfélag Akureyrar (Hof, Samkomuhúsið)** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri) — Akureyri's culture house operator listing family shows such as a Skoppa and Skrítla Christmas party and Skilaboðaskjóðan; no baby-specific shows seen. · [mak.is](https://www.mak.is) · *Age recommendations on individual event pages*

### G21-08 · Sveitarfélög: leikskólar og dagvistun — Municipalities: preschool & daycare applications

**Problem:** How and when to apply  
**Stig:** 3–12 mán · **Tenging:** Sterk · **Eftirspurn:** Allir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 0 (+15 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Every municipality runs its own preschool (leikskóli) and childminder (dagforeldrar) system with different ages and rules; Reykjavik is documented in detail (waiting lists from 6 months, autumn places allocated in March by age). Many front pages only link to the relevant sections, so rules must be quoted from each municipality's own pages. 15 municipalities listed; the other ~50 not checked.

- ✓ **Reykjavíkurborg - leikskólar** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Apply in the Vala portal; children join waiting lists from 6 months, children who turn 18 months by 1 September get an autumn offer, places are allocated by age from March. · [reykjavik.is/innritun-i-leikskola](https://reykjavik.is/innritun-i-leikskola) · *Leikskólareiknir estimates waiting-list position; fee discounts page /afslattur-af-leikskolagjaldi; priority places from 12 months*
- ○ **Kópavogsbær** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Municipal site with services, forms and fee-schedule sections where preschool and childminder information sits; front page shows no preschool details. · [kopavogur.is](https://www.kopavogur.is) · *Look under Þjónusta and Gjaldskrár; service desk phone on site*
- ✓ ⚠ **Hafnarfjarðarkaupstaður** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Municipal pages for preschools, childminders (dagforeldrar) and home payments (heimgreiðslur) for parents caring for children at home, plus fee schedules. · [hafnarfjordur.is/thjonusta/skolar-og-born/leikskolar/leikskolar](https://hafnarfjordur.is/thjonusta/skolar-og-born/leikskolar/leikskolar/) · *Dagforeldrar page: hafnarfjordur.is/thjonusta/skolar-og-born/dagforeldrar/*
- ○ **Garðabær** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Municipal site with a 'Skólar og frístund' section for preschool and childcare information; front page shows no preschool details. · [gardabaer.is](https://www.gardabaer.is) · *Service desk contact on site*
- ✓ **Mosfellsbær** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Municipal sections for preschools and childminders under 'Börn og ungmenni'; page contents not read. · [mos.is/ibuar/born-og-ungmenni/leikskolar](https://mos.is/ibuar/born-og-ungmenni/leikskolar/) · *Dagforeldrar page: mos.is/ibuar/born-og-ungmenni/dagforeldrar/*
- ✓ **Seltjarnarnesbær** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Municipal pages for preschools, an infant preschool (ungbarnaleikskóli), admission rules, childminder subsidies and a preschool fee calculator. · [seltjarnarnes.is/is/ibuar/menntun-og-skolar/leikskoli](https://www.seltjarnarnes.is/is/ibuar/menntun-og-skolar/leikskoli) · *Admission rules and fee calculator linked from front page; also payments to parents for home childcare*
- ✓ **Akureyrarbær** (Samtök/opinbert · Akureyri) — Municipal hub 'Skólar og dagvistun' for preschool and daycare information, plus fee schedules, leisure grant and child-protection pages. · [akureyri.is/skola-og-fristundastarf](https://www.akureyri.is/skola-og-fristundastarf) · *Front page does not show application rules*
- ○ **Reykjanesbær** (Samtök/opinbert · Suðurnes) — Municipal site with a 'Börn 0-6 ára' services section and child-protection pages; preschool application details not visible on front page. · [reykjanesbaer.is](https://www.reykjanesbaer.is) · *Preschool pages not found from front page*
- ✓ **Sveitarfélagið Árborg** (Samtök/opinbert · Suðurland) — Municipal pages for preschools, childminders, a 'Skólar, daggæsla, frístundastarf og heimgreiðslur' e-application page and fee schedules. · [arborg.is/ibuar/skolar-og-daggaesla/leikskolar](https://www.arborg.is/ibuar/skolar-og-daggaesla/leikskolar/) · *Digital applications page marked as in progress*
- ✓ **Akraneskaupstaður** (Samtök/opinbert · Vesturland) — Municipal preschool and childminder pages plus fee schedules; four municipal preschools have their own sites. · [akranes.is/thjonusta/menntun/leikskolar](https://www.akranes.is/thjonusta/menntun/leikskolar) · *Preschools Akrasel, Garðasel, Teigasel and Vallarsel; dagforeldrar page akranes.is/thjonusta/menntun/dagforeldrar*
- ✓ **Sveitarfélagið Skagafjörður** (Samtök/opinbert · Norðurland) — Municipal pages for preschools and for childminders and parental payments (dagforeldrar / foreldragreiðslur) plus fee schedules. · [skagafjordur.is](https://www.skagafjordur.is) · *Sauðárkrókur area; relative links /is/thjonusta/skolarogborn/leikskolar and /dagforeldrar*
- ✓ **Norðurþing** (Samtök/opinbert · Norðurland) — Municipal preschool page for Húsavík area under Þjónusta > Menntun, with fee schedule and booking-appointment link. · [nordurthing.is/is/thjonusta/menntun/leikskolar](https://www.nordurthing.is/is/thjonusta/menntun/leikskolar) · *Page contents not read*
- ✓ **Múlaþing** (Samtök/opinbert · Austurland) — Municipal pages for preschools, practical preschool information, childminders (dagforeldrar) and a childcare subsidy (daggæsluframlag) for East Iceland. · [mulathing.is](https://www.mulathing.is) · *Egilsstaðir area; general applications page at /is/stjornsysla/starfsemin/eydublod-umsoknir-og-tilkynningar*
- ○ **Vestmannaeyjabær** (Samtök/opinbert · Suðurland) — Municipal site with residents' section and fee schedules; recent news on after-school care fees; no preschool details on front page. · [vestmannaeyjar.is](https://www.vestmannaeyjar.is) · *Check /ibuar for childcare pages*
- ○ **Fjarðabyggð** (Samtök/opinbert · Austurland) — Municipal site with residents' section and fee schedules for East Iceland; no preschool details on front page. · [fjardabyggd.is](https://www.fjardabyggd.is) · *Check /ibuar for childcare pages*

### G21-N1 · Geðheilbrigði og stuðningslínur (félagasamtök) — Mental-health NGOs, peer support & helplines *(new — added during research)*

**Problem:** Not coping and not knowing who to call  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 0 (+6 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Four national NGOs offer free or peer support (Geðhjálp counselling, Píeta crisis line and centres including Akureyri, Hugarafl peer groups, Red Cross helpline) plus the church's family service and the 112 chat. Good content partners for a 'when it gets too much' page; none is an advertiser.

- ✓ **Geðhjálp** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — National mental-health organisation offering free counselling by trained counsellors, support for relatives, rights advocacy and education. · [gedhjalp.is](https://gedhjalp.is/) · **Discount:** Free counselling · *Page does not mention postnatal mental health specifically*
- ✓ **Píeta Ísland** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Suicide and self-harm prevention organisation with a 24/7 helpline, free counselling for people at risk and worried relatives, grief support and local centres including Akureyri. · [pieta.is](https://www.pieta.is) · **Discount:** Free counselling, no referral needed · *Centres in Akureyri, Ísafjörður, Reyðarfjörður and Þorlákshöfn; groups for ages 18+*
- ✓ **Hugarafl** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið) — Peer-run mental-health community offering peer support, recovery work, mental-health education, group activities and a free group for relatives. · [hugarafl.is](https://www.hugarafl.is) · **Discount:** Free introductory meetings for newcomers · *Based in Reykjavík; next intro meeting 8 January 2027*
- ✓ **Rauði krossinn - Hjálparsíminn og netspjall** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Red Cross helpline and online chat for anyone needing someone to talk to, free and open to all. · [raudikrossinn.is/verkefni/innanlandsverkefni/heilbrigdi-og-velferd/hjalparsiminn-1717-og-netspjallid](https://www.raudikrossinn.is/verkefni/innanlandsverkefni/heilbrigdi-og-velferd/hjalparsiminn-1717-og-netspjallid/) · *Page seen as a link on the Red Cross site; details not read*
- ✓ **Fjölskylduþjónusta kirkjunnar** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Church-run pastoral care with one-hour grief, family, cohabitation and pastoral interviews; open to people across Iceland. · [kirkjan.is/salgaeslu-og-fjolskylduthjonusta](https://kirkjan.is/salgaeslu-og-fjolskylduthjonusta) · *Run from a parish hall in Reykjavík; also reachable via local parishes*
- ✓ **Neyðarlínan 112 - netspjall** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Emergency line with a web chat and an app for people who find it hard to call; for acute crises. · [112.is](https://www.112.is) · *Emergencies only*

### G21-N2 · Aðstoð við foreldra í fjárhagsvanda (hjálparsamtök) — Charities helping families in financial hardship *(new — added during research)*

**Problem:** Cannot afford food or baby essentials  
**Stig:** Allt tímabilið · **Tenging:** Miðlungs · **Eftirspurn:** Sumir · **Mögulegir auglýsendur:** 0 (+3 samtök/opinber)

*Market:* Three charities seen: Mæðrastyrksnefnd Hafnarfjarðar (mainly Christmas aid), Hjálparstarf kirkjunnar (emergency and Christmas aid) and Fjölskylduhjálp Íslands (food aid in Reykjavik and Reykjanesbær). Mæðrastyrksnefnd Reykjavíkur and other local committees (e.g. Akureyri) exist but their sites could not be read. None of the pages mention baby items specifically.

- ✓ **Mæðrastyrksnefnd Hafnarfjarðar** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið (Hafnarfjörður)) — Charity helping low-income families with children in Hafnarfjörður, mainly with grocery gift cards and Christmas gifts for children; online application. · [maedrastyrksnefnd.is](https://www.maedrastyrksnefnd.is) · *Aid concentrated before Christmas; no baby items mentioned on page*
- ✓ **Hjálparstarf kirkjunnar** (Samtök/opinbert · Landið allt) — Church charity giving emergency aid and annual Christmas aid (mainly food gift cards) to low-income families, plus empowerment and advocacy programmes. · [hjalparstarfkirkjunnar.is](https://www.hjalparstarfkirkjunnar.is/) · *Application route not described on front page; aid assessed individually*
- ✓ **Fjölskylduhjálp Íslands** (Samtök/opinbert · Höfuðborgarsvæðið + Suðurnes) — Family-aid charity running a food fund and food distribution days for families in need, in Reykjavík and Reykjanesbær. · [fjolskylduhjalp.is](https://www.fjolskylduhjalp.is) · *No Akureyri branch seen; baby items and clothing not mentioned on page*
